# Voxel Wars

Fort War ("Undermine") rebuilt on destructible voxel terrain, in the spirit of Donkey Kong Bananza: a sparse signed
distance voxel world (0.25 m), Surface Nets / dual contouring meshing in workers, box3d physics (wasm) with detached
terrain chunks that fall, shatter and settle back into diggable terrain.

Play: https://sh1ftmaker.github.io/voxel-wars/

```
npm install
npm run dev      # http://localhost:5180
```

## Play online

On the title card, **Play online** opens a new room and puts its invite link on the clipboard; send it to your
friends. Or open `https://sh1ftmaker.github.io/voxel-wars/?room=<any name>` yourself (add `&name=<you>` to pick a
name). The first player in a room hosts: bots run on their machine, and the host keeps health, crates, the score and
the clock. If the host leaves, the next player takes over. Players are put on the team with fewer people; bots fill
both teams (the host's Bots toggle).

The rooms run on a small Cloudflare Worker (`server/`, PartyServer + Durable Objects). To run your own:

```
cd server
npm install
npx wrangler deploy                 # prints https://voxel-wars.<your subdomain>.workers.dev
```

then point the game at it with `?server=wss://voxel-wars.<your subdomain>.workers.dev` (or change `DEFAULT_SERVER` in
`src/net/transports.js`). Locally: `npx wrangler dev --port 8787` in `server/` and `?room=test&server=ws://127.0.0.1:8787`.
`?room=test&local=1` makes a room of browser tabs without any server. Test: `node tools/test/mp.mjs` (three players,
each in its own headless browser; `node tools/test/mp.mjs <page url> deployed` goes through the deployed server).

See `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`. The Twin Forts level, textures (Poly Haven, CC0) and game design come from
[WorldParty](https://github.com/zalo/WorldParty) (MIT).

### src/config.js
```
/** World-wide constants shared by every module. Units are metres. */

/** Edge of one voxel (the spacing of the density lattice). */
export const VOXEL = 0.25;
/** Samples per chunk edge. A chunk owns samples [c*CHUNK, (c+1)*CHUNK) on each axis. */
export const CHUNK = 32;
export const CHUNK_M = VOXEL * CHUNK;
/** Stored signed distance units per voxel: stored = round(d / VOXEL * SDF_SCALE), clamped to Int8.
 *  Distances are therefore exact to about 1/32 voxel within ±4 voxels of the surface and saturated beyond. */
export const SDF_SCALE = 32;
export const SDF_MAX = 127;
/** Mesher padding: a chunk's mesh job sees samples [-PAD, CHUNK + PAD) around it. */
export const PAD = 2;

/** World extent in chunks: [min, max) per axis. Below min y is unbreakable bedrock; everything else outside is air. */
export const WORLD_CHUNKS = { min: [-10, -2, -5], max: [10, 2, 5] };
/** Samples this close to the bottom of the world are never carved (bedrock). */
export const BEDROCK_SAMPLES = 2;
```

### src/main.js
```
/**
 * Entry point. Builds the shared context every subsystem receives and runs the frame loop.
 *
 *   ctx = { renderer, scene, camera, sun, world (VoxelWorld), terrain (TerrainRenderer), physics (Physics),
 *           destruction (Destruction), params, addSystem(system), events }
 * A system is any object with optional update(dt) (called every frame after physics, in the order added).
 * ctx.events is an EventTarget for cross-module game events (see docs/ARCHITECTURE.md for the event list).
 */
import * as THREE from 'three/webgpu';
import { VoxelWorld } from './voxel/VoxelWorld.js';
import { voxelize } from './voxel/voxelize.js';
import { TerrainSync } from './voxel/TerrainSync.js';
import { Level } from './level/fortwar.js';
import { TerrainRenderer } from './render/TerrainRenderer.js';
import { Environment, configureRenderer } from './render/Environment.js';
import { Physics } from './physics/Physics.js';
import { Destruction } from './physics/Destruction.js';
import { FlyCamera } from './dev/FlyCamera.js';
import { FortWar } from './game/FortWar.js';
import { Profiler } from './dev/Profiler.js';
import { SoftGround } from './soft/SoftGround.js';
import { createNet } from './net/index.js';

const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
const status = (s) => { $('loading').textContent = s; };
const nextFrame = () => new Promise(r => requestAnimationFrame(r));
const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);

const renderer = new THREE.WebGPURenderer({ antialias: true, trackTimestamp: params.has('profile') }); // GPU timings for ?profile
await renderer.init();
configureRenderer(renderer); // pixel ratio per device, ACES, shadows (src/render/Environment.js)
renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);
document.body.prepend(renderer.domElement);
const prof = new Profiler({ renderer, params, ctx: {} }); // ?profile or F9: record frame timings to a file (src/dev/Profiler.js)
prof.loadMark('renderer');

const scene = new THREE.Scene();
const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(70, innerWidth / innerHeight, 0.05, 500);
camera.position.set(-70, 14, 0);
const env = new Environment({ scene, camera, renderer }); // sky, fog, hemisphere + sun with camera-following shadows
const sun = env.sun;
addEventListener('resize', () => { camera.aspect = innerWidth / innerHeight; camera.updateProjectionMatrix(); renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight); });

status('Building the world...');
await nextFrame();
const world = new VoxelWorld();
let t0 = performance.now();
// the terrain worker (src/voxel/TerrainSync.js) owns the world and its edits; `world` here is its read-only replica
// (?terrainWorker=0: the same core runs on the main thread)
let sync = null;
if(params.get('terrainWorker') === 'sync') voxelize(world, new Level()); // no worker at all: edits on the main thread
else await (sync = new TerrainSync(world, { worker: params.get('terrainWorker') !== '0' })).init();
// the edit log (src/net): every terrain edit is sequenced and applied in order (single player: synchronously)
if(params.get('room')) status(`Joining room "${params.get('room')}"...`);
let net = null;
try { net = sync ? await createNet(params, sync) : null; }
catch(e) { status(`Could not reach the room server (${e.message}). Reload to try again, or play offline without ?room= in the address.`); throw e; }
console.log(`world ready in ${(performance.now() - t0).toFixed(0)} ms`);
prof.loadMark('world');
const terrain = new TerrainRenderer(world, scene, camera);
status('Starting physics...');
const physics = await Physics.create({ world, terrain, scene });
terrain.remeshAll();

const systems = [];
const ctx = { renderer, scene, camera, sun, env, world, terrain, physics, params, net, events: new EventTarget(), addSystem: (s, name) => { s.profileName = name || s.constructor.name; systems.push(s); return s; } };
prof.ctx = ctx;
ctx.destruction = new Destruction(ctx);
window.ctx = ctx; // for debugging and headless tests

// ---- game systems are added here (one line each) ----
ctx.addSystem(params.get('fly') === '1' ? new FlyCamera(ctx) : new FortWar(ctx), 'game');
ctx.addSystem(new SoftGround(ctx), 'soft'); // soft ground wake zones (src/soft), ctx.soft
net?.ready(); // the edit handlers are in: apply the log (a late joiner catches up here)

status('Meshing...');
while(terrain.pending > 0) await nextFrame();
physics.flushColliders(); // terrain colliders are otherwise built a few per frame
$('loading').style.display = 'none';
prof.ready();

const timer = new THREE.Timer();
const loggedErrors = new Set();
let frames = 0, fpsT = 0, fps = 0;
renderer.setAnimationLoop((now) => {
    prof.frameStart(now);
    timer.update(now);
    const dt = Math.min(timer.getDelta(), 0.1);
    physics.step(dt);
    prof.mark('physics');
    // one failing system must not stop the game: log each distinct error once and keep running
    for(const s of systems) {
        try { s.update?.(dt); } catch(e) { if(!loggedErrors.has(e.message)) { loggedErrors.add(e.message); console.error(e); } }
        prof.mark(s.profileName);
    }
    env.update(); // after the camera moved: sky dome and shadow box follow it
    prof.mark('env');
    renderer.render(scene, camera);
    prof.mark('render');
    frames++; fpsT += dt;
    if(fpsT > 0.5) { fps = frames / fpsT; frames = 0; fpsT = 0; }
    $('stats').textContent = `${fps.toFixed(0)} fps · chunks ${terrain.meshes.size} · pending ${terrain.pending} · bodies ${physics.props.size} · soft ground: ${ctx.soft?.label ?? 'off'}`;
    prof.frameEnd();
});
```

### server/src/index.js
```
/**
 * Voxel Wars room server: one Durable Object per room (PartyServer), running the same Sequencer the browser rooms use
 * (src/net/sequencer.js). It numbers terrain edits, relays game messages between clients and keeps the edit log and
 * the host's world snapshot, so late joiners catch up and a room survives its host leaving. It never simulates
 * anything: the host client runs physics, bots and the match.
 *
 *   wss://<worker>/parties/room/<name>?cid=<client id>     one socket per client (a reconnect reuses its cid)
 *
 * Wire: every message is one binary frame in the net codec (src/net/codec.js). Server-only messages:
 *   { t: 'ping', c } -> { t: 'pong', c }
 * Limits: messages over MAX_MSG bytes (MAX_SNAPSHOT for the host's snapshots) are dropped; edits are rate limited
 * per client (token bucket: EDIT_RATE per s, EDIT_BURST), other messages at MSG_RATE.
 *
 * Storage (survives the object being evicted when the room is idle): 'snap' (+ 'snap:<i>' parts, values are capped at
 * 128 KiB), 'e:<seq>' log entries after the snapshot, 'meta' { seq, game, at }. A room idle for IDLE_RESET_MS starts
 * a fresh match on the next join.
 */
import { Server, routePartykitRequest } from 'partyserver';
import { Sequencer } from '../../src/net/sequencer.js';
import { encode, decode } from '../../src/net/codec.js';

const MAX_MSG = 512 * 1024, MAX_SNAPSHOT = 4 * 1024 * 1024;
const EDIT_RATE = 25, EDIT_BURST = 60, MSG_RATE = 120, MSG_BURST = 240;
const PART = 120 * 1024;
const IDLE_RESET_MS = 30 * 60 * 1000;
const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(10, '0');

export class Room extends Server {
    static options = { hibernate: false };

    async onStart() {
        this.conns = new Map();   // cid -> connection
        this.cidOf = new Map();   // connection id -> cid
        this.bucket = new Map();  // cid -> { edits, msgs, t }
        this.seq = new Sequencer({ send: (to, m) => this._send(to, m) });
        this.seq.onLogged = (entry) => this._persistEntry(entry);
        this.seq.onSnapshot = (snap) => this._persistSnap(snap);
        this.seq.onReset = () => this._wipe();
        this.dirty = Promise.resolve();
        const st = this.ctx.storage;
        const meta = await st.get('meta');
        if(meta && Date.now() - (meta.at || 0) > IDLE_RESET_MS) { await st.deleteAll(); return; }
        if(!meta) return;
        this.seq.seq = meta.seq | 0;
        this.seq.game = meta.game || null;
        const snapMeta = await st.get('snap');
        if(snapMeta) {
            const parts = [];
            for(let i = 0; i < snapMeta.parts; i++) parts.push(await st.get(`snap:${i}`));
            const data = new Uint8Array(snapMeta.bytes);
            let o = 0; for(const p of parts) { data.set(new Uint8Array(p), o); o += p.byteLength; }
            this.seq.snap = { seq: snapMeta.seq, data };
        }
        const entries = await st.list({ prefix: 'e:' });
        for(const [, v] of entries) this.seq.log.push(decode(new Uint8Array(v)));
        this.seq.log.sort((a, b) => a.seq - b.seq);
    }

    onConnect(conn, ctx) {
        const url = new URL(ctx.request.url);
        const cid = (url.searchParams.get('cid') || conn.id).slice(0, 32);
        // a reconnect: the old socket (if still half-open) is closed without counting as leaving
        const old = this.conns.get(cid);
        if(old && old !== conn) { this.cidOf.delete(old.id); try { old.close(4000, 'replaced'); } catch(e) {} }
        this.conns.set(cid, conn);
        this.cidOf.set(conn.id, cid);
        if(this.seq.empty && this.lastEmpty && Date.now() - this.lastEmpty > IDLE_RESET_MS) this._wipe(true);
    }

    onMessage(conn, message) {
        const cid = this.cidOf.get(conn.id);
        if(!cid || typeof message === 'string') return;
        const bytes = new Uint8Array(message);
        let m;
        try { m = decode(bytes); } catch(e) { return; }
        if(bytes.length > (m.t === 'snapshot' ? MAX_SNAPSHOT : MAX_MSG)) return;
        if(m.t === 'ping') { conn.send(encode({ t: 'pong', c: m.c, n: this.seq.clients.length })); return; }
        if(!this._allow(cid, m.t === 'edit')) return;
        this.seq.receive(cid, m);
        this._touch();
    }

    onClose(conn) { this._gone(conn); }
    onError(conn) { this._gone(conn); }
    _gone(conn) {
        const cid = this.cidOf.get(conn.id);
        this.cidOf.delete(conn.id);
        if(!cid || this.conns.get(cid) !== conn) return; // replaced by a newer socket
        this.conns.delete(cid);
        this.bucket.delete(cid);
        this.seq.disconnect(cid);
        if(this.seq.empty) this.lastEmpty = Date.now();
        this._touch();
    }

    _allow(cid, edit) {
        const now = Date.now();
        let b = this.bucket.get(cid);
        if(!b) { b = { edits: EDIT_BURST, msgs: MSG_BURST, t: now }; this.bucket.set(cid, b); }
        const dt = (now - b.t) / 1000; b.t = now;
        b.edits = Math.min(EDIT_BURST, b.edits + dt * EDIT_RATE);
        b.msgs = Math.min(MSG_BURST, b.msgs + dt * MSG_RATE);
        if(edit) { if(b.edits < 1) return false; b.edits--; return true; }
        if(b.msgs < 1) return false;
        b.msgs--; return true;
    }

    _send(to, m) {
        const b = encode(m);
        if(to === '*') { for(const c of this.conns.values()) try { c.send(b); } catch(e) {} return; }
        const c = this.conns.get(to);
        if(c) try { c.send(b); } catch(e) {}
    }

    // ---- storage
    _touch() {
        if(this.touchT) return;
        this.touchT = setTimeout(() => { this.touchT = null; this._chain(() => this.ctx.storage.put('meta', { seq: this.seq.seq, game: this.seq.game, at: Date.now() })); }, 2000);
    }
    _chain(fn) { this.dirty = this.dirty.then(fn).catch(e => console.error('storage', e)); return this.dirty; }
    _persistEntry(entry) {
        const v = encode(entry);
        if(v.length > PART) return; // too big to keep (never happens with today's edits); a snapshot will cover it
        this._chain(() => this.ctx.storage.put(`e:${pad(entry.seq)}`, v.buffer.slice(v.byteOffset, v.byteOffset + v.byteLength)));
        this._touch();
    }
    _persistSnap(snap) {
        const data = snap.data, parts = Math.ceil(data.length / PART), seq = snap.seq;
        this._chain(async () => {
            const st = this.ctx.storage, puts = {};
            for(let i = 0; i < parts; i++) puts[`snap:${i}`] = data.slice(i * PART, (i + 1) * PART).buffer;
            const keys = Object.keys(puts);
            for(let i = 0; i < keys.length; i += 64) { const b = {}; for(const k of keys.slice(i, i + 64)) b[k] = puts[k]; await st.put(b); }
            await st.put('snap', { seq, parts, bytes: data.length });
            const old = [...(await st.list({ prefix: 'e:', end: `e:${pad(seq + 1)}` })).keys()];
            for(let i = 0; i < old.length; i += 128) await st.delete(old.slice(i, i + 128));
        });
    }
    _wipe(memory = false) {
        if(memory) { this.seq.log = []; this.seq.snap = null; this.seq.game = null; }
        this._chain(() => this.ctx.storage.deleteAll());
    }

    onRequest() { return new Response(JSON.stringify(this.seq.stats), { headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'access-control-allow-origin': '*' } }); }
}

export default {
    async fetch(request, env) {
        const res = await routePartykitRequest(request, env);
        if(res) return res;
        return new Response('Voxel Wars room server. Rooms: /parties/room/<name>', { status: 200, headers: { 'access-control-allow-origin': '*' } });
    },
};
```

### src/audio/Music.js
```
/** Music: "Gold Rush Heist" (assets/music/gold-rush-heist.mp3, from WorldParty's Undermine), looped under the game
 *  through the effects' master bus (so M mutes it). Streams from an <audio> element (decoding it whole would hold
 *  ~56 MB of samples). Fades in once audio starts; while dead it drops to half behind a lowpass; the last minute and
 *  carrying a crate push it a little louder. ?music=0 turns it off. */
const TRACK = 'assets/music/gold-rush-heist.mp3';
const LEVEL = 0.16;

export class Music {
    constructor(game, sfx) {
        this.game = game; this.sfx = sfx; this.started = false; this.state = '';
        this.enabled = game.ctx.params.get('music') !== '0';
        if(this.enabled) sfx.onReady(() => this.start());
        this.onVisibility = () => { if(!this.audio) return; if(document.hidden) this.audio.pause(); else this.play(); };
    }
    start() {
        if(this.started || !this.sfx.ready) return;
        try {
            const ctx = this.ctx = this.sfx.ctx;
            this.audio = new Audio(TRACK);
            this.audio.loop = true;
            this.lowpass = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); this.lowpass.type = 'lowpass'; this.lowpass.frequency.value = 18000; this.lowpass.Q.value = 0.3;
            this.bus = ctx.createGain(); this.bus.gain.value = 0;
            ctx.createMediaElementSource(this.audio).connect(this.lowpass);
            this.lowpass.connect(this.bus); this.bus.connect(this.sfx.master);
            document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', this.onVisibility);
            this.started = true;
            if(!document.hidden) this.play();
        } catch(e) { console.warn('music unavailable', e); }
    }
    play() { this.audio.play()?.then(() => { this.state = ''; }, (e) => { if(e?.name === 'NotAllowedError') { const retry = () => { removeEventListener('pointerdown', retry); this.play(); }; addEventListener('pointerdown', retry); } }); }
    update() {
        if(!this.started) return;
        const g = this.game, p = g.player;
        const dead = !p.alive || g.over;
        const hot = !dead && (p.carrying || g.timeLeft < 60);
        const s = dead ? 'dead' : hot ? 'hot' : 'calm';
        if(s === this.state) return;
        this.state = s;
        const t = this.ctx.currentTime;
        this.bus.gain.setTargetAtTime(LEVEL * (dead ? 0.5 : hot ? 1.35 : 1), t, 0.8);
        this.lowpass.frequency.setTargetAtTime(dead ? 600 : 18000, t, 0.6);
    }
}
```

### src/audio/Sfx.js
```
/** Sound effects, all synthesized in WebAudio (no files). The synth voices, the sound palette (D Mixolydian) and the
 *  output bus come from WorldParty's Undermine (src/client/Sfx.js, same author, MIT); the Sfx class at the bottom is
 *  new: it listens to the game's events (src/game/FortWar.js) and to ctx.events 'destroyed' / 'impact', and adds the
 *  Donkey Kong sounds (punch, rip, shatter) and material-coloured impacts.
 *
 *  The context starts on the first user gesture, as browsers require; every call is a no-op until then. M mutes.
 */

export const SCALE = [62, 64, 66, 67, 69, 71, 72]; // D Mixolydian in octave 4, as midi notes
export const midiHz = (m) => 440 * Math.pow(2, (m - 69) / 12);
/** Scale degree (0 = D, 7 = D an octave up, negatives allowed) to midi, `octave` octaves above the 4th. */
export const degreeMidi = (deg, octave = 0) => SCALE[((deg % 7) + 7) % 7] + 12 * (Math.floor(deg / 7) + octave);
const clamp = (v, a, b) => Math.max(a, Math.min(b, v));
const rnd = (a, b) => a + Math.random() * (b - a);
/** Distance attenuation for world sounds. */
export const atten = (d) => d <= 5 ? 1 : Math.max(0.03, 1 / (1 + (d - 5) / 18));
/** Lowpass cutoff by distance: high frequencies die first, so far events sound muffled. */
const muffle = (d, near = 8, full = 9000) => d > near ? full / (1 + (d - near) / 12) : 0;

/** Sound primitives bound to one context and output. Times are absolute on the context's clock. */
export class Synth {
    constructor(ctx, dest, reverbSend = null) { this.ctx = ctx; this.dest = dest; this.reverbSend = reverbSend; this._noise = null; }
    get now() { return this.ctx.currentTime; }

    /** Two seconds of white noise, shared by every noise source (each starts at a random offset). */
    noiseBuffer() {
        if(this._noise) return this._noise;
        let ctx = this.ctx, n = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * 2), b = ctx.createBuffer(1, n, ctx.sampleRate), d = b.getChannelData(0);
        let s = 0x9e3779b9;
        for(let i = 0; i < n; i++) { s ^= s << 13; s ^= s >>> 17; s ^= s << 5; d[i] = ((s >>> 0) / 2147483648) - 1; }
        return this._noise = b;
    }

    /** The output chain of one sound: [lowpass] -> [pan] -> dest, with a share into the reverb. Connect into the result. */
    bus({ pan = 0, lowpass = 0, send = 0 } = {}) {
        let ctx = this.ctx, head = this.dest;
        if(send > 0 && this.reverbSend) {
            let split = ctx.createGain(), g = ctx.createGain(); g.gain.value = send;
            split.connect(this.dest); split.connect(g); g.connect(this.reverbSend); head = split;
        }
        if(pan && ctx.createStereoPanner) { let p = ctx.createStereoPanner(); p.pan.value = clamp(pan, -1, 1); p.connect(head); head = p; }
        if(lowpass > 0 && lowpass < 18000) { let f = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); f.type = 'lowpass'; f.frequency.value = lowpass; f.Q.value = 0.5; f.connect(head); head = f; }
        return head;
    }

    /** Gain envelope: linear attack to `peak`, hold, exponential decay to silence. Returns the GainNode (connected to `out`). */
    env(at, peak, attack, decay, hold = 0, out = this.dest) {
        let g = this.ctx.createGain(), p = g.gain;
        p.setValueAtTime(0.0001, at);
        p.linearRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(0.0002, peak), at + attack);
        if(hold > 0) p.setValueAtTime(Math.max(0.0002, peak), at + attack + hold);
        p.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, at + attack + hold + decay);
        g.connect(out);
        return g;
    }

    /** Filtered noise burst. `freqEnd` sweeps the filter over the sound. */
    noise(at, { dur = 0.2, gain = 0.5, attack = 0.002, hold = 0, decay = dur, type = 'lowpass', freq = 1000, freqEnd = 0, q = 0.7, out = this.dest } = {}) {
        let ctx = this.ctx, end = at + attack + hold + decay;
        let src = ctx.createBufferSource(); src.buffer = this.noiseBuffer(); src.loop = true;
        let f = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); f.type = type; f.Q.value = q;
        f.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, at);
        if(freqEnd > 0) f.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(freqEnd, end);
        let g = this.env(at, gain, attack, decay, hold, out);
        src.connect(f); f.connect(g);
        src.start(at, Math.random() * 1.5); src.stop(end + 0.03);
        return src;
    }

    /** An oscillator with an envelope; `freqEnd` glides the pitch over the sound; `lowpass` tames bright waveforms. */
    tone(at, freq, { dur = 0.2, gain = 0.3, attack = 0.003, hold = 0, decay = dur, type = 'sine', freqEnd = 0, detune = 0, lowpass = 0, out = this.dest } = {}) {
        let ctx = this.ctx, end = at + attack + hold + decay;
        let o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = type; o.detune.value = detune;
        o.frequency.setValueAtTime(Math.max(1, freq), at);
        if(freqEnd > 0) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(freqEnd, end);
        let g = this.env(at, gain, attack, decay, hold, out), n = o;
        if(lowpass > 0) { let f = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); f.type = 'lowpass'; f.frequency.value = lowpass; o.connect(f); n = f; }
        n.connect(g);
        o.start(at); o.stop(end + 0.03);
        return o;
    }

    /** FM metal: a carrier with an inharmonic modulator whose index decays fast. Clinks, pings, clanks. */
    metal(at, freq, { dur = 0.15, gain = 0.3, ratio = 2.76, index = 3, out = this.dest } = {}) {
        let ctx = this.ctx, c = ctx.createOscillator(), m = ctx.createOscillator(), mg = ctx.createGain();
        c.frequency.value = freq; m.frequency.value = freq * ratio;
        mg.gain.setValueAtTime(freq * index, at); mg.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(freq * 0.05, at + dur);
        m.connect(mg); mg.connect(c.frequency);
        let g = this.env(at, gain, 0.001, dur, 0, out);
        c.connect(g);
        c.start(at); m.start(at); c.stop(at + dur + 0.03); m.stop(at + dur + 0.03);
    }

    /** Bell / pluck for jingles and the arpeggio: fundamental, octave, a slightly sharp twelfth, a short bright partial. */
    bell(at, freq, { dur = 0.5, gain = 0.3, bright = 1, out = this.dest } = {}) {
        this.tone(at, freq, { dur, gain, attack: 0.002, out });
        this.tone(at, freq * 2, { dur: dur * 0.6, gain: gain * 0.35, attack: 0.002, out });
        this.tone(at, freq * 3.01, { dur: dur * 0.3, gain: gain * 0.15 * bright, attack: 0.002, out });
        this.tone(at, freq * 5.4, { dur: 0.08, gain: gain * 0.12 * bright, attack: 0.001, out });
    }

    /** Brass-ish stinger voice: three detuned saws through a lowpass that opens with the attack. */
    brass(at, freq, { dur = 0.4, gain = 0.2, attack = 0.03, out = this.dest } = {}) {
        let ctx = this.ctx, f = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); f.type = 'lowpass'; f.Q.value = 1;
        f.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq * 2, at); f.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(freq * 6, at + attack + 0.05); f.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(freq * 2, at + attack + dur);
        let g = this.env(at, gain, attack, dur * 0.5, dur * 0.5, out);
        f.connect(g);
        for(let d of [-7, 0, 7]) { let o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = 'sawtooth'; o.frequency.value = freq; o.detune.value = d; o.connect(f); o.start(at); o.stop(at + attack + dur + 0.05); }
    }

    /** Kick: a sine dropping an octave and a half, with a click. */
    kick(at, { gain = 0.6, freq = 130, out = this.dest } = {}) {
        this.tone(at, freq, { dur: 0.3, gain, freqEnd: 42, attack: 0.002, out });
        this.noise(at, { dur: 0.02, gain: gain * 0.5, type: 'lowpass', freq: 2500, attack: 0.001, out });
    }
}

/** The sounds. Each takes the synth and options (`at` defaults to now); loops return a stop(time) function. */
export const SOUNDS = {
    /** Rocket r2.4, grenade r3.2, buster r5.6: size scales the layers; distance attenuates and muffles. */
    explosion(s, { at = s.now, radius = 3, distance = 0, pan = 0, kind = 'rocket' } = {}) {
        let size = clamp(radius / 3, 0.6, 2.2), a = atten(distance), r = rnd(0.92, 1.08);
        let v = a * clamp(0.6 + size * 0.35, 0.7, 1.0); // bigger blasts get longer and deeper, not much louder (headroom)
        let out = s.bus({ pan, lowpass: muffle(distance), send: 0.3 * clamp(size, 0.8, 1.6) });
        s.tone(at, 64 * r / Math.sqrt(size), { dur: 0.45 * size, gain: 0.7 * v, freqEnd: 24, attack: 0.004, out });                   // sub thump
        s.noise(at, { dur: 0.75 * size, gain: 0.6 * v, type: 'lowpass', freq: 480 * r, freqEnd: 60, q: 0.9, attack: 0.004, out });     // body
        s.noise(at, { dur: 0.05, gain: 0.45 * v * a, type: 'bandpass', freq: 2400 * r, q: 0.7, attack: 0.001, out });                   // crack
        let ticks = 4 + Math.round(size * 5);
        for(let i = 0; i < ticks; i++) {                                                                                                // debris
            let dt = 0.08 + Math.pow(Math.random(), 1.6) * 0.9 * size, k = clamp(1 - dt / (1.1 * size), 0.1, 1);
            s.noise(at + dt, { dur: rnd(0.02, 0.05), gain: 0.2 * v * k, type: 'bandpass', freq: rnd(500, 3200), q: 2.5, attack: 0.001, out });
        }
        if(size > 1.15) {                                                                                                               // rumble for the big ones
            s.noise(at + 0.08, { dur: 1.5 * size, gain: 0.25 * v, type: 'lowpass', freq: 170, q: 1, attack: 0.06, out });
            s.tone(at + 0.02, 40 * r, { dur: 1.3 * size, gain: 0.2 * v, freqEnd: 22, attack: 0.03, out });
        }
        if(kind === 'buster') s.tone(at + 0.05, 48 * r, { dur: 1.8, gain: 0.14 * v, type: 'triangle', freqEnd: 30, attack: 0.1, out });
    },
    /** Rocket launch: ignition thump (only in your own hands), a whoosh that recedes and drops in pitch, the motor falling away. */
    rocket(s, { at = s.now, distance = 0, pan = 0 } = {}) {
        let a = atten(distance), r = rnd(0.9, 1.1);
        let out = s.bus({ pan, lowpass: muffle(distance, 10, 6000), send: 0.2 });
        if(distance < 3) { s.noise(at, { dur: 0.05, gain: 0.45, type: 'lowpass', freq: 1200, attack: 0.001, out }); s.tone(at, 150 * r, { dur: 0.16, gain: 0.4, freqEnd: 50, out }); }
        s.noise(at, { gain: 0.42 * a, type: 'bandpass', freq: 600 * r, freqEnd: 150, q: 1.1, attack: 0.05, hold: 0.08, decay: 0.95, out });
        s.noise(at + 0.01, { dur: 0.45, gain: 0.18 * a, type: 'highpass', freq: 2200, attack: 0.02, out });
        s.tone(at + 0.02, 360 * r, { dur: 0.75, gain: 0.08 * a, type: 'sawtooth', freqEnd: 80, lowpass: 1400, attack: 0.02, out });
    },
    /** Grenade: an arm swish scaled by the charge and the pin's ping. Buster: a heavy heave and a clank. */
    throw(s, { at = s.now, power = 0.6, kind = 'grenade' } = {}) {
        let out = s.bus({ send: 0.1 }), r = rnd(0.95, 1.05);
        if(kind === 'buster') {
            s.noise(at, { dur: 0.3, gain: 0.4, type: 'bandpass', freq: 400, freqEnd: 900, q: 1, attack: 0.04, out });
            s.metal(at, 420 * r, { dur: 0.3, gain: 0.25, ratio: 1.41, index: 2.5, out });
            s.tone(at, 110, { dur: 0.2, gain: 0.3, freqEnd: 60, out });
        } else {
            s.noise(at, { dur: 0.14 + power * 0.1, gain: 0.16 + power * 0.2, type: 'highpass', freq: 900, freqEnd: 2600, attack: 0.02, out });
            s.metal(at, 2300 * r, { dur: 0.1, gain: 0.1, ratio: 3.1, index: 2, out });
        }
    },
    /** A grenade's hollow metal tick or a buster's thud when it lands; `impact` 0..1 from the landing speed. */
    bounce(s, { at = s.now, kind = 'grenade', impact = 1, distance = 0, pan = 0 } = {}) {
        let a = atten(distance) * clamp(impact, 0.2, 1), r = rnd(0.9, 1.1), out = s.bus({ pan, lowpass: muffle(distance), send: 0.2 });
        if(kind === 'buster') {
            s.tone(at, 120 * r, { dur: 0.16, gain: 0.45 * a, freqEnd: 55, out });
            s.noise(at, { dur: 0.1, gain: 0.35 * a, type: 'lowpass', freq: 500, attack: 0.001, out });
            s.metal(at, 380 * r, { dur: 0.25, gain: 0.15 * a, ratio: 1.41, index: 1.5, out });
        } else {
            s.metal(at, 780 * r, { dur: 0.1, gain: 0.3 * a, ratio: 2.76, index: 2, out });
            s.noise(at, { dur: 0.018, gain: 0.25 * a, type: 'bandpass', freq: 3000, q: 1, attack: 0.001, out });
        }
    },
    /** One fuse tick; GameClient calls it on every blink of FuseFxSystem, `progress` 0..1 of the fuse so the pitch climbs. */
    fuse(s, { at = s.now, kind = 'grenade', progress = 0, distance = 0, pan = 0 } = {}) {
        let a = atten(distance), out = s.bus({ pan, lowpass: muffle(distance, 12) });
        if(kind === 'buster') {
            s.tone(at, 520 + progress * 480, { dur: 0.05, gain: 0.25 * a, type: 'triangle', attack: 0.002, out });
            s.noise(at, { dur: 0.012, gain: 0.18 * a, type: 'bandpass', freq: 2500, q: 2, attack: 0.001, out });
            if(progress > 0.7) s.tone(at, 70, { dur: 0.08, gain: 0.18 * a, out });
        } else s.tone(at, 1400 + progress * 1300, { dur: 0.03, gain: 0.14 * a, type: 'square', attack: 0.001, lowpass: 5000, out });
    },
    /** One strike of the pickaxe (per actual carve, every 420 ms while the button is held): the point's tink on stone,
     *  a dry thunk of earth giving way, a little grit falling. Short and bright, nothing deep: it replaced a drill
     *  motor (saws at 46-92 Hz under a chopped grind) and a deep thud per bite, which together read as menacing. */
    pick(s, { at = s.now } = {}) {
        let r = rnd(0.93, 1.07), out = s.bus({ send: 0.1 });
        s.noise(at, { dur: 0.012, gain: 0.3, type: 'bandpass', freq: 2400 * r, q: 1.2, attack: 0.001, out }); // the point's contact
        s.metal(at, 1250 * r, { dur: 0.16, gain: 0.08, ratio: 2.4, index: 1.5, out });                        // a short ring, not a clang
        s.noise(at + 0.004, { dur: 0.08, gain: 0.4, type: 'lowpass', freq: 1100 * r, freqEnd: 380, q: 0.8, attack: 0.002, out }); // earth giving way
        s.tone(at + 0.004, 240 * r, { dur: 0.05, gain: 0.07, freqEnd: 170, out });                           // a little body
        for(let i = 0; i < 3; i++) s.noise(at + 0.05 + Math.random() * 0.18, { dur: rnd(0.01, 0.025), gain: rnd(0.04, 0.08), type: 'bandpass', freq: rnd(1500, 4000), q: 2.5, attack: 0.001, out }); // grit
    },
    /** A glow rock dug out: it shatters with a glassy tinkle (high bell partials in D, a breath of sparkle noise). */
    crystal(s, { at = s.now, count = 1, distance = 0, pan = 0 } = {}) {
        let out = s.bus({ send: 0.35, pan, lowpass: distance > 12 ? 4000 : 0 }), g = clamp(0.2 / (1 + distance / 8), 0.02, 0.2);
        let notes = [86, 90, 93, 98, 102], n = Math.min(4, 1 + Math.ceil(count / 2));
        for(let i = 0; i < n; i++) s.bell(at + i * rnd(0.025, 0.05), midiHz(notes[Math.floor(Math.random() * notes.length)]), { dur: rnd(0.2, 0.35), gain: g * rnd(0.6, 1), bright: 1.4, out });
        s.noise(at, { dur: 0.08, gain: g * 0.5, type: 'highpass', freq: 5000, attack: 0.001, out });
    },
    /** Mason: a stone block set down; `size` is the cube root of its volume (wall 1.8, block 2, plank 1.3, pillar 1.6). */
    build(s, { at = s.now, size = 1.6 } = {}) {
        let r = rnd(0.93, 1.07), k = clamp(size / 1.6, 0.7, 1.4), out = s.bus({ send: 0.25 });
        s.tone(at, 110 * r / k, { dur: 0.22 * k, gain: 0.5, freqEnd: 40, attack: 0.002, out });
        s.noise(at, { dur: 0.12 * k, gain: 0.5, type: 'lowpass', freq: 600 * r, freqEnd: 150, q: 0.8, attack: 0.002, out });
        s.noise(at, { dur: 0.03, gain: 0.28, type: 'bandpass', freq: 1900 * r, q: 4, attack: 0.001, out });              // stone on stone
        s.noise(at + 0.05, { dur: 0.25 * k, gain: 0.09, type: 'highpass', freq: 1600, attack: 0.04, out });               // grit settling
    },
    /** Grapple: the launch, the rope paying out, and the hook biting 150 ms later. */
    hook(s, { at = s.now } = {}) {
        let r = rnd(0.95, 1.05), out = s.bus({ send: 0.2 });
        s.noise(at, { dur: 0.07, gain: 0.35, type: 'highpass', freq: 2500, attack: 0.002, out });
        s.tone(at, 620 * r, { dur: 0.09, gain: 0.08, type: 'square', freqEnd: 180, lowpass: 2500, out });
        s.noise(at + 0.01, { dur: 0.2, gain: 0.2, type: 'bandpass', freq: 800, freqEnd: 2600, q: 1.5, attack: 0.03, out });
        s.metal(at + 0.15, 1900 * r, { dur: 0.25, gain: 0.3, ratio: 2.76, index: 3, out });
        s.noise(at + 0.15, { dur: 0.015, gain: 0.25, type: 'bandpass', freq: 3500, q: 1, attack: 0.001, out });
    },
    /** The reel while the grapple pulls: a ratchet (noise chopped at 22 Hz) and a small motor whose pitch climbs. */
    reelLoop(s, { at = s.now } = {}) {
        let ctx = s.ctx, out = s.bus({ send: 0.1 });
        let master = ctx.createGain(); master.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, at); master.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1, at + 0.08); master.connect(out);
        let n = ctx.createBufferSource(); n.buffer = s.noiseBuffer(); n.loop = true;
        let bp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); bp.type = 'bandpass'; bp.frequency.value = 1500; bp.Q.value = 2;
        let chop = ctx.createGain(); chop.gain.value = 0.5; let lfo = ctx.createOscillator(); lfo.type = 'square'; lfo.frequency.value = 22; let lg = ctx.createGain(); lg.gain.value = 0.5; lfo.connect(lg); lg.connect(chop.gain);
        let g1 = ctx.createGain(); g1.gain.value = 0.14; n.connect(bp); bp.connect(chop); chop.connect(g1); g1.connect(master);
        let o = ctx.createOscillator(); o.type = 'sawtooth'; o.frequency.setValueAtTime(170, at); o.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(240, at + 2.5);
        let lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(); lp.type = 'lowpass'; lp.frequency.value = 500; let g2 = ctx.createGain(); g2.gain.value = 0.08; o.connect(lp); lp.connect(g2); g2.connect(master);
        n.start(at, Math.random()); lfo.start(at); o.start(at);
        return (t = ctx.currentTime) => {
            let g = master.gain; if(g.cancelAndHoldAtTime) g.cancelAndHoldAtTime(t); else { g.cancelScheduledValues(t); g.setValueAtTime(1, t); }
            g.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.12);
            for(let x of [n, lfo, o]) x.stop(t + 0.15);
            setTimeout(() => { for(let x of [master, bp, chop, g1, lp, g2]) x.disconnect(); }, (t - ctx.currentTime) * 1000 + 300);
        };
    },
    unhook(s, { at = s.now } = {}) {
        s.noise(at, { dur: 0.06, gain: 0.22, type: 'highpass', freq: 1800, attack: 0.002 });
        s.tone(at, 500, { dur: 0.09, gain: 0.07, freqEnd: 1100, type: 'triangle' });
    },
    /** Taking damage: a body thud, a grunt-ish formant, ears ringing after a big one. */
    hurt(s, { at = s.now, damage = 30 } = {}) {
        let k = clamp(0.5 + damage / 70, 0.5, 1.1), r = rnd(0.9, 1.1), out = s.bus({ send: 0.1 });
        s.noise(at, { dur: 0.22, gain: 0.45 * k, type: 'lowpass', freq: 260 * r, freqEnd: 90, attack: 0.002, out });
        s.tone(at, 180 * r, { dur: 0.22, gain: 0.4 * k, freqEnd: 55, out });
        s.noise(at + 0.01, { dur: 0.14, gain: 0.18 * k, type: 'bandpass', freq: 650 * r, freqEnd: 350, q: 4, attack: 0.01, out });
        if(damage > 45) s.tone(at + 0.03, 3100, { dur: 0.6, gain: 0.04, attack: 0.05, out });
    },
    /** Death: a long sub drop and the parallel-minor fall A F D A. */
    death(s, { at = s.now } = {}) {
        let out = s.bus({ send: 0.4 });
        s.tone(at, 110, { dur: 1.3, gain: 0.45, freqEnd: 32, attack: 0.01, out });
        s.noise(at, { dur: 1.0, gain: 0.3, type: 'lowpass', freq: 400, freqEnd: 80, attack: 0.01, out });
        [69, 65, 62, 57].forEach((m, i) => s.brass(at + 0.1 + i * 0.19, midiHz(m), { dur: i === 3 ? 0.9 : 0.3, gain: 0.14, attack: 0.02, out }));
    },
    /** Respawn: a rising sweep and D A D. */
    respawn(s, { at = s.now } = {}) {
        let out = s.bus({ send: 0.35 });
        s.noise(at, { dur: 0.5, gain: 0.16, type: 'bandpass', freq: 300, freqEnd: 3200, q: 1.2, attack: 0.15, out });
        [62, 69, 74].forEach((m, i) => s.bell(at + 0.12 + i * 0.09, midiHz(m + 12), { dur: 0.5, gain: 0.14, out }));
    },
    kill(s, { at = s.now } = {}) { let out = s.bus({ send: 0.3 }); s.bell(at, midiHz(81), { dur: 0.3, gain: 0.2, out }); s.bell(at + 0.09, midiHz(86), { dur: 0.5, gain: 0.22, out }); },
    /** Pickup: D F# A up. */
    pickup(s, { at = s.now } = {}) { let out = s.bus({ send: 0.3 }); [74, 78, 81].forEach((m, i) => s.bell(at + i * 0.075, midiHz(m), { dur: i === 2 ? 0.6 : 0.25, gain: 0.2, out })); },
    /** A teammate (or an enemy, lower) grabbed a crate: a short version of the pickup, far away. */
    pickupOther(s, { at = s.now, friendly = true } = {}) {
        let out = s.bus({ send: 0.3 });
        if(friendly) [74, 81].forEach((m, i) => s.bell(at + i * 0.08, midiHz(m), { dur: 0.3, gain: 0.08, out }));
        else [57, 55].forEach((m, i) => s.tone(at + i * 0.12, midiHz(m), { dur: 0.3, gain: 0.08, type: 'triangle', out }));
    },
    /** Deposit: the team fanfare, D F# A D up, then C -> D home over a D3 root. */
    deposit(s, { at = s.now } = {}) {
        let out = s.bus({ send: 0.35 });
        s.tone(at, midiHz(50), { dur: 1.4, gain: 0.22, attack: 0.02, out });
        [62, 66, 69, 74].forEach((m, i) => s.bell(at + i * 0.1, midiHz(m + 12), { dur: 0.35, gain: 0.18, out }));
        s.bell(at + 0.5, midiHz(84), { dur: 0.3, gain: 0.16, out }); s.bell(at + 0.62, midiHz(86), { dur: 1.0, gain: 0.24, out });
        for(let [m, g] of [[62, 0.07], [66, 0.05], [69, 0.05]]) s.brass(at + 0.62, midiHz(m), { dur: 0.9, gain: g, out });
    },
    /** Tunnel rat: a quick scamper up the scale and a D chord. */
    bonus(s, { at = s.now } = {}) {
        let out = s.bus({ send: 0.35 });
        [67, 69, 71, 74, 76, 78].forEach((m, i) => s.bell(at + i * 0.06, midiHz(m + 12), { dur: 0.22, gain: 0.14, out }));
        [74, 78, 81].forEach(m => s.bell(at + 0.42, midiHz(m + 12), { dur: 0.9, gain: 0.12, out }));
    },
    /** Round start: drum, D D A D in brass, a bell chord on the landing. */
    roundStart(s, { at = s.now } = {}) {
        let out = s.bus({ send: 0.35 });
        s.kick(at, { gain: 0.45, out }); s.noise(at, { dur: 0.4, gain: 0.2, type: 'lowpass', freq: 300, attack: 0.002, out });
        for(let [m, dt, dur] of [[62, 0, 0.18], [62, 0.2, 0.18], [69, 0.4, 0.3], [74, 0.75, 0.9]]) { s.brass(at + dt, midiHz(m), { dur, gain: 0.14, out }); s.brass(at + dt, midiHz(m - 12), { dur, gain: 0.08, out }); }
        s.kick(at + 0.75, { gain: 0.4, out });
        [74, 78, 81].forEach(m => s.bell(at + 0.75, midiHz(m + 12), { dur: 1.0, gain: 0.1, out }));
    },
    /** Round over: win climbs A D F# A, loss falls A F D A over a low D, a draw hovers. */
    roundOver(s, { at = s.now, result = 'draw' } = {}) {
        let out = s.bus({ send: 0.4 });
        s.kick(at, { gain: 0.5, out });
        if(result === 'win') {
            for(let [m, dt] of [[69, 0], [74, 0.15], [78, 0.3], [81, 0.45]]) s.brass(at + dt, midiHz(m), { dur: dt === 0.45 ? 1.4 : 0.35, gain: 0.16, out });
            [74, 78, 81, 86].forEach(m => s.bell(at + 0.45, midiHz(m + 12), { dur: 1.4, gain: 0.1, out }));
        } else if(result === 'loss') {
            for(let [m, dt] of [[69, 0], [65, 0.25], [62, 0.5], [57, 0.75]]) s.brass(at + dt, midiHz(m), { dur: dt === 0.75 ? 1.6 : 0.4, gain: 0.15, out });
            s.tone(at + 0.75, midiHz(38), { dur: 1.8, gain: 0.22, attack: 0.05, out });
        } else for(let [m, dt] of [[69, 0], [67, 0.3], [69, 0.6]]) s.brass(at + dt, midiHz(m), { dur: dt === 0.6 ? 1.2 : 0.4, gain: 0.14, out });
    },
    votePass(s, { at = s.now } = {}) { let out = s.bus({ send: 0.3 }); s.bell(at, midiHz(81), { dur: 0.3, gain: 0.18, out }); s.bell(at + 0.12, midiHz(86), { dur: 0.6, gain: 0.18, out }); },
    /** UI: weapon switch, vote cast, menu. */
    click(s, { at = s.now } = {}) { s.noise(at, { dur: 0.012, gain: 0.14, type: 'highpass', freq: 2500, attack: 0.001 }); s.tone(at, 1700, { dur: 0.03, gain: 0.07, type: 'triangle' }); },
    /** Rubble: a collapse of `count` fragments, thuds scattered over half a second and a rumble. */
    rubble(s, { at = s.now, count = 1, distance = 0, pan = 0 } = {}) {
        let a = atten(distance), n = Math.min(9, 2 + count), k = clamp(count / 6, 0.3, 1), out = s.bus({ pan, lowpass: muffle(distance, 10, 7000), send: 0.3 });
        for(let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            let dt = Math.random() * 0.55, r = rnd(0.8, 1.25);
            s.noise(at + dt, { dur: 0.09, gain: 0.26 * a, type: 'lowpass', freq: 380 * r, freqEnd: 120, q: 1, attack: 0.002, out });
            s.tone(at + dt, 75 * r, { dur: 0.1, gain: 0.2 * a, freqEnd: 40, out });
        }
        s.noise(at, { dur: 0.7, gain: 0.26 * a * k, type: 'lowpass', freq: 130, q: 1, attack: 0.05, out });
    },
    /** Landing after a fall; `speed` in m/s. */
    land(s, { at = s.now, speed = 10 } = {}) {
        let k = clamp((speed - 6) / 20, 0.15, 1), out = s.bus({ send: 0.1 });
        s.noise(at, { dur: 0.09, gain: 0.32 * k, type: 'lowpass', freq: 450, freqEnd: 150, attack: 0.002, out });
        s.tone(at, 95, { dur: 0.12, gain: 0.28 * k, freqEnd: 45, out });
    },
    step(s, { at = s.now } = {}) {
        let r = rnd(0.85, 1.15);
        s.noise(at, { dur: 0.06, gain: 0.11, type: 'lowpass', freq: 750 * r, freqEnd: 250, attack: 0.002 });
        s.tone(at, 120 * r, { dur: 0.04, gain: 0.05, freqEnd: 70 });
    },
};

/** The output bus for a context: fx -> master -> soft clipper -> destination, plus the reverb (send -> lowpass -> convolver -> master). */
export function createBus(ctx) {
    let fx = ctx.createGain(), master = ctx.createGain(), limiter = ctx.createWaveShaper();
    // unity below the knee, then a tanh shoulder that tops out at 0.89: a safety net, not a gain stage
    let curve = new Float32Array(1025), knee = 0.55;
    for(let i = 0; i < curve.length; i++) { let x = (i / 512) - 1, ax = Math.abs(x); curve[i] = Math.sign(x) * (ax < knee ? ax : knee + (1 - knee) * Math.tanh((ax - knee) / (1 - knee))); }
    limiter.curve = curve; limiter.oversample = '2x';
    fx.connect(master); master.connect(limiter); limiter.connect(ctx.destination);
    let reverbSend = ctx.createGain(), lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter(), conv = ctx.createConvolver(), ret = ctx.createGain();
    lp.type = 'lowpass'; lp.frequency.value = 3200; ret.gain.value = 0.35;
    let len = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * 1.6), ir = ctx.createBuffer(2, len, ctx.sampleRate);
    for(let c = 0; c < 2; c++) { let d = ir.getChannelData(c), s = 12345 + c * 777; for(let i = 0; i < len; i++) { s ^= s << 13; s ^= s >>> 17; s ^= s << 5; let t = i / ctx.sampleRate; d[i] = (((s >>> 0) / 2147483648) - 1) * Math.exp(-t * 4.2) * (t < 0.02 ? t / 0.02 : 1); } }
    conv.buffer = ir;
    reverbSend.connect(lp); lp.connect(conv); conv.connect(ret); ret.connect(master);
    return { fx, master, limiter, reverbSend };
}


/** Material families for impacts and blasts: what a material sounds like when it breaks. */
const FAMILY = { 0: 'stone', 1: 'earth', 2: 'earth', 3: 'sand', 4: 'sand', 5: 'stone', 6: 'metal', 7: 'earth', 8: 'stone', 9: 'stone', 10: 'wood', 11: 'stone', 12: 'stone', 13: 'stone' };

Object.assign(SOUNDS, {
    /** Donkey Kong's punch: a meaty thump and the crack of whatever it hit. */
    punch(s, { at = s.now, kind = 'terrain', family = 'earth' } = {}) {
        const r = rnd(0.9, 1.1), out = s.bus({ send: 0.12 });
        s.noise(at, { dur: 0.05, gain: 0.25, type: 'highpass', freq: 1200, freqEnd: 400, attack: 0.002, out }); // swing
        if(kind === 'air') return;
        s.tone(at, 140 * r, { dur: 0.14, gain: 0.55, freqEnd: 45, attack: 0.002, out });
        s.noise(at, { dur: 0.1, gain: 0.45, type: 'lowpass', freq: 900 * r, freqEnd: 200, attack: 0.001, out });
        if(kind === 'actor') { s.noise(at, { dur: 0.07, gain: 0.3, type: 'bandpass', freq: 500, q: 2, attack: 0.001, out }); return; }
        SOUNDS.crumble(s, { at, family, size: 0.6 });
    },
    /** Breaking material: stone cracks and rattles, earth thuds and pours, wood splinters, metal clanks. */
    crumble(s, { at = s.now, family = 'earth', size = 1, distance = 0, pan = 0 } = {}) {
        const a = atten(distance), k = clamp(size, 0.3, 2), out = s.bus({ pan, lowpass: muffle(distance), send: 0.15 });
        const r = rnd(0.9, 1.1);
        if(family === 'stone') {
            s.noise(at, { dur: 0.03, gain: 0.4 * a, type: 'bandpass', freq: 2800 * r, q: 1.2, attack: 0.001, out });
            for(let i = 0; i < 3 + k * 4; i++) s.noise(at + 0.02 + Math.random() * 0.3 * k, { dur: rnd(0.015, 0.04), gain: rnd(0.08, 0.18) * a, type: 'bandpass', freq: rnd(1200, 4200), q: 3, attack: 0.001, out });
        } else if(family === 'wood') {
            s.noise(at, { dur: 0.06, gain: 0.35 * a, type: 'bandpass', freq: 700 * r, q: 3, attack: 0.001, out });
            s.tone(at, 330 * r, { dur: 0.08, gain: 0.2 * a, type: 'triangle', freqEnd: 180, out });
            for(let i = 0; i < 3; i++) s.noise(at + 0.03 + Math.random() * 0.15, { dur: 0.02, gain: 0.12 * a, type: 'bandpass', freq: rnd(1500, 3000), q: 4, attack: 0.001, out });
        } else if(family === 'metal') {
            s.metal(at, 520 * r, { dur: 0.35, gain: 0.25 * a, ratio: 2.76, index: 3, out });
        } else if(family === 'sand') {
            s.noise(at, { dur: 0.25 * k, gain: 0.25 * a, type: 'highpass', freq: 1800, attack: 0.01, out });
        } else {
            s.noise(at, { dur: 0.18 * k, gain: 0.3 * a, type: 'lowpass', freq: 500 * r, freqEnd: 120, attack: 0.003, out });
            s.noise(at + 0.04, { dur: 0.3 * k, gain: 0.12 * a, type: 'bandpass', freq: 1400, q: 0.8, attack: 0.05, out }); // dirt pouring
        }
    },
    /** Ripping a lump out of the ground: a deep groan and a tearing crack. */
    rip(s, { at = s.now, family = 'earth' } = {}) {
        const out = s.bus({ send: 0.2 }), r = rnd(0.9, 1.1);
        s.tone(at, 90 * r, { dur: 0.35, gain: 0.45, freqEnd: 50, attack: 0.02, out });
        s.noise(at, { dur: 0.3, gain: 0.35, type: 'bandpass', freq: 300 * r, freqEnd: 1600, q: 1.2, attack: 0.03, out });
        SOUNDS.crumble(s, { at: at + 0.05, family, size: 1.2 });
    },
    whoosh(s, { at = s.now, power = 1 } = {}) {
        const out = s.bus({ send: 0.1 });
        s.noise(at, { dur: 0.25, gain: 0.25 * power, type: 'bandpass', freq: 500, freqEnd: 1800, q: 1, attack: 0.05, out });
    },
    /** A thrown boulder bursting: a short explosion plus a lot of its material. */
    shatter(s, { at = s.now, family = 'stone', distance = 0, pan = 0 } = {}) {
        SOUNDS.explosion(s, { at, radius: 1.6, distance, pan });
        SOUNDS.crumble(s, { at: at + 0.01, family, size: 1.8, distance, pan });
    },
    /** Hit marker: you hurt someone. */
    hitmark(s, { at = s.now, kill = false } = {}) {
        const out = s.bus({});
        s.tone(at, kill ? 1800 : 2400, { dur: 0.05, gain: 0.12, type: 'square', lowpass: 6000, out });
        if(kill) s.tone(at + 0.06, 2600, { dur: 0.09, gain: 0.12, type: 'square', lowpass: 6000, out });
    },
    /** The match clock: a woodblock in the last ten seconds. */
    tick(s, { at = s.now } = {}) { s.tone(at, 1250, { dur: 0.05, gain: 0.18, type: 'triangle', out: s.bus({}) }); s.noise(at, { dur: 0.01, gain: 0.1, type: 'bandpass', freq: 3000, q: 2, attack: 0.001 }); },
    /** The enemy took one of your crates. */
    alarm(s, { at = s.now } = {}) { const out = s.bus({ send: 0.2 }); for(let i = 0; i < 3; i++) s.tone(at + i * 0.16, 880, { dur: 0.1, gain: 0.12, type: 'square', lowpass: 2500, out }); },
});

export class Sfx {
    constructor(game) {
        this.game = game;
        this.ctx = null; this.synth = null; this.master = null; this.reverbSend = null;
        this.readyFns = [];
        this.listener = { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, rx: 1, ry: 0, rz: 0 };
        this.recent = new Map(); // throttles per sound name
        let store = (k) => { try { return localStorage.getItem(k); } catch(e) { return null; } };
        this.muted = store('bananza.muted') === '1';
        this.volume = 0.9;
        const start = () => {
            try {
                if(!this.ctx) {
                    const AC = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext; if(!AC) return;
                    this.ctx = new AC();
                    const bus = createBus(this.ctx);
                    this.fx = bus.fx; this.master = bus.master; this.reverbSend = bus.reverbSend;
                    this.master.gain.value = this.muted ? 0 : this.volume;
                    this.synth = new Synth(this.ctx, this.fx, this.reverbSend);
                }
                if(this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume().then(() => this.fireReady()).catch(() => {});
                else this.fireReady();
            } catch(e) { console.warn('audio unavailable', e); }
        };
        for(const ev of ['pointerdown', 'keydown', 'touchstart']) window.addEventListener(ev, start, { passive: true });
        addEventListener('keydown', (e) => { if(e.code === 'KeyM' && !e.repeat) { this.toggleMute(); game.emit('hint', { text: this.muted ? 'Sound off (M)' : 'Sound on (M)' }); } });
        if(game) this.hook(game);
    }
    get ready() { return !!this.ctx && this.ctx.state === 'running'; }
    onReady(fn) { if(this.ready) fn(); else this.readyFns.push(fn); }
    fireReady() { if(!this.ready) return; const fns = this.readyFns; this.readyFns = []; for(const fn of fns) { try { fn(); } catch(e) { console.warn(e); } } }
    setMuted(m) {
        this.muted = !!m;
        try { localStorage.setItem('bananza.muted', this.muted ? '1' : '0'); } catch(e) {}
        if(this.master) { const g = this.master.gain, t = this.ctx.currentTime; g.cancelScheduledValues(t); g.setValueAtTime(g.value, t); g.linearRampToValueAtTime(this.muted ? 0 : this.volume, t + 0.15); }
        return this.muted;
    }
    toggleMute() { return this.setMuted(!this.muted); }
    listen(camera) {
        const l = this.listener, p = camera.position, q = camera.quaternion;
        l.x = p.x; l.y = p.y; l.z = p.z;
        l.rx = 1 - 2 * (q.y * q.y + q.z * q.z); l.ry = 2 * (q.x * q.y + q.w * q.z); l.rz = 2 * (q.x * q.z - q.w * q.y);
    }
    spatial(pos) {
        if(!pos) return { distance: 0, pan: 0 };
        const x = pos.x ?? pos[0], y = pos.y ?? pos[1], z = pos.z ?? pos[2];
        const l = this.listener, dx = x - l.x, dy = y - l.y, dz = z - l.z, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy, dz);
        const pan = d > 0.5 ? (dx * l.rx + dy * l.ry + dz * l.rz) / d * clamp(d / 6, 0, 1) * 0.8 : 0;
        return { distance: d, pan };
    }
    /** Play a sound; `minGap` seconds throttles repeats of the same name (debris storms). */
    play(name, opts = {}, minGap = 0) {
        if(!this.ready || this.muted || !SOUNDS[name]) return;
        const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
        if(minGap > 0) { const last = this.recent.get(name) ?? -1; if(now - last < minGap) return; this.recent.set(name, now); }
        try { SOUNDS[name](this.synth, { at: now, ...opts }); } catch(e) { console.warn(`sfx ${name}`, e); }
    }
    at(name, pos, opts = {}, minGap = 0, maxDist = 90) {
        const sp = this.spatial(pos);
        if(sp.distance > maxDist) return;
        this.play(name, { ...opts, ...sp }, minGap);
    }

    /** Debug: render every sound into an OfflineAudioContext; returns { name: peak | error }. */
    static async selfTest(seconds = 1.5) {
        const OAC = window.OfflineAudioContext || window.webkitOfflineAudioContext, out = {};
        for(const name of Object.keys(SOUNDS)) {
            try {
                const ctx = new OAC(2, Math.ceil(44100 * seconds), 44100), bus = createBus(ctx), synth = new Synth(ctx, bus.fx, bus.reverbSend);
                const stop = SOUNDS[name](synth, { at: 0.01 });
                if(typeof stop === 'function') stop(seconds * 0.6);
                const buf = await ctx.startRendering(), d = buf.getChannelData(0);
                let peak = 0; for(let i = 0; i < d.length; i++) peak = Math.max(peak, Math.abs(d[i]));
                out[name] = +peak.toFixed(3);
            } catch(e) { out[name] = 'ERROR ' + e.message; }
        }
        return out;
    }

    /** Everything the game does that makes a noise. */
    hook(game) {
        const me = () => game.player;
        const fam = (m) => FAMILY[m] || 'earth';
        game.on('explosion', ({ center, radius, kind }) => {
            if(kind === 'rock') return; // the shatter sound covers it
            this.at('explosion', center, { radius, kind }, 0, 200);
        });
        game.on('rocket', ({ origin, owner }) => owner === me() ? this.play('rocket') : this.at('rocket', origin, {}, 0.05, 70));
        game.on('throw', ({ kind, owner }) => { if(owner === me()) kind === 'rock' ? this.play('whoosh', { power: 1.2 }) : this.play('throw', { kind, power: 0.7 }); });
        game.on('bounce', ({ kind, point, impact }) => this.at('bounce', point, { kind, impact }, 0.04, 50));
        game.on('fuse', ({ kind, progress, point }) => this.at('fuse', point, { kind, progress }, 0, 40));
        game.on('punch', ({ kind, mat, owner, point }) => owner === me() ? this.play('punch', { kind, family: fam(mat) }) : this.at('punch', point, { kind, family: fam(mat) }, 0.05, 40));
        game.on('dig', ({ owner, point, volume }) => { if(owner === me()) { this.play('pick'); } else this.at('pick', point, {}, 0.1, 35); });
        game.on('build', ({ size, owner, center }) => owner === me() ? this.play('build', { size: Math.cbrt(size[0] * size[1] * size[2]) }) : this.at('build', center, {}, 0.1));
        game.on('rip', ({ mat, point, _net }) => _net ? this.at('rip', point, { family: fam(mat) }, 0.05, 60) : this.play('rip', { family: fam(mat) })); // _net: another player's
        game.on('shatter', ({ point, mat }) => this.at('shatter', point, { family: fam(mat) }, 0, 150));
        game.on('slam', ({ point, mat }) => this.at('shatter', point, { family: fam(mat) }, 0, 150));
        game.on('damage', ({ actor, amount, by }) => {
            if(actor === me()) this.play('hurt', { damage: amount }, 0.08);
            else if(by === me()) this.play('hitmark', { kill: actor.health <= 0 });
        });
        game.on('death', ({ actor, by }) => { if(actor === me()) this.play('death'); else if(by === me()) this.play('kill'); });
        game.on('respawn', ({ actor }) => { if(actor === me()) this.play('respawn'); });
        game.on('pickup', ({ actor, from }) => {
            if(actor === me()) this.play('pickup');
            else { if(from === me().team && actor.team !== from) this.play('alarm'); else this.play('pickupOther', { friendly: actor.team === me().team }); }
        });
        game.on('deposit', ({ actor }) => actor.team === me().team ? this.play('deposit') : this.play('pickupOther', { friendly: false }));
        game.on('step', ({ actor }) => { if(actor === me()) this.play('step'); });
        game.on('land', ({ actor, speed }) => { if(actor === me()) this.play('land', { speed }); });
        game.on('tool', () => this.play('click'));
        game.on('tick', () => this.play('tick'));
        game.on('ui', ({ sound }) => this.play(sound || 'click'));
        game.on('start', () => this.onReady(() => this.play('roundStart')));
        game.on('matchOver', ({ result }) => this.play('roundOver', { result }));
        // the world: debris from any destruction, bodies hitting things
        game.ctx.events.addEventListener('destroyed', (e) => {
            const d = e.detail; if(!d || d.kind === 'blast') return;
            let best = 1, bv = 0; for(let i = 0; i < 16; i++) if(d.removed[i] > bv) { bv = d.removed[i]; best = i; }
            if(d.author === me()?.name) return; // our own punches and digs already sound
            this.at('crumble', d.center, { family: fam(best), size: Math.cbrt(d.volume) }, 0.06, 50);
        });
        game.ctx.events.addEventListener('impact', (e) => {
            const d = e.detail; if(!d || d.speed < 4) return;
            this.at('crumble', d.point, { family: fam(d.mat ?? 0), size: Math.min(1.5, d.speed / 12) }, 0.05, 50);
        });
        game.ctx.events.addEventListener('detached', (e) => {
            const d = e.detail; if(!d) return;
            const p = d.prop?.body?.translation?.();
            if(p) this.at('rubble', p, { count: Math.min(9, Math.ceil(Math.cbrt(d.volume || 1) * 2)) }, 0.15, 120);
        });
    }
}
```

### src/dev/FlyCamera.js
```
/** Developer fly camera: WASD/QE (Shift fast), mouse look under pointer lock. Left click blasts, right click builds,
 *  B drops a box. Stands in for the player until the game's PlayerController takes over. */
import * as THREE from 'three/webgpu';
import { sphere } from '../voxel/brushes.js';

export class FlyCamera {
    constructor(ctx) {
        this.ctx = ctx;
        const { camera, renderer, world, physics } = ctx;
        this.keys = new Set();
        addEventListener('keydown', e => this.keys.add(e.code));
        addEventListener('keyup', e => this.keys.delete(e.code));
        this.euler = new THREE.Euler(0, -Math.PI / 2, 0, 'YXZ');
        camera.quaternion.setFromEuler(this.euler);
        renderer.domElement.addEventListener('click', () => renderer.domElement.requestPointerLock());
        addEventListener('mousemove', e => {
            if(document.pointerLockElement !== renderer.domElement) return;
            this.euler.y -= e.movementX * 0.0022; this.euler.x = Math.max(-1.55, Math.min(1.55, this.euler.x - e.movementY * 0.0022));
            camera.quaternion.setFromEuler(this.euler);
        });
        let seed = 1;
        addEventListener('mousedown', e => {
            if(document.pointerLockElement !== renderer.domElement) return;
            const d = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, -1).applyQuaternion(camera.quaternion).toArray();
            const hit = world.raycast(camera.position.toArray(), d, 150);
            if(!hit) return;
            if(e.button === 0) ctx.destruction.blast(hit.point, 3);
            else if(e.button === 2) ctx.destruction.carve(sphere(hit.point, 1.5), { op: 'add', mat: 0 });
        });
        addEventListener('keydown', e => {
            if(e.code !== 'KeyB') return;
            const p = camera.position.clone().add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, -3).applyQuaternion(camera.quaternion));
            const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1, 1, 1), new THREE.MeshStandardNodeMaterial({ color: 0xb08040 }));
            mesh.castShadow = mesh.receiveShadow = true;
            physics.addBody({ shape: 'box', size: [0.5, 0.5, 0.5], position: p.toArray(), mesh });
        });
        addEventListener('contextmenu', e => e.preventDefault());
    }
    update(dt) {
        const k = this.keys, cam = this.ctx.camera;
        const v = new THREE.Vector3((k.has('KeyD') ? 1 : 0) - (k.has('KeyA') ? 1 : 0), (k.has('KeyE') || k.has('Space') ? 1 : 0) - (k.has('KeyQ') ? 1 : 0), (k.has('KeyS') ? 1 : 0) - (k.has('KeyW') ? 1 : 0));
        cam.position.addScaledVector(v.applyQuaternion(cam.quaternion), dt * (k.has('ShiftLeft') ? 40 : 14));
    }
}
```

### src/dev/Profiler.js
```
/**
 * Frame profiler that a player can run on their own machine and send back as one file.
 *
 *   ?profile        record 15 s of play once the world is ready, then offer the file (?profile=30 for 30 s)
 *   F9              start / stop a recording at any time (GPU times only with ?profile: timestamps must be
 *                   enabled when the renderer is created)
 *
 * Per frame it keeps: the frame interval (rAF to rAF), CPU time of each section of the main loop (physics, each
 * game system, env, render submission), GPU time when the backend can measure it (WebGPU timestamp queries, or
 * WebGL2's EXT_disjoint_timer_query), draw calls, triangles, physics bodies, pending meshes and JS heap. Plus the
 * machine: browser, OS, CPU cores, memory, screen, pixel ratio, rendering backend and the GPU's name (a software
 * renderer such as SwiftShader or llvmpipe shows up there and explains a 10 fps machine on its own).
 *
 * The file is JSON in Chrome's Trace Event format: open it in https://ui.perfetto.dev (or chrome://tracing) for a
 * timeline, or summarize it with `node tools/profile-report.mjs file.json`. Everything besides `traceEvents` is
 * ignored by those viewers and read by the report script.
 *
 * Main loop use: prof.frameStart(now); ... prof.mark('physics'); ... prof.frameEnd(). Marks cost two
 * performance.now() calls when not recording.
 */
const MAX_FRAMES = 60 * 240; // 60 s at 240 fps

export class Profiler {
    constructor({ renderer, params, ctx }) {
        this.renderer = renderer;
        this.ctx = ctx;
        this.recording = false;
        this.auto = params.has('profile') ? Math.max(3, Math.min(60, +params.get('profile') || 15)) : 0;
        this.gpuEnabled = params.has('profile');
        this.loadMarks = [];
        this.sections = new Map(); // name -> index
        this.frame = { start: 0, last: 0, marks: [] };
        this.prevStart = 0;
        this.gpuPending = false;
        this.longTasks = [];
        try {
            new PerformanceObserver((l) => { if(this.recording) for(const e of l.getEntries()) this.longTasks.push([+(e.startTime - this.t0).toFixed(1), +e.duration.toFixed(1)]); }).observe({ type: 'longtask', buffered: false });
        } catch(e) { /* not supported (Safari, Firefox) */ }
        addEventListener('keydown', (e) => { if(e.code === 'F9') { e.preventDefault(); this.recording ? this.stop() : this.start(this.auto || 15); } });
    }
    /** Timestamp a loading step (shown in the report). */
    loadMark(name) { this.loadMarks.push([name, +performance.now().toFixed(1)]); }

    /** Call when the game is playable; starts the automatic recording if ?profile asked for one. */
    ready() {
        this.loadMark('ready');
        if(this.auto) { this.banner(`Profiling ${this.auto} s... play normally`); setTimeout(() => this.start(this.auto), 1500); }
    }

    start(seconds) {
        if(this.recording) return;
        this.recording = true;
        this.t0 = performance.now();
        this.until = this.t0 + seconds * 1000;
        this.n = 0;
        this.cols = { t: new Float32Array(MAX_FRAMES), interval: new Float32Array(MAX_FRAMES), cpu: new Float32Array(MAX_FRAMES),
            gpu: new Float32Array(MAX_FRAMES).fill(NaN), calls: new Uint32Array(MAX_FRAMES), tris: new Uint32Array(MAX_FRAMES),
            bodies: new Uint16Array(MAX_FRAMES), pending: new Uint16Array(MAX_FRAMES), heap: new Float32Array(MAX_FRAMES) };
        this.sectionCols = [];
        this.longTasks = [];
        this.banner(`● Recording profile (${seconds} s)`);
    }
    stop() {
        if(!this.recording) return;
        this.recording = false;
        this.finish();
    }

    frameStart(now) {
        const f = this.frame;
        f.interval = this.prevStart ? now - this.prevStart : 0;
        this.prevStart = now;
        f.start = f.last = performance.now();
        f.marks.length = 0;
    }
    mark(name) {
        const t = performance.now(), f = this.frame;
        if(this.recording) f.marks.push(name, t - f.last);
        f.last = t;
    }
    frameEnd() {
        if(!this.recording) return;
        const f = this.frame, c = this.cols, i = this.n;
        if(i >= MAX_FRAMES || performance.now() > this.until) { this.stop(); return; }
        c.t[i] = f.start - this.t0;
        c.interval[i] = f.interval;
        c.cpu[i] = f.last - f.start;
        for(let k = 0; k < f.marks.length; k += 2) {
            let s = this.sections.get(f.marks[k]);
            if(s === undefined) { s = this.sections.size; this.sections.set(f.marks[k], s); }
            (this.sectionCols[s] ||= new Float32Array(MAX_FRAMES))[i] = f.marks[k + 1];
        }
        const info = this.renderer.info.render;
        c.calls[i] = info.drawCalls ?? info.calls ?? 0;
        c.tris[i] = info.triangles ?? 0;
        c.bodies[i] = this.ctx.physics?.props.size ?? 0;
        c.pending[i] = this.ctx.terrain?.pending ?? 0;
        c.heap[i] = performance.memory ? performance.memory.usedJSHeapSize / 1048576 : 0;
        // GPU time of the passes submitted since the last resolve, attributed to this frame
        if(this.gpuEnabled && !this.gpuPending && this.renderer.backend.trackTimestamp) {
            this.gpuPending = true;
            this.renderer.resolveTimestampsAsync('render').then((ms) => { if(typeof ms === 'number' && i < MAX_FRAMES) c.gpu[i] = ms; }, () => {}).finally(() => { this.gpuPending = false; });
        }
        this.n++;
    }

    machine() {
        const r = this.renderer, b = r.backend, nav = navigator;
        const m = {
            url: location.href, userAgent: nav.userAgent, platform: nav.userAgentData?.platform || nav.platform,
            cores: nav.hardwareConcurrency, memoryGB: nav.deviceMemory, devicePixelRatio: devicePixelRatio,
            screen: [screen.width, screen.height], canvas: [r.domElement.width, r.domElement.height], pixelRatio: r.getPixelRatio(),
            backend: b.isWebGPUBackend ? 'WebGPU' : 'WebGL2',
        };
        try {
            if(b.isWebGPUBackend) {
                const info = b.adapter?.info || b.device?.adapterInfo;
                if(info) m.gpu = { vendor: info.vendor, architecture: info.architecture, device: info.device, description: info.description };
            } else {
                const gl = b.gl;
                const ext = gl.getExtension('WEBGL_debug_renderer_info');
                m.gpu = { vendor: gl.getParameter(ext ? ext.UNMASKED_VENDOR_WEBGL : gl.VENDOR), renderer: gl.getParameter(ext ? ext.UNMASKED_RENDERER_WEBGL : gl.RENDERER) };
                m.gpuTimer = !!gl.getExtension('EXT_disjoint_timer_query_webgl2');
            }
        } catch(e) { m.gpuError = String(e); }
        if(/swiftshader|llvmpipe|software|basic render/i.test(JSON.stringify(m.gpu || ''))) m.warning = 'software renderer: hardware acceleration is off or the GPU is blocklisted';
        return m;
    }

    finish() {
        const c = this.cols, n = this.n, names = [...this.sections.keys()];
        const frames = { t: [], interval: [], cpu: [], gpu: [], calls: [], tris: [], bodies: [], pending: [], heapMB: [], sections: {} };
        const r1 = (v) => Math.round(v * 100) / 100;
        for(let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            frames.t.push(r1(c.t[i])); frames.interval.push(r1(c.interval[i])); frames.cpu.push(r1(c.cpu[i]));
            frames.gpu.push(Number.isNaN(c.gpu[i]) ? null : r1(c.gpu[i])); frames.calls.push(c.calls[i]); frames.tris.push(c.tris[i]);
            frames.bodies.push(c.bodies[i]); frames.pending.push(c.pending[i]); frames.heapMB.push(r1(c.heap[i]));
        }
        names.forEach((name, s) => { frames.sections[name] = Array.from(this.sectionCols[s]?.subarray(0, n) || [], r1); });
        // Chrome trace events (microseconds): a frame slice on the main track with its sections nested, GPU on its own
        const traceEvents = [
            { ph: 'M', name: 'process_name', pid: 1, args: { name: 'Voxel Wars' } },
            { ph: 'M', name: 'thread_name', pid: 1, tid: 1, args: { name: 'main thread' } },
            { ph: 'M', name: 'thread_name', pid: 1, tid: 2, args: { name: 'GPU (per resolve)' } },
        ];
        for(let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            const ts = c.t[i] * 1000;
            traceEvents.push({ ph: 'X', pid: 1, tid: 1, name: 'frame', ts, dur: c.cpu[i] * 1000, args: { interval: r1(c.interval[i]) } });
            let at = ts;
            names.forEach((name, s) => { const d = this.sectionCols[s]?.[i] || 0; if(d > 0) traceEvents.push({ ph: 'X', pid: 1, tid: 1, name, ts: at, dur: d * 1000 }); at += d * 1000; });
            if(!Number.isNaN(c.gpu[i])) traceEvents.push({ ph: 'X', pid: 1, tid: 2, name: 'gpu', ts, dur: c.gpu[i] * 1000 });
            if(i % 10 === 0) traceEvents.push({ ph: 'C', pid: 1, name: 'fps', ts, args: { fps: c.interval[i] > 0 ? Math.round(1000 / c.interval[i]) : 0 } });
        }
        for(const [start, dur] of this.longTasks) traceEvents.push({ ph: 'X', pid: 1, tid: 3, name: 'long task', ts: start * 1000, dur: dur * 1000 });
        const report = { format: 'voxel-wars-profile', version: 1, recorded: new Date().toISOString(), machine: this.machine(), load: this.loadMarks, summary: summarize(frames), frames, longTasks: this.longTasks, traceEvents };
        this.offer(report);
    }

    // ---- UI ----
    banner(text) {
        let b = this.bannerEl;
        if(!b) {
            b = this.bannerEl = document.createElement('div');
            b.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;top:10px;right:10px;z-index:1000;background:rgba(16,20,28,.85);color:#fff;font:13px system-ui;padding:8px 12px;border-radius:8px;max-width:340px;pointer-events:auto';
            document.body.appendChild(b);
        }
        b.textContent = text;
        return b;
    }
    offer(report) {
        const s = report.summary, m = report.machine;
        const json = JSON.stringify(report);
        const name = `voxel-wars-profile-${report.recorded.replace(/[:.]/g, '-').slice(0, 19)}.json`;
        const b = this.banner('');
        b.innerHTML = `<b>Profile recorded</b><br>${s.frames} frames · ${s.fps.toFixed(0)} fps avg · p99 frame ${s.interval.p99} ms<br>`
            + `CPU p50 ${s.cpu.p50} ms · GPU p50 ${s.gpu ? s.gpu.p50 + ' ms' : 'n/a'}<br><small>${m.backend} · ${(m.gpu && (m.gpu.renderer || m.gpu.description || m.gpu.vendor)) || 'unknown GPU'}</small><br>`
            + `<button data-a="dl">Download profile</button> <button data-a="copy">Copy summary</button> <button data-a="close">✕</button>`;
        for(const btn of b.querySelectorAll('button')) btn.style.cssText = 'margin-top:6px;padding:5px 9px;border-radius:6px;border:0;background:#f0a640;color:#111;font:inherit;cursor:pointer';
        b.querySelector('[data-a=dl]').onclick = () => {
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([json], { type: 'application/json' }));
            const a = Object.assign(document.createElement('a'), { href: url, download: name });
            document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
            setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 10000);
        };
        b.querySelector('[data-a=copy]').onclick = () => {
            const text = JSON.stringify({ machine: report.machine, load: report.load, summary: report.summary }, null, 1);
            navigator.clipboard?.writeText(text).then(() => { b.querySelector('[data-a=copy]').textContent = 'Copied'; }, () => prompt('Copy this:', text));
        };
        b.querySelector('[data-a=close]').onclick = () => { b.remove(); this.bannerEl = null; };
        if(document.pointerLockElement) document.exitPointerLock();
        window.lastProfile = report; // for headless tests
        console.log('[profile]', JSON.stringify(report.summary));
    }
}

/** Percentiles and per-section breakdown; shared with tools/profile-report.mjs via the same shape. */
export function summarize(frames) {
    const pct = (arr) => {
        const a = arr.filter(v => v != null && !Number.isNaN(v)).sort((x, y) => x - y);
        if(!a.length) return null;
        const q = (p) => +a[Math.min(a.length - 1, Math.floor(a.length * p))].toFixed(2);
        return { mean: +(a.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0) / a.length).toFixed(2), p50: q(0.5), p90: q(0.9), p99: q(0.99), max: +a[a.length - 1].toFixed(2) };
    };
    const iv = frames.interval.slice(1);
    const interval = pct(iv), cpu = pct(frames.cpu), gpu = pct(frames.gpu);
    const sections = {};
    for(const [k, v] of Object.entries(frames.sections)) sections[k] = pct(v);
    let verdict = 'smooth';
    // the frame interval sits on a display refresh period while CPU and GPU have room: vsync, not slowness
    const refresh = interval && [1000 / 30, 1000 / 60, 1000 / 75, 1000 / 90, 1000 / 120, 1000 / 144, 1000 / 165, 1000 / 240].find(p => Math.abs(interval.p50 - p) < p * 0.06);
    const busy = Math.max(cpu?.p50 || 0, gpu?.p50 || 0);
    if(refresh && busy < interval.p50 * 0.6) verdict = `capped by the display refresh (${Math.round(1000 / refresh)} Hz vsync); CPU and GPU have headroom` + (interval.p99 > 3 * interval.p50 ? ', with hitches (see worst frames)' : '');
    else if(interval && interval.p50 > 12) {
        if(gpu && gpu.p50 > interval.p50 * 0.6) verdict = 'GPU-bound: the GPU takes most of each frame';
        else if(cpu && cpu.p50 > interval.p50 * 0.6) verdict = 'CPU-bound: the main loop takes most of each frame (see sections)';
        else verdict = 'frame interval far above CPU time: likely GPU-bound (GPU time unavailable) or the browser is throttling';
    } else if(interval && interval.p99 > 3 * interval.p50) verdict = 'mostly smooth with hitches (see worst frames)';
    const worst = frames.interval.map((v, i) => [v, i]).sort((a, b) => b[0] - a[0]).slice(0, 10).map(([v, i]) => {
        const s = {}; for(const [k, arr] of Object.entries(frames.sections)) if(arr[i] > 0.3) s[k] = arr[i];
        return { t: frames.t[i], interval: v, cpu: frames.cpu[i], gpu: frames.gpu[i], sections: s, bodies: frames.bodies[i], pending: frames.pending[i] };
    });
    return { frames: frames.t.length, fps: interval ? 1000 / interval.mean : 0, interval, cpu, gpu, sections, verdict,
        drawCalls: pct(frames.calls), triangles: pct(frames.tris), worst };
}
```

### src/fx/Fx.js
```
/**
 * Destruction effects, pooled and instanced (one draw call per kind): rock chips (bouncing on the voxel SDF, coloured
 * by the material that broke, varied a little), dust puffs, fireballs and a single reused flash light. Driven by
 * ctx.events 'destroyed' / 'impact' / 'detached' and the game's explosion, shatter, dig, build and damage events.
 * Also shakes the player's camera by distance to blasts.
 */
import * as THREE from 'three/webgpu';
import { MATERIALS } from '../voxel/materials.js';

const GRAV = 30;
const _m = new THREE.Matrix4(), _q = new THREE.Quaternion(), _s = new THREE.Vector3(), _p = new THREE.Vector3(), _c = new THREE.Color(), _e = new THREE.Euler();

/** A pool of instances of one geometry, each with position, velocity, life and a per-frame updater. */
class Pool {
    constructor(scene, geo, mat, cap, { shadows = false } = {}) {
        this.mesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(geo, mat, cap);
        this.mesh.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
        this.mesh.setColorAt(0, new THREE.Color(1, 1, 1));
        this.mesh.count = 0;
        this.mesh.frustumCulled = false;
        this.mesh.castShadow = shadows;
        scene.add(this.mesh);
        this.cap = cap;
        this.n = 0;
        // structure of arrays
        this.pos = new Float32Array(cap * 3); this.vel = new Float32Array(cap * 3);
        this.rot = new Float32Array(cap * 3); this.spin = new Float32Array(cap * 3);
        this.size = new Float32Array(cap); this.life = new Float32Array(cap); this.max = new Float32Array(cap);
        this.col = new Float32Array(cap * 3); this.rest = new Uint8Array(cap);
        this.vec3s = [this.pos, this.vel, this.rot, this.spin, this.col];
    }
    add(p, v, size, life, color) {
        let i = this.n;
        if(i >= this.cap) i = Math.floor(Math.random() * this.cap); // full: recycle a random one
        else this.n++;
        this.pos.set(p, i * 3); this.vel.set(v, i * 3);
        this.rot[i * 3] = Math.random() * 6; this.rot[i * 3 + 1] = Math.random() * 6; this.rot[i * 3 + 2] = Math.random() * 6;
        this.spin[i * 3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 14; this.spin[i * 3 + 1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 14; this.spin[i * 3 + 2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 14;
        this.size[i] = size; this.life[i] = life; this.max[i] = life; this.rest[i] = 0;
        this.col[i * 3] = color.r; this.col[i * 3 + 1] = color.g; this.col[i * 3 + 2] = color.b;
        return i;
    }
    kill(i) {
        const j = --this.n;
        if(i === j) return;
        for(const a of this.vec3s) { a[i * 3] = a[j * 3]; a[i * 3 + 1] = a[j * 3 + 1]; a[i * 3 + 2] = a[j * 3 + 2]; }
        this.size[i] = this.size[j]; this.life[i] = this.life[j]; this.max[i] = this.max[j]; this.rest[i] = this.rest[j];
    }
    flush(scaleOf, colorOf) {
        const m = this.mesh;
        if(this.n === 0 && m.count === 0) return;
        for(let i = 0; i < this.n; i++) {
            const s = scaleOf(i);
            _e.set(this.rot[i * 3], this.rot[i * 3 + 1], this.rot[i * 3 + 2]);
            _q.setFromEuler(_e);
            _m.compose(_p.set(this.pos[i * 3], this.pos[i * 3 + 1], this.pos[i * 3 + 2]), _q, _s.set(s, s, s));
            m.setMatrixAt(i, _m);
            colorOf.call(this, i, _c);
            m.setColorAt(i, _c);
        }
        m.count = this.n;
        m.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
        if(m.instanceColor) m.instanceColor.needsUpdate = true;
    }
}

export class Fx {
    constructor(game) {
        this.game = game;
        const ctx = this.ctx = game.ctx;
        const chipGeo = new THREE.DodecahedronGeometry(1, 0);
        this.chips = new Pool(ctx.scene, chipGeo, new THREE.MeshStandardNodeMaterial({ roughness: 0.9, flatShading: true }), 900, { shadows: false });
        this.dust = new Pool(ctx.scene, new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(1, 1), new THREE.MeshStandardNodeMaterial({ roughness: 1, transparent: true, opacity: 0.42, depthWrite: false }), 400);
        this.fire = new Pool(ctx.scene, new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(1, 2), new THREE.MeshBasicNodeMaterial({ transparent: true, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false }), 80);
        this.light = new THREE.PointLight(0xffa040, 0, 40, 1.6);
        ctx.scene.add(this.light);
        this.lightT = 0;
        this._tp = [0, 0, 0]; this._tv = [0, 0, 0];
        // per-instance size and colour functions, made once (flush runs every frame)
        const C = this.chips, D = this.dust, F = this.fire;
        this.chipScale = (i) => C.size[i] * Math.min(1, C.life[i] / 0.5);
```
