        this.dustScale = (i) => { const t = 1 - D.life[i] / D.max[i]; return D.size[i] * (0.6 + t * 1.2) * Math.min(1, D.life[i] / (D.max[i] * 0.4)); };
        this.fireScale = (i) => { const t = 1 - F.life[i] / F.max[i]; return F.size[i] * (0.5 + t * 0.9); };
        this.plainColor = function(i, c) { c.setRGB(this.col[i * 3], this.col[i * 3 + 1], this.col[i * 3 + 2]); };
        this.fireColor = (i, c) => { const t = F.life[i] / F.max[i]; c.setRGB(F.col[i * 3] * t, F.col[i * 3 + 1] * t * t, F.col[i * 3 + 2] * t * t * t); };
        this.matColors = MATERIALS.map(m => new THREE.Color(m.color));
        this.hook();
    }
    /** A material's colour, randomly lighter or darker so a pile of chips reads as rock. */
    tint(mat, out = new THREE.Color()) {
        out.copy(this.matColors[mat] || this.matColors[1]);
        const k = 0.75 + Math.random() * 0.45;
        return out.multiplyScalar(k);
    }
    /** Chips flying out of `center` (spread over `box` if given), mats picked weighted by `removed`. */
    burst(center, { count = 10, speed = 8, size = 0.12, mats = [1], weights = null, box = null, up = 4, dust = 3, dustColor = null, dir = null } = {}) {
        const total = weights ? weights.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) : 0;
        for(let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            let mat = mats[0];
            if(weights && total > 0) { let r = Math.random() * total; for(let k = 0; k < mats.length; k++) { r -= weights[k]; if(r <= 0) { mat = mats[k]; break; } } }
            else mat = mats[Math.floor(Math.random() * mats.length)];
            const p = box ? [box[0][0] + Math.random() * (box[1][0] - box[0][0]), box[0][1] + Math.random() * (box[1][1] - box[0][1]), box[0][2] + Math.random() * (box[1][2] - box[0][2])]
                : [center[0] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.6, center[1] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.6, center[2] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.6];
            let dx = p[0] - center[0] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8, dy = p[1] - center[1] + Math.random() * 0.5, dz = p[2] - center[2] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.8;
            if(dir) { dx += dir[0] * 1.5; dy += dir[1] * 1.5; dz += dir[2] * 1.5; }
            const l = Math.hypot(dx, dy, dz) || 1, s = speed * (0.4 + Math.random() * 0.8);
            this.chips.add(p, [dx / l * s, dy / l * s + up * Math.random(), dz / l * s], size * (0.5 + Math.random()), 1.6 + Math.random() * 1.4, this.tint(mat, _c));
        }
        for(let i = 0; i < dust; i++) {
            const col = dustColor || this.tint(mats[0], _c).lerp(new THREE.Color(0xd8d0c0), 0.5);
            const p = [center[0] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.2, center[1] + (Math.random() - 0.3) * 0.8, center[2] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.2];
            this.dust.add(p, [(Math.random() - 0.5) * 2.5, 0.6 + Math.random() * 1.4, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2.5], 0.35 + Math.random() * 0.5 + size, 1.2 + Math.random() * 1.2, col);
        }
    }
    trail(pos) {
        const p = this._tp, v = this._tv;
        p[0] = pos.x; p[1] = pos.y; p[2] = pos.z;
        v[0] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.6; v[1] = 0.4; v[2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.6;
        this.dust.add(p, v, 0.12 + Math.random() * 0.08, 0.7, _c.setRGB(0.75, 0.75, 0.75));
        v[0] = v[1] = v[2] = 0;
        if(Math.random() < 0.6) this.fire.add(p, v, 0.12, 0.12, _c.setRGB(1, 0.6, 0.2));
    }
    explosion(center, radius, kind) {
        const n = Math.round(4 + radius * 2);
        for(let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            const v = [(Math.random() - 0.5) * radius * 3, Math.random() * radius * 2.5, (Math.random() - 0.5) * radius * 3];
            this.fire.add([center[0] + v[0] * 0.1, center[1] + v[1] * 0.1, center[2] + v[2] * 0.1], v, radius * (0.35 + Math.random() * 0.3), 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.25, _c.setRGB(1, 0.55 + Math.random() * 0.3, 0.15));
        }
        for(let i = 0; i < n + 2; i++) {
            const g = 0.25 + Math.random() * 0.2;
            this.dust.add([center[0] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * radius, center[1] + Math.random() * radius * 0.6, center[2] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * radius],
                [(Math.random() - 0.5) * radius * 1.5, 1 + Math.random() * radius, (Math.random() - 0.5) * radius * 1.5], radius * (0.3 + Math.random() * 0.3), 1.6 + Math.random() * 1.5, _c.setRGB(g, g, g * 0.95));
        }
        this.light.position.set(center[0], center[1] + 0.5, center[2]);
        this.light.intensity = 60 * radius * radius;
        this.light.distance = radius * 8;
        this.lightT = 0.25;
        // camera shake by distance
        const cam = this.ctx.camera.position;
        const d = Math.hypot(cam.x - center[0], cam.y - center[1], cam.z - center[2]);
        const k = Math.min(0.9, radius * 0.9 / (d + 1.5));
        if(k > 0.03) this.game.player?.shake(k);
    }

    hook() {
        const g = this.game, ev = this.ctx.events;
        ev.addEventListener('destroyed', (e) => {
            const d = e.detail; if(!d || !(d.volume > 0)) return;
            const mats = [], weights = [];
            for(let i = 0; i < d.removed.length; i++) if(d.removed[i] > 0) { mats.push(i); weights.push(d.removed[i]); }
            if(!mats.length) return;
            const v = d.volume;
            const blast = d.kind === 'blast';
            const count = Math.round(Math.min(blast ? 70 : 28, 4 + v * (blast ? 4 : 6)));
            const inner = d.box ? [d.box[0].map((x, i) => (x * 2 + d.box[1][i]) / 3), d.box[1].map((x, i) => (x * 2 + d.box[0][i]) / 3)] : null;
            this.burst(d.center, { count, speed: blast ? 14 : 6, size: blast ? 0.16 : 0.1, mats, weights, box: inner, up: blast ? 8 : 4, dust: Math.round(Math.min(blast ? 14 : 6, 1 + v)) });
        });
        ev.addEventListener('impact', (e) => {
            const d = e.detail; if(!d || d.speed < 5) return;
            this.burst(d.point, { count: Math.min(10, Math.round(d.speed / 3)), speed: d.speed * 0.3, mats: [d.mat ?? 0], dust: 2, size: 0.08 });
        });
        g.on('explosion', ({ center, radius, kind }) => this.explosion(center, kind === 'rock' ? radius * 0.6 : radius, kind));
        g.on('shatter', ({ point, mat, volume }) => this.burst(point, { count: Math.min(60, 20 + Math.round(volume * 15)), speed: 12, size: 0.16, mats: [mat], up: 6, dust: 6 }));
        g.on('slam', ({ point, mat }) => this.burst(point, { count: 12, speed: 6, size: 0.12, mats: [mat], up: 4, dust: 5 }));
        g.on('rip', ({ point, mat }) => { if(point) this.burst(point, { count: 14, speed: 5, size: 0.1, mats: [mat], up: 5, dust: 4 }); });
        g.on('punch', ({ point, kind }) => { if(kind === 'prop' || kind === 'actor') this.burst(point, { count: 5, speed: 5, size: 0.06, mats: [kind === 'actor' ? 7 : 10], dust: 1 }); });
        g.on('build', ({ center, size }) => this.burst([center[0], center[1] - size[1] / 2, center[2]], { count: 6, speed: 3, size: 0.08, mats: [8], dust: 6 }));
        g.on('damage', ({ actor }) => { if(!actor.isPlayer) { const c = actor.body.center; this.burst([c.x, c.y + 0.3, c.z], { count: 6, speed: 6, size: 0.07, mats: [7], dust: 0 }); } });
        g.on('deposit', ({ crate }) => { const t = crate.prop.body.translation(); for(let i = 0; i < 24; i++) this.fire.add([t.x, t.y + 0.5, t.z], [(Math.random() - 0.5) * 6, 4 + Math.random() * 6, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 6], 0.08, 0.9, _c.setRGB(1, 0.85, 0.3)); });
    }

    update(dt) {
        const w = this.ctx.world;
        // chips: ballistic, bounce on the terrain's SDF, settle, then shrink away
        const C = this.chips;
        for(let i = C.n - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            C.life[i] -= dt;
            if(C.life[i] <= 0) { C.kill(i); continue; }
            const o = i * 3;
            if(!C.rest[i]) {
                C.vel[o + 1] -= GRAV * dt;
                const nx = C.pos[o] + C.vel[o] * dt, ny = C.pos[o + 1] + C.vel[o + 1] * dt, nz = C.pos[o + 2] + C.vel[o + 2] * dt;
                if(w.sample(nx, ny, nz) < C.size[i] * 0.3) {
                    // bounce: mostly upward, lose most of the speed
                    const sp = Math.hypot(C.vel[o], C.vel[o + 1], C.vel[o + 2]);
                    if(sp < 2.5) { C.rest[i] = 1; C.life[i] = Math.min(C.life[i], 1.2 + Math.random()); }
                    C.vel[o] *= 0.45; C.vel[o + 2] *= 0.45; C.vel[o + 1] = Math.abs(C.vel[o + 1]) * 0.3;
                    C.spin[o] *= 0.5; C.spin[o + 1] *= 0.5; C.spin[o + 2] *= 0.5;
                } else { C.pos[o] = nx; C.pos[o + 1] = ny; C.pos[o + 2] = nz; }
                C.rot[o] += C.spin[o] * dt; C.rot[o + 1] += C.spin[o + 1] * dt; C.rot[o + 2] += C.spin[o + 2] * dt;
            }
        }
        C.flush(this.chipScale, this.plainColor);
        // dust: drifts up, grows, then shrinks
        const D = this.dust;
        for(let i = D.n - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            D.life[i] -= dt;
            if(D.life[i] <= 0) { D.kill(i); continue; }
            const o = i * 3, k = Math.max(0, 1 - dt * 1.5);
            D.vel[o] *= k; D.vel[o + 1] = D.vel[o + 1] * k + dt * 0.4; D.vel[o + 2] *= k;
            D.pos[o] += D.vel[o] * dt; D.pos[o + 1] += D.vel[o + 1] * dt; D.pos[o + 2] += D.vel[o + 2] * dt;
            D.rot[o + 1] += dt * 0.3;
        }
        D.flush(this.dustScale, this.plainColor);
        // fireballs: expand, cool from yellow to red to nothing (additive: darker = more transparent)
        const F = this.fire;
        for(let i = F.n - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            F.life[i] -= dt;
            if(F.life[i] <= 0) { F.kill(i); continue; }
            const o = i * 3, k = Math.max(0, 1 - dt * 4);
            F.vel[o] *= k; F.vel[o + 1] *= k; F.vel[o + 2] *= k;
            F.pos[o] += F.vel[o] * dt; F.pos[o + 1] += F.vel[o + 1] * dt; F.pos[o + 2] += F.vel[o + 2] * dt;
        }
        F.flush(this.fireScale, this.fireColor);
        if(this.lightT > 0) { this.lightT -= dt; this.light.intensity *= Math.max(0, 1 - dt * 12); if(this.lightT <= 0) this.light.intensity = 0; }
    }
}
```

### src/game/Actor.js
```
/**
 * What the player and the bots have in common: a CharacterBody, a team, health with regeneration, a carried crate,
 * the mason's stone budget, a bunker buster per life, and the colliders their own rays and movement pass through.
 */
import { CharacterBody } from './CharacterBody.js';
import { WEAPONS } from './weapons/defs.js';

export const MAX_HEALTH = 100;
export const RESPAWN_S = 4;
export const REGEN_DELAY = 5, REGEN_PER_S = 15;

export class Actor {
    constructor(game, { team, name, spawn, isPlayer = false }) {
        this.game = game;
        this.team = team;
        this.name = name;
        this.isPlayer = isPlayer;
        this.local = true;     // simulated on this client (online: the own player, and the host's bots; src/game/NetGame.js)
        this.netId = null;     // online: 'p:<client>' or 'b:<team><i>'
        this.spawn = spawn;
        this.body = new CharacterBody(game.ctx, { position: spawn, owner: this });
        game.actorByCollider.set(this.body.collider.handle, this);
        this.health = MAX_HEALTH;
        this.alive = true;
        this.respawnIn = 0;
        this.sinceHit = 99;
        this.carrying = null;
        this.held = null;
        this.stone = WEAPONS.mason.budget;
        this.stoneRegen = 0;
        this.busters = WEAPONS.buster.perLife;
        this.temp = new Map(); // collider handle -> seconds left
        this.kills = 0; this.deaths = 0;
        this._ign = new Set();
    }
    /** A person playing (this client's player, or another client's): bots hurt people less (BOT_DAMAGE_SCALE). */
    get isHuman() { return this.isPlayer; }
    /** Where it is drawn (bots interpolate between their 30 Hz ticks). */
    get viewCenter() { return this.view ?? this.body.center; }
    /** Collider handles this actor's rays and movement ignore. */
    ignoreHandles() {
        const s = this._ign; s.clear();
        s.add(this.body.collider.handle);
        if(this.held) s.add(this.held.collider.handle);
        if(this.carrying) s.add(this.carrying.prop.collider.handle);
        for(const h of this.temp.keys()) s.add(h);
        return s;
    }
    ignoreTemp(handle, seconds) { this.temp.set(handle, seconds); }
    addStone(volume) {
        const m = WEAPONS.mason;
        if(volume > 0.02) this.stone = Math.min(m.budget, this.stone + volume * m.digYield);
    }
    tickCommon(dt) {
        for(const [h, t] of this.temp) { if(t - dt <= 0) this.temp.delete(h); else this.temp.set(h, t - dt); }
        // the controller passes through what we hold and carry
        const ign = this.body.ignore; ign.clear();
        if(this.held) ign.add(this.held.collider.handle);
        if(this.carrying) ign.add(this.carrying.prop.collider.handle);
        for(const h of this.temp.keys()) ign.add(h);
        if(!this.alive) return;
        this.sinceHit += dt;
        if(this.sinceHit > REGEN_DELAY && this.health < MAX_HEALTH) this.health = Math.min(MAX_HEALTH, this.health + REGEN_PER_S * dt);
        const m = WEAPONS.mason;
        if(this.stone < m.budget) { this.stoneRegen += dt; if(this.stoneRegen >= m.regen) { this.stoneRegen = 0; this.stone = Math.min(m.budget, this.stone + 1); } }
    }
    respawn() {
        this.alive = true;
        this.health = MAX_HEALTH;
        this.busters = WEAPONS.buster.perLife;
        this.stone = Math.max(this.stone, WEAPONS.mason.budget / 2);
        this.sinceHit = 99;
        this.body.collider.setEnabled(true);
        const s = this.spawn;
        this.body.teleport([s[0] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2, s[1] + 0.3, s[2] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2]);
    }
    die() {
        this.alive = false;
        this.respawnIn = RESPAWN_S;
        this.deaths++;
        this.body.collider.setEnabled(false);
    }
}
```

### src/game/Bot.js
```
/**
 * A Blue bot. Cheap and direct: it picks a goal (the nearest crate it can steal, or home to its vault when carrying),
 * walks straight at it and drills straight through whatever is in the way, so the bots keep tunnelling the world
 * open. When it sees the player it turns and fires rockets (lead and a little error); up close it punches.
 * Thinking runs a few times a second; moving runs every frame (a CharacterBody like the player's).
 */
import * as THREE from 'three/webgpu';
import { Actor } from './Actor.js';
import { WEAPONS } from './weapons/defs.js';
import { makeAvatar } from './avatar.js';

const BOT_STEP = 1 / 30;
const SPEED = 7.2, JUMP = 19, CARRY_SLOW = 0.8, START_DELAY = 5;
const RAMP = 0.5; // rad: how steep a bot digs up toward something above it
const _e = new THREE.Vector3(), _t = new THREE.Vector3();
/** Stands in for "no enemy at all" (a room where every human is on one team and bots are off). */
const NOBODY = { alive: false, carrying: null, body: { center: new THREE.Vector3(), vel: new THREE.Vector3(), grounded: true, eye: (o) => o.set(0, -999, 0) } };

export class Bot extends Actor {
    constructor(game, opts) {
        super(game, opts);
        this.yaw = 0;
        this.aimPitch = 0;
        this.goal = null;          // [x, y, z]
        this.goalKind = '';
        this.think = Math.random() * 0.4;
        this.cool = { rocket: 1.5 + Math.random() * 2, drill: 0, punch: 0, look: 0 };
        this.stuck = 0;
        this.lastPos = new THREE.Vector3().copy(this.body.center);
        this.progressT = 0;
        this.seesPlayer = false;
        this.reaction = 0;
        this.skill = opts.skill ?? 0.5;
        this.role = opts.role ?? 'raider';
        this.dropCooldown = 0;
        this.digging = 0;
        this.mesh = this.makeMesh(opts.color ?? 0x3a7be0);
        game.ctx.scene.add(this.mesh);
        this.walk = 0;
        this._eye = [0, 0, 0]; this._aim = [0, 0, 0]; this._dir = [0, 0, 0]; this._dig = [0, 0, 0];
        this.avoid = new Map(); this.bestD = Infinity; this.bestT = 0;
        this.think = Math.random() * 0.5; // staggered: bots never think on the same frame
        this.acc = (opts.phase ?? Math.random()) * BOT_STEP;
        this.prev = new THREE.Vector3().copy(this.body.center);
        this.view = new THREE.Vector3().copy(this.body.center);
    }
    makeMesh(color) {
        const av = makeAvatar(color);
        this.gun = av.gun; this.legs = av.legs;
        return av.group;
    }
    eye(out = this._eye) { this.body.eye(_e); out[0] = _e.x; out[1] = _e.y; out[2] = _e.z; return out; }
    aimDir(out = this._aim) { const cp = Math.cos(this.aimPitch); out[0] = -Math.sin(this.yaw) * cp; out[1] = Math.sin(this.aimPitch); out[2] = -Math.cos(this.yaw) * cp; return out; }

    /** The enemy this bot cares about: in single player the player; online the nearest living enemy (a crate
     *  carrier within 30 m first). */
    foe() {
        const game = this.game;
        if(!game.netGame) return game.player.team !== this.team ? game.player : NOBODY;
        const me = this.body.center;
        let best = null, bd = Infinity;
        for(const a of game.actors) {
            if(!a.alive || a.team === this.team) continue;
            const d = a.body.center.distanceTo(me) - (a.carrying ? 30 : 0);
            if(d < bd) { bd = d; best = a; }
        }
        return best || NOBODY;
    }
    chooseGoal() {
        const game = this.game, me = this.body.center;
        const pl = this.foe();
        const prevCrate = this.goalCrate;
        this.goalCrate = null;
        if(this.role === 'defender' && !this.carrying) {
            // guard: go after a player who comes over to our side or carries one of our crates, else hang around the vault door
            const pc = pl.body.center;
            if(pl.alive && (pl.carrying || (this.team === 'blue' ? pc.x > 8 : pc.x < -8))) { this.goal = [pc.x, pc.y, pc.z]; this.goalKind = 'hunt'; return; }
            const v = game.crates.vaults[this.team], s = this.team === 'blue' ? 1 : -1;
            this.goal = [s * 44 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 10, 0.2, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 16]; this.goalKind = 'guard'; return;
        }
        if(this.carrying) { const v = game.crates.vaults[this.team]; this.goal = [(v.min[0] + v.max[0]) / 2, v.min[1] + 1.2, (v.min[2] + v.max[2]) / 2]; this.goalKind = 'home'; return; }
        // a player carrying a crate nearby: chase him down
        if(pl.alive && pl.carrying && pl.body.center.distanceTo(me) < 30) { const c = pl.body.center; this.goal = [c.x, c.y, c.z]; this.goalKind = 'chase'; return; }
        let best = null, bd = Infinity;
        for(const c of game.crates.list) {
            if(c.carrier || c.vault === this.team) continue;
            if((this.avoid.get(c) ?? -1) > game.elapsed) continue; // gave up on this one a moment ago
            const t = c.prop.body.translation();
            // spread out: crates a teammate already goes for cost more
            const taken = game.bots.some(b => b !== this && b.goalCrate === c);
            const d = Math.hypot(t.x - me.x, (t.y - me.y) * 1.5, t.z - me.z) + (taken ? 25 : 0) + (c.vault ? -6 : 0);
            if(d < bd) { bd = d; best = c; }
        }
        if(best !== prevCrate) { this.bestD = Infinity; this.bestT = game.elapsed; }
        this.goalCrate = best;
        if(best) { const t = best.prop.body.translation(); this.goal = [t.x, t.y, t.z]; this.goalKind = 'crate'; }
        else { const c = pl.body.center; this.goal = [c.x, c.y, c.z]; this.goalKind = 'hunt'; }
    }

    /** Line of sight from our eye to the player's eye (nothing but the player in between). */
    canSee(target) {
        const e = this.eye(), t = target.body.eye(_t), d = this._dir;
        d[0] = t.x - e[0]; d[1] = t.y - e[1]; d[2] = t.z - e[2];
        const L = Math.hypot(d[0], d[1], d[2]);
        if(L > 55) return false;
        d[0] /= L; d[1] /= L; d[2] /= L;
        const hit = this.game.combat.raycast(e, d, L, this);
        return !hit || hit.actor === target;
    }

    /** Bots simulate at a fixed 30 Hz, each on its own phase (so they never all land on one frame); between
     *  ticks their meshes interpolate. At 240 fps that is an eighth of the controller work. */
    update(dt) {
        this.acc += dt;
        if(this.acc >= BOT_STEP) {
            const h = Math.min(this.acc, 0.1);
            this.acc = this.acc >= BOT_STEP * 2 ? 0 : this.acc - BOT_STEP;
            this.prev.copy(this.body.center);
            this.tick(h);
        }
        if(this.alive) this.place(Math.min(1, this.acc / BOT_STEP));
    }
    tick(dt) {
        this.tickCommon(dt);
        for(const k in this.cool) this.cool[k] -= dt;
        this.dropCooldown -= dt;
        if(!this.alive) { this.mesh.visible = false; return; }
        this.mesh.visible = true;
        const game = this.game, body = this.body, me = body.center;
        if(game.over) { body.step(dt, 0, 0); this.pose(dt, 0); return; }
        this.think -= dt;
        const pl = this.target && this.target.alive ? this.target : (this.target = this.foe());
        if(this.think <= 0) {
            this.think = 0.35 + Math.random() * 0.2;
            this.target = this.foe();
            if(!this.goal || this.goalKind !== 'crate' || !this.goalCrate || this.goalCrate.carrier || Math.random() < 0.3) this.chooseGoal();
            else { const t = this.goalCrate.prop.body.translation(); this.goal = [t.x, t.y, t.z]; }
            // no progress toward a crate for a long while (it sits somewhere we cannot dig up to): try another
            if(this.goalKind === 'crate' && this.goalCrate) {
                const t = this.goalCrate.prop.body.translation(), dd = Math.hypot(t.x - me.x, t.y - me.y, t.z - me.z);
                if(dd < this.bestD - 1) { this.bestD = dd; this.bestT = game.elapsed; }
                else if(game.elapsed - this.bestT > 12) { this.avoid.set(this.goalCrate, game.elapsed + 40); this.goalCrate = null; this.climbing = false; this.chooseGoal(); }
            }
            const was = this.seesPlayer;
            this.seesPlayer = pl.alive && this.canSee(pl);
            if(this.seesPlayer && !was) this.reaction = 0.5 + (1 - this.skill) * 0.5;
        }
        this.reaction -= dt;
        // where to go
        const g = this.goal || [me.x, me.y, me.z];
        const dx = g[0] - me.x, dy = g[1] - (me.y - 0.9), dz = g[2] - me.z, hd = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
        let moveYaw = Math.atan2(-dx, -dz);
        // right over (or under) the goal: keep moving, so it digs a ramp down (or up) to it instead of waiting there
        let wish = hd > 0.6 || Math.abs(dy) > 1.6 ? SPEED * (this.carrying ? CARRY_SLOW : 1) : 0;
        if(this.goalKind === 'guard' && hd < 3) wish = 0;
        if(game.elapsed < START_DELAY) wish = 0;
        // progress check: stuck against something for a while -> dig through it
        this.progressT += dt;
        if(this.progressT > 0.5) {
            const moved = Math.hypot(me.x - this.lastPos.x, me.z - this.lastPos.z);
            this.stuck = moved < 0.8 && wish > 0 ? this.stuck + this.progressT : Math.max(0, this.stuck - this.progressT * 2);
            this.lastPos.copy(me); this.progressT = 0;
        }

        // digging pitch: straight at the goal, kept walkable
        let digPitch = Math.atan2(dy, Math.max(hd, 0.1));
        const below = dy < -1.2 && hd < Math.max(6, -dy * 2.5);
        let above = dy > 1.6 && hd < dy * 4;
        // something high up: back off far enough to dig a walkable ramp up to it
        if(above) {
            const need = dy / Math.tan(RAMP);
            if(!this.climbing && (hd >= need * 0.75 || this.goalKind === 'hunt')) this.climbing = true;
            if(!this.climbing) { moveYaw += Math.PI; digPitch = 0; above = false; wish = SPEED; }
            else digPitch = Math.min(RAMP + 0.05, Math.atan2(dy, hd));
        } else this.climbing = false;
        digPitch = Math.max(-0.75, Math.min(0.6, digPitch));
        const wantDig = below || (above && this.stuck > 0.3) || this.stuck > 0.4;
        // combat: face and shoot the player
        let aimYaw = moveYaw, aimPitch = digPitch;
        let fight = false;
        if(this.seesPlayer && pl.alive && this.reaction <= 0) {
            const e = this.eye(), t = pl.body.center;
            const dist = Math.hypot(t.x - e[0], t.z - e[2]);
            // lead the target a little
            const tl = dist / WEAPONS.rocket.speed;
            const tx = t.x + pl.body.vel.x * tl * this.skill, ty = t.y - 0.3 + (pl.body.grounded ? 0 : pl.body.vel.y * tl * 0.3), tz = t.z + pl.body.vel.z * tl * this.skill;
            const ax = tx - e[0], ay = ty - e[1], az = tz - e[2];
            aimYaw = Math.atan2(-ax, -az);
            aimPitch = Math.atan2(ay, Math.hypot(ax, az)) + Math.hypot(ax, az) * 0.004; // rockets drop a little
            fight = dist < 40;
            if(dist < 2.6 && this.cool.punch <= 0) {
                this.cool.punch = 0.7; this.yaw = aimYaw; this.aimPitch = aimPitch;
                game.combat.punch(this, e, this.aimDir());
            } else if(fight && this.cool.rocket <= 0 && dist > 4) {
                this.cool.rocket = 2.0 + Math.random() * 1.6 - this.skill * 0.6;
                const err = (1.3 - this.skill) * 0.11;
                this.yaw = aimYaw + (Math.random() - 0.5) * err; this.aimPitch = aimPitch + (Math.random() - 0.5) * err;
                game.combat.fireRocket(this, this.muzzle(), this.aimDir());
            }
            // strafe a little while fighting, unless carrying (then run home)
            if(fight && !this.carrying && this.goalKind !== 'crate') { moveYaw += Math.sin(performance.now() / 600 + this.skill * 9) * 1.2; wish = dist > 12 ? SPEED : SPEED * 0.6; }
        }
        // turn the body toward the aim (smoothly)
        const targetYaw = fight ? aimYaw : moveYaw;
        let dyaw = ((targetYaw - this.yaw + Math.PI * 3) % (Math.PI * 2)) - Math.PI;
        this.yaw += dyaw * Math.min(1, dt * 8);
        if(!fight) this.aimPitch += (digPitch - this.aimPitch) * Math.min(1, dt * 8);
        // dig toward the goal when it is below / above / something blocks the way
        if(wantDig && this.cool.drill <= 0 && wish > 0) {
            this.cool.drill = WEAPONS.drill.cooldown * 1.5;
            const cp = Math.cos(digPitch);
            const dd = this._dig; dd[0] = -Math.sin(moveYaw) * cp; dd[1] = Math.sin(digPitch); dd[2] = -Math.cos(moveYaw) * cp;
            const vol = game.combat.dig(this, dd);
            this.digging = 0.5;
            // hard rock barely gives: blast it
            if(vol < 0.15 && this.stuck > 2.5 && this.cool.rocket <= 0) {
                this.cool.rocket = 2.5;
                const d = [-Math.sin(moveYaw), Math.max(-0.5, Math.min(0.5, digPitch)), -Math.cos(moveYaw)];
                game.combat.fireRocket(this, this.eye(), d);
                this.stuck = 0;
            }
        }
        this.digging -= dt;
        const jump = (this.stuck > 0.6 && body.grounded && Math.random() < 0.15) || (above && body.grounded && Math.random() < 0.05);
        if(this.stuck > 6) { this.stuck = 0; this.chooseGoal(); }
        const sp = this.digging > 0 && wantDig ? wish * 0.7 : wish;
        body.step(dt, -Math.sin(moveYaw) * sp, -Math.cos(moveYaw) * sp, { jump, jumpSpeed: JUMP, accel: 60 });
        if(me.y < -24) game.damage(this, 1000, null, null, 'fell');
        this.pose(dt, Math.hypot(body.vel.x, body.vel.z));
    }
    muzzle() { const e = this.eye(); const c = Math.cos(this.yaw), s = Math.sin(this.yaw); return [e[0] + c * 0.36, e[1] - 0.35, e[2] - s * 0.36]; }
    /** Mesh between the last two simulated positions (k 0..1). */
    place(k) {
        const c = this.body.center, p = this.prev, m = this.mesh, drop = this.body.radius + this.body.halfHeight;
        this.view.set(p.x + (c.x - p.x) * k, p.y + (c.y - p.y) * k, p.z + (c.z - p.z) * k);
        m.position.set(this.view.x, this.view.y - drop, this.view.z);
    }
    pose(dt, speed) {
        const m = this.mesh;
        m.rotation.y = this.yaw;
        this.walk += dt * speed * 1.4;
        const s = speed > 0.5 ? Math.sin(this.walk) : 0;
        this.legs[0].rotation.x = s * 0.6; this.legs[1].rotation.x = -s * 0.6;
        this.gun.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2 + this.aimPitch;
    }
    respawn() { super.respawn(); this.prev.copy(this.body.center); this.view.copy(this.body.center); this.goal = null; this.stuck = 0; this.cool.rocket = 2; }
    dispose() { this.mesh.removeFromParent(); }
}
```

### src/game/CharacterBody.js
```
/**
 * A walking capsule (player and bots): box3d's capsule mover (collide, solve the contact planes, cast; B3World.move)
 * against the terrain meshes and bodies, with arcade gravity, jumping, knockback, ground snapping, a step-up over
 * ledges, and a push-out from solid terrain (sampling the voxel SDF) for when the world is built or collapses around
 * it. A kinematic capsule body follows it, so bodies and the other actors' movers collide with it.
 *
 *   const cb = new CharacterBody(ctx, { position: [x, y, z] (feet), radius, halfHeight })
 *   cb.step(dt, wishX, wishZ, { jump, speed, accel, airAccel })  wish = desired horizontal velocity (m/s)
 *   cb.feet / cb.center (THREE.Vector3), cb.vel, cb.grounded, cb.knock([x,y,z]), cb.teleport([x,y,z])
 */
import * as THREE from 'three/webgpu';
import { BodyType } from '../physics/Physics.js';

export const PLAYER_GRAVITY = 8 * 9.81;     // WorldParty: players fall at 8 g, bodies at 30 m/s²
const COYOTE = 0.1;
const STEP_UP = 0.75;         // tallest ledge (or collider seam) walked over without jumping (the vault trench's is 0.67 m)
const MANTLE = 3.0;           // m: a ledge up to this high (arm's reach) is hopped onto after pushing into it for MANTLE_T s
const MANTLE_T = 0.2;
const STEP_PROBE = 0.25;      // m: how far past a ledge's edge the step-up looks for ground
const FLOOR_COS = Math.cos(60 * Math.PI / 180); // walkable up to 60°; steeper is a wall while walking, a slide in the air
const SNAP = 0.5;             // stays on the ground over drops and down slopes up to this
const PUSH_MASS = 90;         // kg the mover pushes bodies with

export class CharacterBody {
    constructor(ctx, { position = [0, 0, 0], radius = 0.4, halfHeight = 0.5, gravity = PLAYER_GRAVITY, owner = null } = {}) {
        this.ctx = ctx;
        const physics = this.physics = ctx.physics;
        this.radius = radius; this.halfHeight = halfHeight;
        this.height = 2 * (radius + halfHeight);
        this.gravity = gravity;
        this.center = new THREE.Vector3(position[0], position[1] + radius + halfHeight, position[2]);
        this.vel = new THREE.Vector3();
        this.grounded = false;
        this.airTime = 0;
        this.landSpeed = 0;     // vertical speed at the last landing (for land sounds / shake)
        this.justLanded = false;
        this.body = physics.createBody({ type: BodyType.Kinematic, position: [this.center.x, this.center.y, this.center.z] });
        this.collider = physics.b3.createCapsule(this.body, halfHeight, radius, { friction: 0 });
        this.collider.userData = { actor: owner };
        this.ignore = new Set();  // collider handles the mover passes through (a carried crate, a held rock)
        this._ign = [];
        this._n = [0, 0, 0];
        this._desired = { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }; this._moved = { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, grounded: false };
    }
    /** One mover query from `from` by `d` (B3World.move). */
    _move(from, d, walk, snap, dt, out, castOnly = false) {
        return this.physics.b3.move(from, this.halfHeight, this.radius, d,
            { walk, castOnly, floorCos: FLOOR_COS, snap, pushMass: dt > 0 ? PUSH_MASS : 0, dt, ignore: this._ign }, out);
    }
    get feet() { return this._feet ??= new THREE.Vector3(), this._feet.set(this.center.x, this.center.y - this.radius - this.halfHeight, this.center.z); }
    /** Eye point (a little below the top of the capsule). */
    eye(out = new THREE.Vector3()) { return out.set(this.center.x, this.center.y + this.halfHeight + this.radius * 0.55, this.center.z); }

    teleport(feet) {
        this.center.set(feet[0], feet[1] + this.radius + this.halfHeight, feet[2]);
        this.vel.set(0, 0, 0);
        this.body.setTranslation(this.center, true);
    }
    knock(v) {
        this.vel.x += v[0]; this.vel.y += v[1]; this.vel.z += v[2];
        if(v[1] > 2) { this.grounded = false; this.airTime = COYOTE; this.knocked = 0.25; }
    }
    canJump() { return this.grounded || this.airTime < COYOTE; }

    /** Push the capsule out of solid terrain (the world was built or fell onto it). Uses the voxel SDF, so it works
     *  even before the new collider mesh arrives. */
    unstick() {
        const w = this.ctx.world, c = this.center, h = this.halfHeight, n = this._n;
        for(let it = 0; it < 4; it++) {
            let worst = 0, wy = 0;
            for(let k = -1; k <= 1; k++) {
                const oy = k * h, d = w.sample(c.x, c.y + oy, c.z);
                if(d < worst) { worst = d; wy = oy; }
            }
            if(worst >= -0.02) return it > 0;
            if(worst < -0.9) { c.y += 0.5; continue; }     // buried deep (saturated distances): climb out
            w.normal(c.x, c.y + wy, c.z, n);
            const push = this.radius * 0.5 - worst;
            c.x += n[0] * push; c.y += n[1] * push; c.z += n[2] * push;
        }
        return true;
    }

    /** Advance one frame. wishX/wishZ: desired horizontal velocity. */
    step(dt, wishX, wishZ, { jump = false, jumpSpeed = 20, accel = 90, airAccel = 28, friction = 10 } = {}) {
        const v = this.vel;
        this.justLanded = false;
        if(this.knocked > 0) this.knocked -= dt;
        // horizontal: approach the wish velocity; extra speed (knockback) bleeds off with friction on the ground
        const a = this.grounded && !(this.knocked > 0) ? accel : airAccel;
        let dx = wishX - v.x, dz = wishZ - v.z;
        const dl = Math.hypot(dx, dz), maxD = a * dt;
        const speed = Math.hypot(v.x, v.z), wishSpeed = Math.hypot(wishX, wishZ);
        if(!this.grounded && speed > wishSpeed && wishSpeed > 0.1) {
            // airborne faster than walking (rocket jump): steer without killing momentum
            const k = Math.min(1, maxD / Math.max(dl, 1e-6)) * 0.35;
            v.x += dx * k; v.z += dz * k;
        } else if(!this.grounded && wishSpeed < 0.1) {
            const k = Math.max(0, 1 - 0.6 * dt); v.x *= k; v.z *= k;
        } else if(dl > maxD) { v.x += dx / dl * maxD; v.z += dz / dl * maxD; }
        else { v.x = wishX; v.z = wishZ; }
        if(this.grounded && speed > wishSpeed + 1 && !(this.knocked > 0)) { const k = Math.max(0, 1 - friction * dt); v.x = wishX + (v.x - wishX) * k; v.z = wishZ + (v.z - wishZ) * k; }

        if(jump && this.canJump()) { v.y = jumpSpeed; this.grounded = false; this.airTime = COYOTE; this.jumped = true; }
        v.y -= this.gravity * dt;
        if(v.y < -60) v.y = -60;

        this.unstick();
        // the mover skips our own capsule and whatever we hold or carry
        const ign = this.physics.ignoreBodies(this.ignore, this._ign);
        if(!ign.includes(this.body)) ign.push(this.body);
        // on the ground: no gravity into the slope (the mover would slide down it), snapping keeps us on the ground
        const walk = this.grounded && v.y <= 0.5;
        const desired = this._desired; desired.x = v.x * dt; desired.y = walk ? 0 : v.y * dt; desired.z = v.z * dt;
        let m = this._move(this.center, desired, walk, walk ? SNAP : 0, dt, this._moved);
        let ground = m.grounded;
        // Blocked on the ground: retry the step from higher up and settle back down (ledges taller than the capsule's
        // round bottom climbs by itself, and seams between terrain meshes)
        const want = Math.hypot(desired.x, desired.z);
        // (a failed retry is not repeated from the same spot for a moment: pushing against a wall would otherwise cost
        // four mover queries a frame)
        const sf = this._stepFail;
        const retry = !sf || sf.t <= 0 || Math.abs(sf.x - this.center.x) + Math.abs(sf.z - this.center.z) > 0.05 || (sf.dx * desired.x + sf.dz * desired.z) <= 0;
        if(sf) sf.t -= dt;
        if(retry && (this.grounded || ground) && want > 1e-4 && Math.hypot(m.x, m.z) < want * 0.6) {
            const r = this._stepUp(desired);
            if(r && Math.hypot(r.x, r.z) > Math.hypot(m.x, m.z) + 1e-4) { m = r; ground = true; this._stepFail = null; }
            else this._stepFail = { t: 0.1, x: this.center.x, z: this.center.z, dx: desired.x, dz: desired.z };
        }
        const wasGrounded = this.grounded;
        this.grounded = ground && v.y <= 0.5;
        // pushing into a wall on the ground (little progress the way we want to go): hop onto it if it has a top within reach (out of craters and pits)
        if(this.mantleCool > 0) this.mantleCool -= dt;
        if(this.grounded && want > 1e-4 && (m.x * desired.x + m.z * desired.z) < want * want * 0.3) this.blockedT = (this.blockedT || 0) + dt; else this.blockedT = 0;
        if(this.blockedT > MANTLE_T && !(this.mantleCool > 0)) {
            this.blockedT = 0; this.mantleCool = 0.5;
            const h = this._ledge(desired);
            if(h > 0) { v.y = Math.sqrt(2 * this.gravity * (h + 0.3)); this.grounded = false; this.airTime = COYOTE; this.mantled = true; }
        }
        // blocked: drop the velocity the obstacle ate. Not while climbing: a slope turns part of the step into height,
        // and taking that off the speed every frame stalled the player halfway up any ramp
        const climbing = m.y > Math.max(desired.y, 0) + 1e-4;
        if(dt > 0 && !climbing) {
            const want = Math.hypot(desired.x, desired.z), got = Math.hypot(m.x, m.z);
            if(got < want * 0.2) { if(Math.abs(m.x) < Math.abs(desired.x) - 1e-4) v.x = m.x / dt; if(Math.abs(m.z) < Math.abs(desired.z) - 1e-4) v.z = m.z / dt; }
            if(desired.y > 0 && m.y < desired.y * 0.5) v.y = Math.min(v.y, 0);           // head bump
        }
        if(this.grounded) {
            if(!wasGrounded) { this.justLanded = true; this.landSpeed = -v.y; }
            v.y = Math.max(v.y, -2);
            this.airTime = 0;
        } else this.airTime += dt;
        this.center.x += m.x; this.center.y += m.y; this.center.z += m.z;
        this.body.setNextKinematicTranslation(this.center);
    }

    /** Move by `desired` horizontally after lifting up to STEP_UP, then drop back onto the ground; when the way straight
     *  ahead has no ground to stand on, 45° to either side is tried (finding the way up a faceted crater wall). Returns
     *  the total movement (into this._stepMoved) if it lands on walkable ground, else null. */
    _stepUp(desired) {
        const c = this.center;
        const d = this._d2 ??= { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }, tmp = this._tmpV ??= {};
        // up
        d.x = 0; d.y = STEP_UP; d.z = 0;
        const up = this._move(c, d, false, 0, 0, tmp).y;
        if(up < 0.1) return null;
        // across: at least STEP_PROBE (pushing against a step kills the speed, and a step only counts if there is room
        // to stand on top of it)
        const want = Math.hypot(desired.x, desired.z), k = Math.max(1, STEP_PROBE / Math.max(want, 1e-6));
        const fx = desired.x * k, fz = desired.z * k, C = Math.SQRT1_2;
        return this._stepAcross(up, fx, fz, desired)
            || this._stepAcross(up, (fx - fz) * C, (fz + fx) * C, desired)
            || this._stepAcross(up, (fx + fz) * C, (fz - fx) * C, desired);
    }
    /** Height of the top of the ledge ahead (within MANTLE) the capsule could stand on, or 0. */
    _ledge(desired) {
        const c = this.center, d = this._d2 ??= { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }, tmp = this._tmpV ??= {}, p = this._p2 ??= { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 };
        d.x = 0; d.y = MANTLE; d.z = 0;
        const up = this._move(c, d, false, 0, 0, tmp).y;
        if(up < STEP_UP) return 0;
        const want = Math.hypot(desired.x, desired.z), k = (this.radius + STEP_PROBE) / Math.max(want, 1e-6);
        p.x = c.x; p.y = c.y + up; p.z = c.z;
        d.x = desired.x * k; d.y = 0; d.z = desired.z * k;
        const a = this._move(p, d, true, 0, 0, tmp);
        if(Math.hypot(a.x, a.z) < this.radius) return 0;
        p.x += a.x; p.y += a.y; p.z += a.z;
        d.x = 0; d.y = -up; d.z = 0;
        const dn = this._move(p, d, false, 0, 0, tmp, true);
        const h = up + a.y + dn.y;
        return dn.grounded && h > 0.3 ? h : 0;
    }
    _stepAcross(up, dx, dz, desired) {
        const c = this.center, d = this._d2, tmp = this._tmpV, out = this._stepMoved ??= { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, grounded: false };
        const p = this._p2 ??= { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 };
        p.x = c.x; p.y = c.y + up; p.z = c.z;
        d.x = dx; d.y = 0; d.z = dz;
        const a = this._move(p, d, true, 0, 0, tmp);
        const ax = a.x, ay = a.y, az = a.z;
        if(Math.hypot(ax, az) < 1e-3) return null;
        p.x += ax; p.y += ay; p.z += az;
        // down: as far as we went up plus the ordinary descent, stopping on whatever is under us (sliding here would
        // pull the capsule back off a ledge's edge)
        d.x = 0; d.y = -(up + Math.max(0, -desired.y) + 0.05); d.z = 0;
        const dn = this._move(p, d, false, 0, 0, tmp, true);
        if(!dn.grounded) return null;
        out.x = ax; out.y = up + ay + dn.y; out.z = az; out.grounded = true;
        return out;
    }

    dispose() {
        this.physics.b3.removeBody(this.body);
    }
}
```

### src/game/Crates.js
```
/**
 * Supply crates: physics boxes scattered over the level and buried in sealed caches (WorldParty's gen-fortwar game
 * config). Walk into one to pick it up (it rides over your head), walk into your vault to bank it. A vault's crates
 * can be stolen by the other team. A carrier who dies drops the crate. Score = crates in your vault.
 *
 * Online (src/game/NetGame.js) the host decides: it sees everyone walk into crates and vaults, picks up, drops,
 * deposits and returns home, and sends each change as a 'crate' message (net.bcast); other clients apply them
 * (applyNet) and carry crates over their carriers' heads. The free crates' bodies belong to the host, which streams
 * their motion.
 */
import * as THREE from 'three/webgpu';
import { GAME, NUDGE } from '../level/fortwar.js';
import { BodyType } from '../physics/Physics.js';

const nudge = p => p.map(v => v + NUDGE);
// gen-fortwar's scatter: bridge stump, gorge bed (two), arch top, the tube, then per side a crag, the sewer jog,
// the cave entrance and the front tower top (beside the catwalk)
const SCATTER = [[0, 1.1, 6.5], [3, -5.5, 9.5], [-3, -5.5, -9.5], [0, 7.5, 32], [0, 0.5, -27.5]];
for(const s of [-1, 1]) SCATTER.push([s * 38, 8.0, 19.5], [s * 30, -5.5, 5], [s * 43, 0.5, -22], [s * 36.5, 10.0, 12.5]);
// materials showcase (src/level/showcase.js): on each watchtower's deck, inside each glass observatory
for(const s of [-1, 1]) SCATTER.push([s * 22, 6.9, -13], [s * 24, 6.5, 30]);
const CACHES = [[-46, -9.5, 6.5], [46, -9.5, 6.5], [-14, -10.5, -11.5], [14, -10.5, -11.5], [-0.8, -14.5, 0.8], [0.8, -14.5, -0.8]];
const SLOTS = [[57.5, -2], [55, -2], [52.5, -2], [57.5, 2], [55, 2], [52.5, 2], [57.5, 0], [55, 0], [52.5, 0], [51, -3.5], [51, 3.5], [58.5, 3.5]];
export const HALF = 0.45;
const _pos = { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }, _rot = { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, w: 1 };
export const PICKUP_RADIUS = 1.7;

export function inBox(b, p) {
    return p.x >= b.min[0] && p.x <= b.max[0] && p.y >= b.min[1] - 0.5 && p.y <= b.max[1] && p.z >= b.min[2] && p.z <= b.max[2];
}

export class Crates {
    constructor(game) {
        this.game = game;
        this.ctx = game.ctx;
        this.list = [];
        this.vaults = { red: GAME.teams.red.vault, blue: GAME.teams.blue.vault };
        this.slots = { red: SLOTS.map(([x, z]) => nudge([-x, -5.5, z])), blue: SLOTS.map(([x, z]) => nudge([x, -5.5, z])) };
        const wood = new THREE.MeshStandardNodeMaterial({ color: 0xc8913f, roughness: 0.8 });
        const strap = new THREE.MeshStandardNodeMaterial({ color: 0x4a3018, roughness: 0.6, metalness: 0.2 });
        const gold = new THREE.MeshStandardNodeMaterial({ color: 0xffd040, emissive: 0xffa000, emissiveIntensity: 0.6, roughness: 0.3, metalness: 0.6 });
        this.mats = { wood, strap, gold };
        this.beamMat = new THREE.MeshBasicNodeMaterial({ color: 0xffe080, transparent: true, opacity: 0.22, depthWrite: false, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending });
        this.beamGeo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.12, 0.3, 30, 8, 1, true); this.beamGeo.translate(0, 15, 0);
        for(const p of SCATTER) this.spawn(nudge(p), false);
        for(const p of CACHES) this.spawn(nudge(p), true);
    }
    makeMesh() {
        const g = new THREE.Group();
        const s = HALF * 2;
        const body = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(s, s, s), this.mats.wood); g.add(body);
        for(const z of [-0.28, 0.28]) { const b = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(s + 0.03, s + 0.03, 0.1), this.mats.strap); b.position.z = z; g.add(b); }
        const gem = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(0.16), this.mats.gold); gem.position.y = HALF + 0.02; gem.scale.y = 0.6; g.add(gem);
        g.traverse(o => { if(o.isMesh) { o.castShadow = true; o.receiveShadow = true; } });
        return g;
    }
    spawn(pos, buried) {
        const mesh = this.makeMesh();
        const prop = this.ctx.physics.addBody({ shape: 'box', size: [HALF, HALF, HALF], position: pos, mesh, density: 400, friction: 0.8, restitution: 0.05, userData: { crate: true, mat: 10 } });
        const beam = new THREE.Mesh(this.beamGeo, this.beamMat); beam.renderOrder = 5;
        this.ctx.scene.add(beam);
        const c = { prop, mesh, home: pos, buried, carrier: null, vault: null, idle: 0, beam, id: this.list.length };
        prop.userData.netId = `crate:${c.id}`; // its body belongs to the host (no owner): streamed online
        this.list.push(c);
        return c;
    }
    pos(c) { const t = c.prop.body.translation(); return t; }
    /** Does this client decide about crates? Always offline; online only the host. */
    get authority() { const ng = this.game.netGame; return !ng || ng.isHost; }
    _send(c, ev) {
        const ng = this.game.netGame;
        if(!ng || !this.authority) return;
        const t = this.pos(c), v = c.prop.body.linvel();
        ng.send({ t: 'crate', id: c.id, ev, c: c.carrier?.netId ?? null, vault: c.vault, p: [t.x, t.y, t.z], v: [v.x, v.y, v.z] });
    }
    /** The host's crate change, on another client. */
    applyNet(m) {
        const c = this.list[m.id]; if(!c) return;
        const actor = m.c ? this.game.netGame.actor(m.c) : null;
        if(m.ev === 'pickup' && actor) {
            if(c.carrier && c.carrier !== actor) { c.carrier.carrying = null; c.carrier = null; }
            if(!c.carrier) { if(actor.carrying && actor.carrying !== c) actor.carrying = null; this.pickUp(c, actor); }
            return;
        }
        if(c.carrier) this.drop(c, m.ev === 'deposit', m.p, m.v);
        else this._place(c, m.p, m.v);
        c.vault = m.vault;
        if(m.ev === 'home') this._place(c, m.p, [0, 0, 0]);
    }
    _place(c, p, v) {
        const b = c.prop.body;
        b.setTranslation({ x: p[0], y: p[1], z: p[2] }, true);
        b.setRotation({ x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, w: 1 }, true);
        b.setLinvel({ x: v[0], y: v[1], z: v[2] }, true);
    }
    /** Which team's vault holds it (null if none). */
    vaultOf(t) { for(const team of ['red', 'blue']) if(inBox(this.vaults[team], t)) return team; return null; }
    count(team) { let n = 0; for(const c of this.list) if(!c.carrier && c.vault === team) n++; return n; }

    pickUp(c, actor) {
        c.carrier = actor; actor.carrying = c;
        c.prop.body.setBodyType(BodyType.Kinematic, true);
        c.prop.collider.setEnabled(false);
        c.vaultBefore = c.vault; c.vault = null;
        this.game.emit('pickup', { crate: c, actor, from: c.vaultBefore });
        this._send(c, 'pickup');
    }
    /** Put the crate down: in the carrier's vault (deposit), or where the carrier is. Online, other clients get the
     *  host's position and velocity (at, vel). */
    drop(c, deposit = false, at = null, vel = null) {
        const a = c.carrier; if(!a) return;
        c.carrier = null; a.carrying = null;
        const b = c.prop.body;
        if(at) { /* the host's spot */ }
        else if(deposit) {
            // the first free slot of the vault
            const used = this.list.filter(o => o !== c && !o.carrier && o.vault === a.team).map(o => this.pos(o));
            at = this.slots[a.team].find(s => used.every(u => Math.hypot(u.x - s[0], u.z - s[2]) > 1.0)) || this.slots[a.team][0];
            at = [at[0], at[1] + 0.3, at[2]];
        } else { const t = a.body.center; at = [t.x, t.y + 0.2, t.z]; }
        b.setBodyType(BodyType.Dynamic, true);
        b.setTranslation({ x: at[0], y: at[1], z: at[2] }, true);
        b.setRotation({ x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, w: 1 }, true);
        const v = deposit ? { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 } : a.body.vel;
        if(vel) b.setLinvel({ x: vel[0], y: vel[1], z: vel[2] }, true);
        else b.setLinvel({ x: v.x * 0.6, y: Math.max(0, v.y * 0.6) + 2, z: v.z * 0.6 }, true);
        c.prop.collider.setEnabled(true);
        a.ignoreTemp(c.prop.collider.handle, 1.0);
        c.idle = 0;
        if(deposit) { c.vault = a.team; this.game.emit('deposit', { crate: c, actor: a }); }
        this._send(c, deposit ? 'deposit' : 'drop');
    }
    returnHome(c) {
        const b = c.prop.body;
        if(c.carrier) this.drop(c);
        b.setTranslation({ x: c.home[0], y: c.home[1] + 0.5, z: c.home[2] }, true);
        b.setLinvel({ x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }, true);
        b.setAngvel({ x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }, true);
        this._send(c, 'home');
    }

    update(dt) {
        const t0 = performance.now() / 1000;
        for(const c of this.list) {
            const b = c.prop.body;
            if(c.carrier) {
                const a = c.carrier;
                if(!a.alive && this.authority) { this.drop(c); continue; }
                const p = a.viewCenter, yaw = a.yaw ?? 0;
                _pos.x = p.x; _pos.y = p.y + a.body.halfHeight + a.body.radius + HALF + 0.15 + Math.sin(t0 * 6) * 0.04; _pos.z = p.z;
                b.setNextKinematicTranslation(_pos);
                _rot.y = Math.sin(yaw / 2); _rot.w = Math.cos(yaw / 2);
                b.setNextKinematicRotation(_rot);
                // banked in our vault?
                if(this.authority && inBox(this.vaults[a.team], p)) this.drop(c, true);
                c.beam.visible = false;
                continue;
            }
            const t = b.translation();
            // the beacon: free crates show a light beam (not the buried ones: those are for diggers)
            if(!this.authority) { c.beam.visible = !c.vault && !c.buried; c.beam.position.set(t.x, t.y + HALF, t.z); continue; }
            if(t.y < -30) { this.returnHome(c); continue; }
            c.vault = this.vaultOf(t);
            // the beacon: free crates show a light beam (not the buried ones: those are for diggers)
            c.beam.visible = !c.vault && !c.buried;
            c.beam.position.set(t.x, t.y + HALF, t.z);
            if(c.buried && Math.hypot(t.x - c.home[0], t.y - c.home[1], t.z - c.home[2]) > 1.5) c.buried = false; // dug out and moved
            // pickups: a living actor walking into it, unless it already sits in their own vault
            for(const a of this.game.actors) {
                if(!a.alive || a.carrying || a.held) continue;
                if(c.vault === a.team) continue;
                if(a.dropCooldown > 0) continue;
                const p = a.body.center;
                if(Math.abs(p.x - t.x) < PICKUP_RADIUS && Math.abs(p.z - t.z) < PICKUP_RADIUS && Math.abs(p.y - t.y) < 1.9 && Math.hypot(p.x - t.x, p.z - t.z) < PICKUP_RADIUS) { this.pickUp(c, a); break; }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### src/game/FortWar.js
```
/**
 * Fort War, single player. You are Red; Blue is a few bots. Supply crates lie around the level and buried in caches;
 * carry them into your vault (under your fort) to score, steal the enemy's. Most crates banked when the clock runs
 * out wins. This is the game system main.js adds: it owns the actors, the combat mechanics, the crates, the HUD, the
 * effects and the sounds, and exposes itself as ctx.game for tests.
 *
 * Game events (game.on / game.emit; also dispatched on ctx.events): explosion, rocket, punch, dig, build, rip, throw,
 * slam, bounce, fuse, fired, tool, hint, damage { actor, amount, by, cause }, death { actor, by, cause },
 * respawn { actor }, pickup { crate, actor, from }, deposit { crate, actor }, step, jump, land, matchOver { result }.
 *
 * Online (?room=name, ctx.net not single): src/game/NetGame.js joins the match: teams are balanced by the host, the
 * other players appear as RemoteActors, bots run on the host only (filling both teams), and the host keeps health,
 * crates, the score and the clock.
 *
 * URL: ?bots=N (0..5, default 3), ?minutes=M (default 10), ?music=0, ?touch=1 (force touch controls), ?name=Nick.
 */
import { GAME } from '../level/fortwar.js';
import { Input } from './Input.js';
import { Player } from './Player.js';
import { Bot } from './Bot.js';
import { Crates } from './Crates.js';
import { Combat } from './weapons/Combat.js';
import { Hud } from '../ui/Hud.js';
import { Fx } from '../fx/Fx.js';
import { Sfx } from '../audio/Sfx.js';
import { Music } from '../audio/Music.js';
import { MAX_HEALTH } from './Actor.js';
import { NetGame } from './NetGame.js';
import { TEAM_COLOR } from './avatar.js';

const BOT_NAMES = { blue: ['Blue Barry', 'Blue Bonnie', 'Blue Boris', 'Blue Bea', 'Blue Bruno'], red: ['Red Rex', 'Red Ruby', 'Red Rory', 'Red Rosa', 'Red Rudy'] };

export class FortWar {
    constructor(ctx) {
        this.ctx = ctx;
        ctx.game = this;
        this.listeners = new Map();
        this.actors = [];
        this.actorByCollider = new Map();
        const p = ctx.params;
        this.matchTime = Math.max(0.5, +(p.get('minutes') || 10)) * 60;
        this.timeLeft = this.matchTime;
        this.over = false;
        this.result = null;
        this.started = false;
        this.sfx = new Sfx(this);
        this.music = new Music(this, this.sfx);
        this.fx = new Fx(this);
        this.combat = new Combat(this);
        this.crates = new Crates(this);
        this.input = new Input(ctx);
        const red = GAME.teams.red, blue = GAME.teams.blue;
        this.player = new Player(this, this.input, { team: 'red', name: 'You', spawn: red.spawns[1] });
        this.player.netId = `p:${ctx.net?.id ?? 'local'}`;
        this.actors.push(this.player);
        this.bots = [];
        const online = ctx.net && !ctx.net.single;
        // bots: ?bots=N wins; else the saved choice (HUD toggle); else 3
        const q = p.get('bots');
        let saved = null; try { saved = localStorage.getItem('bananza.bots'); } catch(e) {}
        this.botCount = q != null ? Math.max(0, Math.min(5, +q || 0)) : 3;
        this.botsOn = q != null ? this.botCount > 0 : saved !== '0';
        if(this.botCount === 0) this.botCount = 3; // turning them on later brings the usual three
        // online the host runs the bots (NetGame fills both teams with them)
        if(online) this.netGame = new NetGame(this);
        else if(this.botsOn) this.spawnBots();
        this.hud = new Hud(this);
        this.netGame?.start();
        this.score = { red: 0, blue: 0 };
        this.on('death', ({ actor, by }) => { if(by && by !== actor) by.kills++; });
        this.emit('hint', { text: this.input.touch ? 'Left thumb: move · drag right: look · grab the crates, bank them in your vault' : 'Click to play · WASD + mouse · steal the crates and bank them in your vault (under your fort)', long: true });
    }

    on(name, fn) { if(!this.listeners.has(name)) this.listeners.set(name, []); this.listeners.get(name).push(fn); }
    emit(name, detail = {}) {
        this.netGame?.mirror(name, detail);
        const fns = this.listeners.get(name);
        if(fns) for(const fn of fns) { try { fn(detail); } catch(e) { console.error(`game event ${name}`, e); } }
        this.ctx.events.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(name, { detail }));
    }

    damage(actor, amount, by, impulse, cause) {
        if(this.netGame) return this.netGame.damage(actor, amount, by, impulse, cause);
        if(!actor.alive || this.over) return;
        if(impulse) actor.body.knock(impulse);
        if(amount <= 0) return;
        actor.health -= amount;
        actor.sinceHit = 0;
        this.emit('damage', { actor, amount, by, cause });
        if(actor.health <= 0) {
            actor.health = 0;
            if(actor.held && actor.isPlayer) actor.arsenal.release(false);
            else if(actor.held) actor.held = null;
            if(actor.carrying) this.crates.drop(actor.carrying);
            actor.die();
            this.emit('death', { actor, by, cause });
        }
    }

    update(dt) {
        if(!this.started) { this.started = true; this.emit('start', {}); }
        this.elapsed = (this.elapsed || 0) + dt;
        if(!this.over) {
            const before = this.timeLeft;
            this.timeLeft = Math.max(0, this.timeLeft - dt);
            if(before > 60 && this.timeLeft <= 60) this.emit('hint', { text: 'One minute left!' });
            if(this.timeLeft < 10 && Math.ceil(before) !== Math.ceil(this.timeLeft)) this.emit('tick', {});
            if(this.timeLeft <= 0 && (!this.netGame || this.netGame.isHost)) this.endMatch(); // online the host ends it
        }
        this.player.update(dt);
        for(const b of this.bots) b.update(dt);
        if(this.netGame) this.netGame.update(dt); // remote actors, streams, respawns (host)
        else for(const a of this.actors) if(!a.alive && !this.over) { a.respawnIn -= dt; if(a.respawnIn <= 0) { a.respawn(); this.emit('respawn', { actor: a }); } }
        this.combat.update(dt);
        this.crates.update(dt);
        this.score.red = this.crates.count('red');
        this.score.blue = this.crates.count('blue');
        this.fx.update(dt);
        this.sfx.listen(this.ctx.camera);
        this.music.update(dt);
        this.hud.update(dt);
    }

    endMatch(score = null) {
        if(this.over) return;
        this.over = true;
        if(score) Object.assign(this.score, score);
        const mine = this.score[this.player.team], theirs = this.score[this.player.team === 'red' ? 'blue' : 'red'];
        this.result = mine > theirs ? 'win' : mine < theirs ? 'loss' : 'draw';
        this.netGame?.over();
        if(document.pointerLockElement) document.exitPointerLock();
        this.emit('matchOver', { result: this.result, score: { ...this.score } });
    }
    restart() { if(this.netGame) this.netGame.restart(); else location.reload(); }

    spawnBots() {
        const n = this.botCount;
        for(let i = 0; i < n; i++) this.addBot('blue', i, n);
    }
    /** Bot i of n on `team` (its name, spawn point, skill and role follow from i). */
    addBot(team, i, n, at = null) {
        const T = GAME.teams[team];
        const b = new Bot(this, { team, name: BOT_NAMES[team][i % 5], spawn: T.spawns[i % T.spawns.length], skill: 0.35 + 0.2 * (i % 3), role: i === 0 && n >= 3 ? 'defender' : 'raider', phase: i / n, color: TEAM_COLOR[team] });
        b.netId = `b:${team}${i}`;
        b.yaw = team === 'blue' ? Math.PI / 2 : -Math.PI / 2; // face the enemy fort
        if(at) { b.body.teleport(at); b.prev.copy(b.body.center); b.view.copy(b.body.center); }
        this.bots.push(b); this.actors.push(b);
        return b;
    }
    removeBot(b) {
        if(b.carrying && this.crates.authority) this.crates.drop(b.carrying);
        if(b.held) b.held = null;
        this.actorByCollider.delete(b.body.collider.handle);
        b.body.dispose();
        b.dispose();
        this.actors = this.actors.filter(a => a !== b);
        this.bots = this.bots.filter(a => a !== b);
    }
    removeBots() { for(const b of [...this.bots]) this.removeBot(b); }
    /** HUD toggle: bots on or off, live; the choice is remembered. */
    setBots(on) {
        on = !!on;
        if(on === this.botsOn) return;
        if(this.netGame && !this.netGame.isHost) { this.emit('hint', { text: 'The host decides about bots' }); return; }
        this.botsOn = on;
        try { localStorage.setItem('bananza.bots', on ? '1' : '0'); } catch(e) {}
        if(this.netGame) this.netGame.fillBots();
        else if(on) this.spawnBots(); else this.removeBots();
        this.emit('hint', { text: on ? 'Blue bots are in the game' : 'Bots off: the fort is yours to wreck' });
    }
    get maxHealth() { return MAX_HEALTH; }
}
```

### src/game/Input.js
```
/**
 * Input: keyboard + mouse (pointer lock) on desktop, twin-zone touch on mobile (left half: a floating move stick,
 * right half: drag to look, plus on-screen buttons the HUD creates and binds through `bindButton`).
 *
 * State read by the player each frame:
 *   move {x (right), y (forward)} in -1..1, consumeLook() -> [dx, dy] in radians, down(name), pressed(name) (edge,
 *   cleared by endFrame()), released(name).
 * Button names: fire, grab, punch, jump, sprint. Tool selection is a callback: onTool(index | 'next' | 'prev').
 */
export class Input {
    constructor(ctx) {
        this.ctx = ctx;
        this.canvas = ctx.renderer.domElement;
        const p = ctx.params;
        this.touch = p.get('touch') === '1' || (p.get('touch') !== '0' && (matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches || 'ontouchstart' in window));
        this.move = { x: 0, y: 0 };
        this.look = [0, 0];
        this.held = new Set();
        this.edges = new Set();
        this.ups = new Set();
        this.keys = new Set();
        this.sensitivity = 0.0022;
        this.touchSensitivity = 0.0055;
        this.toolFns = [];
        this.enabled = true;
        this.lockFns = [];
        if(this.touch) this.initTouch(); else this.initDesktop();
        const release = () => { this.keys.clear(); for(const b of [...this.held]) this.setButton(b, false); this.stick = null; this.lookId = null; this.move.x = this.move.y = 0; };
        addEventListener('blur', release);
        document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { if(document.hidden) release(); });
    }
    onTool(fn) { this.toolFns.push(fn); }
    onLockChange(fn) { this.lockFns.push(fn); }
    tool(t) { for(const fn of this.toolFns) fn(t); }
    setButton(name, on) {
        if(on && !this.held.has(name)) { this.held.add(name); this.edges.add(name); }
        else if(!on && this.held.has(name)) { this.held.delete(name); this.ups.add(name); }
    }
    down(n) { return this.held.has(n); }
    pressed(n) { return this.edges.has(n); }
    released(n) { return this.ups.has(n); }
    consumeLook() { const l = [this.look[0], this.look[1]]; this.look[0] = this.look[1] = 0; return l; }
    endFrame() { this.edges.clear(); this.ups.clear(); }
    get locked() { return this.touch || document.pointerLockElement === this.canvas; }

    lock() {
        if(this.touch) return;
        try {
            const r = this.canvas.requestPointerLock({ unadjustedMovement: true });
            if(r && r.catch) r.catch(() => { try { this.canvas.requestPointerLock(); } catch(e) {} });
        } catch(e) { try { this.canvas.requestPointerLock(); } catch(e2) {} }
    }

    initDesktop() {
        const KEYMAP = { Space: 'jump', ShiftLeft: 'sprint', ShiftRight: 'sprint', KeyF: 'punch', KeyQ: 'punch', KeyE: 'grab' };
        addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
            if(e.repeat) return;
            this.keys.add(e.code);
            if(KEYMAP[e.code]) this.setButton(KEYMAP[e.code], true);
            if(/^Digit[1-9]$/.test(e.code)) this.tool(+e.code.slice(5) - 1);
            if(e.code === 'Space' || e.code === 'Tab') e.preventDefault();
        });
        addEventListener('keyup', (e) => {
            this.keys.delete(e.code);
            if(KEYMAP[e.code]) this.setButton(KEYMAP[e.code], false);
        });
        this.canvas.addEventListener('click', () => { if(!this.locked && this.enabled) this.lock(); });
        document.addEventListener('pointerlockchange', () => { for(const fn of this.lockFns) fn(this.locked); });
        let fresh = true;
        addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            if(!this.locked) { fresh = true; return; }
            const size = Math.max(Math.abs(e.movementX), Math.abs(e.movementY));
            if(size > 600 || (fresh && size > 50)) { fresh = false; return; }   // bogus jumps after locking
            fresh = false;
            this.look[0] += e.movementX * this.sensitivity; this.look[1] += e.movementY * this.sensitivity;
        });
        addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
            if(!this.locked) return;
            if(e.button === 0) this.setButton('fire', true);
            else if(e.button === 2) this.setButton('grab', true);
            else if(e.button === 1) { this.setButton('punch', true); e.preventDefault(); }
        });
        addEventListener('mouseup', (e) => {
            if(e.button === 0) this.setButton('fire', false);
            else if(e.button === 2) this.setButton('grab', false);
            else if(e.button === 1) this.setButton('punch', false);
        });
        addEventListener('wheel', (e) => { if(this.locked) this.tool(e.deltaY > 0 ? 'next' : 'prev'); }, { passive: true });
        addEventListener('contextmenu', (e) => e.preventDefault());
    }

    updateKeys() {
        if(this.touch) return;
        const k = this.keys;
        this.move.x = (k.has('KeyD') || k.has('ArrowRight') ? 1 : 0) - (k.has('KeyA') || k.has('ArrowLeft') ? 1 : 0);
        this.move.y = (k.has('KeyW') || k.has('ArrowUp') ? 1 : 0) - (k.has('KeyS') || k.has('ArrowDown') ? 1 : 0);
        const l = Math.hypot(this.move.x, this.move.y); if(l > 1) { this.move.x /= l; this.move.y /= l; }
    }

    initTouch() {
        // the move stick: a ring and a knob that appear where the left thumb lands
        const ring = document.createElement('div'), knob = document.createElement('div');
        ring.className = 'stick-ring'; knob.className = 'stick-knob';
        document.body.append(ring, knob);
        this.stickEls = { ring, knob };
        const R = 60;
        this.stick = null; this.lookId = null; this.lookLast = null;
        const zone = this.canvas;
        const showStick = (on) => { ring.style.display = knob.style.display = on ? 'block' : 'none'; };
        showStick(false);
        zone.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
            if(e.clientX < innerWidth * 0.42 && !this.stick) {
                this.stick = { id: e.pointerId, x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
                ring.style.left = (e.clientX - R) + 'px'; ring.style.top = (e.clientY - R) + 'px';
                knob.style.left = (e.clientX - 24) + 'px'; knob.style.top = (e.clientY - 24) + 'px';
                showStick(true);
            } else if(this.lookId === null) { this.lookId = e.pointerId; this.lookLast = [e.clientX, e.clientY]; }
            try { zone.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch(err) {}
        });
        zone.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
            if(this.stick && e.pointerId === this.stick.id) {
                let dx = e.clientX - this.stick.x, dy = e.clientY - this.stick.y;
                const l = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
                if(l > R) { // the stick follows a thumb that drifts past the ring
                    this.stick.x += dx / l * (l - R); this.stick.y += dy / l * (l - R);
                    ring.style.left = (this.stick.x - R) + 'px'; ring.style.top = (this.stick.y - R) + 'px';
                    dx = e.clientX - this.stick.x; dy = e.clientY - this.stick.y;
                }
                this.move.x = dx / R; this.move.y = -dy / R;
                this.held.has('sprint') || (Math.hypot(dx, dy) > R * 0.97 ? this.setButton('sprint', true) : null);
                if(Math.hypot(dx, dy) < R * 0.9) this.setButton('sprint', false);
                knob.style.left = (this.stick.x + dx - 24) + 'px'; knob.style.top = (this.stick.y + dy - 24) + 'px';
            } else if(e.pointerId === this.lookId) {
                this.look[0] += (e.clientX - this.lookLast[0]) * this.touchSensitivity;
                this.look[1] += (e.clientY - this.lookLast[1]) * this.touchSensitivity;
                this.lookLast = [e.clientX, e.clientY];
            }
        });
        const end = (e) => {
            if(this.stick && e.pointerId === this.stick.id) { this.stick = null; this.move.x = this.move.y = 0; this.setButton('sprint', false); showStick(false); }
            if(e.pointerId === this.lookId) this.lookId = null;
        };
        zone.addEventListener('pointerup', end);
        zone.addEventListener('pointercancel', end);
    }

    /** Wire an on-screen element as a held button (touch). */
    bindButton(el, name) {
        const on = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); this.setButton(name, true); el.classList.add('held'); };
        const off = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); this.setButton(name, false); el.classList.remove('held'); };
        el.addEventListener('pointerdown', on);
        el.addEventListener('pointerup', off);
        el.addEventListener('pointercancel', off);
        el.addEventListener('pointerleave', off);
    }
}
```

### src/game/NetGame.js
```
/**
 * Online Fort War (?room=name): the game layer on top of the edit log (src/net). Terrain already stays in step
 * through the sequenced edits; this replicates everything else with plain messages (net.bcast / net.direct, not
 * sequenced):
 *
 *   actors   every client simulates its own player (the host also its bots) and streams it ('st', STATE_HZ):
 *            position, velocity, yaw, pitch, flags. Others draw it as a RemoteActor, INTERP_DELAY behind.
 *   roster   the host keeps who is in the match ('roster', and in 'game'): { id: netId, name, team, bot, client }.
 *            Joining clients say 'hello'; the host puts them on the team with fewer humans and fills both teams
 *            with bots (fillBots: each team gets max(3, the larger team's humans) actors while bots are on).
 *   actions  a local actor's rockets and bombs ('act'): others fly visual copies (Combat { visual }); bombs are
 *            bodies whose owner streams them and says 'gone' when they blow. Sounds and effects of local events
 *            (MIRROR) are re-emitted on the others ('ev').
 *   damage   whoever's weapon hit (its owner client) sends 'hit' { tg, by, a, i (knockback), c }; the target's
 *            owner applies the knockback, the host applies the damage and announces the result ('hp'; dead: the
 *            host also drops the carried crate). The host respawns the dead ('spawn'): an owner then moves its
 *            actor to its spawn point. Health regenerates on the host for everyone.
 *   match    the host runs the clock, crates (src/game/Crates.js 'crate') and the score; 'game' (GAME_HZ, and
 *            kept by the server for late joiners) carries all of it; 'over' ends the match everywhere.
 *   bodies   each body (debris, crates, bombs) has one owner client (VoxelBodies: what the terrain shed belongs to
 *            the host, a ripped lump to the ripper; whoever holds a body claims it ('own'), a thrown one goes back
 *            to the host when it rests). Owners stream their moving bodies ('bs', BODY_HZ: pose, velocity, held),
 *            sleeping ones now and then; other clients steer their copies toward it (blendBodies). A punch on
 *            someone else's body is sent to its owner ('push').
 *   host     when the host leaves, the next client becomes host: it takes over the bots where they were, the
 *            orphaned bodies, the crates and the clock (all clients reassign bodies of clients that left).
 *
 * Lockstep with box3d's deterministic physics would need every client to step the same inputs at the same tick
 * (and late joiners the world's full physics state), which variable frame rates, latency and late joins do not give
 * us; owners stream instead.
 */
import * as THREE from 'three/webgpu';
import { GAME } from '../level/fortwar.js';
import { RemoteActor, F_ALIVE, F_GROUNDED } from './RemoteActor.js';
import { MAX_HEALTH } from './Actor.js';
import { TEAM_COLOR } from './avatar.js';
import { BodyType } from '../physics/Physics.js';

const STATE_HZ = 25, BODY_HZ = 15, GAME_HZ = 1;
const MIRROR = new Set(['explosion', 'punch', 'dig', 'build', 'rip', 'throw', 'slam', 'land']);
const REFS = ['owner', 'actor', 'by'];
const MAX_HIT = 250;          // damage per hit message, at most
const BODY_MAX = 40;          // bodies per stream message
const REFRESH_MS = 3000;      // a resting body is re-sent this often (late joiners, lost messages)
const BODY_STALE = 1500;      // ms without news: a copy is left to its own physics
const ADJ = ['Swift', 'Brave', 'Rocky', 'Dusty', 'Lucky', 'Mighty', 'Sly', 'Jolly', 'Grumpy', 'Zippy'];
const NOUN = ['Mole', 'Badger', 'Golem', 'Miner', 'Ferret', 'Digger', 'Rook', 'Boulder', 'Gopher', 'Yeti'];

const _q = new THREE.Quaternion(), _q2 = new THREE.Quaternion(), _p = new THREE.Vector3();
const r2 = (v) => Math.round(v * 100) / 100;

export class NetGame {
    constructor(game) {
        this.game = game;
        this.ctx = game.ctx;
        const net = this.net = game.ctx.net;
        game.netGame = this;
        this.remotes = new Map();   // netId -> RemoteActor
        this.roster = new Map();    // netId -> { id, name, team, bot, client }
        this.left = new Map();      // client -> { team, at } (host: a client that left keeps its team for a while)
        this.clocks = new Map();    // client -> offset (ms) from its clock to ours
        this.idN = 0;
        this.stT = 0; this.bodyT = 0; this.gameT = 0;
        this.stats = { stOut: 0, stIn: 0, bodiesOut: 0, bodiesIn: 0, hits: 0, acts: 0, evs: 0 };
        this.name = this._name();
        const me = game.player;
        me.name = this.name;
        // my team: the one I had (a reload), else the one with fewer humans; the host confirms it
        const gs = net.gameState;
        const was = gs?.roster?.find(r => r.id === me.netId);
        this._setMyTeam(was?.team ?? this._balance(gs?.roster || [], me.netId), true);
        const on = (t, fn) => net.onMsg(t, (from, m) => fn.call(this, from, m));
        on('hello', this._onHello); on('roster', (f, m) => this._applyRoster(m.roster));
        on('st', this._onStates); on('ev', this._onEv); on('act', this._onAct);
        on('hit', this._onHit); on('hp', (f, m) => this._onHp(m)); on('spawn', this._onSpawn);
        on('game', this._onGame); on('over', (f, m) => game.endMatch(m.score));
        on('crate', (f, m) => { if(!this.isHost) game.crates.applyNet(m); });
        on('bs', this._onBodies); on('own', this._onOwn); on('push', this._onPush);
        on('gone', (f, m) => game.combat.removeFusedById(m.id));
        on('peer', (f, m) => this._onPeer(m));
        on('host', () => this._onHostChange());
        on('reset', () => location.reload());
        on('rejoined', () => { this._hello(); this.game.emit('hint', { text: 'Reconnected' }); });
        on('status', (f, m) => { if(m.status === 'closed') this.game.emit('hint', { text: 'Connection lost: reconnecting...', long: true }); });
        if(this.isHost) this._startHost(gs);
        else { if(gs) this._onGame(null, gs); this._hello(); }
    }
    get isHost() { return this.net.isHost; }
    get id() { return this.net.id; }
    send(m) { this.net.bcast(m); }
    /** A new id for something this client makes ('f' bombs). */
    newId(kind) { return `${kind}:${this.id}:${++this.idN}`; }
    actor(netId) {
        if(!netId) return null;
        if(netId === this.game.player.netId) return this.game.player;
        return this.remotes.get(netId) || this.game.bots.find(b => b.netId === netId) || null;
    }

    _name() {
        let n = this.ctx.params.get('name');
        try { if(n) localStorage.setItem('voxelwars.name', n); else n = localStorage.getItem('voxelwars.name'); } catch(e) {}
        if(!n) n = `${ADJ[Math.floor(Math.random() * ADJ.length)]} ${NOUN[Math.floor(Math.random() * NOUN.length)]}`;
        return n.slice(0, 20);
    }
    rename(n) {
        n = (n || '').trim().slice(0, 20); if(!n) return;
        this.name = this.game.player.name = n;
        try { localStorage.setItem('voxelwars.name', n); } catch(e) {}
        if(this.isHost) { const r = this.roster.get(this.game.player.netId); if(r) r.name = n; this._publish(); } else this._hello();
    }
    _balance(roster, me) {
        const n = { red: 0, blue: 0 };
        for(const r of roster) if(!r.bot && r.id !== me) n[r.team]++;
        return n.blue < n.red ? 'blue' : 'red';
    }
    _setMyTeam(team, first = false) {
        const p = this.game.player;
        if(p.team === team && !first) return;
        const changed = p.team !== team;
        p.team = team;
        const sp = GAME.teams[team].spawns;
        p.spawn = sp[(this.id.charCodeAt(0) + this.id.charCodeAt(1)) % sp.length];
        if(changed || first) { p.body.teleport(p.spawn); p.yaw = p.spawn[0] < 0 ? -Math.PI / 2 : Math.PI / 2; }
        if(changed && !first) this.game.emit('hint', { text: `You are on ${team === 'red' ? 'Red' : 'Blue'}` });
    }
    _hello() { this.send({ t: 'hello', id: this.game.player.netId, name: this.name, team: this.game.player.team }); }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ host: roster, bots, game state

    _startHost(gs) {
        const g = this.game, me = g.player;
        if(gs) {
            // a room that was running (the host left, or everyone did): the clock, the crates, who was where
            g.timeLeft = gs.time ?? g.timeLeft;
            if(gs.over) g.over = true;
            for(const c of gs.crates || []) { const cr = g.crates.list[c[0]]; if(cr && !c[1]) { cr.vault = c[2]; g.crates._place(cr, [c[3], c[4], c[5]], [0, 0, 0]); } }
            for(const r of gs.roster || []) if(!r.bot && r.client !== this.id && this.net.peers.has(r.client)) this.roster.set(r.id, { ...r });
            for(const r of this.roster.values()) this._remote(r);
        }
        this.roster.set(me.netId, { id: me.netId, name: this.name, team: me.team, bot: false, client: this.id });
        this.fillBots();
    }
    _onHello(from, m) {
        if(!this.isHost || typeof m.id !== 'string' || !m.id.startsWith('p:')) return;
        let r = this.roster.get(m.id);
        if(!r) {
            const team = this.left.get(from)?.team ?? (m.team === 'red' || m.team === 'blue' ? this._balanceWith(m.team, m.id) : this._balance([...this.roster.values()], m.id));
            r = { id: m.id, name: String(m.name || 'Player').slice(0, 20), team, bot: false, client: from };
            this.roster.set(m.id, r);
            this.game.emit('hint', { text: `${r.name} joined ${team === 'red' ? 'Red' : 'Blue'}` });
        } else r.name = String(m.name || r.name).slice(0, 20);
        this._remote(r);
        this.fillBots();
    }
    /** The team a joiner asked for (it guessed from the last game state), unless that unbalances the humans. */
    _balanceWith(want, id) {
        const n = { red: 0, blue: 0 };
        for(const r of this.roster.values()) if(!r.bot && r.id !== id) n[r.team]++;
        const other = want === 'red' ? 'blue' : 'red';
        return n[want] > n[other] ? other : want;
    }
    /** Bots fill both teams to the same size (host; bots on). */
    fillBots() {
        if(!this.isHost) return;
        const g = this.game, humans = { red: 0, blue: 0 };
        for(const r of this.roster.values()) if(!r.bot) humans[r.team]++;
        const size = g.botsOn ? Math.max(3, humans.red, humans.blue) : 0;
        for(const team of ['red', 'blue']) {
            const want = g.botsOn ? Math.max(0, size - humans[team]) : 0;
            const mine = g.bots.filter(b => b.team === team).sort((a, b) => a.netId.localeCompare(b.netId));
            while(mine.length > want) { const b = mine.pop(); this.roster.delete(b.netId); g.removeBot(b); }
            for(let i = 0; mine.length < want && i < 5; i++) {
