        if(this.queue.has(this.next)) this._pump();
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ host duties: hash checks, snapshots

    _duties(dt) {
        if(!this.isHost || this.single || !this.isReady) return;
        this.hashT += dt; this.snapT += dt;
        if(this.hashT > HASH_EVERY && this.editsSinceHash > 0) { this.hashT = 0; this.editsSinceHash = 0; this.submit({ k: 'mark', what: 'hash' }, { log: false }); }
        if((this.snapT > this.snapEvery && this.editsSinceSnap > 0) || this.editsSinceSnap >= SNAP_EDITS) { this.snapT = 0; this.editsSinceSnap = 0; this.submit({ k: 'mark', what: 'snap' }, { log: false }); }
    }

    /** This client's hashes as of now (in its applied order). */
    async hashNow() { return new Map(await this.terrain.hash()); }

    _mark(e, { seq }) {
        if(this.single) return;
        if(e.what === 'hash') {
            const p = this.terrain.hash();
            this.myHashes.set(seq, p);
            if(this.myHashes.size > 8) this.myHashes.delete(this.myHashes.keys().next().value);
            if(this.isHost) p.then(h => this.transport.send({ t: 'bcast', m: { t: 'hashes', seq, h } }));
            else if(this.hostHashes.has(seq)) this._compare(seq);
        } else if(e.what === 'snap' && this.isHost) {
            const t0 = performance.now();
            this.terrain.chunkData({ modified: true }).then(data => {
                const bytes = encode(data);
                this.transport.send({ t: 'snapshot', seq, data: bytes });
                this.stats.snapshots++; this.stats.snapBytes = bytes.length; this.stats.lastSnapMs = performance.now() - t0;
            });
        }
    }
    async _compare(seq) {
        const host = this.hostHashes.get(seq), mine = this.myHashes.get(seq);
        if(!host || !mine) return;
        this.hostHashes.delete(seq);
        const my = new Map(await mine);
        const bad = [];
        for(const [k, h] of host) if(my.get(k) !== h) bad.push(k);
        this.stats.hashChecks++;
        this.lastCheck = { seq, chunks: host.length, bad: bad.length };
        if(!bad.length) return;
        this.stats.mismatches += bad.length;
        console.warn(`net: ${bad.length} chunk(s) differ from the host at seq ${seq}: ${bad.slice(0, 6).join(' ')}${bad.length > 6 ? ' ...' : ''}; asking for them`);
        this.transport.send({ t: 'direct', to: this.hostId, m: { t: 'resyncReq', keys: bad } });
    }
    _direct(from, m) {
        switch(m.t) {
            case 'hashes':
                this.hostHashes.set(m.seq, m.h);
                if(this.hostHashes.size > 8) this.hostHashes.delete(this.hostHashes.keys().next().value);
                if(this.myHashes.has(m.seq)) this._compare(m.seq);
                break;
            case 'resyncReq': if(this.isHost) this.submit({ k: 'resync', to: from, keys: m.keys }, { log: false }); break;
            case 'resyncData': {
                const w = this.resyncData.get(m.seq);
                if(w?.resolve) w.resolve(m.data); else this.resyncData.set(m.seq, { data: m.data });
                break;
            }
            default: this._emit(m.t, from, m);
        }
    }
    /** 'resync' at this point of the order: the host reads the chunks and sends them; the target overwrites its own. */
    _resync(e, { seq }) {
        if(this.isHost && e.to !== this.id) {
            // queued in the worker at this point of the order: the data is the chunks as of this seq
            this.terrain.chunkData({ keys: e.keys }).then(data => { this.transport.send({ t: 'direct', to: e.to, m: { t: 'resyncData', seq, data } }); });
            return null;
        }
        if(e.to !== this.id) return null;
        const got = this.resyncData.get(seq);
        const data = got ? Promise.resolve(got.data) : new Promise(resolve => this.resyncData.set(seq, { resolve }));
        return data.then(d => { this.resyncData.delete(seq); this.stats.resyncs += d.length; return this.terrain.setChunks(d); });
    }
}
```

### src/net/codec.js
```
/**
 * Wire codec for net messages (src/net): any JSON-shaped message whose leaves may be typed arrays, as one
 * Uint8Array. Numbers travel as JSON (shortest round-trip form, so doubles such as brush specs arrive bit-exact);
 * typed arrays travel as raw bytes after the JSON. Messages over DEFLATE_MIN bytes are deflated (fflate).
 *
 *   bytes = encode(msg)        msg = decode(bytes)        (decode copies: the arrays it returns own their buffers)
 *
 * Layout: 'V' 'W' | version u8 | flags u8 (1 = deflated body) | body
 *         body = jsonLength u32 LE | JSON utf8 | blobs (each typed array's bytes, in order, 8-byte aligned)
 * A typed array in the JSON is replaced by { "$b": blobIndex, "$t": type, "$n": length }.
 * Bump VERSION when the layout or any message's meaning changes; decode rejects other versions.
 *
 * Also: fnv1a(bytes, h) (FNV-1a 32-bit, chained) for chunk hashes, deflate / inflate for snapshots.
 */
import { deflateSync, inflateSync } from 'three/examples/jsm/libs/fflate.module.js';

export const VERSION = 1;
const DEFLATE_MIN = 1024;
const TYPES = { Int8Array, Uint8Array, Uint8ClampedArray, Int16Array, Uint16Array, Int32Array, Uint32Array, Float32Array, Float64Array };
const te = new TextEncoder(), td = new TextDecoder();

export function encode(msg) {
    const blobs = [];
    const json = JSON.stringify(msg, function(k, v) {
        if(ArrayBuffer.isView(v) && !(v instanceof DataView)) { blobs.push(v); return { $b: blobs.length - 1, $t: v.constructor.name, $n: v.length }; }
        return v;
    });
    const j = te.encode(json);
    let size = 4 + j.length;
    const offs = [];
    for(const b of blobs) { size = (size + 7) & ~7; offs.push(size); size += b.byteLength; }
    const body = new Uint8Array(size);
    new DataView(body.buffer).setUint32(0, j.length, true);
    body.set(j, 4);
    blobs.forEach((b, i) => body.set(new Uint8Array(b.buffer, b.byteOffset, b.byteLength), offs[i]));
    const packed = size > DEFLATE_MIN ? deflateSync(body, { level: 1 }) : null;
    const use = packed && packed.length < size ? packed : body;
    const out = new Uint8Array(4 + use.length);
    out[0] = 86; out[1] = 87; out[2] = VERSION; out[3] = use === body ? 0 : 1;
    out.set(use, 4);
    return out;
}

export function decode(bytes) {
    if(!(bytes instanceof Uint8Array)) bytes = new Uint8Array(bytes);
    if(bytes[0] !== 86 || bytes[1] !== 87) throw new Error('net codec: not a message');
    if(bytes[2] !== VERSION) throw new Error(`net codec: version ${bytes[2]}, expected ${VERSION}`);
    let body = bytes.subarray(4);
    if(bytes[3] & 1) body = inflateSync(body);
    const dv = new DataView(body.buffer, body.byteOffset, body.byteLength);
    const jl = dv.getUint32(0, true);
    const json = td.decode(body.subarray(4, 4 + jl));
    let off = 4 + jl;
    return JSON.parse(json, function(k, v) {
        if(v && typeof v === 'object' && v.$t !== undefined && v.$b !== undefined) {
            const T = TYPES[v.$t];
            if(!T) throw new Error(`net codec: type ${v.$t}`);
            off = (off + 7) & ~7;
            const n = v.$n * T.BYTES_PER_ELEMENT;
            const a = new T(body.slice(off, off + n).buffer); // a copy, aligned
            off += n;
            return a;
        }
        return v;
    });
}

/** FNV-1a 32-bit over a byte view, continuing from h. */
export function fnv1a(bytes, h = 0x811c9dc5) {
    for(let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) { h ^= bytes[i]; h = Math.imul(h, 0x01000193); }
    return h >>> 0;
}

export const deflate = (u8) => deflateSync(u8, { level: 6 });
export const inflate = (u8) => inflateSync(u8);
```

### src/net/index.js
```
/**
 * Picks the transport from the URL and connects (src/net/Net.js):
 *   (default)                 single player: LocalTransport, edits apply synchronously
 *   ?room=name                online: the room `name` on the room server (PartyTransport; ?server=wss://host to use
 *                             another server, e.g. ?server=ws://127.0.0.1:8787 for `wrangler dev` in server/)
 *   ?room=name&local=1        tabs of this browser form a room over a BroadcastChannel (the first tab hosts)
 *   ?loopback=latency,jitter  single player through an in-page fake server (ms), to test the networked path
 *   ?snapEvery=s              the host's snapshot interval (tests; default 30 s)
 */
import { Net } from './Net.js';
import { LocalTransport, LoopbackServer, BroadcastChannelTransport, PartyTransport, DEFAULT_SERVER } from './transports.js';

export async function createNet(params, terrain) {
    let transport;
    const room = params.get('room');
    if(room && params.get('local') === '1') transport = new BroadcastChannelTransport(room);
    else if(room) transport = new PartyTransport(room, params.get('server') || DEFAULT_SERVER);
    else if(params.has('loopback')) {
        const [latency = 60, jitter = 20] = (params.get('loopback') || '').split(',').filter(Boolean).map(Number);
        transport = new LoopbackServer({ latency, jitter }).client();
    } else transport = new LocalTransport();
    const net = new Net({ transport, terrain, snapEvery: +params.get('snapEvery') || undefined }); // ?snapEvery=s (tests)
    net.room = room || null;
    await net.connect();
    if(!net.single) console.log(`net: ${transport.kind} ${room ? `room "${room}" ` : ''}as ${net.id}${net.isHost ? ' (host)' : ''}`);
    return net;
}
```

### src/net/sequencer.js
```
/**
 * The room's sequencer: gives every edit a global sequence number, keeps the edit log for late joiners, and relays
 * messages between clients. Pure (no DOM, no timers, no I/O): a transport feeds it what clients send and delivers
 * what it emits, so the same module runs in a tab (src/net/transports.js) and inside the PartyServer Durable Object
 * (server/src/index.js). It never looks inside edits: terrain stays on the clients (the host's terrain worker makes
 * snapshots).
 *
 *   const s = new Sequencer({ send: (to, msg) => ..., keepLog = true })   to: a client id or '*' (everyone, sender included)
 *   s.connect(id)            a client arrived (the first one is the host); the others get 'peer' { id, on: true }
 *   s.disconnect(id)         a client left: 'peer' { id, on: false }; the host passes to the oldest remaining client
 *                            ('host' { id }); s.empty is then true when nobody is left
 *   s.receive(from, msg)     a client's message:
 *     { t: 'join', since = 0 }               -> 'welcome' { you, host, peers, snap: { seq, data } | null, log: [entry],
 *                                                next, game }. since > 0: a client reconnecting that has every edit up
 *                                                to `since`: only the log after it (or the snapshot, if the log no
 *                                                longer reaches back that far)
 *     { t: 'edit', req, e, log = true }      -> '*' 'seq' { seq, from, req, e, log } (log: false = transient, kept as a
 *                                                stub so late joiners see no gap in the numbering)
 *     { t: 'snapshot', seq, data }           (host) the world after `seq` (opaque bytes); the log up to seq is dropped
 *     { t: 'direct', to, m }                 -> to 'direct' { from, m }
 *     { t: 'bcast', m }                      -> '*' except the sender: 'bcast' { from, m }; the host's m.t === 'game'
 *                                                (the game state) is also kept for the next welcome
 *     { t: 'reset' }                         (host) a new match: the log, snapshot and game state are dropped and
 *                                                everyone gets 'reset' (they reload; numbering goes on)
 *
 * Log entries are the 'seq' messages themselves. stats: { seq, logged, snapSeq, snapBytes, clients }.
 * Hooks for a persisting host (the Durable Object): onLogged(entry), onSnapshot(snap), onReset().
 */
export class Sequencer {
    constructor({ send, keepLog = true }) {
        this.send = send;
        this.keepLog = keepLog; // false: single player (nobody can join, so nothing is kept)
        this.seq = 0;
        this.log = [];
        this.snap = null;
        this.game = null;    // the host's last game state message
        this.clients = [];   // join order
        this.host = null;
    }
    get stats() { return { seq: this.seq, logged: this.log.length, snapSeq: this.snap?.seq ?? 0, snapBytes: this.snap?.data?.length ?? 0, clients: this.clients.length }; }
    get empty() { return this.clients.length === 0; }
    connect(id) {
        if(this.clients.includes(id)) return;
        this.clients.push(id);
        if(this.host === null) this.host = id;
        if(this.keepLog) for(const c of this.clients) if(c !== id) this.send(c, { t: 'peer', id, on: true });
    }
    disconnect(id) {
        if(!this.clients.includes(id)) return;
        this.clients = this.clients.filter(c => c !== id);
        for(const c of this.clients) this.send(c, { t: 'peer', id, on: false });
        if(this.host === id) {
            this.host = this.clients[0] ?? null;
            if(this.host !== null) this.send('*', { t: 'host', id: this.host });
        }
    }
    receive(from, msg) {
        switch(msg.t) {
            case 'join': {
                this.connect(from);
                const since = msg.since | 0;
                let snap = this.snap, log = this.log;
                // a reconnecting client that has everything up to `since`: the rest of the log is enough
                if(since > 0 && since >= (this.snap?.seq ?? 0) && (!log.length || log[0].seq <= since + 1)) { snap = null; log = log.filter(m => m.seq > since); }
                this.send(from, { t: 'welcome', you: from, host: this.host, peers: this.clients.filter(c => c !== from), snap, log, next: this.seq + 1, from: snap ? snap.seq + 1 : (log[0]?.seq ?? this.seq + 1), game: this.game });
                break;
            }
            case 'edit': {
                const log = msg.log !== false;
                const m = { t: 'seq', seq: ++this.seq, from, req: msg.req, e: msg.e, log };
                if(this.keepLog) {
                    const entry = log ? m : { t: 'seq', seq: m.seq, from, skip: true };
                    this.log.push(entry);
                    this.onLogged?.(entry);
                }
                this.send('*', m);
                break;
            }
            case 'snapshot':
                if(from !== this.host || msg.seq <= (this.snap?.seq ?? 0)) break;
                this.snap = { seq: msg.seq, data: msg.data };
                this.log = this.log.filter(m => m.seq > msg.seq);
                this.onSnapshot?.(this.snap);
                break;
            case 'direct': if(this.clients.includes(msg.to)) this.send(msg.to, { t: 'direct', from, m: msg.m }); break;
            case 'bcast':
                if(msg.m?.t === 'game' && from === this.host) this.game = msg.m;
                for(const c of this.clients) if(c !== from) this.send(c, { t: 'bcast', from, m: msg.m });
                break;
            case 'reset':
                if(from !== this.host) break;
                this.log = []; this.snap = null; this.game = null;
                this.onReset?.();
                this.send('*', { t: 'reset' });
                break;
        }
    }
}
```

### src/net/transports.js
```
/**
 * Transports: how a client's Net (src/net/Net.js) reaches the room's Sequencer (src/net/sequencer.js).
 *
 *   transport.connect(onMessage) -> Promise    start; onMessage(msg) gets every message for this client, in order
 *   transport.send(msg)                        to the sequencer
 *   transport.id, transport.isHost             set once connect resolves
 *   transport.close()
 *
 *   LocalTransport                single player: the sequencer runs in-process and answers synchronously (no
 *                                 encoding, no copies), so an edit applies inside the call that submitted it
 *   LoopbackServer / .client()    an in-page fake server for tests: every message goes through the codec and
 *                                 arrives after `latency` ± `jitter` ms (order kept per direction)
 *   BroadcastChannelTransport     tabs of one browser on the same origin form a room (`?room=name`): the tab that
 *                                 gets the room's Web Lock first is the host and runs the sequencer; the others
 *                                 talk to it over a BroadcastChannel. The host's own messages are handled
 *                                 synchronously, like LocalTransport.
 *   PartyTransport                the room on the PartyServer (server/): a WebSocket to
 *                                 <server>/parties/room/<room>?cid=<id>, reconnecting with backoff. The client id is
 *                                 kept for the tab's session (sessionStorage), so a reload or a dropped connection
 *                                 comes back as the same client. While it is down, messages wait (up to QUEUE_MAX).
 *                                 transport.status: 'connecting' | 'open' | 'closed'; transport.onStatus(status);
 *                                 transport.onReopen(): called before the queue is flushed (Net re-joins there).
 *                                 A socket that has been silent for SILENT_MS (Net pings every 2 s) is given up on
 *                                 and replaced (half-open connections); transport.drop() does that at once.
 */
import { Sequencer } from './sequencer.js';
import { encode, decode } from './codec.js';

export class LocalTransport {
    constructor() { this.id = 'local'; this.isHost = true; this.single = true; this.kind = 'local'; }
    connect(onMessage) {
        this.seq = new Sequencer({ send: (to, m) => onMessage(m), keepLog: false });
        this.seq.connect(this.id);
        return Promise.resolve();
    }
    send(m) { this.seq.receive(this.id, m); }
    close() {}
}

export class LoopbackServer {
    constructor({ latency = 40, jitter = 20 } = {}) {
        this.latency = latency; this.jitter = jitter;
        this.clients = new Map();
        this.n = 0;
        this.bytes = { up: 0, down: 0 };
        this.seq = new Sequencer({ send: (to, m) => {
            const b = encode(m);
            for(const c of to === '*' ? this.clients.values() : [this.clients.get(to)]) if(c) { this.bytes.down += b.length; this._later(c, 'downAt', () => c.onMessage?.(decode(b))); }
        } });
    }
    client() { const t = new LoopbackTransport(this, `c${++this.n}`); return t; }
    _later(c, slot, fn) {
        const now = performance.now();
        const at = Math.max(c[slot] || 0, now + this.latency + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * this.jitter);
        c[slot] = at;
        setTimeout(fn, Math.max(0, at - now));
    }
}
class LoopbackTransport {
    constructor(server, id) { this.server = server; this.id = id; this.isHost = false; this.kind = 'loopback'; }
    connect(onMessage) {
        this.onMessage = onMessage;
        this.server.clients.set(this.id, this);
        this.server.seq.connect(this.id);
        this.isHost = this.server.seq.host === this.id;
        return Promise.resolve();
    }
    send(m) {
        const b = encode(m);
        this.server.bytes.up += b.length;
        this.server._later(this, 'upAt', () => this.server.seq.receive(this.id, decode(b)));
    }
    close() { this.server.clients.delete(this.id); this.server.seq.disconnect(this.id); }
}

export class BroadcastChannelTransport {
    constructor(room) {
        this.room = room;
        this.id = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10);
        this.isHost = false;
        this.bytes = { up: 0, down: 0 };
        this.kind = 'tabs';
    }
    async connect(onMessage) {
        this.onMessage = onMessage;
        this.ch = new BroadcastChannel(`voxelwars:${this.room}`);
        this.ch.onmessage = (ev) => this._recv(ev.data);
        // the first tab to take the room's lock hosts (it keeps the lock while open)
        this.isHost = await new Promise((resolve) => {
            if(!navigator.locks) { resolve(true); return; }
            navigator.locks.request(`voxelwars-host:${this.room}`, { ifAvailable: true }, (lock) => {
                resolve(!!lock);
                return lock ? new Promise(r => { this.releaseLock = r; }) : undefined;
            });
        });
        if(this.isHost) {
            this.seq = new Sequencer({ send: (to, m) => {
                if(to === this.id || to === '*') this.onMessage(m);
                if(to !== this.id) this._post(to, m);
            } });
            this.seq.connect(this.id);
        }
        addEventListener('pagehide', () => this.close());
    }
    _post(to, m) {
        const b = encode(m);
        this.bytes.up += b.length;
        this.ch.postMessage({ to, from: this.id, b });
    }
    _recv({ to, from, b }) {
        if(to === 'server') { if(this.isHost) { this.bytes.down += b.length; this.seq.receive(from, decode(b)); } return; }
        if(to === 'bye') { if(this.isHost) this.seq.disconnect(from); return; }
        if(to !== '*' && to !== this.id) return;
        this.bytes.down += b.length;
        this.onMessage(decode(b));
    }
    send(m) {
        if(this.isHost) this.seq.receive(this.id, m);
        else this._post('server', m);
    }
    close() {
        if(this.closed) return;
        this.closed = true;
        try { if(!this.isHost) this.ch.postMessage({ to: 'bye', from: this.id, b: new Uint8Array(0) }); } catch(e) { /* closed */ }
        this.releaseLock?.();
        this.ch?.close();
    }
}

export const DEFAULT_SERVER = 'wss://voxel-wars.sh1ftmaker.workers.dev';
const QUEUE_MAX = 2000;
const SILENT_MS = 7000;

export class PartyTransport {
    constructor(room, server = DEFAULT_SERVER) {
        this.room = room;
        this.server = server.replace(/\/+$/, '').replace(/^http/, 'ws');
        let id = null;
        try { id = sessionStorage.getItem(`voxelwars.cid.${room}`); } catch(e) {}
        if(!id) { id = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10); try { sessionStorage.setItem(`voxelwars.cid.${room}`, id); } catch(e) {} }
        this.id = id;
        this.isHost = false;
        this.status = 'connecting';
        this.queue = [];
        this.bytes = { up: 0, down: 0 };
        this.byType = {};
        this.retry = 0;
        this.opens = 0;
        this.kind = 'room server';
    }
    get url() { return `${this.server}/parties/room/${encodeURIComponent(this.room)}?cid=${this.id}&_pk=${this.id}-${this.opens}`; }
    connect(onMessage) {
        this.onMessage = onMessage;
        addEventListener('pagehide', () => this.close());
        this.watch = setInterval(() => { if(this.status === 'open' && performance.now() - this.lastRecv > SILENT_MS) this.drop(); }, 1000);
        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            this.firstOpen = resolve;
            this.firstFail = reject;
            this._open();
        });
    }
    _open() {
        if(this.closed) return;
        this.opens++;
        const ws = this.ws = new WebSocket(this.url);
        ws.binaryType = 'arraybuffer';
        this._setStatus('connecting');
        ws.onopen = () => {
            if(ws !== this.ws) return;
            this.retry = 0;
            this.lastRecv = performance.now();
            this._setStatus('open');
            const q = this.queue; this.queue = [];
            if(this.firstOpen) { const r = this.firstOpen; this.firstOpen = this.firstFail = null; r(); }
            else this.onReopen?.(); // the re-join goes out before what waited
            for(const b of q) this._raw(b);
        };
        ws.onmessage = (ev) => {
            if(ws !== this.ws) return;
            const b = new Uint8Array(ev.data);
            this.bytes.down += b.length;
            this.lastRecv = performance.now();
            let m;
            try { m = decode(b); } catch(e) { console.warn('net: bad message', e); return; }
            if(m.t === 'welcome') this.isHost = m.host === this.id;
            else if(m.t === 'host') this.isHost = m.id === this.id;
            this.onMessage(m);
        };
        ws.onclose = () => {
            if(ws !== this.ws) return;
            this._lost();
        };
        ws.onerror = () => {};
    }
    /** Give up on the current socket now (silent, or a test) and reconnect. */
    drop() {
        const ws = this.ws;
        if(!ws) return;
        this.ws = null;
        try { ws.close(); } catch(e) {}
        this._lost();
    }
    _lost() {
        this._setStatus('closed');
        if(this.closed) return;
        if(this.firstFail && this.retry >= 3) { const r = this.firstFail; this.firstOpen = this.firstFail = null; r(new Error(`cannot reach ${this.server}`)); return; }
        const wait = Math.min(8000, 400 * 2 ** this.retry++) * (0.75 + Math.random() * 0.5);
        setTimeout(() => this._open(), wait);
    }
    _setStatus(s) { if(s !== this.status) { this.status = s; this.onStatus?.(s); } }
    _raw(b) { this.bytes.up += b.length; this.ws.send(b); }
    send(m) {
        const b = encode(m);
        const k = m.t === 'bcast' || m.t === 'direct' ? m.m?.t : m.t; // bytes up by message type (stats)
        this.byType[k] = (this.byType[k] || 0) + b.length;
        if(this.ws?.readyState === 1 && !this.queue.length) this._raw(b);
        else if(this.queue.length < QUEUE_MAX) this.queue.push(b);
    }
    close() {
        if(this.closed) return;
        this.closed = true;
        clearInterval(this.watch);
        try { this.ws?.close(1000, 'bye'); } catch(e) {}
    }
}
```

### src/physics/Destruction.js
```
/**
 * The one entry point for changing matter. Gameplay calls these; they edit the VoxelWorld (and detached voxel bodies),
 * push physics bodies around, detect what lost its support, and announce what was destroyed.
 *
 * API (gameplay depends on these signatures):
 *   destruction.carve(brush, { op = 'carve', mat, hardness = true, author, impulse, kind, dir })
 *       brush from src/voxel/brushes.js. Returns { removed: Float64Array m³ per material, volume, added, center, box }.
 *       `impulse` (number) also kicks dynamic bodies near the brush outward. `dir` (unit vector): the blow's direction (a
 *       punch: the soft ground is driven in along it).
 *       A carve also cuts every voxel body it overlaps (and splits bodies cut in two), then queues a check of the
 *       terrain around the edit for regions that lost their support: those become bodies over the next few frames
 *       ('detached'), pushed by the blast's impulse.
 *   destruction.blast(center, radius, { strength, author, seed })    rock-shaped crater + impulse; returns carve's result
 *   destruction.rip(center, radius, { author }) -> Prop | null        tear a lump of terrain out as a dynamic body
 *                                                                     (Donkey Kong style: grab it, throw it)
 *       The prop's body origin is the lump's centre; userData { voxelBody, ripped: true, mat, volume, mass, author }.
 *
 * Additions (physics agent):
 *   destruction.brushes                    the brush module (sphere, capsule, box, rock, ...), for tools and tests
 *   destruction.bodies                     VoxelBodies (src/physics/VoxelBodies.js): live detached bodies, tuning
 *   result.detached                        always [] now: connectivity runs in the background under a frame budget,
 *                                          detached bodies arrive over the next frames as 'detached' events
 *
 * Additions (perf): with the terrain worker (world.remote, src/voxel/TerrainSync.js) a carve is asynchronous: the
 *   returned result is provisional (result.pending; removed / volume count only the voxel bodies it cut, changed 0)
 *   and `result.done` is a Promise of the final result. 'destroyed' and 'detached' fire when the worker answers
 *   (a frame or two later). Without the worker result.done resolves at once to the same object.
 *
 * Net (src/net): with ctx.net every terrain edit is submitted to the room's edit log and applied when it comes back in
 *   sequence order (single player: LocalTransport answers synchronously, so nothing changes). The submitter carves
 *   voxel bodies and pushes physics bodies at once; other clients do that when the edit arrives. Edit kinds handled
 *   here: 'brush' { spec, op, mat, hardness, conn, erode, debris, lift, kind, impulse, dir, author } and 'samples'
 *   { patch } (soft-ground bakes, world.applySamples); VoxelBodies handles 'stamp' and 'bsplit'.
 *
 * Events on ctx.events (CustomEvent, data in e.detail):
 *   'destroyed' { center: [x,y,z], box: [min, max] (metres), removed: Float64Array, volume, author, kind: 'carve'|'blast'|'impact' }
 *   'detached'  { prop, volume, mat }      a piece of terrain broke loose and is now a body
 *   'impact'    { point, speed, mat, prop } a voxel body hit something hard (speed = its velocity change, m/s)
 */
import { VOXEL } from '../config.js';
import { rock, specOf, brushFromSpec } from '../voxel/brushes.js';
import * as brushes from '../voxel/brushes.js';
import { VoxelBodies, DEBRIS } from './VoxelBodies.js';

export class Destruction {
    constructor(ctx) {
        this.ctx = ctx;
        this.seed = 1;
        this.bodies = new VoxelBodies(ctx);
        this.brushes = brushes; // the brush constructors (src/voxel/brushes.js), for tools and tests
        /** Soft ground (src/soft/SoftGround.js) and other observers: fn({ op, center, size, impulse, kind, changed, box, dir })
         *  after every carve / add (box in metres). */
        this.editHooks = [];
        const net = ctx.net;
        if(net) {
            net.on('brush', (e, meta) => this._applyBrush(e, meta));
            net.on('samples', (e) => ctx.world.remote.samples(e.patch));
            ctx.world.applySamples = (patch) => net.submit({ k: 'samples', patch });
        }
    }
    /** Submit a terrain edit to the edit log (or apply it here when there is no net). Resolves to the worker's reply. */
    submit(e) {
        const net = this.ctx.net;
        return net ? net.submit(e) : this._applyBrush(e, { local: true, seq: 0, from: null });
    }
    /**
     * A 'brush' edit in sequence order: the terrain worker applies it (connectivity, structural collapse); islands that
     * came loose become bodies (ids from the edit's seq), the soft ground hears about it. An edit from another client
     * also carves this client's voxel bodies, pushes bodies around and emits 'destroyed' (the submitter did that
     * already). Returns the reply's promise.
     */
    _applyBrush(e, { local, seq, from, history = false }) {
        const { world, physics, events } = this.ctx;
        const brush = brushFromSpec(e.spec);
        const center = brush.min.map((v, i) => (v + brush.max[i]) / 2);
        const kick = e.impulse ? { center, radius: Math.max(...brush.max.map((v, i) => v - brush.min[i])) * 0.9, strength: e.impulse } : null;
        if(history) return world.remote.brush(e.spec, { op: e.op, mat: e.mat, hardness: e.hardness, conn: e.conn, erode: e.erode, debris: e.debris });
        if(!local && !e.lift) {
            if(e.op === 'carve') this.bodies.carve(brush, { hardness: e.hardness });
            if(kick) physics.explode(kick.center, kick.radius, kick.strength);
        }
        return world.remote.brush(e.spec, { op: e.op, mat: e.mat, hardness: e.hardness, conn: e.conn, erode: e.erode, debris: e.debris, lift: e.lift }).then(rep => {
            rep.seq = seq;
            this.bodies.fromWorker(rep, kick, { seq, from, local, lift: e.lift });
            const r = rep.result;
            const box = [r.box[0].map(x => x * VOXEL), r.box[1].map(x => x * VOXEL)];
            if(this.editHooks.length && !e.lift) {
                const size = Math.max(...brush.max.map((x, i) => x - brush.min[i])) / 2;
                for(const fn of this.editHooks) try { fn({ op: e.op, center, size, impulse: e.impulse || 0, kind: e.kind, changed: r.changed, box, dir: e.dir || null }); } catch(err) { console.error(err); }
            }
            if(!local) {
                let v = 0;
                for(let i = 0; i < 256; i++) v += r.removed[i];
                if(v > 0) events.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('destroyed', { detail: { center, box, removed: r.removed, volume: v, author: e.author ?? null, kind: e.kind || 'carve', remote: true } }));
            }
            return rep;
        });
    }
    carve(brush, opts = {}) {
        if(this.ctx.world.remote) return this._carveRemote(brush, opts);
        const { op = 'carve', mat = 1, hardness = true, author = null, impulse = 0, kind = 'carve', dir = null } = opts;
        const { world, physics, events } = this.ctx;
        const r = world.applyBrush(brush, { op, mat, hardness });
        const center = brush.min.map((v, i) => (v + brush.max[i]) / 2);
        const removed = r.removed;
        const detached = [];
        const kick = impulse ? { center, radius: Math.max(...brush.max.map((v, i) => v - brush.min[i])) * 0.9, strength: impulse } : null;
        if(op === 'carve') {
            const b = this.bodies.carve(brush, { hardness });
            if(b.volume > 0) for(let i = 0; i < 256; i++) removed[i] += b.removed[i];
            // what lost its support breaks loose over the next frames ('detached' events), pushed by this blast
            if(r.changed) this.bodies.queueConnectivity(r.box[0], r.box[1], brush, kick);
        }
        let volume = 0;
        for(let i = 0; i < 256; i++) volume += removed[i];
        if(kick) physics.explode(kick.center, kick.radius, kick.strength);
        const box = [r.box[0].map(v => v * VOXEL), r.box[1].map(v => v * VOXEL)];
        const result = { removed, volume, added: r.added, center, box, changed: r.changed, detached };
        if(this.editHooks.length) {
            const size = Math.max(...brush.max.map((v, i) => v - brush.min[i])) / 2;
            for(const fn of this.editHooks) try { fn({ op, center, size, impulse, kind, changed: r.changed, box, dir }); } catch(e) { console.error(e); }
        }
        if(volume > 0) events.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('destroyed', { detail: { center, box, removed, volume, author, kind } }));
        result.done = Promise.resolve(result);
        return result;
    }
    /**
     * The terrain worker's path (world.remote, src/voxel/TerrainSync.js): voxel bodies are carved and pushed now; the
     * terrain edit, connectivity and detaching run in the worker and land a frame or two later. The result returned
     * at once is provisional (removed / volume count the bodies only, changed 0, box the brush's); `result.done`
     * resolves to the final one, and 'destroyed', 'detached' and the edit hooks fire then.
     */
    _carveRemote(brush, { op = 'carve', mat = 1, hardness = true, author = null, impulse = 0, kind = 'carve', dir = null } = {}) {
        const { world, physics, events } = this.ctx;
        const center = brush.min.map((v, i) => (v + brush.max[i]) / 2);
        const removed = new Float64Array(256);
        const kick = impulse ? { center, radius: Math.max(...brush.max.map((v, i) => v - brush.min[i])) * 0.9, strength: impulse } : null;
        let volume = 0;
        let bodyDone = null;
        if(op === 'carve') {
            const b = this.bodies.carve(brush, { hardness });
            if(b.volume > 0) for(let i = 0; i < 256; i++) { removed[i] += b.removed[i]; volume += b.removed[i]; }
            bodyDone = b.done;
        }
        if(kick) physics.explode(kick.center, kick.radius, kick.strength);
        const result = { removed, volume, added: 0, center, box: [brush.min.map(v => v - 4 * VOXEL), brush.max.map(v => v + 4 * VOXEL)], changed: 0, detached: [], pending: true };
        const bodies = this.bodies;
        const terrainDone = this.submit({ k: 'brush', spec: specOf(brush), op, mat, hardness, conn: op === 'carve', erode: Math.max(0, bodies.erode), debris: DEBRIS, kind, impulse, dir, author });
        result.done = Promise.all([terrainDone, bodyDone]).then(([rep, b]) => {
            if(b) for(let i = 0; i < 256; i++) removed[i] += b.removed[i]; // the bodies' share (carved in the worker too)
            const r = rep.result;
            let v = 0;
            for(let i = 0; i < 256; i++) { removed[i] += r.removed[i]; v += removed[i]; }
            result.volume = v; result.added = r.added; result.changed = r.changed; result.pending = false;
            result.box = [r.box[0].map(x => x * VOXEL), r.box[1].map(x => x * VOXEL)];
            if(v > 0) events.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('destroyed', { detail: { center, box: result.box, removed, volume: v, author, kind } }));
            return result;
        });
        return result;
    }
    blast(center, radius, { strength = 14, author = null, seed } = {}) {
        return this.carve(rock(center, radius, seed ?? this.seed++), { author, impulse: strength, kind: 'blast' });
    }
    /** Tear a roughly spherical lump out of the terrain and return it as a dynamic body (null if there is no matter). */
    rip(center, radius, { author = null, seed } = {}) {
        const vb = this.bodies.rip(rock(center, radius, seed ?? this.seed++), { author });
        return vb ? vb.prop : null;
    }
}
```

### src/physics/Physics.js
```
/**
 * Rigid body physics (box3d, src/physics/box3d/B3.js). Owns the box3d world, the terrain's static colliders (one
 * triangle mesh per chunk octant, rebuilt from each new chunk mesh), dynamic props, and a fixed 60 Hz step.
 *
 * Public API (other modules depend on these signatures; extend, don't break):
 *   await Physics.create(ctx)                      ctx: { world: VoxelWorld, terrain: TerrainRenderer, scene }
 *   physics.b3                                     the B3World; BodyType (exported here too) for setBodyType
 *   physics.step(dt)                               advance (fixed substeps); call once per frame
 *   physics.addBody({ shape, size, position, velocity, angularVelocity, density, friction, restitution, mesh, userData, bullet, kinematic })
 *        shape 'box' (size = half extents [x,y,z]) | 'sphere' (size = radius) | 'capsule' (size = [halfHeight, radius])
 *        returns a Prop { body, collider, mesh, userData }; `mesh` (THREE.Object3D, optional) follows the body
 *   physics.removeBody(prop)
 *   physics.raycast(origin, dir, maxDist, { exclude, ignore }) -> { point, normal, distance, collider, prop|null } | null
 *        exclude: a body, collider or prop to skip; ignore: collider handles (or bodies) to skip
 *   physics.explode(center, radius, strength)      radial velocity change on dynamic bodies, falling off with distance
 *   physics.propOf(collider)                       the Prop owning a collider (null for terrain)
 *   physics.onStep(fn)                             fn(h) after every fixed substep
 *
 * Bodies (B3Body) keep the method names the game was written against: translation(), rotation(), linvel(),
 * setLinvel(v, wake), applyImpulse(j, wake), isDynamic(), isSleeping(), wakeUp(), setBodyType(BodyType.X, wake),
 * setNextKinematicTranslation(t), ... A call on a removed body or collider does nothing (handles are checked in C).
 *
 * Additions:
 *   physics.createBody({ type, position, rotation, linvel, angvel, linearDamping, angularDamping, bullet, enabled })
 *   physics.addProp({ body, colliders, mesh, userData }) -> Prop   register a body made with createBody
 *        (several colliders allowed: prop.collider is the first, prop.colliders all of them)
 *   physics.attachCollider(prop, spec) -> collider / physics.detachColliders(prop)   rebuild a prop's shape;
 *        spec { shape: 'box', half, center } | { shape: 'hull', points } | { shape: 'sphere', radius, center } with
 *        { density, friction, restitution, groups, hitEvents }
 *   physics.onHit(fn)                              fn(collider1, collider2, approachSpeed (m/s), point, normal) for
 *        contacts that begin faster than the hit threshold between colliders with hitEvents, after every substep
 *   physics.onFrame(fn)                            fn(dt) once per frame after the substeps (budgeted background work)
 *   physics.colliderPending(key), flushColliders() terrain colliders are rebuilt under a per-frame budget
 *        (colliderBudgetMs); prop meshes are interpolated between fixed steps (physics.interpolate)
 *   physics.stepMs, physics.stepMsMax              smoothed and recent peak cost of step() per frame (ms)
 *   physics.voxelBodies                            the VoxelBodies manager (src/physics/VoxelBodies.js), set by Destruction
 *   Voxel bodies (detached terrain, src/voxel/VoxelBody.js) are Props with userData.voxelBody; their body origin is
 *   their centre of mass.
 *
 * Determinism (for lockstep debris later): box3d gives bit-identical results everywhere for the same sequence of
 * calls. Everything here creates bodies and colliders in a deterministic order; explode() and some game code still
 * draw spins from Math.random().
 */
import { CHUNK_M } from '../config.js';
import { loadBox3d, B3World, BodyType } from './box3d/B3.js';

export { BodyType };
export const GRAVITY = 30; // m/s²: real gravity reads as slow motion at this scale (WorldParty's DEFAULT_BODY_GRAVITY)
export const STEP = 1 / 60;
/** Collision groups ((memberships << 16) | filter): terrain colliders are in group 0; GROUPS_NO_TERRAIN collides
 *  with everything but the terrain (bodies whose terrain chunks are still being remeshed use it). */
export const GROUPS_TERRAIN = (0x0001 << 16) | 0xffff;
export const GROUPS_NO_TERRAIN = ((0x0002 << 16) | 0xfffe) >>> 0;
export const GROUPS_ALL = 0xffffffff;
const HIT_THRESHOLD = 1.5; // m/s approach speed for hit events

export class Physics {
    static async create(ctx) {
        const M = await loadBox3d();
        Physics.warmup(M);
        return new Physics(ctx, M);
    }
    /** Run the contact code once on a scratch world (box, hull, sphere and capsule on a bumpy mesh), so the browser
     *  has compiled it before the first blast. */
    static warmup(M) {
        const t0 = performance.now();
        const w = new B3World(M, { gravity: [0, -GRAVITY, 0], hitThreshold: HIT_THRESHOLD });
        const ground = w.createBody({ type: BodyType.Fixed });
        const n = 9, v = new Float32Array(n * n * 3), idx = [];
        for(let z = 0; z < n; z++) for(let x = 0; x < n; x++) { const i = (x + z * n) * 3; v[i] = x - 4; v[i + 1] = ((x * 7 + z * 3) % 5) * 0.08; v[i + 2] = z - 4; }
        for(let z = 0; z < n - 1; z++) for(let x = 0; x < n - 1; x++) { const a = x + z * n; idx.push(a, a + n, a + 1, a + 1, a + n, a + n + 1); }
        w.createMesh(ground, v, new Int32Array(idx), { groups: GROUPS_TERRAIN });
        const bodies = [];
        for(let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
            const b = w.createBody({ position: [-2 + k * 1.3, 1.2 + k * 0.4, 0], linvel: [0, -12, 0], angvel: [1, 2, 0.5], bullet: k === 2 });
            const o = { hitEvents: true };
            if(k === 0) for(let i = 0; i < 4; i++) w.createBox(b, [0.3, 0.2, 0.25], { ...o, center: [i * 0.3 - 0.45, (i & 1) * 0.3, 0] });
            else if(k === 1) w.createHull(b, new Float32Array([0, 0, 0, 0.6, 0, 0, 0, 0.6, 0, 0, 0, 0.6, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5]), o);
            else if(k === 2) w.createSphere(b, 0.35, o);
            else w.createCapsule(b, 0.4, 0.3, o);
            bodies.push(b);
        }
        const hits = [];
        for(let i = 0; i < 45; i++) {
            hits.length = 0;
            w.step(STEP, hits);
            if(i === 20) for(const b of bodies) { b.setLinvel({ x: 3, y: 6, z: -2 }, true); for(const c of b.colliders) c.setCollisionGroups(GROUPS_NO_TERRAIN); }
            if(i === 30) for(const b of bodies) for(const c of b.colliders) c.setCollisionGroups(GROUPS_ALL);
        }
        w.castRay([0, 5, 0], [0, -1, 0], 10);
        w.move({ x: 0, y: 1.5, z: 0 }, 0.5, 0.4, { x: 0.1, y: -0.2, z: 0 }, { walk: true, snap: 0.5, pushMass: 90, dt: STEP });
        w.free();
        Physics.warmupMs = performance.now() - t0;
    }
    constructor({ world, terrain, scene }, M) {
        this.world = world;
        this.scene = scene;
        this.b3 = new B3World(M, { gravity: [0, -GRAVITY, 0], hitThreshold: HIT_THRESHOLD });
        // overlaps are pushed apart at up to 1 m/s (box3d's default 3 m/s makes pieces jump when the surface under
        // them changes, e.g. a body they rest on is stamped into the terrain)
        M._bw_set_contact_tuning(30, 10, 1);
        this.BodyType = BodyType;
        this.terrainColliders = new Map(); // chunk key -> 8 octant slots { hash, collider } | null
        this.terrainBody = this.b3.createBody({ type: BodyType.Fixed });
        this.props = new Set();
        this.byCollider = new Map(); // collider handle -> prop
        this.stepListeners = [];
        this.hitListeners = [];
        this.hits = [];
        this.acc = 0;
        this.stepMs = 0; this.stepMsMax = 0;
        this.frameListeners = [];
        // terrain colliders are rebuilt from new chunk meshes under a per-frame time budget (a mesh build costs
        // ~0.4 ms, and a blast remeshes a dozen chunks or more)
        this.pendingColliders = new Map(); // chunk key -> geometry | null, oldest first
        this.colliderBudgetMs = 0.8;
        this.interpolate = true;
        terrain.onChunkMesh((key, geometry) => this.pendingColliders.set(key, geometry));
    }

    /**
     * Replace a chunk's static colliders from its new render geometry (null removes them). A chunk is collided as 8
     * meshes, one per octant (4 m cube; a triangle goes to the octant of its centroid), and only the octants whose
     * triangles changed are rebuilt: an edit usually touches a few.
     */
    setChunkCollider(key, geometry, deadline = Infinity) {
        let slots = this.terrainColliders.get(key);
        if(!slots) this.terrainColliders.set(key, slots = new Array(8).fill(null));
        const c = this.world.chunks.get(key);
        const ox = c.cx * CHUNK_M, oy = c.cy * CHUNK_M, oz = c.cz * CHUNK_M, H = CHUNK_M / 2;
        const changed = [];
        const b3 = this.b3, body = this.terrainBody;
        const build = (vtx, ind) => b3.createMesh(body, vtx, ind, { friction: 0.9, groups: GROUPS_TERRAIN });
        if(!geometry) {
            for(let o = 0; o < 8; o++) if(slots[o]) { b3.removeCollider(slots[o].collider); slots[o] = null; changed.push(o); }
        } else if(geometry.userData?.octants) {
            // octant arrays and hashes made by the mesh worker (src/voxel/meshPrep.js): only the box3d builds here
            const octs = geometry.userData.octants;
            for(let o = 0; o < 8; o++) {
                const old = slots[o], d = octs[o];
                if(!d) { if(old) { b3.removeCollider(old.collider); slots[o] = null; changed.push(o); } continue; }
                if(old && old.hash === d.hash) continue;
                if(old) b3.removeCollider(old.collider);
                const col = build(d.vtx, d.ind);
                slots[o] = col ? { hash: d.hash, collider: col } : null;
                changed.push(o);
                if(o < 7 && performance.now() > deadline) { this.pendingColliders.set(key, geometry); break; }
            }
        } else {
            const pos = geometry.attributes.position.array, idx = geometry.index.array;
            const nt = idx.length / 3, nv = pos.length / 3;
            // octant of every triangle, and triangle counts per octant
            const oct = new Uint8Array(nt), count = new Int32Array(8);
            for(let t = 0; t < nt; t++) {
                const a = idx[t * 3] * 3, b = idx[t * 3 + 1] * 3, d = idx[t * 3 + 2] * 3;
                const o = ((pos[a] + pos[b] + pos[d]) >= 3 * H ? 1 : 0) | ((pos[a + 1] + pos[b + 1] + pos[d + 1]) >= 3 * H ? 2 : 0) | ((pos[a + 2] + pos[b + 2] + pos[d + 2]) >= 3 * H ? 4 : 0);
                oct[t] = o; count[o]++;
            }
            const remap = new Int32Array(nv), stamp = new Int32Array(nv);
            for(let o = 0; o < 8; o++) {
                const old = slots[o];
                if(!count[o]) { if(old) { b3.removeCollider(old.collider); slots[o] = null; changed.push(o); } continue; }
                // the octant's own vertex and index arrays (world space), and a hash of them
                const ind = new Uint32Array(count[o] * 3);
                const vtx = [];
                let ni = 0, vn = 0, h = 0x811c9dc5 ^ count[o];
                for(let t = 0; t < nt; t++) {
                    if(oct[t] !== o) continue;
                    for(let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
                        const v = idx[t * 3 + k];
                        if(stamp[v] !== o + 1) {
                            stamp[v] = o + 1; remap[v] = vn++;
                            const x = pos[v * 3] + ox, y = pos[v * 3 + 1] + oy, z = pos[v * 3 + 2] + oz;
                            vtx.push(x, y, z);
                            h = Math.imul(h ^ Math.round(x * 4096), 16777619); h = Math.imul(h ^ Math.round(y * 4096), 16777619); h = Math.imul(h ^ Math.round(z * 4096), 16777619);
                        }
                        ind[ni++] = remap[v];
                        h = Math.imul(h ^ remap[v], 16777619);
                    }
                }
                if(old && old.hash === h) continue;
                if(old) b3.removeCollider(old.collider);
                const col = build(new Float32Array(vtx), ind);
                slots[o] = col ? { hash: h, collider: col } : null;
                changed.push(o);
                // out of time: the rest of this chunk goes back in the queue (unchanged octants are skipped then)
                if(o < 7 && performance.now() > deadline) { this.pendingColliders.set(key, geometry); break; }
            }
        }
        if(!changed.length) return;
        // bodies resting on a changed octant must notice the change
        const m = 3;
        for(const p of this.props) {
            if(!p.body.isSleeping() || !p.body.isDynamic()) continue;
            const t = p.body.translation();
            for(const o of changed) {
                const x0 = ox + (o & 1 ? H : 0), y0 = oy + (o & 2 ? H : 0), z0 = oz + (o & 4 ? H : 0);
                if(t.x > x0 - m && t.x < x0 + H + m && t.y > y0 - m && t.y < y0 + H + m && t.z > z0 - m && t.z < z0 + H + m) { p.body.wakeUp(); break; }
            }
        }
    }

    createBody(opts) { return this.b3.createBody(opts); }

    addBody({ shape = 'box', size = [0.5, 0.5, 0.5], position = [0, 0, 0], quaternion, velocity, angularVelocity, density = 1000, friction = 0.7, restitution = 0.1, mesh = null, userData = {}, bullet = false, kinematic = false } = {}) {
        const b3 = this.b3;
        const body = b3.createBody({ type: kinematic ? BodyType.Kinematic : BodyType.Dynamic, position, rotation: quaternion || [0, 0, 0, 1],
            linvel: velocity || [0, 0, 0], angvel: angularVelocity || [0, 0, 0], bullet });
        const o = { density, friction, restitution };
        const collider = shape === 'sphere' ? b3.createSphere(body, size, o)
            : shape === 'capsule' ? b3.createCapsule(body, size[0], size[1], o)
            : b3.createBox(body, size, o);
        const prop = { body, collider, colliders: [collider], mesh, userData };
        this.props.add(prop);
        this.byCollider.set(collider.handle, prop);
        if(mesh) { mesh.matrixAutoUpdate = true; this.scene.add(mesh); }
        return prop;
    }
    addProp({ body, colliders = [], mesh = null, userData = {} }) {
        const prop = { body, collider: colliders[0] || null, colliders: [...colliders], mesh, userData };
        this.props.add(prop);
        for(const c of colliders) this.byCollider.set(c.handle, prop);
        if(mesh) { mesh.matrixAutoUpdate = true; this.scene.add(mesh); }
        return prop;
    }
    attachCollider(prop, { shape = 'box', half, center, points, radius, ...opts }) {
        const b3 = this.b3, body = prop.body;
        const c = shape === 'hull' ? b3.createHull(body, points, opts)
            : shape === 'sphere' ? b3.createSphere(body, radius, { center, ...opts })
            : b3.createBox(body, half, { center, ...opts });
        if(!c) return null;
        (prop.colliders ||= prop.collider ? [prop.collider] : []).push(c);
        if(!prop.collider) prop.collider = c;
        this.byCollider.set(c.handle, prop);
        return c;
    }
    detachColliders(prop) {
        for(const c of prop.colliders || [prop.collider]) { if(!c) continue; this.byCollider.delete(c.handle); this.b3.removeCollider(c, false); }
        prop.colliders = []; prop.collider = null;
    }
    onHit(fn) { this.hitListeners.push(fn); }
    /** fn(dt) once per frame at the end of step() (after the fixed substeps), for budgeted background work. */
    onFrame(fn) { this.frameListeners.push(fn); }
    /** True while a chunk's new terrain collider is waiting to be built. */
    colliderPending(key) { return this.pendingColliders.has(key); }
    /** Build pending terrain colliders now (e.g. after loading, before play starts). */
    flushColliders() { for(const [k, g] of this.pendingColliders) this.setChunkCollider(k, g); this.pendingColliders.clear(); }

    removeBody(prop) {
        if(!this.props.delete(prop)) return;
        for(const c of prop.colliders || []) if(c) this.byCollider.delete(c.handle);
        if(prop.collider) this.byCollider.delete(prop.collider.handle);
        this.b3.removeBody(prop.body);
        if(prop.mesh) prop.mesh.removeFromParent();
        prop.removed = true;
    }
    propOf(collider) { return collider ? this.byCollider.get(collider.handle) || null : null; }

    /** Bodies for a query's ignore list from collider handles, colliders, bodies or props. */
    ignoreBodies(list, out = []) {
        out.length = 0;
        if(list) for(const x of list) {
            const c = typeof x === 'number' ? this.b3.colliders.get(x) : x;
            const b = c?.body ?? c;
            if(b && b.handle !== undefined && !out.includes(b)) out.push(b);
        }
        return out;
    }
    raycast(origin, dir, maxDist = 100, { exclude, ignore } = {}) {
        const ign = this.ignoreBodies(ignore, this._ign ??= []);
        if(exclude) { const b = exclude.body ?? exclude; if(b?.handle !== undefined && !ign.includes(b)) ign.push(b); }
        const hit = this.b3.castRay(origin, dir, maxDist, { ignore: ign });
        if(!hit) return null;
        return { point: hit.point, normal: hit.normal, distance: hit.distance, collider: hit.collider, prop: this.propOf(hit.collider) };
    }

    explode(center, radius, strength) {
        for(const p of this.props) {
            if(!p.body.isDynamic()) continue;
            if(p.userData?.voxelBody) { p.userData.voxelBody.explode(center, radius, strength); continue; }
            const t = p.body.translation();
            const dx = t.x - center[0], dy = t.y - center[1], dz = t.z - center[2];
            const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy, dz);
            if(d > radius) continue;
            const k = strength * (1 - d / radius) / Math.max(d, 0.3);
            const v = p.body.linvel();
            p.body.setLinvel({ x: v.x + dx * k, y: v.y + dy * k + strength * 0.3 * (1 - d / radius), z: v.z + dz * k }, true);
            const a = p.body.angvel();
            p.body.setAngvel({ x: a.x + (Math.random() - 0.5) * strength * 0.5, y: a.y + (Math.random() - 0.5) * strength * 0.5, z: a.z + (Math.random() - 0.5) * strength * 0.5 }, true);
        }
    }

    onStep(fn) { this.stepListeners.push(fn); }

    step(dt) {
        const t0 = performance.now();
        this.acc = Math.min(this.acc + dt, STEP * 4);
        // the first step builds every collider queued while loading; later ones go oldest first, at least one per frame
        if(!this.started) { this.started = true; this.flushColliders(); }
        if(this.pendingColliders.size) {
            const end = t0 + this.colliderBudgetMs;
            for(const [k, g] of this.pendingColliders) {
                this.pendingColliders.delete(k);
                this.setChunkCollider(k, g, end);
                if(performance.now() > end) break;
            }
        }
        const interp = this.interpolate, b3 = this.b3;
        while(this.acc >= STEP) {
            if(interp) {
                const xf = b3.xf();
                for(const p of this.props) {
                    if(!p.mesh) continue;
                    const r = p.body.row, q = p._prev || (p._prev = new Float64Array(7));
                    for(let i = 0; i < 7; i++) q[i] = xf[r + i];
                }
            }
            const hits = this.hits; hits.length = 0;
            b3.step(STEP, this.hitListeners.length ? hits : null);
            for(const h of hits) for(const fn of this.hitListeners) try { fn(h.a, h.b, h.speed, h.point, h.normal); } catch(err) { console.error(err); }
            for(const fn of this.stepListeners) fn(STEP);
            this.acc -= STEP;
        }
        for(const fn of this.frameListeners) fn(dt);
        // meshes follow their bodies, interpolated between the last two fixed steps (smooth at any frame rate)
        const a = this.acc / STEP, xf = b3.xf();
        for(const p of this.props) {
            if(!p.mesh) continue;
            const r = p.body.row, q = p._prev;
            const tx = xf[r], ty = xf[r + 1], tz = xf[r + 2], rx = xf[r + 3], ry = xf[r + 4], rz = xf[r + 5], rw = xf[r + 6];
            if(!interp || !q) { p.mesh.position.set(tx, ty, tz); p.mesh.quaternion.set(rx, ry, rz, rw); continue; }
            const b = 1 - a;
            p.mesh.position.set(q[0] * b + tx * a, q[1] * b + ty * a, q[2] * b + tz * a);
            const s = q[3] * rx + q[4] * ry + q[5] * rz + q[6] * rw < 0 ? -a : a; // shortest arc
            const x = q[3] * b + rx * s, y = q[4] * b + ry * s, z = q[5] * b + rz * s, w = q[6] * b + rw * s;
            const l = 1 / (Math.hypot(x, y, z, w) || 1);
            p.mesh.quaternion.set(x * l, y * l, z * l, w * l);
        }
        const ms = performance.now() - t0;
        this.stepMs = this.stepMs * 0.95 + ms * 0.05; // smoothed cost of physics.step per frame (ms)
        this.stepMsMax = Math.max(this.stepMsMax * 0.995, ms);
    }
}
```

### src/physics/VoxelBodies.js
```
/**
 * Detached terrain as rigid bodies: connectivity after edits, fracture on impact, carving bodies, ripping lumps, and
 * settling rubble back into the terrain. Owned by Destruction (ctx.destruction.bodies, also physics.voxelBodies).
 *
 *   bodies.queueConnectivity(lo, hi, brush?, kick?)   connectivity after a terrain edit, run in the background:
 *        unsupported regions become bodies over the next frames ('detached' events); kick { center, radius,
 *        strength } is the blast's push, applied to them when they appear
 *   bodies.detachAround(lo, hi, brush?) -> { bodies, removed, volume, ms }   the same, synchronously (tests, tools)
 *   bodies.carve(brush, { hardness }) -> { removed, volume, done }                 apply a carve to overlapping bodies
 *        (with the terrain worker the grids are carved there: removed / volume arrive with `done`, a Promise of
 *        { removed, volume }, or null when no body was touched)
 *   bodies.rip(brush) -> VoxelBody | null                                          tear a lump out of the world
 *   bodies.fracture(vb, point, dv)                                                 break a body (Voronoi pieces)
 *   bodies.crack(vb, split)                                                        break a body along its soft
 *        lattice's broken faces (bodies.soft: src/softbody/SoftBodies.js calls it)
 *   bodies.list                                                                    the live VoxelBody set
 *   bodies.budgetMs, maxBodies, breakScale, erode, impactDv                        tuning
 *
 * Frame budget: connectivity (a resumable flood fill), detaching, fracturing and stamping run as jobs under
 * `budgetMs` per frame (physics.onFrame), so a blast never costs a frame spike beyond the edit itself. A job whose
 * box a newer edit touches restarts merged with it.
 *
 * Lifecycle of a body:
 *   - impacts: box3d hit events (approach speed), the fastest per body per step, capped by the body's measured velocity
 *     change, give dv.
 *     dv > impactDv emits 'impact' { point, speed, mat, prop }; dv over the body's break threshold (scaled by
 *     material hardness, falling with mass, rising with each generation of fracture) splits it into Voronoi pieces
 *     seeded densest around the hit. Pieces under DEBRIS samples are pulverized: 'destroyed' { kind: 'impact' }.
 *   - soft layer (bodies.soft): impacts and blasts also kick the body's tet lattice, so it bends and jiggles. A hit
 *     over the break threshold but under SHATTER (8) times it cracks the lattice instead of the Voronoi split: faces
 *     break where it bent most and the body splits along them (crack()); if no crack forms within CRACK_WAIT s, a hit
 *     over CRACK_FALLBACK times the threshold cleaves it into 2-3 Voronoi pieces. Only harder hits shatter.
 *   - structural collapse: pieces the terrain worker cut off a failing overhang (src/voxel/structure.js) start
 *     turning about the broken root, their soft layer sagging (_hinge).
 *   - settling: a body asleep (or barely moving) for SETTLE seconds is stamped back into the VoxelWorld at its pose
 *     and removed once the terrain under it has been remeshed: rubble becomes diggable terrain again.
 *   - budget: over maxBodies (48), the stillest bodies are stamped early and small moving ones are pulverized; near
 *     the cap fractures make fewer pieces and more dust.
 *   - bodies leaving the world are removed; bodies the game removes (physics.removeBody) are forgotten.
 *   - a kinematic body (held by a player) or one with userData.held never settles or fractures; one with
 *     userData.thrown (a thrown rock) only cracks along its soft lattice, it never shatters.
 *
 * Net (ctx.net, src/net): every body has `netId` (the same on every client: `${seq}:${island}` for a piece of terrain
 * that came loose in edit `seq`, `${seq}:r${i}` for a ripped lump, `${parent}.f${i}` / `.c${i}` for fracture and
 * component pieces) and `owner` (the client that decides about it: the host for what the terrain shed, the ripper
 * for a lump). Body motion differs between clients (bodies are not stepped in lockstep), so only the owner decides
 * what depends on it: settling (a 'stamp' edit { id, grid, t, q } in the log: everyone stamps the owner's grid at the owner's pose; other clients
 * drop their copy of that body) and fracture (a transient 'bsplit' { id, p (body-local), dv, n, minCount, seed,
 * cells } that the others replay on their copy, seeded alike). Other clients keep simulating their copies until then
 * (transform streaming comes later) and drop a copy that has been still for NONOWNER_REST s.
 */
import { VOXEL, CHUNK } from '../config.js';
import { MATERIALS } from '../voxel/materials.js';
import { VoxelWorld } from '../voxel/VoxelWorld.js';
import { sphere, box, rock, specOf, rng } from '../voxel/brushes.js';
import { stampGrid, findIslands, islandsJob, detachIsland, splitGrid, voronoiLabels, carveGrid, stampJob, liftGrid, gridStats, gridBoxes, gridHullPoints, newGrid, SAMPLE_VOLUME } from '../voxel/islands.js';
import { PAD } from '../config.js';
import { VoxelBody } from '../voxel/VoxelBody.js';
import { GRAVITY, BodyType } from './Physics.js';
import { createTerrainMaterial } from '../render/terrainMaterial.js';
import { SoftBodies } from '../softbody/SoftBodies.js';
import { cellSampleLabels } from '../softbody/lattice.js';

/** Rotate vector v by quaternion q = [x,y,z,w]. */
function rotate(q, v) {
    const [x, y, z, w] = q;
    const ix = w * v[0] + y * v[2] - z * v[1], iy = w * v[1] + z * v[0] - x * v[2], iz = w * v[2] + x * v[1] - y * v[0], iw = -x * v[0] - y * v[1] - z * v[2];
    return [ix * w + iw * -x + iy * -z - iz * -y, iy * w + iw * -y + iz * -x - ix * -z, iz * w + iw * -z + ix * -y - iy * -x];
}

/** Islands and pieces with fewer solid samples than this are dust, not bodies (0.25 m³). */
export const DEBRIS = 16;
const MAX_BODIES = 48;
const SETTLE = 2;
/** A budgeted job waiting for the terrain worker's answer. */
const WAIT = 'wait';
/** Bodies smaller than this never fracture (≈ 1.9 m³). */
const MIN_FRACTURE = 120;
/** Hits up to this many times the break threshold crack the soft lattice (a few big pieces along where it bent);
 *  only harder ones shatter (Voronoi). Thrown lumps and falling spans stay physics objects and break apart. */
const SHATTER = 8;
/** A crack attempt that formed no crack within CRACK_WAIT s: hits over CRACK_FALLBACK times the threshold cleave
 *  the body into a few pieces anyway (Voronoi, CLEAVE pieces at most, a gentle push apart). */
const CRACK_WAIT = 0.4, CRACK_FALLBACK = 1.8, CLEAVE = 3;
/** Velocity changes (m/s) under this do not shake the soft layer. */
const SOFT_DV = 1.5;
/** A body this client does not own that has been still this long is dropped (its owner stamps or dusts it). */
const NONOWNER_REST = 20;
/** A number from a body's netId (seeds its fracture alike on every client). */
const seedOf = (id) => { let h = 0x811c9dc5; for(let i = 0; i < id.length; i++) { h ^= id.charCodeAt(i); h = Math.imul(h, 0x01000193); } return h >>> 0; };

export class VoxelBodies {
    constructor(ctx) {
        this.ctx = ctx;
        this.world = ctx.world;
        this.physics = ctx.physics;
        this.scene = ctx.scene;
        this.events = ctx.events;
        this.pool = ctx.terrain.pool;
        this.material = createTerrainMaterial({ local: true });
        this.list = new Set();
        this.ghosts = [];          // meshes of broken bodies shown until their pieces are meshed
        this.hits = new Map();     // VoxelBody -> { f, fmax, c, o } this step
        this.impactDv = 3;         // m/s: weaker contacts are not reported
        this.maxBodies = MAX_BODIES;
        this.breakScale = 1;       // tuning knob for fracture thresholds
        this.erode = 48;           // stored SDF units (32 per voxel): necks thinner than ~2x this break after an edit (-1: off)
        this.budgetMs = 1.2;       // background work per frame (connectivity, detach, fracture, stamping)
        this.jobs = [];            // FIFO: { kind: 'conn' | 'stamp', ... }
        this.breaks = new Map();   // VoxelBody -> [point, dv]: fractures waiting for the next frame's budget
        this.stats = { detached: 0, fractured: 0, stamped: 0, pulverized: 0, lastIslandsMs: 0, lastFractureMs: 0, frameMs: 0, frameMsMax: 0, jobs: 0 };
        this.physics.voxelBodies = this;
        this.net = ctx.net || null;
        if(this.net) {
            this.net.on('stamp', (e, m) => this._applyStamp(e, m));
            this.net.on('bsplit', (e, m) => this._applySplit(e, m));
        }
        this.soft = new SoftBodies({ material: this.material, params: ctx.params });
        this.soft.onSplit = (vb, split) => this.crack(vb, split);
        // later generations are tougher (as breakDv); pieces too small to fracture only wobble
        this.soft.toughOf = (vb) => vb.grid.count >= MIN_FRACTURE ? 1 + 0.4 * vb.generation : 0;
        this.physics.onHit((c1, c2, speed, point) => this._contact(c1, c2, speed, point));
        this.physics.onStep((h) => this._step(h));
        this.physics.onFrame(() => this._frame());
        const w = this.world;
        this.bounds = { min: w.smin.map(v => v * VOXEL - 8), max: w.smax.map(v => v * VOXEL + 8) };
        // Warm the JIT up on a scratch world: every code path of an edit, a detach, a body, a fracture and a stamp
        // runs once now, so the first blast in play does not pay several times its warm cost.
        const t0 = performance.now();
        this._warmup();
        this.stats.warmupMs = performance.now() - t0;
    }

    _warmup() {
        const w = new VoxelWorld({ min: [0, 0, 0], max: [2, 2, 2] }); // 16 m, its own chunks
        for(let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
            w.applyBrush(sphere([8, 9, 8], 3.2), { op: 'add', mat: 1 });
            w.applyBrush(box([2, 0, 2], [5, 6, 5]), { op: 'add', mat: 8 });
            w.applyBrush(rock([8, 9, 8], 1.4, 1 + k), { op: 'carve' });
            w.applyBrush(rock([3.5, 1.5, 3.5], 1.2, 1 + k), { op: 'paint', mat: 5 });
            const res = findIslands(w, [0, 0, 0], [63, 63, 63], { erode: Math.max(0, this.erode), near: sphere([8, 9, 8], 4) });
            for(const isl of res.islands) {
                const { grid } = detachIsland(w, isl);
                if(!grid || grid.count < DEBRIS) continue;
                stampGrid(w, grid, [2, 3, 2], [0, Math.sin(0.2), 0, Math.cos(0.2)]);
                // a body far below the level, broken up and thrown away
                const vb = this.add(grid, { origin: [0, -900 - k * 50, 0] });
                const dust = new Float64Array(256);
                carveGrid(vb.grid, sphere([1, 1, 1], 1));
                const pieces = splitGrid(vb.grid, voronoiLabels(vb.grid, [1, 1, 1], 5), { minCount: DEBRIS, dust });
                const { bodies } = this.replace(vb, pieces, { point: [0, -900, 0], spread: 2, generation: 1 });
                for(const b of bodies) { b.dispose(); this.list.delete(b); }
            }
        }
        for(const g of this.ghosts) g.vb.disposeMesh();
        this.ghosts.length = 0;
    }

    get count() { return this.list.size; }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ net: ownership, stamps and splits as edits

    /** Does this client decide about `vb` (settling, fracture)? Always in single player. */
    isOwner(vb) { const n = this.net; return !n || n.single || vb.owner == null || vb.owner === n.id; }
    get multi() { return !!this.net && !this.net.single; }
    byId(id) { for(const vb of this.list) if(vb.netId === id && !vb.dead) return vb; return null; }
    /** A 'stamp' edit in sequence order: union the owner's grid at the owner's pose; others drop their copy. */
    _applyStamp(e, { local }) {
        const p = this.world.remote.stamp(e.grid, e.t, e.q);
        if(!local && e.id) {
            for(const vb of [...this.list]) {
                const id = vb.netId;
                if(!id || this.isOwner(vb) || vb.stamping) continue;
                if(id === e.id || e.id.startsWith(id + '.') || id.startsWith(e.id + '.')) { vb.dispose(); this.list.delete(vb); }
            }
        }
        return p;
    }
    /** Another client's fracture of a body: replay it on this client's copy (same sites, same piece ids). */
    _applySplit(e, { local, later = false }) {
        if(local) return;
        const st = this.stats.net ||= { splitsIn: 0, replayed: 0, splitsLost: 0 };
        if(!later) st.splitsIn++;
        const vb = this.byId(e.id);
        // a piece this client has not made yet (its parent's split is still being replayed): keep it a while
        if(!vb || vb.fracturing) { (this.laterSplits ||= new Map()).set(e.id, { e, t: 0 }); return; }
        if(this.isOwner(vb) || vb.stamping) return;
        st.replayed++;
        this.breaks.set(vb, [vb.toWorld(e.p), e.dv, e.cells || null, 0, { n: e.n, minCount: e.minCount, seed: e.seed }]);
    }
    _laterSplits(dt) {
        for(const [id, w] of this.laterSplits) {
            const vb = this.byId(id);
            if(vb && !vb.fracturing) { this.laterSplits.delete(id); this._applySplit(w.e, { local: false, later: true }); }
            else if((w.t += dt) > 5) { this.laterSplits.delete(id); this.stats.net.splitsLost++; }
        }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ creation

    add(grid, opts) {
        const vb = new VoxelBody(this, grid, opts);
        this.list.add(vb);
        return vb;
    }

    /** Queue connectivity after a terrain edit inside the sample box [lo, hi] (see the header). */
    queueConnectivity(lo, hi, brush = null, kick = null) {
        const job = { kind: 'conn', lo: [...lo], hi: [...hi], brush, kicks: kick ? [kick] : [], gen: null, islands: null, i: 0 };
        const m = 8;
        for(let k = this.jobs.length - 1; k >= 0; k--) {
            const j = this.jobs[k];
            if(j.kind !== 'conn') continue;
            if(j.lo[0] > job.hi[0] + m || j.lo[1] > job.hi[1] + m || j.lo[2] > job.hi[2] + m || j.hi[0] < job.lo[0] - m || j.hi[1] < job.lo[1] - m || j.hi[2] < job.lo[2] - m) continue;
            // overlapping: abort that one and redo both boxes together
            if(j.gen) j.gen.return();
            for(let a = 0; a < 3; a++) { job.lo[a] = Math.min(job.lo[a], j.lo[a]); job.hi[a] = Math.max(job.hi[a], j.hi[a]); }
            const b1 = job.brush, b2 = j.brush;
            job.brush = b1 && b2 ? { min: b1.min.map((v, a) => Math.min(v, b2.min[a])), max: b1.max.map((v, a) => Math.max(v, b2.max[a])), d: (x, y, z) => Math.min(b1.d(x, y, z), b2.d(x, y, z)) } : null;
            job.kicks.push(...j.kicks);
            this.jobs.splice(k, 1);
        }
        this.jobs.push(job);
        return job;
    }

    /** Connectivity after a terrain edit inside the sample box [lo, hi], synchronously. */
    detachAround(lo, hi, brush = null) {
        for(const j of this.jobs) if(j.kind === 'conn' && j.gen) { j.gen.return(); j.gen = null; j.islands = null; } // they share scratch state
        const removed = new Float64Array(256);
        let volume = 0;
        const out = [];
        const res = findIslands(this.world, lo, hi, { erode: Math.max(0, this.erode), near: brush });
        const ms = res.ms;
        this._detach(res.islands, removed, out, (v) => { volume += v; });
        this.stats.lastIslandsMs = ms;
        return { bodies: out, removed, volume, ms };
    }
    _detach(islands, removed, out, addVolume) {
        for(const isl of islands) {
            const small = isl.count < DEBRIS;
            const d = detachIsland(this.world, isl, { keepGrid: !small });
            if(small) { for(let i = 0; i < 256; i++) removed[i] += d.removed[i]; addVolume(isl.count * SAMPLE_VOLUME); continue; }
            const g = d.grid;
            const vb = this.add(g, { origin: [g.offset[0] * VOXEL, g.offset[1] * VOXEL, g.offset[2] * VOXEL], waitKeys: [...d.dirty] });
            vb.body.wakeUp();
            this.stats.detached++;
            out.push(vb);
            this.events.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('detached', { detail: { prop: vb.prop, volume: vb.volume, mat: vb.mat } }));
        }
    }
    /** Bodies and dust from a terrain worker reply (TerrainCore 'brush' with conn): islands cut out of the world. */
    fromWorker(rep, kick = null, { seq = 0, from = null, local = true, lift = false } = {}) {
        const kicks = kick ? [kick] : [];
        if(rep.islandsMs !== undefined) this.stats.lastIslandsMs = rep.islandsMs;
        for(const d of rep.dust || []) this._dust(d.center, d.removed, 'carve');
        // what the terrain shed belongs to the host
        const owner = this.net ? this.net.hostId ?? this.net.id : null;
        // one body per budgeted unit (a frame each when several arrive): a body's first contacts cost a physics step
        (rep.islands || []).forEach((isl, i) => this.jobs.push({ kind: 'island', isl, kicks, id: `${seq}:${i}`, owner }));
        // another client's rip: the same lump, from the worker's lift (the ripper made its own from its replica)
        if(lift && !local && rep.lift) this._liftBodies(rep.lift, `${seq}:r`, from);
    }
    /** Bodies for a lifted grid (a rip): its connected pieces, the largest first; crumbs are skipped. */
    _liftBodies(g, id, owner, userData = {}, waitKeys = null) {
        const pieces = splitGrid(g).filter(p => p.count > 0);
        pieces.sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count);
        const made = [];
        pieces.forEach((p, i) => {
            if(p.count < DEBRIS && made.length) return;
            const o = [(g.offset[0] + p.offset[0]) * VOXEL, (g.offset[1] + p.offset[1]) * VOXEL, (g.offset[2] + p.offset[2]) * VOXEL];
            const vb = this.add(p, { origin: o, waitKeys, userData: made.length ? {} : userData });
            vb.netId = `${id}${i}`; vb.owner = owner;
            made.push(vb);
        });
        return made;
    }
    /** A body for an island the terrain worker cut out (dust when far over the body cap). */
    _runIsland(j) {
        if(this.islandFrame === this.frameNo) return WAIT;
        const isl = j.isl;
        if(this.list.size >= this.maxBodies * 2) this._dust(isl.grid.offset.map(v => v * VOXEL), isl.grid.prep.stats.volumes, 'carve');
        else this._bodyFromIsland(isl, j.kicks, j.id, j.owner);
        this.islandFrame = this.frameNo; // one new body per frame
        return true;
    }
    /** Detach one island found by a background job: a body, or dust if tiny. */
    _detachOne(isl, kicks) { const d = this._cutIsland(isl); if(d) this._bodyFromIsland(d, kicks); }
    /** Remove an island from the world; tiny ones are dust (returns null), others return { grid, dirty }. */
    _cutIsland(isl) {
        const small = isl.count < DEBRIS;
        const d = detachIsland(this.world, isl, { keepGrid: !small });
        if(!d.count) return null;
        if(small || !d.grid.count) { const c = isl.min.map((v, a) => (v + isl.max[a]) * 0.5 * VOXEL); this._dust(c, d.removed, 'carve'); return null; }
        return d;
    }
    _bodyFromIsland(d, kicks, id = null, owner = null) {
        const g = d.grid;
        const vb = this.add(g, { origin: [g.offset[0] * VOXEL, g.offset[1] * VOXEL, g.offset[2] * VOXEL], waitKeys: [...d.dirty], prep: g.prep || null });
        vb.netId = id; vb.owner = owner;
        vb.body.wakeUp();
        this.stats.detached++;
        if(d.collapse) this._hinge(vb, d.collapse);
        for(const k of kicks) vb.explode(k.center, k.radius, k.strength);
        this.events.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('detached', { detail: { prop: vb.prop, volume: vb.volume, mat: vb.mat, collapse: d.collapse?.reason ?? null } }));
    }
    /** A structural failure (src/voxel/structure.js) snapped this piece off at `root`: it starts turning about the
     *  broken root (the free end drops first) and its soft layer sags (the far end lags the root's give). */
    _hinge(vb, { root, dir }) {
        this.stats.collapsed = (this.stats.collapsed || 0) + 1;
        const b = vb.body, com = b.worldCom();
        const h = Math.hypot(dir[0], dir[2]);
        if(h > 0.1) {
            const lever = Math.max(0.5, Math.hypot(com.x - root[0], com.z - root[2]));
            const w = Math.min(1.2, 2.2 / Math.sqrt(lever)); // rad/s: a long span tips slower
            const ax = dir[2] / h * w, az = -dir[0] / h * w;   // up x dir: the free end goes down
            const rx = com.x - root[0], ry = com.y - root[1], rz = com.z - root[2];
            b.setAngvel({ x: ax, y: 0, z: az }, true);
            b.setLinvel({ x: -az * ry, y: az * rx - ax * rz, z: ax * ry }, true); // w x r: the root stays put
        }
        this.soft.impact(vb, root, [0, 3.5, 0], 1);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ per frame: budgeted jobs

    _frame() {
        const t0 = performance.now(), end = t0 + this.budgetMs;
        this.soft.frame(Math.min(0.1, (t0 - (this.lastFrame ?? t0)) / 1000));
        this.lastFrame = t0;
        this.frameNo = (this.frameNo || 0) + 1;
        if(this.laterSplits?.size) this._laterSplits(Math.min(0.1, (t0 - (this.lastSplitT ?? t0)) / 1000) || 1 / 60);
        this.lastSplitT = t0;
        // fractures go first (staged jobs: pieces, then one disabled body per unit, then an atomic swap)
        for(const [vb, [point, dv, cells, few, forced]] of this.breaks) {
            this.breaks.delete(vb);
            if(vb.dead || vb.fracturing || vb.stamping || !this.list.has(vb) || !vb.body.isDynamic() || (cells && vb.prop.userData.held)) { if(cells) this.soft.gridChanged(vb); continue; }
            vb.fracturing = true;
