            // how many pieces and the smallest kept: decided here (by the owner) and sent with the split
            const energy = Math.min(4, dv / this.breakDv(vb));
            const crowd = Math.max(0.35, Math.min(1, 1.3 - this.pressure()));
            const n = forced ? forced.n : few || Math.max(2, Math.min(14, Math.round((1 + energy * 2 + Math.log2(1 + vb.volume)) * crowd)));
            const minCount = forced ? forced.minCount : this.debrisMin();
            const seed = forced ? forced.seed : vb.netId ? seedOf(vb.netId) : null;
            this.jobs.unshift({ kind: 'frac', vb, point, dv, cells, few: few || 0, n, minCount, seed, pieces: null, kids: [], i: 0, dust: new Float64Array(256) });
            if(!forced && this.multi && vb.netId) {
                const c = cells ? { labels: cells.labels, cellOf: cells.cellOf, cs: cells.cs, dims: cells.dims } : null;
                this.net.submit({ k: 'bsplit', id: vb.netId, p: vb.toLocal(point), dv, n, minCount, seed, cells: c }, { log: false });
            }
        }
        // jobs run in order; one waiting for the terrain worker (WAIT) lets the next ones run meanwhile
        let first = true, k = 0;
        while(k < this.jobs.length && (first || performance.now() < end)) {
            first = false;
            const j = this.jobs[k];
            const done = j.kind === 'conn' ? this._runConn(j, end) : j.kind === 'stamp' ? this._runStamp(j, end) : j.kind === 'frac' ? this._runFracture(j) : j.kind === 'island' ? this._runIsland(j) : this._rebody(j.vb);
            if(done === WAIT) { k++; first = true; continue; }
            if(done) this.jobs.splice(this.jobs.indexOf(j), 1);
        }
        const ms = performance.now() - t0;
        this.stats.jobs = this.jobs.length;
        this.stats.frameMs = this.stats.frameMs * 0.95 + ms * 0.05;
        this.stats.frameMsMax = Math.max(this.stats.frameMsMax * 0.995, ms);
    }
    _runConn(j, end) {
        if(!j.islands) {
            if(!j.gen) { j.gen = islandsJob(this.world, j.lo, j.hi, { erode: Math.max(0, this.erode), near: j.brush }, 2048); j.ms = 0; }
            const t = performance.now();
            let r;
            do r = j.gen.next(); while(!r.done && performance.now() < end);
            j.ms += performance.now() - t;
            if(!r.done) return false;
            j.gen = null;
            j.islands = r.value.islands;
            this.stats.lastIslandsMs = j.ms;
            return !j.islands.length;
        }
        // one unit per call (the frame loop calls again while there is time): cut an island out of the world, then
        // make its body
        if(j.pending) { this._bodyFromIsland(j.pending, j.kicks); j.pending = null; return j.i >= j.islands.length; }
        j.pending = this._cutIsland(j.islands[j.i++]);
        return !j.pending && j.i >= j.islands.length;
    }
    _runFracture(j) {
        const vb = j.vb;
        if(vb.dead || !this.list.has(vb) || vb.stamping || !vb.body.isDynamic()) {
            for(const k of j.kids) k.kid.dispose();
            vb.fracturing = false;
            if(j.cells && !vb.dead) this.soft.gridChanged(vb); // the lattice stopped at the split: start over
            return true;
        }
        const t0 = performance.now();
        const remote = this.world.remote;
        if(!j.pieces && remote) {
            // the Voronoi split runs in the terrain worker; pieces come back with their stats and collider boxes
            if(j.asked) return WAIT;
            j.asked = true;
            const g = vb.grid;
            const c = j.cells, cells = c ? { labels: c.labels, cellOf: c.cellOf, cs: c.cs, dims: c.dims } : null;
            remote.split(g, { point: vb.toLocal(j.point), n: j.n, cells, minCount: j.minCount, generation: vb.generation + 1, seed: j.seed }).then(rep => {
                j.pieces = rep.same ? [g] : rep.pieces;
                j.dust.set(rep.dust);
            }, () => { j.pieces = []; });
            j.sibling = 1 << (2 + (this.siblingNext = ((this.siblingNext || 0) + 1) % 12));
            j.held = { ...vb.prop.userData }; delete j.held.voxelBody; delete j.held.ripped;
            j.ms = performance.now() - t0;
            return WAIT;
        }
        if(!j.pieces) {
            const g = vb.grid;
            if(this.debug) console.log(`fracture n=${g.count} gen=${vb.generation} age=${vb.age.toFixed(2)} dv=${j.dv.toFixed(1)} break=${this.breakDv(vb).toFixed(1)} mass=${(vb.mass / 1000).toFixed(1)}t`);
            const c = j.cells;
            j.pieces = splitGrid(g, c ? cellSampleLabels(g, c.labels, c.cellOf, c.cs, c.dims) : voronoiLabels(g, vb.toLocal(j.point), j.n, j.seed != null ? rng(j.seed) : Math.random), { minCount: j.minCount, dust: j.dust });
            j.sibling = 1 << (2 + (this.siblingNext = ((this.siblingNext || 0) + 1) % 12));
            j.held = { ...vb.prop.userData }; delete j.held.voxelBody; delete j.held.ripped;
            j.ms = performance.now() - t0;
            return false;
        }
        if(j.i < j.pieces.length) {
            // one piece per unit, parked (disabled, hidden) at the parent's current pose
            const p = j.pieces[j.i++];
            const kid = new VoxelBody(this, p, { origin: vb.toWorld([p.offset[0] * VOXEL, p.offset[1] * VOXEL, p.offset[2] * VOXEL]), rotation: vb.rotation(), generation: vb.generation + 1, userData: { ...j.held }, waitKeys: vb.waitKeys, sibling: j.sibling, enabled: false, prep: p.prep || null });
            kid.netId = vb.netId ? `${vb.netId}.f${j.i - 1}` : null; kid.owner = vb.owner;
            j.kids.push({ kid, p });
            j.ms += performance.now() - t0;
            return false;
        }
        // swap: the pieces take over the parent's motion where it is now, plus a spread away from the hit (a crack:
        // a nudge apart; the pieces keep the parent's wobble)
        const b = vb.body, v = b.linvel(), w = b.angvel(), com = b.worldCom(), rot = vb.rotation();
        const spread = j.cells ? 0.8 : j.few ? 1.2 : Math.min(8, 1.5 + j.dv * 0.15), point = j.cells ? [com.x, com.y, com.z] : j.point;
        for(const { kid, p } of j.kids) this.soft.inherit(kid, vb, [p.offset[0] * VOXEL, p.offset[1] * VOXEL, p.offset[2] * VOXEL]);
        for(const { kid, p } of j.kids) {
            const o = vb.toWorld([p.offset[0] * VOXEL, p.offset[1] * VOXEL, p.offset[2] * VOXEL]);
            const rc = rotate(rot, kid.stats.com), c = [o[0] + rc[0], o[1] + rc[1], o[2] + rc[2]];
            const rx = c[0] - com.x, ry = c[1] - com.y, rz = c[2] - com.z;
            const lv = [v.x + w.y * rz - w.z * ry, v.y + w.z * rx - w.x * rz, v.z + w.x * ry - w.y * rx];
            const av = [w.x, w.y, w.z];
            let dx = c[0] - point[0], dy = c[1] - point[1], dz = c[2] - point[2];
            const l = Math.hypot(dx, dy, dz) || 1, k = spread * (0.5 + Math.random() * 0.7) / l;
            lv[0] += dx * k; lv[1] += dy * k + spread * 0.25; lv[2] += dz * k;
            for(let a = 0; a < 3; a++) av[a] += (Math.random() - 0.5) * spread * 0.6;
            kid.activate({ origin: o, rotation: rot, linvel: lv, angvel: av });
            this.list.add(kid);
        }
        vb.dispose({ keepMesh: true });
        this.list.delete(vb);
        const ghost = { vb, t: 0 };
        this.ghosts.push(ghost);
        Promise.all(j.kids.map(k => k.kid.meshed)).then(() => { ghost.done = true; });
        this.stats.fractured++;
        this.stats.lastFractureMs = j.ms + performance.now() - t0; // total over its frames
        if(j.cells) j.dust[vb.mat] += 0.1; // crumbs along the crack
        this._dust(j.point, j.dust, 'impact', vb);
        return true;
    }
    _runStamp(j, end) {
        const vb = j.vb;
        if(vb.dead) return true;
        let r;
        do r = j.gen.next(); while(!r.done && performance.now() < end);
        if(!r.done) return false;
        vb.stampKeys = [...r.value.dirty];
        vb.stampT = 0;
        return true;
    }

    /** Tear the world's matter inside `brush` out as one body (the largest connected piece; the rest becomes rubble). */
    rip(brush, userData = {}) {
        const g = liftGrid(this.world, brush);
        if(g.count < DEBRIS) return null;
        const remote = this.world.remote;
        const r = remote ? null : this.world.applyBrush(brush, { op: 'carve', hardness: false });
        // with the terrain worker the world is cut a frame or two later (the lump ignores the terrain until then)
        const waitKeys = r ? [...r.dirty] : [...this.world.chunksInSampleBox(brush.min.map(v => Math.floor(v / VOXEL) - 4 - PAD - 1), brush.max.map(v => Math.ceil(v / VOXEL) + 4 + PAD + 1))];
        // the ripper's own lump, at once (from its replica); the other clients make theirs from the worker's lift
        const made = this._liftBodies(g, 'pending:r', this.net ? this.net.id : null, { ripped: true, ...userData }, waitKeys);
        const lump = made[0] || null;
        if(remote) {
            for(const vb of made) vb.waitRemote = true;
            const release = () => { for(const vb of made) vb.waitRemote = false; };
            this.ctx.destruction.submit({ k: 'brush', spec: specOf(brush), op: 'carve', mat: 1, hardness: false, conn: true, erode: Math.max(0, this.erode), debris: DEBRIS, lift: true, kind: 'rip', author: userData.author ?? null })
                .then(rep => { release(); for(const vb of made) vb.netId = vb.netId.replace('pending:', `${rep.seq}:`); }, release);
        }
        else if(r.changed) this.queueConnectivity(r.box[0], r.box[1], brush);
        return lump;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ carving

    /** Apply a carving brush (world metres) to every body it overlaps. Returns { removed, volume }. */
    carve(brush, { hardness = true } = {}) {
        const removed = new Float64Array(256);
        let volume = 0;
        const pending = [];
        for(const vb of [...this.list]) {
            if(vb.dead || vb.stamping) continue;
            const [mn, mx] = vb.worldBox();
            if(mn[0] > brush.max[0] || mn[1] > brush.max[1] || mn[2] > brush.max[2] || mx[0] < brush.min[0] || mx[1] < brush.min[1] || mx[2] < brush.min[2]) continue;
            // the brush in the body's grid-local frame
            const lmin = [Infinity, Infinity, Infinity], lmax = [-Infinity, -Infinity, -Infinity];
            for(let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
                const l = vb.toLocal([k & 1 ? brush.max[0] : brush.min[0], k & 2 ? brush.max[1] : brush.min[1], k & 4 ? brush.max[2] : brush.min[2]]);
                for(let a = 0; a < 3; a++) { lmin[a] = Math.min(lmin[a], l[a]); lmax[a] = Math.max(lmax[a], l[a]); }
            }
            const t = vb.body.translation(), q = vb.body.rotation(), f = vb.frame;
            // rotation matrix columns (local -> world) for the brush's distance query
            const { x: qx, y: qy, z: qz, w: qw } = q;
            const m00 = 1 - 2 * (qy * qy + qz * qz), m01 = 2 * (qx * qy - qz * qw), m02 = 2 * (qx * qz + qy * qw);
            const m10 = 2 * (qx * qy + qz * qw), m11 = 1 - 2 * (qx * qx + qz * qz), m12 = 2 * (qy * qz - qx * qw);
            const m20 = 2 * (qx * qz - qy * qw), m21 = 2 * (qy * qz + qx * qw), m22 = 1 - 2 * (qx * qx + qy * qy);
            const remote = this.world.remote;
            if(remote) {
                // carved in the terrain worker; edits of one body are chained so each starts from the last one's grid
                const spec = specOf(brush), tt = [t.x, t.y, t.z], M = [m00, m01, m02, m10, m11, m12, m20, m21, m22], ff = [...f];
                const p = (vb.gridOp || Promise.resolve()).then(() => {
                    if(vb.dead || vb.stamping) return null;
                    return remote.carveGrid(vb.grid, spec, { lmin, lmax, t: tt, m: M, f: ff, hardness }).then(rep => {
                        if(vb.dead || vb.stamping || !rep.changed) return null;
                        vb.grid = rep.grid;
                        this._carved(vb);
                        return rep.removed;
                    });
                }).catch(() => null);
                vb.gridOp = p;
                pending.push(p);
                continue;
            }
            const local = { min: lmin, max: lmax, d: (x, y, z) => {
                const lx = x - f[0], ly = y - f[1], lz = z - f[2];
                return brush.d(t.x + m00 * lx + m01 * ly + m02 * lz, t.y + m10 * lx + m11 * ly + m12 * lz, t.z + m20 * lx + m21 * ly + m22 * lz);
            } };
            const r = carveGrid(vb.grid, local, { hardness });
            if(!r.changed) continue;
            for(let i = 0; i < 256; i++) { removed[i] += r.removed[i]; volume += r.removed[i]; }
            this._carved(vb);
        }
        // with the worker: removed / volume are 0 here, `done` resolves to the full { removed, volume }
        const done = pending.length ? Promise.all(pending).then(list => {
            for(const rm of list) if(rm) for(let i = 0; i < 256; i++) { removed[i] += rm[i]; volume += rm[i]; }
            return { removed, volume };
        }) : null;
        return { removed, volume, done };
    }
    /** A body's grid was carved: remesh at once, the shape (split, colliders) in a budgeted job. */
    _carved(vb) {
        vb.carves = (vb.carves || 0) + 1;
        this.soft.gridChanged(vb);
        vb.remesh();
        vb.body.wakeUp();
        vb.still = 0;
        if(!vb.rebody) { vb.rebody = true; this.jobs.unshift({ kind: 'rebody', vb }); }
    }
    /** After carving: split a body cut in two, or rebuild its colliders. */
    _rebody(vb) {
        const remote = this.world.remote;
        if(remote) return this._rebodyRemote(vb, remote);
        vb.rebody = false;
        if(vb.dead || vb.stamping || !this.list.has(vb)) return true;
        const dust = new Float64Array(256);
        const t = vb.body.translation(); // before replace() removes the body
        const pieces = splitGrid(vb.grid, null, { minCount: DEBRIS, dust });
        if(pieces.length === 1 && pieces[0] === vb.grid) {
            vb.stats = gridStats(vb.grid);
            vb.updateUserData();
            vb.buildColliders();
        } else {
            const r = this.replace(vb, pieces, {});
            for(let i = 0; i < 256; i++) dust[i] += r.removed[i];
        }
        this._dust([t.x, t.y, t.z], dust, 'carve', vb);
        return true;
    }
    /** _rebody with the component split in the terrain worker (a job: WAIT until the answer is in). */
    _rebodyRemote(vb, remote) {
        const j = vb.rebodyJob ||= { asked: false, rep: null, v: 0 };
        if(vb.dead || vb.stamping || !this.list.has(vb)) { vb.rebody = false; vb.rebodyJob = null; return true; }
        if(!j.asked) {
            j.asked = true; j.v = vb.carves || 0;
            remote.split(vb.grid, { minCount: DEBRIS, generation: vb.generation }).then(rep => { j.rep = rep; }, () => { j.rep = { same: true, prep: null, dust: new Float64Array(256) }; });
            return WAIT;
        }
        if(!j.rep) return WAIT;
        vb.rebodyJob = null;
        // carved again meanwhile: split the newer grid instead
        if((vb.carves || 0) !== j.v) { vb.rebodyJob = null; return this._rebodyRemote(vb, remote); }
        vb.rebody = false;
        const rep = j.rep, dust = rep.dust;
        const t = vb.body.translation();
        if(rep.same) {
            vb.stats = rep.prep?.stats || gridStats(vb.grid);
            vb.grid.count = vb.stats.count;
            vb.updateUserData();
            vb.buildColliders(rep.prep && rep.prep.generation === vb.generation ? rep.prep.boxes : null);
        } else {
            const r = this.replace(vb, rep.pieces, {});
            for(let i = 0; i < 256; i++) dust[i] += r.removed[i];
        }
        this._dust([t.x, t.y, t.z], dust, 'carve', vb);
        return true;
    }

    /**
     * Swap a body for pieces of its grid (each with `offset` in the body's grid-local samples). Pieces inherit the
     * body's motion (v + w x r) plus `spread` m/s away from `point`. Tiny pieces are dust. Returns { removed, bodies }.
     */
    replace(vb, pieces, { point = null, spread = 0, generation = vb.generation, tag = 'c' }) {
        const b = vb.body;
        const v = b.linvel(), w = b.angvel(), com = b.worldCom();
        const rot = vb.rotation();
        const removed = new Float64Array(256);
        const held = { ...vb.prop.userData };
        delete held.voxelBody; delete held.ripped;
        const kids = [];
        const sibling = 1 << (2 + (this.siblingNext = ((this.siblingNext || 0) + 1) % 12));
        const pieceData = pieces.map(p => {
            const st = p.prep?.stats || gridStats(p);
            // world origin of the piece's local 0 (same rotation as the parent)
            const o = vb.toWorld([p.offset[0] * VOXEL, p.offset[1] * VOXEL, p.offset[2] * VOXEL]);
            return { p, st, o };
        });
        vb.dispose({ keepMesh: true });
        this.list.delete(vb);
        for(const [idx, { p, st, o }] of pieceData.entries()) {
            if(st.count < this.debrisMin()) { for(let i = 0; i < 256; i++) removed[i] += st.volumes[i]; continue; }
            // centre of mass of the piece in world space
            const rc = rotate(rot, st.com), c = [o[0] + rc[0], o[1] + rc[1], o[2] + rc[2]];
            const rx = c[0] - com.x, ry = c[1] - com.y, rz = c[2] - com.z;
            const lv = [v.x + w.y * rz - w.z * ry, v.y + w.z * rx - w.x * rz, v.z + w.x * ry - w.y * rx];
            const av = [w.x, w.y, w.z];
            if(point && spread) {
                let dx = c[0] - point[0], dy = c[1] - point[1], dz = c[2] - point[2];
                const l = Math.hypot(dx, dy, dz) || 1;
                const k = spread * (0.5 + Math.random() * 0.7) / l;
                lv[0] += dx * k; lv[1] += dy * k + spread * 0.25; lv[2] += dz * k;
                for(let a = 0; a < 3; a++) av[a] += (Math.random() - 0.5) * spread * 0.6;
            }
            const kid = this.add(p, { origin: o, rotation: rot, linvel: lv, angvel: av, generation, userData: { ...held }, waitKeys: vb.waitKeys, sibling: pieces.length > 1 ? sibling : 0, prep: p.prep || null });
            kid.netId = vb.netId ? `${vb.netId}.${tag}${idx}` : null; kid.owner = vb.owner;
            this.soft.inherit(kid, vb, [p.offset[0] * VOXEL, p.offset[1] * VOXEL, p.offset[2] * VOXEL]);
            kids.push(kid);
        }
        // keep showing the old mesh until the pieces have theirs (a frame or two)
        const ghost = { vb, t: 0 };
        this.ghosts.push(ghost);
        Promise.all(kids.map(k => k.meshed)).then(() => { ghost.done = true; });
        return { removed, bodies: kids };
    }

    /** Bodies in play over the cap (0 = empty, 1 = at the cap). */
    pressure() { let n = 0; for(const vb of this.list) if(!vb.stamping) n++; return n / this.maxBodies; }
    /** Pieces smaller than this (samples) become dust; grows as the body count nears the cap. */
    debrisMin() { const p = this.pressure(); return p > 0.6 ? Math.round(DEBRIS * (1 + (p - 0.6) * 8)) : DEBRIS; }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ fracture

    /** The soft lattice of `vb` came apart (a SoftBodies split): break the body along it (a 'frac' job). */
    crack(vb, split) {
        if(this.breaks.has(vb) || vb.dead || !this.list.has(vb)) return;
        if(!this.isOwner(vb)) { this.soft.gridChanged(vb); return; } // its owner decides (a 'bsplit' follows)
        this.stats.cracked = (this.stats.cracked || 0) + 1;
        this.breaks.set(vb, [vb.toWorld(split.at || vb.frame), 0, split]);
    }

    /** Velocity change (m/s) a body takes before breaking. */
    breakDv(vb) {
        const hard = MATERIALS[vb.mat]?.hardness ?? 1;
        const massT = Math.max(0.2, vb.mass / 1000);
        return this.breakScale * 6.5 * Math.sqrt(Math.min(hard, 6)) * Math.pow(massT, -0.12) * (1 + 0.4 * vb.generation);
    }

    fracture(vb, point, dv) {
        const t0 = performance.now();
        const g = vb.grid;
        if(this.debug) console.log(`fracture n=${g.count} gen=${vb.generation} age=${vb.age.toFixed(2)} dv=${dv.toFixed(1)} break=${this.breakDv(vb).toFixed(1)} mass=${(vb.mass/1000).toFixed(1)}t`);
        const energy = Math.min(4, dv / this.breakDv(vb));
        const crowd = Math.max(0.35, Math.min(1, 1.3 - this.pressure()));
        const n = Math.max(2, Math.min(14, Math.round((1 + energy * 2 + Math.log2(1 + vb.volume)) * crowd)));
        const labels = voronoiLabels(g, vb.toLocal(point), n);
        const dust = new Float64Array(256);
        const pieces = splitGrid(g, labels, { minCount: this.debrisMin(), dust });
        const { removed } = this.replace(vb, pieces, { point, spread: Math.min(8, 1.5 + dv * 0.15), generation: vb.generation + 1, tag: 'f' });
        for(let i = 0; i < 256; i++) removed[i] += dust[i];
        this.stats.fractured++;
        this.stats.lastFractureMs = performance.now() - t0;
        this._dust(point, removed, 'impact', vb);
    }

    /** Emit 'destroyed' for pulverized material. */
    _dust(point, removed, kind, vb = null) {
        let volume = 0;
        for(let i = 0; i < 256; i++) volume += removed[i];
        if(volume <= 0) return;
        this.stats.pulverized += volume;
        const r = Math.max(0.5, Math.cbrt(volume));
        const box = [point.map(v => v - r), point.map(v => v + r)];
        this.events.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('destroyed', { detail: { center: [...point], box, removed, volume, author: null, kind, prop: vb?.prop ?? null } }));
    }

    _contact(c1, c2, speed, point) {
        const p1 = this.physics.propOf(c1), p2 = this.physics.propOf(c2);
        const a = p1?.userData?.voxelBody, b = p2?.userData?.voxelBody;
        if(a) this._hit(a, speed, point);
        if(b) this._hit(b, speed, point);
    }
    _hit(vb, speed, point) {
        let h = this.hits.get(vb);
        if(!h) this.hits.set(vb, h = { speed: 0, point });
        if(speed > h.speed) { h.speed = speed; h.point = point; }
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ per step

    _step(h) {
        // impacts and fracture
        if(this.hits.size) {
            for(const [vb, hit] of this.hits) {
                if(vb.dead || !this.list.has(vb)) continue;
                const b = vb.body;
                if(!b.isDynamic() || vb.prop.userData.held) continue;
                // velocity change: the hit's approach speed and the body's measured change, whichever is smaller (a
                // light piece hitting a heavy one stops; a heavy one barely notices; kicks change velocity without contact)
                const v = b.linvel(), p = vb.prevV;
                const dx = v.x - p[0], dy = v.y - p[1] + GRAVITY * h, dz = v.z - p[2];
                const dvV = Math.hypot(dx, dy, dz);
                const dv = Math.min(hit.speed, dvV);
                if(dv < Math.min(SOFT_DV, this.impactDv)) continue;
                const point = hit.point;
                const brk = this.breakDv(vb), energy = dv / brk;
                // the soft layer: matter away from the contact keeps going (a crack attempt when hit hard enough)
                const sc = dv / (dvV || 1);
                this.soft.impact(vb, point, [dx * sc, dy * sc, dz * sc], energy > 1 ? energy * 1.3 : 1);
                if(dv < this.impactDv) continue;
                if(vb.cool <= 0) {
                    vb.cool = 0.12;
                    this.events.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('impact', { detail: { point, speed: dv, mat: vb.mat, prop: vb.prop } }));
                }
                if(vb.age > 0.2 && vb.grid.count >= MIN_FRACTURE && dv > brk && this.isOwner(vb)) {
                    // under SHATTER: let the soft lattice crack it (crack()), with a Voronoi fallback if it holds
                    // (a thrown body always cracks rather than bursting: Combat.throwHeld)
                    const soft = this.soft.enabled && (energy < SHATTER || vb.prop.userData.thrown) && vb.softRec?.state !== 'dead';
                    if(soft) { if(!vb.crackWait || vb.crackWait.dv < dv) vb.crackWait = { t: vb.age, point, dv, energy }; }
                    else {
                        // no soft layer (off, or too big for one): cleave into a few pieces; only extreme hits shatter
                        const q = this.breaks.get(vb);
                        if(!q || q[1] < dv) this.breaks.set(vb, [point, dv, null, energy < SHATTER ? Math.min(CLEAVE, 2 + Math.floor(energy / 4)) : 0]);
                    }
                }
            }
            this.hits.clear();
        }
        // lifecycle
        const { min, max } = this.bounds;
        for(const vb of this.list) {
            if(vb.dead || !this.physics.props.has(vb.prop)) { if(!vb.dead) { vb.dead = true; vb.disposeMesh(); } this.list.delete(vb); continue; }
            vb.age += h; vb.cool -= h;
            if(vb.sibling && vb.age > 0.3) vb.touchSiblings();
            if(vb.waitKeys) {
                vb.waitT += h;
                const pool = this.pool;
                if(vb.waitT > 1.5 || (!vb.waitRemote && !vb.waitKeys.some(k => pool.queue.has(k) || pool.inflight.has(k) || this.physics.colliderPending(k)))) vb.touchTerrain();
            }
            if(vb.stamping) { if(vb.stampKeys) this._finishStamp(vb, h); continue; }
            const cw = vb.crackWait;
            if(cw && vb.age - cw.t > CRACK_WAIT) {
                vb.crackWait = null;
                // the lattice held: a hit far over the threshold shatters it after all
            if(cw.energy >= CRACK_FALLBACK && !vb.prop.userData.thrown && !vb.fracturing && vb.softRec?.state !== 'split' && !this.breaks.has(vb))
                    this.breaks.set(vb, [cw.point, cw.dv, null, Math.min(CLEAVE, 2 + Math.floor(cw.energy / 4))]);
            }
            const b = vb.body;
            const lv = b.linvel(); vb.prevV[0] = lv.x; vb.prevV[1] = lv.y; vb.prevV[2] = lv.z;
            if(!b.isDynamic() || vb.prop.userData.held) { vb.still = 0; continue; }
            const t = b.translation();
            if(t.y < min[1] || t.x < min[0] || t.z < min[2] || t.x > max[0] || t.z > max[2] || t.y > max[1] + 50) { vb.dispose(); this.list.delete(vb); continue; }
            if(b.isSleeping()) vb.still += h;
            else {
                const v = b.linvel(), w = b.angvel();
                // slow bodies get heavy damping so they come to rest (and sleep) soon instead of jittering on the
                // terrain: resting contacts are what the physics step spends its time on
                const slow = vb.age > 0.4 && v.x * v.x + v.y * v.y + v.z * v.z < 4 && w.x * w.x + w.y * w.y + w.z * w.z < 4;
                if(slow !== !!vb.damped) { vb.damped = slow; b.setLinearDamping(slow ? 0.6 : 0.02); b.setAngularDamping(slow ? 2 : 0.15); }
                // barely moving counts too (bodies woken by a terrain remesh nearby settle again at once)
                if(v.x * v.x + v.y * v.y + v.z * v.z < 0.25 && w.x * w.x + w.y * w.y + w.z * w.z < 0.25) vb.still += h;
                else vb.still = Math.max(0, vb.still - h * 4);
            }
            if(!this.isOwner(vb)) { if(vb.still > NONOWNER_REST) { vb.dispose(); this.list.delete(vb); } continue; }
            if(vb.still > SETTLE || (vb.age > 25 && vb.still > 0.5)) this.stamp(vb);
        }
        // budget: over the cap, stamp the stillest bodies, then pulverize the smallest moving ones
        let over = Math.round((this.pressure() - 1) * this.maxBodies);
        if(over > 0) {
            const cands = [...this.list].filter(vb => !vb.stamping && !vb.dead && vb.body.isDynamic() && !vb.prop.userData.held);
            cands.sort((a, b) => b.still - a.still);
            for(const vb of cands) { if(over <= 0 || vb.still < 0.3) break; this.stamp(vb); over--; }
            if(over > 0) {
                cands.sort((a, b) => a.grid.count - b.grid.count);
                for(const vb of cands) {
                    if(over <= 0 || vb.grid.count > 250) break;
                    if(vb.stamping || vb.dead) continue;
                    const t = vb.body.translation();
                    this._dust([t.x, t.y, t.z], vb.stats.volumes, 'impact', vb);
                    vb.dispose(); this.list.delete(vb); over--;
                }
            }
        }
        // ghosts of broken bodies
        for(let i = this.ghosts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const g = this.ghosts[i];
            g.t += h;
            if(g.done || g.t > 0.5) { g.vb.disposeMesh(); this.ghosts.splice(i, 1); }
        }
    }

    /** Union a body into the terrain at its pose (a background job); it is removed once the terrain there has been
     *  remeshed and has its new colliders. */
    stamp(vb) {
        if(vb.stamping || vb.dead || vb.fracturing || !this.isOwner(vb)) return;
        this.stats.stamped++;
        this.soft.gridChanged(vb); // stamped as built: no more wobble
        vb.body.setBodyType(BodyType.Fixed, false);
        vb.stamping = true;
        vb.stampKeys = null;
        // settled pieces touching it are stamped with it: one resting on it would otherwise move from its collider
        // boxes onto the exact terrain surface (or the other way round) and shift
        const [mn, mx] = vb.worldBox();
        for(const o of this.list) {
            if(o === vb || o.stamping || o.dead || o.fracturing || !o.body.isDynamic() || o.prop.userData.held || !this.isOwner(o)) continue;
            if(!(o.body.isSleeping() || o.still > 0.3)) continue;
            const [a, b] = o.worldBox();
            if(a[0] < mx[0] + 0.3 && b[0] > mn[0] - 0.3 && a[1] < mx[1] + 0.3 && b[1] > mn[1] - 0.3 && a[2] < mx[2] + 0.3 && b[2] > mn[2] - 0.3) this.stamp(o);
        }
        const remote = this.world.remote;
        if(remote) {
            const g = vb.grid, t = vb.gridOrigin(), q = vb.rotation();
            const grid = { nx: g.nx, ny: g.ny, nz: g.nz, sdf: g.sdf, mat: g.mat, count: g.count, offset: g.offset };
            const done = this.net ? this.net.submit({ k: 'stamp', id: vb.netId, grid, t: [...t], q: [...q] }) : remote.stamp(grid, t, q);
            done.then(rep => { vb.stampKeys = rep.dirty; vb.stampT = 0; }, () => { vb.stampKeys = []; vb.stampT = 0; });
            return;
        }
        this.jobs.push({ kind: 'stamp', vb, gen: stampJob(this.world, vb.grid, vb.gridOrigin(), vb.rotation(), 4096) });
    }
    _finishStamp(vb, h) {
        vb.stampT += h;
        const pool = this.pool;
        const pending = vb.stampKeys.some(k => pool.queue.has(k) || pool.inflight.has(k) || this.physics.colliderPending(k));
        if(pending && vb.stampT < 2) return;
        vb.dispose();
        this.list.delete(vb);
    }

    /** Stamp every body now (e.g. before a level reset or a save). */
    settleAll() { for(const vb of [...this.list]) if(!vb.dead && vb.body.isDynamic()) this.stamp(vb); }
}
```

### src/render/Environment.js
```
/**
 * Sky, sun, ambient light and fog: a bright, saturated daytime look.
 *
 *  - Sky: a dome that follows the camera, an analytic gradient (zenith blue to a pale horizon), a sun disk with glow and
 *    a soft cloud layer from a small tiling noise texture made at startup (one texture fetch per sky pixel).
 *  - Fog uses the sky's horizon colour so distant terrain melts into the sky.
 *  - Sun shadows follow the camera: the shadow camera is a box of SHADOW_HALF metres around a point a little ahead of
 *    the viewer, snapped to whole shadow texels so edges do not crawl as the camera moves. Phones get a smaller map.
 *
 * `new Environment({ scene, camera, renderer })`; call `update()` every frame (it is a system: ctx.addSystem(env)).
 * `env.sun` is the DirectionalLight (ctx.sun), `env.hemi` the HemisphereLight, `env.sunDir` the unit vector to the sun.
 */
import * as THREE from 'three/webgpu';
import { positionLocal, uniform, color, texture, vec2, vec3, float, mix, smoothstep, max, pow, dot } from 'three/tsl';
import { isMobile } from './device.js';

const ZENITH = new THREE.Color(0x2a78e0);
const HORIZON = new THREE.Color(0xb6daf5);
const GROUND = new THREE.Color(0xb9cfdc);
const SUN_COLOR = new THREE.Color(0xfff2dc);

/** Recommended renderer settings for this device (pixel ratio cap and shadow map size). */
export const quality = {
    pixelRatio: isMobile ? 1.25 : 1.5,
    shadowMapSize: isMobile ? 2048 : 4096,
    shadowHalf: isMobile ? 32 : 48,
};

/** Apply the device's pixel ratio, tone mapping and shadow settings to a renderer. */
export function configureRenderer(renderer) {
    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(devicePixelRatio, quality.pixelRatio));
    renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
    renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.1;
    renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
    renderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFShadowMap;
}

export class Environment {
    constructor({ scene, camera }) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.camera = camera;
        this.sunDir = new THREE.Vector3(0.36, 0.7, 0.6).normalize();

        scene.background = HORIZON.clone();
        scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(HORIZON.clone(), 70, 330);

        this.hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xcfe4ff, 0x8d7a5c, 1.35);
        scene.add(this.hemi);

        const sun = this.sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(SUN_COLOR, 3.2);
        sun.castShadow = true;
        sun.shadow.mapSize.set(quality.shadowMapSize, quality.shadowMapSize);
        const H = this.shadowHalf = quality.shadowHalf;
        Object.assign(sun.shadow.camera, { left: -H, right: H, top: H, bottom: -H, near: 1, far: 260 });
        sun.shadow.bias = -0.0004;
        sun.shadow.normalBias = 0.04;
        sun.shadow.radius = 2;
        scene.add(sun, sun.target);

        this.sky = this._sky();
        scene.add(this.sky);
        this._focus = new THREE.Vector3();
        this._fwd = new THREE.Vector3();
        this.update();
    }

    _sky() {
        const dir = positionLocal.normalize();
        const y = dir.y;
        const sunDir = this.sunDirUniform = uniform(this.sunDir.clone());
        const up = max(y, 0);
        let col = mix(color(HORIZON), color(ZENITH), pow(up, 0.55));
        col = mix(col, color(GROUND), smoothstep(0, 0.25, y.negate()));
        // clouds: a tiling noise texture projected on a high plane
        const cuv = dir.xz.div(y.add(0.12)).mul(0.22).add(vec2(0.31, 0.17));
        const n = texture(cloudTexture(), cuv).r;
        const cloud = smoothstep(0.5, 0.78, n).mul(smoothstep(0.02, 0.22, y)).mul(0.85);
        const cloudCol = mix(vec3(0.82, 0.86, 0.93), vec3(1.15, 1.15, 1.15), smoothstep(0.55, 0.9, n));
        col = mix(col, cloudCol, cloud);
        // sun: disk and glow
        const s = max(dot(dir, sunDir), 0);
        const glow = pow(s, 12).mul(0.25).add(pow(s, 220).mul(0.6));
        const disk = smoothstep(0.99935, 0.9997, s).mul(float(1).sub(cloud.mul(0.8))).mul(18);
        col = col.add(color(SUN_COLOR).mul(glow.add(disk)));
        const m = new THREE.MeshBasicNodeMaterial({ side: THREE.BackSide, depthWrite: false, fog: false });
        m.colorNode = col;
        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(440, 32, 16), m);
        mesh.name = 'sky';
        mesh.frustumCulled = false;
        mesh.renderOrder = -1000;
        mesh.castShadow = mesh.receiveShadow = false;
        return mesh;
    }

    update() {
        const cam = this.camera, sun = this.sun, H = this.shadowHalf;
        this.sky.position.copy(cam.position);
        this.sunDirUniform.value.copy(this.sunDir);
        // shadow focus: where the view ray meets the ground (y = 1), at most SHADOW_HALF/2 ahead of the camera
        const fw = cam.getWorldDirection(this._fwd);
        let t = H * 0.5;
        if(fw.y < -0.02) t = Math.min(t, Math.max(0, (cam.position.y - 1) / -fw.y));
        const f = this._focus.copy(cam.position).addScaledVector(fw, t);
        // snap to shadow texels in light space
        const z = this.sunDir, x = _x.set(0, 1, 0).cross(z).normalize(), yv = _y.copy(z).cross(x);
        const texel = (2 * H) / sun.shadow.mapSize.x;
        const lx = Math.round(f.dot(x) / texel) * texel, ly = Math.round(f.dot(yv) / texel) * texel, lz = f.dot(z);
        f.copy(x).multiplyScalar(lx).addScaledVector(yv, ly).addScaledVector(z, lz);
        sun.target.position.copy(f);
        sun.position.copy(f).addScaledVector(z, 130);
        sun.target.updateMatrixWorld();
        sun.updateMatrixWorld();
    }
}
const _x = new THREE.Vector3(), _y = new THREE.Vector3();

let clouds = null;
/** A 256² tiling value-noise fbm (R channel), made once. */
function cloudTexture() {
    if(clouds) return clouds;
    const N = 256, data = new Uint8Array(N * N * 4);
    const field = new Float32Array(N * N);
    let seed = 7;
    const rnd = () => { seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) & 0x7fffffff; return seed / 0x7fffffff; };
    let amp = 1, total = 0;
    for(let cells = 4; cells <= 64; cells *= 2) {
        const g = new Float32Array(cells * cells).map(rnd);
        const step = N / cells;
        for(let y = 0; y < N; y++) for(let x = 0; x < N; x++) {
            const fx = x / step, fy = y / step, ix = Math.floor(fx), iy = Math.floor(fy);
            let tx = fx - ix, ty = fy - iy;
            tx = tx * tx * (3 - 2 * tx); ty = ty * ty * (3 - 2 * ty);
            const a = g[(iy % cells) * cells + ix % cells], b = g[(iy % cells) * cells + (ix + 1) % cells];
            const c = g[((iy + 1) % cells) * cells + ix % cells], d = g[((iy + 1) % cells) * cells + (ix + 1) % cells];
            field[y * N + x] += amp * ((a * (1 - tx) + b * tx) * (1 - ty) + (c * (1 - tx) + d * tx) * ty);
        }
        total += amp; amp *= 0.5;
    }
    for(let i = 0; i < N * N; i++) { const v = Math.round(field[i] / total * 255); data[i * 4] = data[i * 4 + 1] = data[i * 4 + 2] = v; data[i * 4 + 3] = 255; }
    clouds = new THREE.DataTexture(data, N, N);
    clouds.wrapS = clouds.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
    clouds.magFilter = THREE.LinearFilter;
    clouds.minFilter = THREE.LinearMipmapLinearFilter;
    clouds.generateMipmaps = true;
    clouds.needsUpdate = true;
    return clouds;
}
```

### src/render/TerrainRenderer.js
```
/**
 * Draws the VoxelWorld: one mesh per chunk, meshed in the worker pool and remeshed whenever an edit dirties it.
 * Chunk meshes sit at their chunk's world origin; geometry attributes: position, normal, ao (float), mat (float id).
 */
import * as THREE from 'three/webgpu';
import { CHUNK_M } from '../config.js';
import { MeshPool } from '../voxel/MeshPool.js';
import { createTerrainMaterial, meshMaterial } from './terrainMaterial.js';
import { geometryFromMesh } from './geometry.js';

export class TerrainRenderer {
    constructor(world, scene, camera) {
        this.world = world;
        this.scene = scene;
        this.camera = camera;
        this.group = new THREE.Group();
        this.group.name = 'terrain';
        scene.add(this.group);
        this.meshes = new Map();
        this.material = createTerrainMaterial();
        this.listeners = [];
        this.pool = new MeshPool({
            onMesh: (key, mesh) => this._apply(key, mesh),
            priority: (key) => {
                const c = world.chunks.get(key), p = camera.position;
                if(!c) return -1; // one-shot grids (detached bodies) first
                return Math.hypot((c.cx + 0.5) * CHUNK_M - p.x, (c.cy + 0.5) * CHUNK_M - p.y, (c.cz + 0.5) * CHUNK_M - p.z);
            },
        });
        // edits from the terrain worker come with the dirty chunks' padded windows (no extraction on this thread)
        world.onChange(({ dirty, windows }) => { for(const k of dirty) { const w = windows?.get(k); if(w) this.remeshWindow(k, w); else this.remesh(k); } });
    }
    /** The shared material for detached bodies (createTerrainMaterial({ local: true })): triplanar in object space,
     *  so textures stick to a moving piece. One instance for all bodies keeps a single pipeline. */
    get bodyMaterial() { return this._bodyMaterial ||= createTerrainMaterial({ local: true }); }
    /** Called with (key, geometry | null) after a chunk's mesh changes. */
    onChunkMesh(fn) { this.listeners.push(fn); }
    remeshAll() { for(const k of this.world.chunks.keys()) this.remesh(k); }
    remesh(key) {
        const c = this.world.chunks.get(key);
        if(!c) return;
        this.pool.request(key, (sdf, mat) => this.world.extractPadded(c.cx, c.cy, c.cz, sdf, mat));
    }
    /** Remesh a chunk from a ready padded window { sdf, mat } ({ empty: true }: nothing to mesh). */
    remeshWindow(key, w) {
        this.pool.request(key, (sdf, mat) => {
            if(w.empty) return false;
            sdf.set(w.sdf); mat.set(w.mat);
            this.world.remote?.recycle(w.sdf, w.mat); w.sdf = w.mat = null; w.empty = true; // (a window is used once)
            return true;
        });
    }
    get pending() { return this.pool.pending; }
    _apply(key, mesh) {
        let m = this.meshes.get(key);
        if(!mesh || !mesh.indices.length) {
            if(m) { this.group.remove(m); m.geometry.dispose(); this.meshes.delete(key); }
            for(const fn of this.listeners) fn(key, null);
            return;
        }
        const g = geometryFromMesh(mesh);
        if(m) { m.geometry.dispose(); m.geometry = g; m.material = meshMaterial(this.material, g); }
        else {
            const c = this.world.chunks.get(key);
            m = new THREE.Mesh(g, meshMaterial(this.material, g));
            m.position.set(c.cx * CHUNK_M, c.cy * CHUNK_M, c.cz * CHUNK_M);
            m.castShadow = true; m.receiveShadow = true;
            m.matrixAutoUpdate = false; m.updateMatrix();
            m.userData.chunkKey = key;
            this.group.add(m);
            this.meshes.set(key, m);
        }
        for(const fn of this.listeners) fn(key, g);
    }
}
```

### src/render/TerrainTextures.js
```
/** The terrain's texture array: one layer per distinct texture in the material table (src/voxel/materials.js) plus
 *  one flat grey layer for untextured materials, and per material id a uniform table the shader indexes:
 *  `tints` (linear RGB multiplier) and `props` (layer, tiles per metre, glow, 0).
 *
 *  Before the images arrive the array is a tiny white placeholder and each tint is the material's flat colour, so the
 *  world shows its palette immediately. When a texture is shared by several materials (red and blue stone) or a material
 *  has none, its tint maps the layer's mean colour onto the material colour; otherwise the tint is white. One shared
 *  instance (`terrainTextures()`), so every terrain and body material samples the same GPU texture. */
import * as THREE from 'three/webgpu';
import { MATERIALS } from '../voxel/materials.js';
import { isMobile } from './device.js';

const FLAT = 0.55; // the flat layer's grey (sRGB 0..1)
/** How far a texture used by one material is pulled towards the material's palette hue (0 = the texture as is):
 *  the grass photo is a dry yellow-green, and the cliff photo is orange where the palette wants grey stone. */
const HUE_TINT = { Grass: 0.6, Stone: 0.6, Dirt: 0.25 };

class TerrainTextures {
    constructor() {
        this.size = isMobile ? 512 : 1024;
        this.paths = [...new Set(MATERIALS.filter(m => m.texture).map(m => m.texture))];
        this.flatLayer = this.paths.length;
        this.depth = this.paths.length + 1;
        const ph = new Uint8Array(4 * 4 * 4 * this.depth).fill(255);
        this.texture = new THREE.DataArrayTexture(ph, 4, 4, this.depth);
        this.texture.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
        this.texture.wrapS = this.texture.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping;
        this.texture.minFilter = THREE.LinearMipmapLinearFilter;
        this.texture.magFilter = THREE.LinearFilter;
        this.texture.generateMipmaps = true;
        this.texture.anisotropy = isMobile ? 2 : 8;
        this.texture.needsUpdate = true;
        this.count = Math.max(16, MATERIALS.length);
        this.tints = [];
        this.props = [];
        for(let i = 0; i < this.count; i++) {
            const m = MATERIALS[i] || MATERIALS[1];
            this.tints.push(new THREE.Color(m.color));
            const layer = m.texture ? this.paths.indexOf(m.texture) : this.flatLayer;
            this.props.push(new THREE.Vector4(layer, 1 / (m.scale || 4), m.name === 'Lava' ? 1 : 0, 0));
        }
        this.ready = this._load().catch(e => console.warn('terrain textures:', e.message));
    }
    async _load() {
        const S = this.size, L = S * S * 4;
        const data = new Uint8Array(L * this.depth);
        const lin = (v) => { v /= 255; return v <= 0.04045 ? v / 12.92 : ((v + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4; };
        const means = [];
        await Promise.all(this.paths.map(async (path, i) => {
            const base = i * L;
            try {
                const blob = await (await fetch(new URL(path, document.baseURI))).blob();
                const bmp = await createImageBitmap(blob, { resizeWidth: S, resizeHeight: S, resizeQuality: 'high' });
                const canvas = new OffscreenCanvas(S, S), c2 = canvas.getContext('2d');
                c2.drawImage(bmp, 0, 0);
                bmp.close?.();
                data.set(c2.getImageData(0, 0, S, S).data, base);
            } catch(e) {
                console.warn(`terrain texture ${path} failed: ${e.message}`);
                data.fill(255, base, base + L);
            }
            const sum = [0, 0, 0]; let n = 0;
            for(let p = base; p < base + L; p += 4 * 13) { sum[0] += lin(data[p]); sum[1] += lin(data[p + 1]); sum[2] += lin(data[p + 2]); n++; }
            means[i] = sum.map(v => v / n);
        }));
        const flat = Math.round(FLAT * 255);
        data.fill(flat, this.flatLayer * L, this.flatLayer * L + L);
        means[this.flatLayer] = [lin(flat), lin(flat), lin(flat)];
        // the GPU texture was created at the placeholder's size: dispose so the backend recreates it
        this.texture.dispose();
        this.texture.image = { data, width: S, height: S, depth: this.depth };
        this.texture.needsUpdate = true;
        const users = new Map();
        for(const m of MATERIALS) if(m.texture) users.set(m.texture, (users.get(m.texture) || 0) + 1);
        for(let i = 0; i < this.count; i++) {
            const m = MATERIALS[i] || MATERIALS[1];
            const layer = this.props[i].x;
            const amount = m.texture && users.get(m.texture) === 1 ? (HUE_TINT[m.name] || 0) : 1;
            if(amount === 0) { this.tints[i].setRGB(1, 1, 1); continue; }
            // the material's hue at the geometric mean of its own and the texture's brightness
            const c = new THREE.Color(m.color), mean = means[layer];
            const lum = (r, g, b) => 0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b;
            const k = Math.sqrt(lum(...mean) / Math.max(1e-4, lum(c.r, c.g, c.b)));
            const t = (v, mv) => 1 + (Math.min(4, k * v / mv) - 1) * amount;
            this.tints[i].setRGB(t(c.r, mean[0]), t(c.g, mean[1]), t(c.b, mean[2]));
        }
        this.loaded = true;
    }
}

let shared = null;
/** The shared TerrainTextures instance (created on first use). */
export function terrainTextures() { return shared ||= new TerrainTextures(); }
```

/** Device class for render quality choices: phones and tablets get smaller shadow maps, textures and pixel ratio. */
const ua = typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? navigator.userAgent : '';
const params = typeof location !== 'undefined' ? new URLSearchParams(location.search) : new URLSearchParams();
export const isMobile = params.has('mobile') ? params.get('mobile') !== '0'
    : /Android|iPhone|iPad|iPod|Mobile|Silk/i.test(ua) || (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.maxTouchPoints > 1 && /Macintosh/.test(ua));

### src/render/geometry.js
```
/** Turn a mesher result ({ positions, normals, ao, materials, materials2?, blend?, indices }) into a THREE.BufferGeometry
 *  with the attributes the terrain material reads: position, normal, ao (float), mat (float material id A) and matBlend
 *  (vec3: material A, material B, blend 0 = A .. 1 = B). Meshes without materials2/blend get B = A, blend = 0. */
import * as THREE from 'three/webgpu';

export function geometryFromMesh(mesh) {
    const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    const n = mesh.positions.length / 3;
    g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(mesh.positions, 3));
    g.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(mesh.normals, 3));
    g.setAttribute('ao', new THREE.BufferAttribute(mesh.ao, 1));
    // material ids as (A, B, blend) in one vec3 attribute; precomputed by the mesh worker (src/voxel/meshPrep.js)
    let m = mesh.matBlend, mf = mesh.matF;
    if(!m) {
        m = new Float32Array(n * 3);
        const A = mesh.materials, B = mesh.materials2 || A, W = mesh.blend;
        for(let i = 0; i < n; i++) { m[i * 3] = A[i]; m[i * 3 + 1] = B[i]; m[i * 3 + 2] = W ? W[i] : 0; }
        mf = Float32Array.from(A);
    }
    g.setAttribute('mat', new THREE.BufferAttribute(mf, 1)); // kept for other readers
    g.setAttribute('matBlend', new THREE.BufferAttribute(m, 3));
    g.setIndex(new THREE.BufferAttribute(mesh.indices, 1));
    // glass (meshPrep put its triangles last): group 0 opaque terrain, group 1 glass (see terrainMaterial meshMaterial)
    if(mesh.glassStart !== undefined) { g.addGroup(0, mesh.glassStart, 0); g.addGroup(mesh.glassStart, mesh.indices.length - mesh.glassStart, 1); }
    const b = mesh.bounds;
    if(b) {
        g.boundingBox = new THREE.Box3(new THREE.Vector3(b[0], b[1], b[2]), new THREE.Vector3(b[3], b[4], b[5]));
        g.boundingSphere = new THREE.Sphere(new THREE.Vector3(b[6], b[7], b[8]), b[9]);
    } else {
        g.computeBoundingSphere();
        g.computeBoundingBox();
    }
    // a terrain chunk's collider octants (Physics.setChunkCollider), when the worker made them
    if(mesh.octants) g.userData.octants = mesh.octants;
    return g;
}
```

### src/render/terrainMaterial.js
```
/** Terrain material: triplanar textures from the terrain texture array (TerrainTextures), per material id a layer,
 *  scale and tint, blended between the two materials of a triangle (geometry attribute `matBlend` = (A, B, blend),
 *  see surfaceNets.js) with a height-like bias from the textures' brightness so seams look like grout and grass tufts
 *  rather than a linear fade. Ambient occlusion from the mesher (attribute `ao`) darkens indirect light fully and the
 *  albedo a little.
 *
 *  createTerrainMaterial()               chunks: triplanar in world space
 *  createTerrainMaterial({ local: true }) detached bodies: triplanar in object space, so the texture rides along
 *                                         with a moving, rotating piece (build its geometry with geometryFromMesh). */
import * as THREE from 'three/webgpu';
import { attribute, uniformArray, texture, positionWorld, positionLocal, normalWorld, normalLocal, normalView, positionViewDirection, float, vec3, mix, clamp, dot, select, uniform, varying, Fn, abs, pow } from 'three/tsl';
import { MATERIALS, GLASS } from '../voxel/materials.js';
import { terrainTextures } from './TerrainTextures.js';

const LUMA = vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722);
let tables = null;

export function createTerrainMaterial({ local = false } = {}) {
    const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardNodeMaterial({ roughness: 0.88, metalness: 0 });
    // terrain is closed solid: cast shadows with front faces (back faces leak light at the foot of walls)
    mat.shadowSide = THREE.FrontSide;
    buildTerrainNodes(mat, { local });
    return mat;
}

/**
 * Soft ground (src/soft): deform chunk meshes in the vertex stage. `deform(skip)` (called inside the vertex
 * Fn) returns TSL { position (local), normal (local), rest (world position before deformation) }; `skip` is a
 * per-object int uniform (bits from `object.geometry.userData.softSkip`) that the deform provider may use to leave
 * meshes out. Textures follow the rest position, so they ride along with the wobble. WebGPU only: do not call
 * this on the WebGL2 backend (storage buffers in the vertex stage).
 */
export function enableTerrainDeform(mat, deform) {
    buildTerrainNodes(mat, { local: false, deform });
    mat.needsUpdate = true;
}

function buildTerrainNodes(mat, { local, deform = null }) {
    const tt = terrainTextures();
    tables ||= { tints: uniformArray(tt.tints, 'color'), props: uniformArray(tt.props, 'vec4') };
    let pWorld = positionWorld, nWorld = normalWorld;
    if(deform) {
        const skip = uniform(0, 'int').onObjectUpdate(({ object }) => object.geometry?.userData.softSkip | 0);
        const vRest = varying(vec3(0), 'vSoftRest');
        mat.positionNode = Fn(() => {
            const d = deform(skip);
            vRest.assign(d.rest);
            normalLocal.assign(d.normal);
            return d.position;
        })();
        pWorld = vRest;
    } else mat.positionNode = null;
    const mb = attribute('matBlend', 'vec3');
    const ia = mb.x.add(0.5).toInt(), ib = mb.y.add(0.5).toInt();
    const pa = tables.props.element(ia), pb = tables.props.element(ib);
    const p = local ? positionLocal : pWorld;
    const nAbs = (local ? normalLocal : nWorld).abs();
    const n4 = nAbs.mul(nAbs).mul(nAbs).mul(nAbs);
    const w = n4.div(n4.x.add(n4.y).add(n4.z).max(1e-5));
    const tri = (props) => {
        const layer = props.x.add(0.5).toInt();
        const q = p.mul(props.y);
        const sx = texture(tt.texture, q.zy).depth(layer).rgb;
        const sy = texture(tt.texture, q.xz).depth(layer).rgb;
        const sz = texture(tt.texture, q.xy).depth(layer).rgb;
        return sx.mul(w.x).add(sy.mul(w.y)).add(sz.mul(w.z));
    };
    const ca = tri(pa).mul(vec3(tables.tints.element(ia)));
    const blend = mb.z;
    // second material only where the triangle mixes two (the branch is uniform across most triangles)
    const cb = tri(pb).mul(vec3(tables.tints.element(ib)));
    // height blend: brighter texels of the incoming material win earlier
    const hb = clamp(blend.sub(0.5).mul(2.5).add(dot(cb, LUMA).sub(dot(ca, LUMA)).mul(0.8)).add(0.5), 0, 1);
    const wb = select(ia.equal(ib), float(0), hb);
    const albedo = mix(ca, cb, wb);
    const ao = attribute('ao', 'float');
    mat.colorNode = albedo.mul(mix(float(0.7), float(1), ao));
    mat.aoNode = mix(float(0.2), float(1), ao.mul(ao));
    const glow = mix(pa.z, pb.z, wb);
    mat.emissiveNode = albedo.mul(glow.mul(2.5));
    mat.userData.terrainSpace = local ? 'local' : 'world';
    mat.userData.textures = tt;
    if(mat.userData.glass) { mat.userData.glass.positionNode = mat.positionNode; mat.userData.glass.needsUpdate = true; }
}

/** The glass material paired with a terrain material (same vertex stage: soft-ground or soft-debris deformation). */
export function glassMaterialFor(base) {
    let g = base.userData.glass;
    if(g) { if(g.positionNode !== base.positionNode) { g.positionNode = base.positionNode; g.needsUpdate = true; } return g; }
    g = base.userData.glass = new THREE.MeshStandardNodeMaterial({ roughness: 0.04, metalness: 0, transparent: true, depthWrite: false });
    const tint = new THREE.Color(MATERIALS[GLASS].color);
    const facing = abs(dot(normalView, positionViewDirection));
    const rim = pow(float(1).sub(facing), 3);
    g.colorNode = vec3(tint.r, tint.g, tint.b).mul(0.6);
    g.opacityNode = mix(float(0.22), float(0.85), rim);
    g.emissiveNode = vec3(0.55, 0.68, 0.8).mul(rim.mul(0.6)); // the sky in the rim, a cheap reflection
    g.positionNode = base.positionNode;
    g.shadowSide = THREE.FrontSide;
    return g;
}
/** The material (or [terrain, glass] for meshes with a glass draw group) for a mesh with terrain geometry. */
export function meshMaterial(base, geometry) { return geometry.groups.length ? [base, glassMaterialFor(base)] : base; }

/** Resolves when the terrain textures have loaded (the material shows flat material colours until then). */
export function terrainTexturesReady() { return terrainTextures().ready; }
```

### src/soft/GroundRider.js
```
/**
 * Ground riders: things resting on soft ground ride its wobble. The soft ground only bends the terrain's drawn mesh
 * (collisions stay on the undeformed terrain), so without this a heave swallows a resting chunk and a dip leaves it
 * floating. Each frame, after physics placed every prop's mesh and the game placed the actors and the camera, this
 * samples the ground's displacement u under them and moves their drawn pose with it: a purely visual offset, never
 * fed back into physics or the network.
 *
 *   props (detached chunks, crates, ...): five support points under the drawn AABB (four inset corners and the
 *     middle, at its bottom). Each counts by how close it is to the ground (world SDF: full within 0.2 m, none past
 *     0.5 m) or, failing that, to the top of a lower rider it rests on (stacks ride along). Offset = their weighted
 *     mean u; tilt from the difference in heave across them (clamped), about the support centre.
 *   actors (bots, remote players) and the local player's camera: u at the feet while grounded.
 *
 * Sampling: the CPU solver's displacement texture is on the main thread, so its lookup runs here (SoftSimCPU.sample);
 * with GPU compute, a small gather pass reads u at the query points back asynchronously (SoftSim.gather: one or two
 * frames late, never stalling). Slots a chunk mesh already has baked in (geometry.userData.softSkip) are skipped,
 * so when a plastic bake lands the offset fades as the terrain (and the physics pose) takes over.
 * Offsets are smoothed (RATE per second) so zones starting, sleeping or being skipped never pop.
 */
import * as THREE from 'three/webgpu';
import { VOXEL, CHUNK_M } from '../config.js';
import { chunkKey } from '../voxel/VoxelWorld.js';
import { GATHER } from './SoftSim.js';

const PTS = 5;          // support points per prop
const NEAR = 0.2, FAR = 0.5; // m above the ground: full weight / none
const MAX_TILT = 0.3;   // rad
const RATE = 200;       // 1/s smoothing toward the target offset (light: the ground's own motion is smooth)
const SLOW = 6;         // 1/s ... for RELEASE s after a bake lands under it (the physics pose catches up meanwhile)
const RELEASE = 0.6;
const EPS = 1e-4;

const _box = new THREE.Box3(), _v = new THREE.Vector3(), _c = new THREE.Vector3(), _q = new THREE.Quaternion(), _e = new THREE.Euler();
const _u = [0, 0, 0], _sw = new Float32Array(PTS), _su = new Float32Array(PTS);

export class GroundRider {
    constructor(soft) {
        this.soft = soft;
        this.ctx = soft.ctx;
        this.state = new Map();       // prop | actor | 'camera' -> rider state
        this.q = new Float32Array(GATHER * 4);
        this.qMap = [];               // GPU: query i -> [state, point index]
        this.stats = { riders: 0, ms: 0, msMax: 0, maxOffset: 0 };
    }

    _st(key, kind) {
        let s = this.state.get(key);
        if(!s) this.state.set(key, s = { kind, u: new Float32Array(PTS * 3), w: new Float32Array(PTS), pts: new Float32Array(PTS * 3),
            box: [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0], off: [0, 0, 0], ax: 0, az: 0, seen: 0, skip: 0, skipNow: 0, release: 0 });
        return s;
    }

    /** Skip mask of the terrain chunk mesh at a point (slots its mesh has baked in). */
    _skip(x, y, z) {
        const m = this.ctx.terrain.meshes.get(chunkKey(Math.floor(x / CHUNK_M), Math.floor(y / CHUNK_M), Math.floor(z / CHUNK_M)));
        return m ? m.geometry.userData.softSkip | 0 : 0;
    }

    update(dt) {
        const soft = this.soft, sim = soft.sim;
        const live = soft.zones.length > 0;
        if(!live && !this.state.size) return;
        const t0 = performance.now();
        const ctx = this.ctx, world = ctx.world, frame = (this.frame = (this.frame || 0) + 1);
        const gpu = !sim.cpu;
        let nq = 0;
        const query = (s, i, x, y, z) => {
            const skip = this._skip(x, y, z);
            s.skipNow |= skip;
            if(gpu) {
                if(nq >= GATHER) { s.w[i] = 0; return; }
                const o = nq * 4;
                this.q[o] = x; this.q[o + 1] = y; this.q[o + 2] = z; this.q[o + 3] = skip;
                this.qMap[nq++] = [s, i];
            } else {
                sim.sample(x, y, z, skip, _u);
                s.u[i * 3] = _u[0]; s.u[i * 3 + 1] = _u[1]; s.u[i * 3 + 2] = _u[2];
            }
        };

        // only what is near a live zone is sampled (the rest eases back to its physics pose)
        const zb = soft.zones.map(z => z.box);
        const nearZone = (x0, y0, z0, x1, y1, z1) => {
            for(const b of zb) if(x0 < b[1][0] + 1 && x1 > b[0][0] - 1 && y0 < b[1][1] + 1 && y1 > b[0][1] - 1 && z0 < b[1][2] + 1 && z1 > b[0][2] - 1) return true;
            return false;
        };

        // ---- props: support points under the drawn AABB
        const props = [];
        if(live) for(const p of ctx.physics?.props || []) {
            const m = p.mesh, b = p.body;
            if(!m || !m.visible || p.userData?.held || b.isKinematic?.()) continue;
            const t = m.position;
            if(!nearZone(t.x - 8, t.y - 8, t.z - 8, t.x + 8, t.y + 8, t.z + 8)) continue; // cheap cull before the box
            const vb = p.userData?.voxelBody;
            if(vb) {
                if(vb.dead) continue;
                const g = vb.grid, f = vb.frame;
                _box.min.set(-VOXEL - f[0], -VOXEL - f[1], -VOXEL - f[2]);
                _box.max.set(g.nx * VOXEL - f[0], g.ny * VOXEL - f[1], g.nz * VOXEL - f[2]);
                m.updateMatrix();
                _box.applyMatrix4(m.matrix);
            } else {
                m.updateMatrixWorld(true);
                _box.setFromObject(m);
                if(_box.isEmpty()) continue;
            }
            if(!nearZone(_box.min.x, _box.min.y, _box.min.z, _box.max.x, _box.max.y, _box.max.z)) continue;
            const s = this._st(p, 'prop');
            s.seen = frame; s.skipNow = 0;
            const bx = s.box;
            bx[0] = _box.min.x; bx[1] = _box.min.y; bx[2] = _box.min.z; bx[3] = _box.max.x; bx[4] = _box.max.y; bx[5] = _box.max.z;
            const x0 = bx[0] + (bx[3] - bx[0]) * 0.25, x1 = bx[0] + (bx[3] - bx[0]) * 0.75;
            const z0 = bx[2] + (bx[5] - bx[2]) * 0.25, z1 = bx[2] + (bx[5] - bx[2]) * 0.75, y = bx[1];
            const P = s.pts;
            P[0] = x0; P[2] = z0; P[3] = x1; P[5] = z0; P[6] = x0; P[8] = z1; P[9] = x1; P[11] = z1;
            P[12] = (x0 + x1) / 2; P[14] = (z0 + z1) / 2;
            for(let i = 0; i < PTS; i++) {
                P[i * 3 + 1] = y;
                const d = world.sample(P[i * 3], y, P[i * 3 + 2]);
                const w = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (FAR - d) / (FAR - NEAR)));
                s.w[i] = w;
                if(w > 0) query(s, i, P[i * 3], y - 0.05, P[i * 3 + 2]);
            }
            props.push([p, s]);
        }

        // ---- actors (bots, remote players) and the local player's eye
        const actors = [];
        if(live) for(const a of ctx.game?.actors || []) {
            const f = a.alive && a.body?.feet;
            if(!f || !nearZone(f.x, f.y, f.z, f.x, f.y, f.z) || !(a.body.grounded || world.sample(f.x, f.y, f.z) < 0.25)) continue;
            const s = this._st(a, 'actor');
            s.seen = frame; s.skipNow = 0;
            s.pts[0] = f.x; s.pts[1] = f.y; s.pts[2] = f.z;
            s.w[0] = 1;
            query(s, 0, f.x, f.y - 0.05, f.z);
            actors.push([a, s]);
        }

        // ---- GPU: read the queries back (a frame or two late); the results land in the states then
        if(gpu && nq) {
            const map = this.qMap.slice(0, nq);
            const pr = sim.gather(this.q, nq);
            if(pr) pr.then((r) => { for(let i = 0; i < map.length; i++) { const [s, k] = map[i]; s.u[k * 3] = r[i * 4]; s.u[k * 3 + 1] = r[i * 4 + 1]; s.u[k * 3 + 2] = r[i * 4 + 2]; } })
                .catch(() => {});
        }

        // ---- apply, lowest props first (a stack's upper pieces take their support's offset)
        const kFast = 1 - Math.exp(-RATE * dt), kSlow = 1 - Math.exp(-SLOW * dt);
        // a slot newly skipped under a rider: a bake landed there (the terrain itself moved): ease over instead of popping
        const rate = (s) => {
            if(s.skipNow & ~s.skip) s.release = RELEASE;
            s.skip = s.skipNow;
            s.release = Math.max(0, s.release - dt);
            return s.release > 0 ? kSlow : kFast;
        };
        let maxOff = 0;
        props.sort((a, b) => a[1].box[1] - b[1].box[1]);
        const placed = [];
        for(const [p, s] of props) {
            let W = 0, ox = 0, oy = 0, oz = 0;
            let hx0 = 0, wx0 = 0, hx1 = 0, wx1 = 0, hz0 = 0, wz0 = 0, hz1 = 0, wz1 = 0;
            const sw = _sw, su = _su;
            for(let i = 0; i < PTS; i++) {
                sw[i] = 0;
                let w = s.w[i], ux = s.u[i * 3], uy = s.u[i * 3 + 1], uz = s.u[i * 3 + 2];
                if(w < 1) {
                    // resting on a lower rider: take its offset
                    const px = s.pts[i * 3], py = s.pts[i * 3 + 1], pz = s.pts[i * 3 + 2];
                    for(const o of placed) {
                        const b = o.box;
                        if(px < b[0] || px > b[3] || pz < b[2] || pz > b[5] || Math.abs(py - b[4]) > 0.35) continue;
                        const wo = 1 - w;
                        ux = ux * w + o.off[0] * wo; uy = uy * w + o.off[1] * wo; uz = uz * w + o.off[2] * wo; w = 1;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if(!(w > 0)) continue;
                sw[i] = w; su[i] = uy;
                W += w; ox += ux * w; oy += uy * w; oz += uz * w;
                if(i === 0 || i === 2) { hx0 += uy * w; wx0 += w; } else if(i === 1 || i === 3) { hx1 += uy * w; wx1 += w; }
                if(i === 0 || i === 1) { hz0 += uy * w; wz0 += w; } else if(i === 2 || i === 3) { hz1 += uy * w; wz1 += w; }
            }
            let tx = 0, ty = 0, tz = 0, tax = 0, taz = 0;
            if(W > 0) {
                const fade = Math.min(1, W / 2); // barely touching: a share of it
                tx = ox / W; tz = oz / W;
                // the ground under it as a plane (heave at the middle + slopes across), lifted until no support point
                // is above it: a rigid piece rests on the highest ground (bridges a dip, is never swallowed by a bump)
                const b = s.box, cx = (b[0] + b[3]) / 2, cz = (b[2] + b[5]) / 2;
                const dx = (b[3] - b[0]) * 0.5, dz = (b[5] - b[2]) * 0.5;
                const sx = wx0 > 0 && wx1 > 0 && dx > 0.1 ? (hx1 / wx1 - hx0 / wx0) / dx : 0;
                const sz = wz0 > 0 && wz1 > 0 && dz > 0.1 ? (hz1 / wz1 - hz0 / wz0) / dz : 0;
                let a = oy / W, lift = -Infinity;
                for(let i = 0; i < PTS; i++) {
                    if(!(sw[i] > 0.5)) continue;
                    lift = Math.max(lift, su[i] - (a + sx * (s.pts[i * 3] - cx) + sz * (s.pts[i * 3 + 2] - cz)));
                }
                if(lift > -Infinity) a += lift;
                tx *= fade; ty = a * fade; tz *= fade;
                taz = clamp(Math.atan(sx) * fade, MAX_TILT);
                tax = clamp(-Math.atan(sz) * fade, MAX_TILT);
            }
            const k = rate(s);
            s.off[0] += (tx - s.off[0]) * k; s.off[1] += (ty - s.off[1]) * k; s.off[2] += (tz - s.off[2]) * k;
            s.ax += (tax - s.ax) * k; s.az += (taz - s.az) * k;
            placed.push(s);
            const mag = Math.abs(s.off[0]) + Math.abs(s.off[1]) + Math.abs(s.off[2]) + Math.abs(s.ax) + Math.abs(s.az);
            maxOff = Math.max(maxOff, Math.hypot(s.off[0], s.off[1], s.off[2]));
            if(mag < EPS) continue;
            const m = p.mesh, b = s.box;
            _c.set((b[0] + b[3]) / 2, b[1], (b[2] + b[5]) / 2); // tilt about the support centre
            _q.setFromEuler(_e.set(s.ax, 0, s.az));
            _v.copy(m.position).sub(_c).applyQuaternion(_q).add(_c);
            m.position.set(_v.x + s.off[0], _v.y + s.off[1], _v.z + s.off[2]);
            m.quaternion.premultiply(_q);
        }
        for(const [a, s] of actors) {
            const k = rate(s);
            s.off[0] += (s.u[0] - s.off[0]) * k; s.off[1] += (s.u[1] - s.off[1]) * k; s.off[2] += (s.u[2] - s.off[2]) * k;
            maxOff = Math.max(maxOff, Math.hypot(s.off[0], s.off[1], s.off[2]));
            this._shift(a, s.off);
        }

        // ---- riders that left the ground (or zones that ended) ease back to their physics pose
        for(const [key, s] of this.state) {
            if(s.seen === frame) continue;
            const k = kSlow * 2;
            s.off[0] -= s.off[0] * k; s.off[1] -= s.off[1] * k; s.off[2] -= s.off[2] * k; s.ax -= s.ax * k; s.az -= s.az * k;
            const mag = Math.abs(s.off[0]) + Math.abs(s.off[1]) + Math.abs(s.off[2]) + Math.abs(s.ax) + Math.abs(s.az);
            if(mag < EPS || (s.kind === 'prop' && !ctx.physics?.props.has(key)) || (s.kind === 'actor' && !key.alive)) { this.state.delete(key); continue; }
            if(s.kind === 'prop') {
                if(!key.mesh) continue;
                const m = key.mesh;
                _c.copy(m.position);
                _q.setFromEuler(_e.set(s.ax, 0, s.az));
                m.position.set(_c.x + s.off[0], _c.y + s.off[1], _c.z + s.off[2]);
                m.quaternion.premultiply(_q);
            } else this._shift(key, s.off);
        }
        this.stats.riders = props.length + actors.length;
        this.stats.maxOffset = maxOff;
        const ms = performance.now() - t0;
        this.stats.ms = this.stats.ms * 0.95 + ms * 0.05; this.stats.msMax = Math.max(this.stats.msMax * 0.999, ms);
        if(this.stats.riders) { this.stats.peakRiders = Math.max(this.stats.peakRiders || 0, this.stats.riders); }
    }

    /** An actor's drawn body (its mesh), or the camera for the local player in first person. */
    _shift(a, off) {
        if(a.isPlayer && a === this.ctx.game?.player && a.alive) this.ctx.camera.position.add(_v.set(off[0], off[1], off[2]));
        else if(a.mesh?.visible) a.mesh.position.add(_v.set(off[0], off[1], off[2]));
    }
}

const clamp = (v, m) => Math.max(-m, Math.min(m, v));
```

### src/soft/SoftGround.js
```
/**
 * Soft ground ("wake zones"): impacts wake a local box of terrain into a tetrahedral lattice simulated on the GPU
 * (src/soft/SoftSim.js); the terrain chunk meshes are deformed in the vertex shader by the zone's node displacements
 * (no remeshing while it wobbles). When a zone has been calm for its lifetime it sleeps: elastic matter has sprung
 * back; plastic matter (dirt, grass, sand, snow) keeps its deformation, which is read back once and baked into the
 * SDF (src/soft/core.js bakeZone, in the softZone worker), so mounds and dents stay and remain diggable.
 *
 * Wakes: Destruction edits with an impulse (blasts, punches: destruction.editHooks), the player's hard landings
 * ('land'), voxel bodies hitting the ground ('impact'). `ctx.soft.wake(point, { speed, radius, dir, up })` for others.
 *
 * Zone lifecycle: building (worker classifies the lattice) -> sim -> [frozen: read back + bake in the worker ->
 * remesh: wait until every chunk mesh in the box has been rebuilt from the baked SDF; those new meshes skip the
 * zone's displacement] -> free.
 *
 * Backends: WebGPU runs the solver in compute shaders (SoftSim); WebGL2 (no compute, no storage buffers in the vertex
 * stage) or `?softCpu=1` runs it in a worker and feeds the vertex stage through a float texture (SoftSimCPU).
 * `?soft=0`: off. `?tets=1` or F7: draw the zones' tetrahedra (TetOverlay). `label`: which one is running.
 *
 * Tuning (ctx.soft): subDt, maxSubsteps, life, settle.
 */
import { VOXEL, BEDROCK_SAMPLES } from '../config.js';
import { MATERIALS } from '../voxel/materials.js';
import { enableTerrainDeform } from '../render/terrainMaterial.js';
import { SPC, SPC_FAR, MAX_ZONES, CELL_CAP, maxDim } from './core.js';
import { SoftSim, MAX_KICKS } from './SoftSim.js';
import { SoftSimCPU } from './SoftSimCPU.js';
import { TetOverlay } from './TetOverlay.js';
import { GroundRider } from './GroundRider.js';
import { readRegion, chunkVersions, applyPatch } from './region.js';

const BAKE_MIN = 0.012; // m: smaller final displacements are dropped instead of baked
const MAX_FAR = 2;      // far zones at once (the CPU solver's budget)
const RINGS = 4, RING_DT = 0.07; // a blast's ripple: rings of kicks, s apart

export class SoftGround {
    constructor(ctx) {
        this.ctx = ctx;
        this.enabled = false;
        this.zones = [];                  // live zones
        this.slots = new Array(MAX_ZONES).fill(null);
        this.kicks = [];
        this.deferred = [];
        this.timed = [];                  // ripple rings waiting for their time: { t, point, opts }
        this.subDt = 1 / 240;
        this.maxSubsteps = 4;
        this.acc = 0;
        this.settle = 0.5;                // s of extra damping before a zone sleeps
        this.stats = { zones: 0, built: 0, baked: 0, dispatches: 0, buildMs: 0, cpuMs: 0, cpuMax: 0, lastBake: null };
        ctx.soft = this;
        this.label = 'off';
        const backend = ctx.renderer.backend, params = ctx.params;
        if(params.get('soft') === '0') { this.label = 'off (?soft=0)'; console.log('soft ground: off (?soft=0)'); return; }
        // GPU compute on WebGPU; elsewhere (WebGL2, or ?softCpu=1) the CPU solver in a worker
        const gpu = backend?.isWebGPUBackend && params.get('softCpu') !== '1';
        try {
            if(gpu) this.sim = new SoftSim(ctx.renderer, { iterations: +(params.get('softIters') || 4) });
            else {
                this.sim = new SoftSimCPU(ctx.renderer, { iterations: +(params.get('softIters') || 2), rate: 60 });
                this.subDt = 1 / 60; this.maxSubsteps = 2;
            }
            enableTerrainDeform(ctx.terrain.material, (skip) => this.sim.deformNode(skip));
            this.sim.warmup();
        } catch(e) { this.label = 'off (error)'; console.error('soft ground: disabled', e); return; }
        this.label = gpu ? 'GPU compute' : 'CPU worker';
        console.log(`soft ground: ${this.label}`);
        this.enabled = true;
        this.overlay = new TetOverlay(ctx.scene, this.sim);
        // things resting on the ground ride its wobble (drawn pose only); ?ride=0: off
        if(params.get('ride') !== '0') this.rider = new GroundRider(this);
        if(params.get('tets') === '1') this.overlay.setVisible(true);
        addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
            if(e.code !== 'F7' || e.repeat) return;
            e.preventDefault();
            this.overlay.setVisible(!this.overlay.visible);
            for(const z of this.zones) if(z.lattice) this.overlay.set(z.slot, z);
        });
        this.bedrockY = ctx.world.smin[1] + BEDROCK_SAMPLES;
        readRegion(ctx.world, [0, 0, 0], [40, 24, 40]); // warm the JIT: the first wake should not hitch
        this.worker = new Worker(new URL('./softZone.js', import.meta.url), { type: 'module' });
        this.jobs = new Map(); this.jobId = 1;
        this.worker.onmessage = (e) => { const j = this.jobs.get(e.data.id); this.jobs.delete(e.data.id); if(e.data.error) console.error('softZone', e.data.error); j?.(e.data); };
        // wakes
        ctx.destruction.editHooks?.push((e) => this._onEdit(e));
        ctx.events.addEventListener('land', (e) => {
            const a = e.detail.actor, sp = e.detail.speed;
            if(!a?.body) return;
            const f = a.body.feet;
            this.wake([f.x, f.y - 0.1, f.z], { speed: Math.min(11, (sp - 6) * 0.7), radius: 1.8 + sp * 0.05, dir: [0, -1, 0] });
        });
        ctx.events.addEventListener('impact', (e) => {
            const { point, speed, prop } = e.detail;
            if(!point || speed < 4) return;
            if(ctx.world.sample(point[0], point[1], point[2]) > 0.6) return; // body against body, in the air
            const m = prop?.userData?.mass ?? 200;
            this.wake(point, { speed: Math.min(9, speed * 0.4 * Math.min(2, Math.cbrt(m / 200))), radius: 1.4 + Math.min(1.8, Math.cbrt(m) * 0.1) });
        });
        ctx.terrain.onChunkMesh((key, g) => this._onChunkMesh(key, g));
        // before the renderer's listener (which requests the remeshes): which chunks are being meshed right now
        ctx.world.onChange((r) => this._onWorldChange(r));
        ctx.world.listeners.unshift(ctx.world.listeners.pop());
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ wakes

    _onEdit({ op, center, size, impulse, kind, box, dir }) {
        // a carve or build inside a zone: rebuild that zone's lattice from the new SDF (keeps its motion)
        for(const z of this.zones) if(overlaps(z.box, box)) {
            if(z.state === 'sim' || z.state === 'building') z.refreshAt = 3; // a few frames: the edit may land late
        }
        if(op !== 'carve' || !impulse) return;
        if(kind === 'blast') this.blastWave(center, size, impulse);
        // a punch drives the surface in along the swing; it springs back and wobbles
        else if(dir) this.wake(center, { speed: Math.min(14, 7 + impulse), radius: size * 2.2 + 1.2, dir });
        else this.wake(center, { speed: Math.min(11, 3 + impulse * 0.8), radius: size * 2.4 + 0.8, up: 0.15 });
    }

    /** A blast: a heave around the crater in a far zone (1 m cells, ~12 m around), then rings of upward kicks running
     *  outward through it, so a ripple crosses the ground and walls nearby shudder along their length. */
    blastWave(center, size, impulse) {
        const speed = Math.min(24, 7 + impulse * 0.9);
        this.wake(center, { speed, radius: size * 2.2 + 1.6, up: 0.9, far: true });
        const width = 2.6;
        for(let i = 1; i <= RINGS; i++) {
            const rin = size * 1.1 + (i - 1) * width * 0.8;
            this.timed.push({ t: i * RING_DT, point: [...center], opts: { speed: speed * 0.5 * Math.pow(0.8, i), radius: rin + width, rin, dir: [0, 1, 0], far: true, ring: true } });
        }
    }

    /**
     * Wake the ground around `point` (m): nodes within `radius` get up to `speed` m/s along `dir` (or radially from
     * the point, tilted up by `up`), scaled by each material's softness. `rin` > 0: only a ring band (rin..radius).
     * `far`: a far zone (1 m cells, reaching ~12 m; at most MAX_FAR of them) instead of a near one (0.5 m, ~5 m).
     * `ring`: a ripple's follow-up kick, only for a zone that is already there.
     */
    wake(point, { speed = 4, radius = 2, dir = null, up = 0, rin = 0, far = false, ring = false } = {}) {
        if(!this.enabled || !(speed > 0.2)) return;
        const kick = { p: [...point], speed, radius, dir, up, rin };
        // a live zone holding the point takes the kick (a far wake skips near zones: it reaches past them)
        const inZone = this.zones.find(z => inside(z.box, point, 0.25) && (!far || z.spc === SPC_FAR)) || (ring ? null : this.zones.find(z => inside(z.box, point, 0.25)));
        if(inZone) {
            if(inZone.state === 'sim') { this._kick(inZone, kick); return; }
            if(inZone.state === 'building') { inZone.pendingKicks.push(kick); inZone.life = Math.max(inZone.life, inZone.maxLife); return; }
            if(!ring) this.deferred.push({ point, opts: { speed, radius, dir, up, rin, far }, t: 0 }); // baking: try again shortly
            return;
        }
        if(ring) return;
        if(far && this.zones.filter(z => z.spc === SPC_FAR).length >= MAX_FAR) far = false;
        const spc = far ? SPC_FAR : SPC, h = spc * VOXEL, dim = maxDim(spc);
        // the zone box: cells of h, biased downward (the ground under an impact matters more than the air above);
        // a far zone stands on the ground under the impact (2.5 m into it) and reaches a tower's height above it, so
        // walls and towers shudder whole instead of being pinned where the box would cut them
