 *                                                   -> { op: 'built', id, layout | null }
 *    { op: 'inherit', id, parent, off, frag }       start from the parent's motion (off: id's grid origin in the parent's
 *                                                   grid-local metres)
 *    { op: 'remove', id }
 *    { op: 'step', dt, kicks: [{ id, ...kick }] }   kicks, then fixed 60 Hz substeps over the accumulated time
 *          -> { op: 'stepped', ids, nodes, data (Float32Array RGBA per node, packed), splits: [{ id, labels, cellOf,
 *               cs, dims, at (grid-local m) }], ms }   bodies not in `ids` are asleep (u = 0); a split body stops until removed */
import { buildLattice, SoftLattice } from '../softbody/lattice.js';

const STEP = 1 / 60, ITERS = 2, BUDGET = 5; // ms per message: bodies beyond it wait for the next one
const bodies = new Map();
let acc = 0, rr = 0;

self.onmessage = (e) => {
    const m = e.data;
    try {
        if(m.op === 'add') {
            const lat = buildLattice(m.grid);
            if(lat) { const b = new SoftLattice(lat); b.tough = m.tough > 0 ? b.tough * m.tough : Infinity; bodies.set(m.id, b); } // tough 0: never cracks
            self.postMessage({ op: 'built', id: m.id, layout: lat ? { cs: lat.cs, h: lat.h, origin: lat.origin, nodes: lat.nodes } : null });
        } else if(m.op === 'inherit') {
            const b = bodies.get(m.id), p = bodies.get(m.parent);
            if(b && p) { b.inherit(p, m.off); b.frag = Math.max(1, m.frag || 1); }
        } else if(m.op === 'remove') bodies.delete(m.id);
        else if(m.op === 'step') step(m);
    } catch(err) { self.postMessage({ op: 'error', error: String(err?.stack || err) }); }
};

function step(m) {
    const t0 = performance.now();
    for(const k of m.kicks) { const b = bodies.get(k.id); if(b && !b.split) b.kick(k); }
    acc = Math.min(acc + m.dt, STEP * 3);
    let n = 0;
    while(acc >= STEP && n < 2) { acc -= STEP; n++; }
    if(!n) { self.postMessage({ op: 'stepped', same: true, splits: [] }); return; } // nothing moved (frames faster than 60 Hz)
    const splits = [];
    const list = [...bodies].filter(([, b]) => !b.asleep && !b.split);
    // round robin under the budget: a crowd of wobbling debris slows the wobble down instead of the worker
    for(let k = 0; k < list.length; k++) {
        const [id, b] = list[(k + rr) % list.length];
        if(k && performance.now() - t0 > BUDGET) { rr = (k + rr) % list.length; break; }
        for(let s = 0; s < n && !b.asleep; s++) {
            if(b.step(STEP, ITERS)) {
                const c = b.components();
                if(c.count > 1) {
                    b.split = true;
                    splits.push({ id, labels: c.labels, sizes: c.sizes, cellOf: b.cellOf, cs: b.cs, dims: b.dims, at: b.crackAt });
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    // node displacements of every awake body (asleep: u = 0, not sent)
    const ids = [], nodes = [];
    let total = 0;
    for(const [id, b] of bodies) if(!b.asleep) { ids.push(id); nodes.push(b.nn); total += b.nn; }
    const data = new Float32Array(total * 4);
    let at = 0;
    for(const id of ids) { const b = bodies.get(id); b.writeTexels(data, at); at += b.nn; }
    self.postMessage({ op: 'stepped', ids, nodes, data, splits, ms: performance.now() - t0, awake: list.length }, [data.buffer]);
}
```

### src/workers/softSim.js
```
/** Soft ground CPU solver worker (src/soft/cpuSolver.js), used when there is no WebGPU compute (src/soft/SoftSimCPU.js).
 *  { op: 'init', opts }                                  create the solver ({ iterations, rate })
 *  { op: 'upload', slot, z, init }                       a zone's lattice
 *  { op: 'step', P, substeps, dt, kicks, init, buf }     parameters, then a frame of solver work -> { op: 'frame', buf, ms, work }
 *                                                        (buf: the deformation texture's data, filled and sent back)
 *  { op: 'read', id, slot, nodes }                       -> { op: 'read', id, u } (4 floats per node)             */
import { CpuSoft } from '../soft/cpuSolver.js';
import { MAX_ZONES } from '../soft/core.js';

let sim = null;
self.onmessage = (e) => {
    const m = e.data;
    try {
        if(m.op === 'init') { sim = new CpuSoft(m.opts); sim.warmup(); return; }
        if(m.op === 'upload') { sim.upload(m.slot, m.z, m.init); return; }
        if(m.op === 'step') {
            const t0 = performance.now();
            sim.P.set(m.P);
            for(let s = 0; s < MAX_ZONES; s++) if(m.init & (1 << s)) sim.reset(s);
            const work = m.substeps || m.kicks ? sim.step(m.substeps, m.dt, m.kicks) : 0;
            if(m.buf) sim.write(m.buf);
            self.postMessage({ op: 'frame', buf: m.buf, ms: performance.now() - t0, work }, m.buf ? [m.buf.buffer] : []);
            return;
        }
        if(m.op === 'read') { const u = sim.read(m.slot, m.nodes); self.postMessage({ op: 'read', id: m.id, u }, [u.buffer]); }
    } catch(err) { self.postMessage({ op: 'error', id: m.id, error: String(err?.stack || err) }); }
};
```

### src/workers/softZone.js
```
/** Soft ground worker: classifies zone lattices (tets) and computes plastic bakes off the main thread.
 *  { op: 'classify', id, region, c0, n, bedrockY, shell, spc } -> { id, cells, count, cellIdx, flags, solid }
 *  { op: 'bake', id, region, zone }                 -> { id, patch }                              (src/soft/core.js) */
import { classifyZone, bakeZone } from '../soft/core.js';

self.onmessage = (e) => {
    const m = e.data;
    try {
        if(m.op === 'classify') {
            const r = classifyZone(m.region, m.c0, m.n, m.bedrockY, undefined, { shell: m.shell, spc: m.spc });
            self.postMessage({ id: m.id, ...r }, [r.cells.buffer, r.cellIdx.buffer, r.flags.buffer]);
        } else if(m.op === 'bake') {
            const patch = bakeZone(m.region, m.zone);
            self.postMessage({ id: m.id, patch }, [patch.sdf.buffer, patch.mat.buffer]);
        }
    } catch(err) { self.postMessage({ id: m.id, error: String(err?.stack || err) }); }
};
```

### src/workers/terrain.js
```
/** Terrain worker: runs TerrainCore (src/voxel/TerrainCore.js), the authoritative VoxelWorld and every edit of it.
 *  Messages in order; each reply { id, ... } transfers its chunk arrays and grids. Errors reply { id, error }. */
import { TerrainCore } from '../voxel/TerrainCore.js';

const core = new TerrainCore();
self.onmessage = (e) => {
    const msg = e.data;
    try {
        const t0 = performance.now();
        const { reply, transfer } = core.handle(msg);
        reply.workerMs = performance.now() - t0;
        self.postMessage(reply, transfer);
    } catch(err) {
        self.postMessage({ id: msg.id, type: msg.type, error: String(err?.stack || err) });
    }
};
```

### tools/box3d/bind.c
```
// Flat C binding over box3d for voxel-wars (src/physics/box3d/). Built to wasm by tools/box3d/build.sh.
//
// Bodies and shapes are referred to by int handles: (generation << 17) | slot. Every entry point checks the handle
// against the slot's live generation and does nothing (or returns 0 / -1) for a stale one, so JS can never reach a
// destroyed box3d object. Nothing calls back into JS: events and queries write into static buffers that JS reads
// through typed-array views after the call.
//
// Collision filters are 32-bit (category, mask) pairs (the same bits as the old Rapier groups); a disabled shape keeps
// its filter aside and collides with nothing.
#include "box3d/box3d.h"
#include "box3d/collision.h"
#include "box3d/math_functions.h"
#ifdef BW_NATIVE
#define API
#else
#include <emscripten/emscripten.h>
#define API EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE
#endif
#include <float.h>
#include <math.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#define MAXB 32768
#define MAXS 131072
#define SLOT_BITS 17
#define SLOT_MASK 0x1ffff
#define GEN_MASK 0x3fff
#define XF 8          // floats per body in the transform table: p xyz, q xyzw, awake
#define MAX_HITS 2048
#define HIT 10        // floats (or int bits) per hit event
#define MAX_PLANES 16
#define MAX_IGNORE 32

typedef struct { b3BodyId id; uint16_t gen; uint8_t used; } BSlot;
typedef struct { b3ShapeId id; int body; uint16_t gen; uint8_t used; uint8_t disabled; uint32_t cat, mask; b3MeshData* mesh; b3HullData* hull; } SSlot;

static b3WorldId world;
static int hasWorld = 0;
static BSlot bs[MAXB];
static SSlot ss[MAXS];
static int bFree[MAXB], bFreeN = 0, bHigh = 0;
static int sFree[MAXS], sFreeN = 0, sHigh = 0;
static float xf[MAXB * XF];
static float hits[MAX_HITS * HIT];
static int hitCount = 0;
static float out[64];
static int ignoreList[MAX_IGNORE];
static int ignoreN = 0;
static int meshMedian = 1;

static inline int bslot(int h) {
    if(h < 0) return -1;
    int s = h & SLOT_MASK, g = (h >> SLOT_BITS) & GEN_MASK;
    if(s >= MAXB || !bs[s].used || bs[s].gen != g || !b3Body_IsValid(bs[s].id)) return -1;
    return s;
}
static inline int sslot(int h) {
    if(h < 0) return -1;
    int s = h & SLOT_MASK, g = (h >> SLOT_BITS) & GEN_MASK;
    if(s >= MAXS || !ss[s].used || ss[s].gen != g || !b3Shape_IsValid(ss[s].id)) return -1;
    return s;
}
static inline int bhandle(int s) { return (bs[s].gen << SLOT_BITS) | s; }
static inline int shandle(int s) { return (ss[s].gen << SLOT_BITS) | s; }
static inline int shapeSlotOf(b3ShapeId id) { return (int)(intptr_t)b3Shape_GetUserData(id) - 1; }
static inline int bodySlotOf(b3BodyId id) { return (int)(intptr_t)b3Body_GetUserData(id) - 1; }
static inline b3Filter mkfilter(uint32_t cat, uint32_t mask) { b3Filter f = b3DefaultFilter(); f.categoryBits = cat; f.maskBits = mask; return f; }

static void writeXf(int s) {
    b3WorldTransform t = b3Body_GetTransform(bs[s].id);
    float* o = xf + s * XF;
    o[0] = (float)t.p.x; o[1] = (float)t.p.y; o[2] = (float)t.p.z;
    o[3] = t.q.v.x; o[4] = t.q.v.y; o[5] = t.q.v.z; o[6] = t.q.s;
    o[7] = b3Body_IsAwake(bs[s].id) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ world

API void bw_world_create(float gx, float gy, float gz, float hitThreshold) {
    if(hasWorld) b3DestroyWorld(world);
    b3WorldDef def = b3DefaultWorldDef();
    def.gravity = (b3Vec3){ gx, gy, gz };
    def.hitEventThreshold = hitThreshold;
    // no gyroscopic torque: an oblong piece spinning about its middle axis would flip over and over (Dzhanibekov);
    // physically right, but reads as a wrong centre of gravity in play (Rapier never had it)
    def.isotropicInertiaTolerance = 1e9f;
    world = b3CreateWorld(&def);
    hasWorld = 1;
    memset(bs, 0, sizeof(bs)); memset(ss, 0, sizeof(ss));
    bFreeN = sFreeN = bHigh = sHigh = 0;
    hitCount = 0;
}
API void bw_world_destroy(void) { if(hasWorld) b3DestroyWorld(world); hasWorld = 0; }
API void bw_mesh_median(int on) { meshMedian = on; }
API void bw_set_contact_tuning(float hertz, float damping, float speed) { b3World_SetContactTuning(world, hertz, damping, speed); }
API void bw_set_max_speed(float v) { b3World_SetMaximumLinearSpeed(world, v); }

API float* bw_xf(void) { return xf; }
API float* bw_hits(void) { return hits; }
API float* bw_out(void) { return out; }
API int* bw_ignore(void) { return ignoreList; }
API void bw_ignore_count(int n) { ignoreN = n < 0 ? 0 : n > MAX_IGNORE ? MAX_IGNORE : n; }
API void* bw_malloc(int n) { return malloc(n); }
API void bw_free(void* p) { free(p); }

/** Step; then the transform table rows of every body that moved are refreshed (move events) and the hit events are
 *  copied out. Returns the hit count. */
API int bw_step(float dt, int substeps) {
    b3World_Step(world, dt, substeps);
    b3BodyEvents be = b3World_GetBodyEvents(world);
    for(int i = 0; i < be.moveCount; i++) {
        const b3BodyMoveEvent* e = be.moveEvents + i;
        int s = (int)(intptr_t)e->userData - 1;
        if(s < 0 || s >= MAXB || !bs[s].used) continue;
        float* o = xf + s * XF;
        o[0] = (float)e->transform.p.x; o[1] = (float)e->transform.p.y; o[2] = (float)e->transform.p.z;
        o[3] = e->transform.q.v.x; o[4] = e->transform.q.v.y; o[5] = e->transform.q.v.z; o[6] = e->transform.q.s;
        o[7] = e->fellAsleep ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
    }
    b3ContactEvents ce = b3World_GetContactEvents(world);
    int n = 0;
    for(int i = 0; i < ce.hitCount && n < MAX_HITS; i++) {
        const b3ContactHitEvent* e = ce.hitEvents + i;
        if(!b3Shape_IsValid(e->shapeIdA) || !b3Shape_IsValid(e->shapeIdB)) continue;
        int a = shapeSlotOf(e->shapeIdA), b = shapeSlotOf(e->shapeIdB);
        if(a < 0 || b < 0) continue;
        float* o = hits + n * HIT;
        int* oi = (int*)o;
        oi[0] = shandle(a); oi[1] = shandle(b);
        o[2] = (float)e->point.x; o[3] = (float)e->point.y; o[4] = (float)e->point.z;
        o[5] = e->normal.x; o[6] = e->normal.y; o[7] = e->normal.z;
        o[8] = e->approachSpeed; o[9] = 0;
        n++;
    }
    hitCount = n;
    return n;
}
API int bw_awake_count(void) { return b3World_GetAwakeBodyCount(world); }

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ bodies

/** type 0 static, 1 kinematic, 2 dynamic. Returns a handle or -1. */
API int bw_body_create(int type, float px, float py, float pz, float qx, float qy, float qz, float qw,
                       float vx, float vy, float vz, float wx, float wy, float wz,
                       float linDamp, float angDamp, int bullet, int enabled, float gravityScale) {
    int s;
    if(bFreeN) s = bFree[--bFreeN]; else if(bHigh < MAXB) s = bHigh++; else return -1;
    b3BodyDef bd = b3DefaultBodyDef();
    bd.type = (b3BodyType)type;
    bd.position = (b3Pos){ px, py, pz };
    bd.rotation = (b3Quat){ { qx, qy, qz }, qw };
    bd.linearVelocity = (b3Vec3){ vx, vy, vz };
    bd.angularVelocity = (b3Vec3){ wx, wy, wz };
    bd.linearDamping = linDamp;
    bd.angularDamping = angDamp;
    bd.isBullet = bullet != 0;
    bd.isEnabled = enabled != 0;
    bd.gravityScale = gravityScale;
    bd.userData = (void*)(intptr_t)(s + 1);
    bs[s].id = b3CreateBody(world, &bd);
    bs[s].used = 1;
    bs[s].gen = (uint16_t)((bs[s].gen + 1) & GEN_MASK);
    if(bs[s].gen == 0) bs[s].gen = 1;
    writeXf(s);
    return bhandle(s);
}

static void freeShapeSlot(int s) {
    if(ss[s].mesh) { b3DestroyMesh(ss[s].mesh); ss[s].mesh = NULL; }
    if(ss[s].hull) { b3DestroyHull(ss[s].hull); ss[s].hull = NULL; }
    ss[s].used = 0;
    sFree[sFreeN++] = s;
}

API void bw_body_destroy(int h) {
    int s = bslot(h); if(s < 0) return;
    // the body's shapes go with it: free their slots (and meshes/hulls) first
    int n = b3Body_GetShapeCount(bs[s].id);
    int* slots = NULL;
    if(n > 0) {
        b3ShapeId* ids = (b3ShapeId*)malloc(sizeof(b3ShapeId) * n);
        slots = (int*)malloc(sizeof(int) * n);
        n = b3Body_GetShapes(bs[s].id, ids, n);
        for(int i = 0; i < n; i++) slots[i] = shapeSlotOf(ids[i]);
        free(ids);
    }
    b3DestroyBody(bs[s].id);
    for(int i = 0; i < n; i++) if(slots[i] >= 0 && ss[slots[i]].used && ss[slots[i]].body == s) freeShapeSlot(slots[i]);
    free(slots);
    bs[s].used = 0;
    bFree[bFreeN++] = s;
}
API int bw_body_valid(int h) { return bslot(h) >= 0; }
API void bw_body_set_type(int h, int type) { int s = bslot(h); if(s >= 0) b3Body_SetType(bs[s].id, (b3BodyType)type); }
API int bw_body_type(int h) { int s = bslot(h); return s < 0 ? -1 : (int)b3Body_GetType(bs[s].id); }
API void bw_body_set_transform(int h, float px, float py, float pz, float qx, float qy, float qz, float qw, int wake) {
    int s = bslot(h); if(s < 0) return;
    b3Body_SetTransform(bs[s].id, (b3Pos){ px, py, pz }, (b3Quat){ { qx, qy, qz }, qw });
    if(wake && b3Body_GetType(bs[s].id) != b3_staticBody) b3Body_SetAwake(bs[s].id, true);
    writeXf(s);
}
API void bw_body_set_target(int h, float px, float py, float pz, float qx, float qy, float qz, float qw, float dt) {
    int s = bslot(h); if(s < 0) return;
    b3Body_SetTargetTransform(bs[s].id, (b3WorldTransform){ { px, py, pz }, { { qx, qy, qz }, qw } }, dt, true);
}
API void bw_body_set_linvel(int h, float x, float y, float z, int wake) {
    int s = bslot(h); if(s < 0) return;
    if(wake) b3Body_SetAwake(bs[s].id, true);
    b3Body_SetLinearVelocity(bs[s].id, (b3Vec3){ x, y, z });
}
API void bw_body_set_angvel(int h, float x, float y, float z, int wake) {
    int s = bslot(h); if(s < 0) return;
    if(wake) b3Body_SetAwake(bs[s].id, true);
    b3Body_SetAngularVelocity(bs[s].id, (b3Vec3){ x, y, z });
}
/** out[0..2] linear velocity, out[3..5] angular velocity, out[6] awake, out[7] mass, out[8..10] world centre of mass. */
API int bw_body_get(int h) {
    int s = bslot(h); if(s < 0) return 0;
    b3BodyId id = bs[s].id;
    b3Vec3 v = b3Body_GetLinearVelocity(id), w = b3Body_GetAngularVelocity(id);
    b3Pos c = b3Body_GetWorldCenter(id);
    out[0] = v.x; out[1] = v.y; out[2] = v.z; out[3] = w.x; out[4] = w.y; out[5] = w.z;
    out[6] = b3Body_IsAwake(id) ? 1.0f : 0.0f; out[7] = b3Body_GetMass(id);
    out[8] = (float)c.x; out[9] = (float)c.y; out[10] = (float)c.z;
    return 1;
}
API void bw_body_impulse(int h, float jx, float jy, float jz, float px, float py, float pz, int atPoint, int wake) {
    int s = bslot(h); if(s < 0 || b3Body_GetType(bs[s].id) != b3_dynamicBody) return;
    if(atPoint) b3Body_ApplyLinearImpulse(bs[s].id, (b3Vec3){ jx, jy, jz }, (b3Pos){ px, py, pz }, wake != 0);
    else b3Body_ApplyLinearImpulseToCenter(bs[s].id, (b3Vec3){ jx, jy, jz }, wake != 0);
}
API void bw_body_angular_impulse(int h, float x, float y, float z, int wake) {
    int s = bslot(h); if(s < 0 || b3Body_GetType(bs[s].id) != b3_dynamicBody) return;
    b3Body_ApplyAngularImpulse(bs[s].id, (b3Vec3){ x, y, z }, wake != 0);
}
API void bw_body_set_awake(int h, int awake) { int s = bslot(h); if(s >= 0 && b3Body_GetType(bs[s].id) != b3_staticBody) { b3Body_SetAwake(bs[s].id, awake != 0); xf[s * XF + 7] = awake ? 1.0f : 0.0f; } }
API void bw_body_enable(int h, int on) { int s = bslot(h); if(s < 0) return; if(on) b3Body_Enable(bs[s].id); else b3Body_Disable(bs[s].id); }
API void bw_body_set_damping(int h, float lin, float ang) { int s = bslot(h); if(s < 0) return; b3Body_SetLinearDamping(bs[s].id, lin); b3Body_SetAngularDamping(bs[s].id, ang); }
API void bw_body_set_bullet(int h, int on) { int s = bslot(h); if(s >= 0) b3Body_SetBullet(bs[s].id, on != 0); }
API void bw_body_set_gravity_scale(int h, float g) { int s = bslot(h); if(s >= 0) b3Body_SetGravityScale(bs[s].id, g); }
/** Scale the mass (and inertia) computed from the shapes to `mass`. */
API void bw_body_set_mass(int h, float mass) {
    int s = bslot(h); if(s < 0) return;
    b3MassData md = b3Body_GetMassData(bs[s].id);
    if(!(md.mass > 0.0f) || !(mass > 0.0f)) return;
    float k = mass / md.mass;
    md.mass = mass;
    md.inertia.cx = b3MulSV(k, md.inertia.cx); md.inertia.cy = b3MulSV(k, md.inertia.cy); md.inertia.cz = b3MulSV(k, md.inertia.cz);
    b3Body_SetMassData(bs[s].id, md);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ shapes

static int newShape(int bodySlot, b3ShapeId id, uint32_t cat, uint32_t mask, b3MeshData* mesh, b3HullData* hull) {
    int s;
    if(sFreeN) s = sFree[--sFreeN]; else if(sHigh < MAXS) s = sHigh++; else { b3DestroyShape(id, false); if(mesh) b3DestroyMesh(mesh); if(hull) b3DestroyHull(hull); return -1; }
    ss[s].id = id; ss[s].body = bodySlot; ss[s].used = 1; ss[s].disabled = 0; ss[s].cat = cat; ss[s].mask = mask;
    ss[s].mesh = mesh; ss[s].hull = hull;
    ss[s].gen = (uint16_t)((ss[s].gen + 1) & GEN_MASK);
    if(ss[s].gen == 0) ss[s].gen = 1;
    b3Shape_SetUserData(id, (void*)(intptr_t)(s + 1));
    return shandle(s);
}
static b3ShapeDef shapeDef(float density, float friction, float restitution, uint32_t cat, uint32_t mask, int hitEvents, int updateMass) {
    b3ShapeDef sd = b3DefaultShapeDef();
    sd.density = density;
    sd.baseMaterial.friction = friction;
    sd.baseMaterial.restitution = restitution;
    sd.filter = mkfilter(cat, mask);
    sd.enableHitEvents = hitEvents != 0;
    sd.enableContactEvents = false;
    sd.updateBodyMass = updateMass != 0;
    return sd;
}

/** Box (half extents) at a local offset and rotation. */
API int bw_shape_box(int body, float hx, float hy, float hz, float cx, float cy, float cz, float qx, float qy, float qz, float qw,
                     float density, float friction, float restitution, uint32_t cat, uint32_t mask, int hitEvents, int updateMass) {
    int b = bslot(body); if(b < 0) return -1;
    b3Transform t = { { cx, cy, cz }, { { qx, qy, qz }, qw } };
    b3BoxHull box = b3MakeTransformedBoxHull(hx, hy, hz, t);
    b3ShapeDef sd = shapeDef(density, friction, restitution, cat, mask, hitEvents, updateMass);
    return newShape(b, b3CreateHullShape(bs[b].id, &sd, &box.base), cat, mask, NULL, NULL);
}
API int bw_shape_sphere(int body, float r, float cx, float cy, float cz, float density, float friction, float restitution, uint32_t cat, uint32_t mask, int hitEvents, int updateMass) {
    int b = bslot(body); if(b < 0) return -1;
    b3Sphere sp = { { cx, cy, cz }, r };
    b3ShapeDef sd = shapeDef(density, friction, restitution, cat, mask, hitEvents, updateMass);
    return newShape(b, b3CreateSphereShape(bs[b].id, &sd, &sp), cat, mask, NULL, NULL);
}
/** Capsule along y: centres at +-halfHeight. */
API int bw_shape_capsule(int body, float halfHeight, float r, float density, float friction, float restitution, uint32_t cat, uint32_t mask, int hitEvents, int updateMass) {
    int b = bslot(body); if(b < 0) return -1;
    b3Capsule cp = { { 0, -halfHeight, 0 }, { 0, halfHeight, 0 }, r };
    b3ShapeDef sd = shapeDef(density, friction, restitution, cat, mask, hitEvents, updateMass);
    return newShape(b, b3CreateCapsuleShape(bs[b].id, &sd, &cp), cat, mask, NULL, NULL);
}
/** Convex hull of points (xyz f32, body-local). -1 when no hull can be made. */
API int bw_shape_hull(int body, float* pts, int n, float density, float friction, float restitution, uint32_t cat, uint32_t mask, int hitEvents, int updateMass) {
    int b = bslot(body); if(b < 0 || n < 4) return -1;
    b3HullData* hull = b3CreateHull((b3Vec3*)pts, n, 40); // more vertices can exceed the 256 half-edge limit
    if(!hull) return -1;
    b3ShapeDef sd = shapeDef(density, friction, restitution, cat, mask, hitEvents, updateMass);
    // the hull is copied into the world's hull database: ours can go now
    b3ShapeId id = b3CreateHullShape(bs[b].id, &sd, hull);
    b3DestroyHull(hull);
    return newShape(b, id, cat, mask, NULL, NULL);
}
/** Static triangle mesh (world-space vertices, CCW outward) on `body`. The mesh data lives as long as the shape. */
API int bw_shape_mesh(int body, float* verts, int vcount, int* idx, int tcount, float friction, uint32_t cat, uint32_t mask) {
    int b = bslot(body); if(b < 0 || tcount <= 0) return -1;
    b3MeshDef md = { 0 };
    md.vertices = (b3Vec3*)verts;
    md.stride = sizeof(b3Vec3);
    md.vertexCount = vcount;
    md.indices = idx;
    md.triangleCount = tcount;
    md.identifyEdges = true;
    md.weldVertices = false;
    md.useMedianSplit = meshMedian != 0;
    b3MeshData* mesh = b3CreateMesh(&md, NULL, 0);
    if(!mesh) return -1;
    b3ShapeDef sd = shapeDef(0, friction, 0, cat, mask, 0, 0);
    return newShape(b, b3CreateMeshShape(bs[b].id, &sd, mesh, (b3Vec3){ 1, 1, 1 }), cat, mask, mesh, NULL);
}
API void bw_shape_destroy(int h, int updateMass) {
    int s = sslot(h); if(s < 0) return;
    b3DestroyShape(ss[s].id, updateMass != 0);
    freeShapeSlot(s);
}
API int bw_shape_valid(int h) { return sslot(h) >= 0; }
API int bw_shape_body(int h) { int s = sslot(h); return s < 0 ? -1 : bhandle(ss[s].body); }
API void bw_shape_set_filter(int h, uint32_t cat, uint32_t mask) {
    int s = sslot(h); if(s < 0) return;
    ss[s].cat = cat; ss[s].mask = mask;
    if(!ss[s].disabled) b3Shape_SetFilter(ss[s].id, mkfilter(cat, mask), true);
}
API void bw_shape_enable(int h, int on) {
    int s = sslot(h); if(s < 0) return;
    if((!on) == ss[s].disabled) return;
    ss[s].disabled = !on;
    b3Shape_SetFilter(ss[s].id, on ? mkfilter(ss[s].cat, ss[s].mask) : mkfilter(0, 0), true);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ queries

static inline int ignored(int shapeSlot) {
    if(shapeSlot < 0 || ss[shapeSlot].disabled) return 1;
    int bh = bhandle(ss[shapeSlot].body);
    for(int i = 0; i < ignoreN; i++) if(ignoreList[i] == bh) return 1;
    return 0;
}

typedef struct { int hit; int shape; float fraction; b3Pos p; b3Vec3 n; } RayCtx;
static float rayCb(b3ShapeId id, b3Pos point, b3Vec3 normal, float fraction, uint64_t mat, int tri, int child, void* ctx) {
    (void)mat; (void)tri; (void)child;
    RayCtx* c = (RayCtx*)ctx;
    int s = shapeSlotOf(id);
    if(ignored(s) || b3Shape_IsSensor(id)) return -1.0f;
    if(c->hit && fraction >= c->fraction) return c->fraction;
    c->hit = 1; c->shape = s; c->fraction = fraction; c->p = point; c->n = normal;
    return fraction;
}
/** Closest hit along origin + t * dir (t <= maxDist) against shapes accepted by (cat, mask), skipping the ignore list.
 *  out: [hit, shape handle (int bits), distance, px, py, pz, nx, ny, nz] */
API int bw_raycast(float ox, float oy, float oz, float dx, float dy, float dz, float maxDist, uint32_t cat, uint32_t mask) {
    b3QueryFilter f = b3DefaultQueryFilter();
    f.categoryBits = cat; f.maskBits = mask;
    RayCtx c = { 0 };
    b3World_CastRay(world, (b3Pos){ ox, oy, oz }, (b3Vec3){ dx * maxDist, dy * maxDist, dz * maxDist }, f, rayCb, &c);
    out[0] = (float)c.hit;
    if(!c.hit) return 0;
    ((int*)out)[1] = shandle(c.shape);
    out[2] = c.fraction * maxDist;
    out[3] = (float)c.p.x; out[4] = (float)c.p.y; out[5] = (float)c.p.z;
    out[6] = c.n.x; out[7] = c.n.y; out[8] = c.n.z;
    return 1;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ character mover

typedef struct { b3CollisionPlane planes[MAX_PLANES]; b3Pos points[MAX_PLANES]; int shapes[MAX_PLANES]; int count; b3Pos origin; } PlaneCtx;
static bool moverFilter(b3ShapeId id, void* ctx) { (void)ctx; return !ignored(shapeSlotOf(id)) && !b3Shape_IsSensor(id); }
static bool planeCb(b3ShapeId id, const b3PlaneResult* pr, int count, void* ctx) {
    PlaneCtx* c = (PlaneCtx*)ctx;
    int s = shapeSlotOf(id);
    if(ignored(s) || b3Shape_IsSensor(id)) return true;
    for(int i = 0; i < count && c->count < MAX_PLANES; i++) {
        c->planes[c->count] = (b3CollisionPlane){ .plane = pr[i].plane, .pushLimit = FLT_MAX, .push = 0, .clipVelocity = true };
        c->points[c->count] = b3OffsetPos(c->origin, pr[i].point);
        c->shapes[c->count] = s;
        c->count++;
    }
    return true;
}
static void gather(PlaneCtx* pc, b3Pos pos, const b3Capsule* cap, b3QueryFilter f) {
    pc->count = 0; pc->origin = pos;
    b3World_CollideMover(world, pos, cap, f, planeCb, pc);
}
/** Walking: steep planes (normal.y below floorCos, above -0.1) become vertical walls, so they block instead of
 *  being climbed. */
static void wallify(PlaneCtx* pc, float floorCos) {
    for(int i = 0; i < pc->count; i++) {
        b3Plane* p = &pc->planes[i].plane;
        float ny = p->normal.y;
        if(ny >= floorCos || ny < -0.1f) continue;
        float l = sqrtf(p->normal.x * p->normal.x + p->normal.z * p->normal.z);
        if(l < 1e-4f) continue;
        p->normal = (b3Vec3){ p->normal.x / l, 0, p->normal.z / l };
        p->offset = p->offset / l;
    }
}
static int floorIn(const PlaneCtx* pc, float floorCos, float* ny) {
    int f = 0; float best = -2;
    for(int i = 0; i < pc->count; i++) {
        float y = pc->planes[i].plane.normal.y;
        // only planes the capsule actually rests on (pushes it, or touches within a few mm)
        float sep = -pc->planes[i].plane.offset;
        if(y >= floorCos && sep < 0.02f) { f = 1; if(y > best) best = y; }
    }
    *ny = best;
    return f;
}

/**
 * Move a capsule (centre c, half height hh, radius r) by t against the world: collide, solve the contact planes, cast,
 * up to 5 times (box3d's mover recipe). mode bit 2 (cast only): one swept cast, stopping at the first contact (no
 * sliding; a step-down onto a ledge). mode bit 1 (walk): on the ground (no vertical intent beyond `snap`: steep planes are walls,
 * and after the move the capsule is snapped down onto walkable ground up to `snap` metres below). pushMass > 0 pushes
 * dynamic bodies touched at the end (impulse of a body of that mass moving at t / dt).
 * The ignore list (bw_ignore) holds body handles whose shapes are skipped.
 * out: [dx, dy, dz, grounded, floor normal y, plane count]
 */
API void bw_move(float cx, float cy, float cz, float hh, float r, float tx, float ty, float tz, int mode, float floorCos, float snap,
                 float pushMass, float dt, uint32_t cat, uint32_t mask) {
    b3QueryFilter f = b3DefaultQueryFilter();
    f.categoryBits = cat; f.maskBits = mask;
    b3Capsule cap = { { 0, -hh, 0 }, { 0, hh, 0 }, r };
    b3Pos start = { cx, cy, cz }, pos = start;
    b3Pos target = { cx + tx, cy + ty, cz + tz };
    PlaneCtx pc;
    int walk = mode & 1;
    if(mode & 2) {
        b3Vec3 d = { tx, ty, tz };
        float frac = b3World_CastMover(world, pos, &cap, d, f, moverFilter, NULL);
        pos = b3OffsetPos(pos, b3MulSV(frac, d));
    }
    else for(int it = 0; it < 5; it++) {
        gather(&pc, pos, &cap, f);
        if(walk) wallify(&pc, floorCos);
        b3Vec3 want = { (float)(target.x - pos.x), (float)(target.y - pos.y), (float)(target.z - pos.z) };
        b3PlaneSolverResult res = b3SolvePlanes(want, pc.planes, pc.count);
        b3Vec3 d = res.delta;
        float frac = b3World_CastMover(world, pos, &cap, d, f, moverFilter, NULL);
        d = b3MulSV(frac, d);
        pos = b3OffsetPos(pos, d);
        if(b3LengthSquared(d) < 1e-6f) break;
    }
    gather(&pc, pos, &cap, f);
    float ny = -2;
    int grounded = floorIn(&pc, floorCos, &ny);
    if(walk && !grounded && snap > 0) {
        // stay on the ground going downhill and over small drops
        b3Vec3 down = { 0, -snap, 0 };
        float frac = b3World_CastMover(world, pos, &cap, down, f, moverFilter, NULL);
        if(frac < 1.0f) {
            b3Pos p2 = b3OffsetPos(pos, b3MulSV(frac, down));
            PlaneCtx pc2;
            gather(&pc2, p2, &cap, f);
            float ny2;
            if(floorIn(&pc2, floorCos, &ny2)) { pos = p2; pc = pc2; grounded = 1; ny = ny2; }
        }
    }
    if(pushMass > 0 && dt > 0) {
        b3Vec3 vc = { tx / dt, ty / dt, tz / dt };
        float invMassA = 1.0f / pushMass;
        for(int i = 0; i < pc.count; i++) {
            int s = pc.shapes[i];
            if(s < 0) continue;
            b3BodyId bid = bs[ss[s].body].id;
            if(b3Body_GetType(bid) != b3_dynamicBody) continue;
            b3Pos point = pc.points[i];
            b3Vec3 normal = b3Neg(pc.planes[i].plane.normal);
            float invMassB = b3Body_GetInverseMass(bid);
            b3Matrix3 invIB = b3Body_GetWorldInverseRotationalInertia(bid);
            b3Vec3 rB = b3SubPos(point, b3Body_GetWorldCenter(bid));
            b3Vec3 rnB = b3Cross(rB, normal);
            float k = invMassA + invMassB + b3Dot(rnB, b3MulMV(invIB, rnB));
            float nm = k > 0 ? 1.0f / k : 0;
            b3Vec3 vB = b3Add(b3Body_GetLinearVelocity(bid), b3Cross(b3Body_GetAngularVelocity(bid), rB));
            float vn = b3Dot(b3Sub(vB, vc), normal);
            float imp = -nm * vn;
            if(imp <= 0) continue;
            b3Body_ApplyLinearImpulse(bid, b3MulSV(imp, normal), point, true);
        }
    }
    out[0] = (float)(pos.x - start.x); out[1] = (float)(pos.y - start.y); out[2] = (float)(pos.z - start.z);
    out[3] = (float)grounded; out[4] = ny; out[5] = (float)pc.count;
}
```

### src/game/weapons/Arsenal.js
```
/**
 * The player's tools: selection (number keys, wheel, toolbar), cooldowns (exposed for the HUD), and what each does
 * with the fire button. Punch (F / middle mouse) and grab (E / right mouse) work whatever tool is selected.
 *
 *   punch   click: bite a rock out of the terrain ahead, knock bodies and enemies
 *   grab    hold: tear a lump of terrain out (the ground stretches toward the hand, then gives) or grab a loose body,
 *           and carry it overhead; release: throw (it stays a body, and cracks if it hits hard)
 *   rocket  hold: fire rockets (rocket jump by aiming at your feet)
 *   grenade hold to charge, release to throw
 *   drill   hold: dig a walkable tunnel the way you look
 *   mason   click: build the selected shape (press 6 again or the tool's button to switch shape); costs stone
 *   buster  click: one bunker buster per life
 */
import * as THREE from 'three/webgpu';
import { WEAPONS, TOOLS, MASON_SHAPES } from './defs.js';
import { GAME } from '../../level/fortwar.js';

const MASON_GRID = 0.5, MASON_ORIGIN = 0.1;
const _q = new THREE.Quaternion(), _e = new THREE.Euler(), _tgt = { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 };
const snap = (v) => MASON_ORIGIN + Math.round((v - MASON_ORIGIN) / MASON_GRID) * MASON_GRID;

export class Arsenal {
    constructor(game, player) {
        this.game = game;
        this.player = player;
        this.combat = game.combat;
        this.index = 2; // start on the rocket launcher
        this.cool = Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(WEAPONS).map(k => [k, 0]));
        this.charge = 0;           // grenade charge 0..1 while held
        this.charging = false;
        this.masonShape = 0;
        this.masonPreview = null;  // { min, max, ok } for the HUD / ghost
        this.grabFrom = null;      // 'fire' or 'grab': which button holds the rock
        this.tearing = null;       // a lump being torn out of the ground (Combat.startRip)
        this.holdT = 0;
        player.input.onTool((t) => this.select(t));
        // ghost box for the mason
        this.ghost = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1, 1, 1), new THREE.MeshBasicNodeMaterial({ color: 0xffd080, transparent: true, opacity: 0.28, depthWrite: false }));
        this.ghost.visible = false;
        this.ghostEdges = new THREE.LineSegments(new THREE.EdgesGeometry(new THREE.BoxGeometry(1, 1, 1)), new THREE.LineBasicNodeMaterial({ color: 0xffe0a0 }));
        this.ghost.add(this.ghostEdges);
        game.ctx.scene.add(this.ghost);
    }
    get tool() { return TOOLS[this.index]; }
    select(t) {
        let i = t === 'next' ? (this.index + 1) % TOOLS.length : t === 'prev' ? (this.index + TOOLS.length - 1) % TOOLS.length : t;
        if(typeof i !== 'number' || i < 0 || i >= TOOLS.length) return;
        if(i === this.index && TOOLS[i] === 'mason') { this.masonShape = (this.masonShape + 1) % MASON_SHAPES.length; this.game.emit('ui', { sound: 'click' }); return; }
        if(i === this.index) return;
        if(this.charging) { this.charging = false; this.charge = 0; }
        if((this.held || this.tearing) && this.grabFrom === 'fire') this.release(false);
        this.index = i;
        this.game.emit('tool', { tool: this.tool });
    }
    get held() { return this.player.held; }
    ready(k) { return this.cool[k] <= 0; }
    use(k) { this.cool[k] = WEAPONS[k].cooldown; }
    /** Cooldown fraction 0..1 remaining, for the HUD. */
    coolFrac(k) { return Math.max(0, this.cool[k] / WEAPONS[k].cooldown); }

    update(dt, input) {
        const p = this.player;
        for(const k in this.cool) this.cool[k] = Math.max(0, this.cool[k] - dt);
        if(this.game.over) return;
        const tool = this.tool;
        // universal buttons
        if(input.down('punch') && this.ready('punch') && !this.held && !this.tearing) this.doPunch();
        if(input.pressed('grab')) this.grab('grab');
        if(input.released('grab') && this.grabFrom === 'grab') this.release(true);
        // the selected tool on the fire button
        const fireDown = input.down('fire'), firePressed = input.pressed('fire'), fireReleased = input.released('fire');
        switch(tool) {
            case 'punch': if(fireDown && this.ready('punch') && !this.held && !this.tearing) this.doPunch(); break;
            case 'grab':
                if(firePressed) this.grab('fire');
                if(fireReleased && this.grabFrom === 'fire') this.release(true);
                break;
            case 'rocket':
                if(fireDown && this.ready('rocket')) {
                    this.use('rocket');
                    this.combat.fireRocket(p, this.muzzle(), p.aimDir());
                    p.kick(0.07); p.shake(0.18);
                    this.game.emit('fired', { tool });
                }
                break;
            case 'grenade':
                if(firePressed && this.ready('grenade')) { this.charging = true; this.charge = 0; }
                if(this.charging) this.charge = Math.min(1, this.charge + dt / WEAPONS.grenade.charge);
                if(fireReleased && this.charging) {
                    const g = WEAPONS.grenade;
                    this.charging = false;
                    this.use('grenade');
                    this.combat.throwFused(p, 'grenade', p.eye(), p.aimDir(), g.minSpeed + (g.maxSpeed - g.minSpeed) * this.charge);
                    this.charge = 0; p.kick(0.03);
                    this.game.emit('fired', { tool });
                }
                break;
            case 'drill':
                if(fireDown && this.ready('drill')) {
                    this.use('drill');
                    this.combat.dig(p, p.aimDir());
                    p.shake(0.12); p.kick(0.015);
                    this.game.emit('fired', { tool });
                }
                break;
            case 'mason':
                this.updateMasonPreview();
                if(firePressed && this.ready('mason')) this.doBuild();
                break;
            case 'buster':
                if(firePressed && this.ready('buster')) {
                    if(p.busters <= 0) { this.game.emit('hint', { text: 'One bunker buster per life' }); break; }
                    p.busters--;
                    this.use('buster');
                    this.combat.throwFused(p, 'buster', p.eye(), p.aimDir(), WEAPONS.buster.speed);
                    p.kick(0.06); p.shake(0.2);
                    this.game.emit('fired', { tool });
                }
                break;
        }
        this.ghost.visible = tool === 'mason' && !!this.masonPreview;
        if(this.tearing) this.updateTear(dt);
        if(this.held) this.updateHeld(dt);
    }

    muzzle() { const e = this.player.eye(), d = this.player.aimDir(); const s = Math.sin(this.player.yaw), c = Math.cos(this.player.yaw); return [e[0] + c * 0.18, e[1] - 0.12, e[2] - s * 0.18].map((v, i) => v + d[i] * 0.1); }

    doPunch() {
        const p = this.player;
        this.use('punch');
        const r = this.combat.punch(p, p.eye(), p.aimDir());
        p.shake(r === 'air' ? 0.04 : 0.22); p.kick(r === 'air' ? 0.01 : 0.04);
        this.game.emit('fired', { tool: 'punch', result: r });
    }

    grab(from) {
        const p = this.player;
        if(this.held || this.tearing || !this.ready('grab') || p.carrying) return;
        const r = this.combat.startRip(p, p.eye(), p.aimDir());
        this.use('grab');
        if(!r) { this.game.emit('fired', { tool: 'grab', result: 'miss' }); return; }
        this.grabFrom = from;
        if(r.prop) { this.take(r.prop); return; }
        // the ground: it stretches toward the hand for a moment (harder matter longer), then tears
        this.tearing = { ...r, age: 0, release: null };
        p.shake(0.12);
    }
    /** A frame of tearing a lump out; at the end it is held (and thrown or dropped at once if the button was let go). */
    updateTear(dt) {
        const p = this.player, tr = this.tearing;
        if(!p.alive) { this.tearing = null; this.grabFrom = null; return; }
        tr.age += dt;
        const e = p.eye(), to = [e[0] - Math.sin(p.yaw) * 0.6, e[1] + 1.2, e[2] - Math.cos(p.yaw) * 0.6];
        const d = [to[0] - tr.center[0], to[1] - tr.center[1], to[2] - tr.center[2]], l = Math.hypot(...d) || 1;
        const dir = d.map(v => v / l);
        this.combat.pullRip(tr, dir, dt);
        p.shake(0.02 * dt * 60);
        if(tr.age < tr.dur) return;
        this.tearing = null;
        const prop = this.combat.finishRip(p, tr, dir);
        if(!prop) { this.grabFrom = null; this.game.emit('fired', { tool: 'grab', result: 'miss' }); return; }
        this.take(prop);
        if(tr.release) this.release(tr.release === 'throw');
    }
    take(prop) {
        const p = this.player;
        p.held = prop;
        this.holdT = 0;
        this.combat.hold(prop, true);
        p.shake(0.25);
        this.game.emit('fired', { tool: 'grab', result: 'rip' });
    }
    release(throwIt) {
        if(this.tearing) { this.tearing.release = throwIt ? 'throw' : 'drop'; return; } // as soon as it is out
        const p = this.player, prop = p.held;
        if(!prop) return;
        p.held = null; this.grabFrom = null;
        if(!this.game.ctx.physics.props.has(prop)) return;
        if(throwIt) {
            const d = p.aimDir();
            this.combat.throwHeld(p, prop, d, WEAPONS.grab.throwSpeed);
            p.kick(0.05); p.shake(0.15);
            this.game.emit('fired', { tool: 'throw' });
        } else this.combat.dropHeld(p, prop);
    }
    updateHeld(dt) {
        const p = this.player, prop = p.held;
        if(!this.game.ctx.physics.props.has(prop)) { p.held = null; this.grabFrom = null; return; }
        this.holdT += dt;
        const e = p.eye(), s = Math.sin(p.yaw), c = Math.cos(p.yaw);
        const target = _tgt; Object.assign(target, { x: e[0] - s * 1.05, y: e[1] + 1.3 + Math.sin(this.holdT * 5) * 0.04, z: e[2] - c * 1.05 });
        const b = prop.body, t = b.translation(), k = Math.min(1, dt * 18);
        target.x = t.x + (target.x - t.x) * k; target.y = t.y + (target.y - t.y) * k; target.z = t.z + (target.z - t.z) * k;
        b.setNextKinematicTranslation(target);
        _q.setFromEuler(_e.set(this.holdT * 0.7, p.yaw + this.holdT * 0.4, 0));
        b.setNextKinematicRotation(_q);
    }

    updateMasonPreview() {
        const p = this.player, e = p.eye(), d = p.aimDir();
        const hit = this.combat.raycast(e, d, WEAPONS.mason.maxDistance, p);
        if(!hit) { this.masonPreview = null; return; }
        const shape = MASON_SHAPES[this.masonShape];
        // the shape's x axis runs across the view: swap x/z when looking mostly along x
        const alongX = Math.abs(Math.sin(p.yaw)) > Math.abs(Math.cos(p.yaw));
        const sx = alongX ? shape.size[2] : shape.size[0], sz = alongX ? shape.size[0] : shape.size[2], sy = shape.size[1];
        const n = hit.normal, pt = hit.point;
        // stand it on what we hit (floors) or against it (walls)
        let cx = pt[0] + n[0] * sx / 2, cy = pt[1] + (n[1] > 0.5 ? sy / 2 : n[1] < -0.5 ? -sy / 2 : 0), cz = pt[2] + n[2] * sz / 2;
        if(n[1] > 0.5) { cx = pt[0]; cz = pt[2]; }
        const min = [snap(cx - sx / 2), snap(cy - sy / 2), snap(cz - sz / 2)];
        const max = [min[0] + sx, min[1] + sy, min[2] + sz];
        if(n[1] > 0.5) { min[1] = snap(pt[1]) - MASON_GRID; max[1] = min[1] + sy; } // sunk one cell into the floor, so it is part of it
        const vol = sx * sy * sz;
        this.masonPreview = { min, max, volume: vol, ok: vol <= p.stone + 1e-6 };
        this.ghost.position.set((min[0] + max[0]) / 2, (min[1] + max[1]) / 2, (min[2] + max[2]) / 2);
        this.ghost.scale.set(sx, sy, sz);
        this.ghost.material.color.set(this.masonPreview.ok ? 0xffd080 : 0xff4030);
    }
    doBuild() {
        const p = this.player, pv = this.masonPreview;
        if(!pv) return;
        if(!pv.ok) { this.game.emit('hint', { text: `Not enough stone (${pv.volume.toFixed(0)} needed): dig or punch for more` }); return; }
        this.use('mason');
        const added = this.combat.build(p, pv.min, pv.max, GAME.teams[p.team].material);
        if(!added) { this.game.emit('hint', { text: 'Someone is in the way' }); return; }
        p.stone = Math.max(0, p.stone - pv.volume);
        p.shake(0.12);
        this.game.emit('fired', { tool: 'mason' });
    }
}
```

### src/game/weapons/Combat.js
```
/**
 * The mechanics every weapon shares, used by the player's tools and by the bots alike: explosions (crater, knockback,
 * damage), rockets (integrated projectiles), fused bodies (grenade, bunker buster), punching, digging, building,
 * ripping terrain out and throwing it. All matter changes go through ctx.destruction.
 *
 * Game events emitted (game.emit): 'explosion' { center, radius, kind, owner }, 'rocket' { origin, owner },
 * 'punch' { point, kind: 'terrain'|'prop'|'actor'|'air', mat }, 'dig' { point, volume }, 'build' { center, size },
 * 'rip' { prop }, 'throw' { kind, owner }, 'slam' { point, mat, volume, speed } (a thrown rock lands its hit),
 * 'bounce' { kind, point, impact }.
 *
 * Online (src/game/NetGame.js): only a local actor's (this client's player, the host's bots) weapons have effects.
 * Their rockets and bombs are sent as actions; other clients fly visual copies ({ visual: true }) that vanish where
 * they hit: the owner's explosion arrives as its own event and terrain edit, its damage through the host.
 */
import * as THREE from 'three/webgpu';
import { rock, capsule, box } from '../../voxel/brushes.js';
import { MATERIALS } from '../../voxel/materials.js';
import { WEAPONS, SELF_DAMAGE_SCALE, BOT_DAMAGE_SCALE } from './defs.js';
import { BodyType } from '../../physics/Physics.js';

const _v = new THREE.Vector3();
/** m/s added each frame to the ground under a lump being torn out (src/soft: SoftGround.pull). */
const RIP_PULL = 1.6;
const dist3 = (a, b) => Math.hypot(a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1], a[2] - b[2]);

export class Combat {
    constructor(game) {
        this.game = game;
        this.ctx = game.ctx;
        this.rockets = [];
        this.fused = [];     // { prop, kind, owner, at, born, lastBounce }
        this.thrown = [];    // { prop, owner, born, prevV: [x,y,z], radius }
        this.seed = 11;
        const geo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.07, 0.11, 0.6, 8); geo.rotateX(Math.PI / 2);
        this.rocketGeo = geo;
        this.rocketMat = new THREE.MeshStandardNodeMaterial({ color: 0x40464f, emissive: 0xff7a20, emissiveIntensity: 0.6, roughness: 0.5 });
        this.grenadeMat = new THREE.MeshStandardNodeMaterial({ color: 0x2f3a2a, roughness: 0.6, metalness: 0.3, emissive: 0xff2010, emissiveIntensity: 0 });
        this.busterMat = new THREE.MeshStandardNodeMaterial({ color: 0xe0b020, roughness: 0.5, metalness: 0.4, emissive: 0xff3010, emissiveIntensity: 0 });
        this.rockMats = new Map();
        ctx_events(this);
    }

    // ---- queries ----
    /** Ray against terrain colliders, bodies and actors, skipping the owner's capsule and whatever it holds. */
    raycast(origin, dir, maxDist, owner = null) {
        const physics = this.ctx.physics;
        const hit = physics.raycast(origin, dir, maxDist, { ignore: owner ? owner.ignoreHandles() : null });
        if(!hit) return null;
        const actor = this.game.actorByCollider.get(hit.collider?.handle) || null;
        return { point: hit.point, normal: hit.normal, distance: hit.distance, collider: hit.collider, actor, prop: actor ? null : hit.prop };
    }

    // ---- explosions ----
    explode(center, kind, owner, def = WEAPONS[kind]) {
        const R = def.radius, reach = R * 1.6;
        this.ctx.destruction.blast(center, R, { strength: def.knock * 0.9, author: owner?.name ?? null, seed: this.seed++ });
        for(const a of this.game.actors) {
            if(!a.alive) continue;
            const c = a.body.center;
            const d = Math.hypot(c.x - center[0], c.y - center[1], c.z - center[2]);
            if(d > reach + 0.8) continue;
            const f = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, 1 - (d - 0.8) / reach));
            const dir = d > 1e-3 ? [(c.x - center[0]) / d, (c.y - center[1]) / d, (c.z - center[2]) / d] : [0, 1, 0];
            const k = def.knock * f;
            const self = a === owner ? 1.5 : 1; // rocket jumps should fly
            const impulse = [dir[0] * k * self, Math.max(dir[1], 0.35) * k * 1.15 * self, dir[2] * k * self];
            let dmg = def.damage * f;
            if(a === owner) dmg *= SELF_DAMAGE_SCALE;
            else if(owner && !owner.isHuman && a.isHuman) dmg *= BOT_DAMAGE_SCALE;
            if(owner && a !== owner && owner.team === a.team) dmg = 0; // no friendly fire (knockback still applies)
            this.game.damage(a, dmg, owner, impulse, kind);
        }
        this.game.emit('explosion', { center, radius: R, kind, owner });
    }

    // ---- rockets ----
    fireRocket(owner, origin, dir, { visual = false } = {}) {
        const def = WEAPONS.rocket;
        if(!visual) this.game.netGame?.act({ k: 'rocket', a: owner?.netId, o: [...origin], d: [...dir] }, owner);
        // point blank: born inside the wall ahead it would fly through it
        const pre = this.raycast(origin, dir, 1.2, owner);
        this.game.emit('rocket', { origin, owner });
        if(pre && visual) return;
        if(pre) {
            if(pre.actor) this.game.damage(pre.actor, def.direct * (owner.isHuman ? 1 : BOT_DAMAGE_SCALE), owner, [dir[0] * 4, 2, dir[2] * 4], 'rocket');
            this.explode([pre.point[0] - dir[0] * 0.15, pre.point[1] - dir[1] * 0.15, pre.point[2] - dir[2] * 0.15], 'rocket', owner);
            return;
        }
        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(this.rocketGeo, this.rocketMat);
        mesh.position.set(origin[0] + dir[0] * 1.2, origin[1] + dir[1] * 1.2, origin[2] + dir[2] * 1.2);
        this.ctx.scene.add(mesh);
        this.rockets.push({ pos: mesh.position, vel: new THREE.Vector3(dir[0] * def.speed, dir[1] * def.speed, dir[2] * def.speed), owner, age: 0, mesh, trail: 0, visual });
    }

    updateRockets(dt) {
        const def = WEAPONS.rocket;
        for(let i = this.rockets.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const r = this.rockets[i];
            r.age += dt;
            r.vel.y -= def.gravity * dt;
            const step = _v.copy(r.vel).multiplyScalar(dt);
            const len = step.length();
            const dir = this._rd ??= [0, 0, 0], o = this._ro ??= [0, 0, 0];
            dir[0] = step.x / len; dir[1] = step.y / len; dir[2] = step.z / len;
            o[0] = r.pos.x; o[1] = r.pos.y; o[2] = r.pos.z;
            const hit = this.raycast(o, dir, len + 0.2, r.age < 0.25 ? r.owner : null);
            let at = null;
            if(hit && r.visual) { this.removeRocket(i); continue; }
            if(hit) {
                if(hit.actor && hit.actor !== r.owner && hit.actor.team !== r.owner?.team) this.game.damage(hit.actor, def.direct * (r.owner?.isHuman ? 1 : BOT_DAMAGE_SCALE), r.owner, [dir[0] * 4, 2, dir[2] * 4], 'rocket');
                at = [hit.point[0] - dir[0] * 0.15, hit.point[1] - dir[1] * 0.15, hit.point[2] - dir[2] * 0.15];
            } else if(r.age > def.life || r.pos.y < -30) { this.removeRocket(i); continue; }
            if(at) { this.removeRocket(i); this.explode(at, 'rocket', r.owner); continue; }
            r.pos.add(step);
            r.mesh.lookAt(r.pos.x + step.x, r.pos.y + step.y, r.pos.z + step.z);
            r.trail -= dt;
            if(r.trail <= 0) { r.trail = 0.025; this.game.fx?.trail(r.pos); }
        }
    }
    removeRocket(i) { const r = this.rockets[i]; r.mesh.removeFromParent(); this.rockets.splice(i, 1); }

    // ---- fused bodies ----
    throwFused(owner, kind, origin, dir, speed, { visual = false, id = null, spin = null } = {}) {
        const def = WEAPONS[kind];
        const ng = this.game.netGame;
        if(!visual && ng) {
            id = ng.newId('f');
            spin = [Math.random() * 8 - 4, Math.random() * 8 - 4, Math.random() * 8 - 4];
            const ov = owner?.body?.vel ?? { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 };
            ng.act({ k: 'fused', a: owner?.netId, kind, o: [...origin], d: [...dir], s: speed, id, spin, ov: [ov.x, ov.y, ov.z] }, owner);
        }
        const r = kind === 'buster' ? 0.42 : 0.2;
        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(r, 14, 10), kind === 'buster' ? this.busterMat.clone() : this.grenadeMat.clone());
        mesh.castShadow = true;
        if(kind === 'buster') { const band = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(r * 1.02, r * 0.18, 6, 16), new THREE.MeshStandardNodeMaterial({ color: 0x222222 })); mesh.add(band); }
        const start = [origin[0] + dir[0] * 0.9, origin[1] + dir[1] * 0.9 - 0.15, origin[2] + dir[2] * 0.9];
        const ov = owner?.body?.vel ?? { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 };
        const prop = this.ctx.physics.addBody({ shape: 'sphere', size: r, position: start, mesh,
            velocity: [dir[0] * speed + ov.x * 0.5, dir[1] * speed + 2.5, dir[2] * speed + ov.z * 0.5],
            angularVelocity: spin || [Math.random() * 8 - 4, Math.random() * 8 - 4, Math.random() * 8 - 4],
            density: kind === 'buster' ? 3000 : 2500, restitution: kind === 'buster' ? 0.15 : 0.45, friction: 0.6, userData: { kind } });
        if(id) { prop.userData.netId = id; prop.userData.owner = visual ? owner?.client ?? null : ng?.id ?? null; }
        owner?.ignoreTemp?.(prop.collider.handle, 0.3);
        this.fused.push({ prop, kind, owner, at: def.fuse, age: 0, prevV: [0, 0, 0], tick: 0, visual });
        this.game.emit('throw', { kind, owner });
        return prop;
    }
    updateFused(dt) {
        for(let i = this.fused.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const f = this.fused[i];
            f.age += dt;
            const b = f.prop.body;
            const t = b.translation(), v = b.linvel();
            const dv = Math.hypot(v.x - f.prevV[0], v.y - f.prevV[1], v.z - f.prevV[2]);
            if(dv > 4 && f.age > 0.1) this.game.emit('bounce', { kind: f.kind, point: [t.x, t.y, t.z], impact: Math.min(1, dv / 15) });
            f.prevV[0] = v.x; f.prevV[1] = v.y; f.prevV[2] = v.z;
            // blink faster as the fuse burns down
            const prog = f.age / f.at;
            f.tick -= dt;
            if(f.tick <= 0) { f.tick = Math.max(0.08, 0.5 * (1 - prog)); f.prop.mesh.material.emissiveIntensity = 2.5; this.game.emit('fuse', { kind: f.kind, progress: prog, point: [t.x, t.y, t.z] }); }
            else f.prop.mesh.material.emissiveIntensity *= 0.85;
            // a visual copy waits a little for the owner's explosion (it is removed by NetGame on 'gone')
            if(f.age >= f.at + (f.visual ? 1.5 : 0) || t.y < -30) this.removeFused(i, !f.visual && t.y > -30);
        }
    }
    /** Remove fused body i (exploding it there if `boom`); a visual copy also goes when its owner's one went. */
    removeFused(i, boom = false) {
        const f = this.fused[i], t = f.prop.body.translation();
        this.fused.splice(i, 1);
        this.ctx.physics.removeBody(f.prop);
        f.prop.mesh.geometry.dispose();
        if(!f.visual && f.prop.userData.netId) this.game.netGame?.gone(f.prop.userData.netId);
        if(boom) this.explode([t.x, t.y, t.z], f.kind, f.owner);
    }
    removeFusedById(id) {
        const i = this.fused.findIndex(f => f.prop.userData.netId === id);
        if(i >= 0) this.removeFused(i, false);
    }

    // ---- melee ----
    punch(owner, origin, dir) {
        const def = WEAPONS.punch;
        const hit = this.raycast(origin, dir, def.reach, owner);
        if(!hit) { this.game.emit('punch', { point: [origin[0] + dir[0] * 2, origin[1] + dir[1] * 2, origin[2] + dir[2] * 2], kind: 'air', owner }); return 'air'; }
        if(hit.actor) {
            const a = hit.actor;
            if(a.team !== owner.team) this.game.damage(a, def.damage * (owner.isHuman ? 1 : BOT_DAMAGE_SCALE), owner, [dir[0] * def.knock, 6, dir[2] * def.knock], 'punch');
            else a.body.knock([dir[0] * def.knock * 0.5, 4, dir[2] * def.knock * 0.5]);
            this.game.emit('punch', { point: hit.point, kind: 'actor', owner });
            return 'actor';
        }
        if(hit.prop) {
            const b = hit.prop.body;
            if(b.isDynamic()) {
                const m = b.mass(), s = def.propSpeed * Math.min(1, 60 / Math.max(m, 1)) + 4;
                const j = { x: dir[0] * s * m, y: (dir[1] + 0.35) * s * m, z: dir[2] * s * m }, tq = { x: (Math.random() - 0.5) * m, y: (Math.random() - 0.5) * m, z: (Math.random() - 0.5) * m };
                b.applyImpulse(j, true);
                b.applyTorqueImpulse(tq, true);
                this.game.netGame?.push(hit.prop, j, tq); // the body's owner pushes its copy too
                // a voxel body's soft layer: the far side lags behind the fist (a jiggle at least as big as a 6 m/s hit)
                const vb = hit.prop.userData?.voxelBody, k = Math.max(s, 6);
                if(vb) this.ctx.destruction.bodies?.soft?.impact(vb, hit.point, [dir[0] * k, (dir[1] + 0.35) * k, dir[2] * k], 1);
            }
            this.game.emit('punch', { point: hit.point, kind: 'prop', owner, mat: hit.prop.userData?.mat ?? 10 });
            return 'prop';
        }
        // terrain: bite a rock-shaped chunk out just past the surface
        const p = hit.point;
        const c = [p[0] + dir[0] * 0.35, p[1] + dir[1] * 0.35, p[2] + dir[2] * 0.35];
        const mat = this.ctx.world.materialAt(p[0] + dir[0] * 0.1, p[1] + dir[1] * 0.1, p[2] + dir[2] * 0.1);
        const res = this.ctx.destruction.carve(rock(c, def.radius, this.seed++), { author: owner?.name ?? null, impulse: 5, kind: 'carve', dir });
        // the terrain edit runs in the terrain worker: the stone counts once it is done
        res.done.then(r => owner?.addStone?.(r.volume));
        this.game.emit('punch', { point: p, kind: 'terrain', owner, mat, volume: res.volume });
        return 'terrain';
    }

    // ---- drill ----
    dig(owner, dir) {
        const def = WEAPONS.drill, r = def.radius, f = owner.body.feet;
        const a = [f.x, f.y + r - def.sink, f.z];
        const b = [a[0] + dir[0] * def.reach, a[1] + dir[1] * def.reach, a[2] + dir[2] * def.reach];
        const res = this.ctx.destruction.carve(capsule(a, b, r), { author: owner?.name ?? null, kind: 'carve' });
        // (asynchronous with the terrain worker: the volume is known a frame or two later; bots read the last dig's)
        if(owner) res.done.then(r => { owner.addStone?.(r.volume); owner.lastDigVolume = r.volume; });
        const front = [a[0] + dir[0] * (def.reach + r * 0.8), a[1] + dir[1] * (def.reach + r * 0.8), a[2] + dir[2] * (def.reach + r * 0.8)];
        this.game.emit('dig', { point: front, volume: res.volume, owner });
        return res.pending ? (owner?.lastDigVolume ?? 1) : res.volume;
    }

    // ---- mason ----
    /** Add a box of the owner's team stone; returns the volume added (0 if refused). */
    build(owner, min, max, mat) {
        for(const a of this.game.actors) {
            if(!a.alive) continue;
            const c = a.body.center, rr = a.body.radius + 0.05, hh = a.body.halfHeight + a.body.radius;
            if(c.x + rr > min[0] && c.x - rr < max[0] && c.z + rr > min[2] && c.z - rr < max[2] && c.y + hh > min[1] && c.y - hh < max[1]) return 0;
        }
        const res = this.ctx.destruction.carve(box(min, max, 0.06), { op: 'add', mat, author: owner?.name ?? null, kind: 'build' });
        const center = min.map((v, i) => (v + max[i]) / 2);
        this.game.emit('build', { center, size: max.map((v, i) => v - min[i]), owner });
        return Math.max(res.added, 0.01);
    }

    // ---- grab & throw ----
    rockMaterial(mat) {
        let m = this.rockMats.get(mat);
        if(!m) { m = new THREE.MeshStandardNodeMaterial({ color: MATERIALS[mat]?.color ?? 0x888888, roughness: 0.9, flatShading: true }); this.rockMats.set(mat, m); }
        return m;
    }
    /** Rip a lump out of the terrain (or grab a loose body) in front of the owner at once (bots). Returns a held prop
     *  or null. The player tears it out over a moment instead: startRip, pullRip each frame, finishRip. */
    rip(owner, origin, dir) {
        const r = this.startRip(owner, origin, dir);
        if(!r) return null;
        return r.prop || this.finishRip(owner, r, [0, 1, 0]);
    }
    /**
     * Take hold of what is in front of the owner: { prop } for a loose body, or a tear for the ground
     * { point, center, mat, g (soft ground handle or null), dur (s it holds on: harder matter longer) }; null for
     * nothing (or bedrock).
     */
    startRip(owner, origin, dir) {
        const def = WEAPONS.grab;
        const hit = this.raycast(origin, dir, def.reach, owner);
        if(!hit || hit.actor) return null;
        if(hit.prop) {
            const ud = hit.prop.userData || {};
            if(ud.crate || ud.kind) return null;   // crates are carried by walking into them, bombs are not toys
            return hit.prop.body.isDynamic() ? { prop: hit.prop } : null;
        }
        const p = hit.point;
        const c = [p[0] + dir[0] * 0.45, p[1] + dir[1] * 0.45, p[2] + dir[2] * 0.45];
        const mat = this.ctx.world.materialAt(p[0] + dir[0] * 0.1, p[1] + dir[1] * 0.1, p[2] + dir[2] * 0.1);
        const hard = MATERIALS[mat]?.hardness ?? 1;
        if(hard === Infinity) return null;
        const g = this.ctx.soft?.grab?.(c, def.radius) || null;
        return { point: p, center: c, mat, g, dur: g ? 0.14 + 0.07 * Math.min(6, hard) : 0 };
    }
    /** A frame of tearing: the soft ground is dragged along `dir` (toward the hand). */
    pullRip(tear, dir, dt) {
        if(tear.g) this.ctx.soft.pull(tear.g, dir, RIP_PULL * Math.min(2, dt * 60));
    }
    /** The ground lets go: the lump comes out as a body (null if there was nothing), the rim springs back. */
    finishRip(owner, tear, dir) {
        const def = WEAPONS.grab, { point: p, center: c, mat } = tear;
        let prop = null;
        try { prop = this.ctx.destruction.rip?.(c, def.radius, { author: owner?.name ?? null }) || null; } catch(e) { console.warn('rip failed', e); prop = null; }
        if(tear.g) this.ctx.soft.tear(tear.g, dir, 7);
        if(prop) {
            prop.userData ||= {}; prop.userData.mat ??= mat;
            if(tear.g) {
                // it comes out where the stretched ground drew it, still wobbling from the pull
                const t = prop.body.translation(), lift = 0.45;
                prop.body.setTranslation({ x: t.x + dir[0] * lift, y: t.y + dir[1] * lift, z: t.z + dir[2] * lift }, true);
                const vb = prop.userData.voxelBody;
                if(vb) this.ctx.destruction.bodies?.soft?.impact(vb, [c[0] - dir[0], c[1] - dir[1], c[2] - dir[2]], [dir[0] * 8, dir[1] * 8, dir[2] * 8], 1);
            }
            this.game.emit('rip', { prop, point: p, mat });
            return prop;
        }
        // no voxel body came out: carve a lump and make a boulder of what came out
        const res = this.ctx.destruction.carve(rock(c, def.radius, this.seed++), { author: owner?.name ?? null, kind: 'carve' });
        if(res.volume < 0.08) return null;
        let best = 1, bv = 0;
        for(let i = 0; i < res.removed.length; i++) if(res.removed[i] > bv) { bv = res.removed[i]; best = i; }
        const r = Math.max(0.35, Math.min(0.85, Math.cbrt(res.volume * 3 / (4 * Math.PI)) * 0.9));
        const geo = new THREE.DodecahedronGeometry(r, 0);
        const pos = geo.attributes.position; // knobbly: jitter vertices
        for(let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) { const k = 0.8 + Math.random() * 0.35; pos.setXYZ(i, pos.getX(i) * k, pos.getY(i) * k, pos.getZ(i) * k); }
        geo.computeVertexNormals();
        const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, this.rockMaterial(best));
        mesh.castShadow = true;
        prop = this.ctx.physics.addBody({ shape: 'sphere', size: r * 0.92, position: c, mesh, density: Math.min(2200, MATERIALS[best]?.density ?? 1600), friction: 0.8, restitution: 0.15, userData: { boulder: true, mat: best, volume: res.volume } });
        this.game.emit('rip', { prop, point: p, mat: best });
        return prop;
    }
    /** Hold: a kinematic body following `target`. */
    hold(prop, on) {
        const b = prop.body;
        if(on) this.game.netGame?.claim(prop); // online: whoever holds a body simulates it (and streams it)
        b.setBodyType(on ? BodyType.Kinematic : BodyType.Dynamic, true);
        prop.userData ||= {};
        prop.userData.held = on; // VoxelBodies: a held body never settles or fractures
    }
    throwHeld(owner, prop, dir, speed) {
        this.hold(prop, false);
        const b = prop.body, ov = owner.body.vel;
        b.setBullet(true); // fast: continuous collision against other bodies too
        b.setLinvel({ x: dir[0] * speed + ov.x * 0.5, y: dir[1] * speed + 3, z: dir[2] * speed + ov.z * 0.5 }, true);
        // a forward end-over-end tumble (up x dir) with a little wobble, not a random spin on every axis
        const h = Math.hypot(dir[0], dir[2]) || 1, spin = 3 + Math.random() * 2, j = () => Math.random() - 0.5;
        b.setAngvel({ x: dir[2] / h * spin + j(), y: j(), z: -dir[0] / h * spin + j() }, true);
        owner?.ignoreTemp?.(prop.collider.handle, 0.35);
        // VoxelBodies: a thrown body cracks along its soft lattice when it hits hard, it never bursts into rubble
        prop.userData.thrown = true;
        this.thrown.push({ prop, owner, age: 0, prevV: [b.linvel().x, b.linvel().y, b.linvel().z] });
        this.game.emit('throw', { kind: 'rock', owner });
    }
    /** Drop a held body gently. */
    dropHeld(owner, prop) {
        this.hold(prop, false);
        const ov = owner.body.vel;
        prop.body.setLinvel({ x: ov.x, y: ov.y, z: ov.z }, true);
        owner?.ignoreTemp?.(prop.collider.handle, 0.4);
    }
    updateThrown(dt) {
        const def = WEAPONS.grab;
        for(let i = this.thrown.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const t = this.thrown[i];
            t.age += dt;
            if(!this.ctx.physics.props.has(t.prop)) { this.thrown.splice(i, 1); continue; } // cracked into pieces, settled or pulverized
            const b = t.prop.body, p = b.translation(), v = b.linvel();
            const dv = Math.hypot(v.x - t.prevV[0], v.y - t.prevV[1], v.z - t.prevV[2]);
            const speed = Math.hypot(t.prevV[0], t.prevV[1], t.prevV[2]);
            t.prevV[0] = v.x; t.prevV[1] = v.y; t.prevV[2] = v.z;
            if(!t.slammed) {
                let hit = t.slamAt || (t.age > 0.06 && dv > def.shatterSpeed && speed > def.shatterSpeed ? [p.x, p.y, p.z] : null);
                // a direct hit on an enemy
                if(!hit && speed > 8) for(const a of this.game.actors) {
                    if(!a.alive || a === t.owner || a.team === t.owner?.team) continue;
                    const c = a.body.center;
                    if(Math.abs(c.x - p.x) < 1.2 && Math.abs(c.z - p.z) < 1.2 && Math.abs(c.y - p.y) < 1.6) { hit = [p.x, p.y, p.z]; break; }
                }
                if(hit) { t.slammed = true; this.slam(t, hit, Math.max(speed, def.shatterSpeed)); }
            }
            if(t.age > 6 || (t.age > 0.5 && speed < 1.5)) { this.thrown.splice(i, 1); this.game.netGame?.unclaim(t.prop); } // came to rest: plain rubble now (the host's again)
        }
    }
    /** A thrown lump lands its hit: whoever it strikes takes damage and knockback (no blast: the rock stays a body
     *  and cracks only if the hit was hard enough for its lattice). */
    slam(t, point, speed) {
        const def = WEAPONS.grab, vol = t.prop.userData?.volume ?? 1;
        const reach = 1.1 + Math.cbrt(vol) * 0.55, f0 = Math.min(1, speed / def.throwSpeed + 0.3);
        for(const a of this.game.actors) {
            if(!a.alive || a === t.owner) continue;
            const c = a.body.center;
            const d = Math.hypot(c.x - point[0], c.y - point[1], c.z - point[2]);
            if(d > reach + 0.6) continue;
            const f = f0 * Math.min(1, 1.2 - d / (reach + 0.6) * 0.6);
            const dir = d > 1e-3 ? [(c.x - point[0]) / d, (c.y - point[1]) / d, (c.z - point[2]) / d] : [0, 1, 0];
            let dmg = def.damage * f;
            if(t.owner && !t.owner.isHuman && a.isHuman) dmg *= BOT_DAMAGE_SCALE;
            if(t.owner && t.owner.team === a.team) dmg = 0;
            this.game.damage(a, dmg, t.owner, [dir[0] * def.knock * f, Math.max(dir[1], 0.4) * def.knock * f, dir[2] * def.knock * f], 'rock');
        }
        this.game.emit('slam', { point, mat: t.prop.userData?.mat ?? 1, volume: vol, speed });
    }

    update(dt) {
        this.updateRockets(dt);
        this.updateFused(dt);
        this.updateThrown(dt);
    }
}

/** 'impact' from physics: a thrown prop that hits hard lands its blow there. */
function ctx_events(combat) {
    combat.ctx.events.addEventListener('impact', (e) => {
        const d = e.detail; if(!d?.prop) return;
        const t = combat.thrown.find(t => t.prop === d.prop);
        if(t && t.age > 0.06 && d.speed > WEAPONS.grab.shatterSpeed * 0.7) t.slamAt = d.point; // handled in update (not inside the physics step)
    });
}
```

### src/game/weapons/defs.js
```
/** Tool and weapon numbers. Started from WorldParty's rules.js (src/game/rules.js) and tuned for a single-player demo.
 *  Radii and speeds in metres; blast damage falls off linearly to zero at 1.6 radii. */
export const WEAPONS = {
    punch:   { name: 'Punch',          icon: '👊', cooldown: 0.28, reach: 3.0, radius: 1.05, damage: 34, knock: 13, propSpeed: 16 },
    grab:    { name: 'Grab & Throw',   icon: '✊', cooldown: 0.35, reach: 4.2, radius: 0.95, throwSpeed: 36, shatterSpeed: 13, blast: 1.7, damage: 65, knock: 16 },
    rocket:  { name: 'Rocket',         icon: '🚀', cooldown: 0.9,  speed: 46, gravity: 10, radius: 2.4, damage: 70, direct: 40, knock: 18, life: 6 },
    grenade: { name: 'Grenade',        icon: '💣', cooldown: 0.9,  minSpeed: 9, maxSpeed: 27, charge: 1.3, fuse: 2.6, radius: 3.2, damage: 85, knock: 22 },
    drill:   { name: 'Drill',          icon: '⛏️', cooldown: 0.36, radius: 1.6, reach: 1.3, sink: 0.1 },
    mason:   { name: 'Mason',          icon: '🧱', cooldown: 0.3,  budget: 40, regen: 4, digYield: 0.3, maxDistance: 9 },
    buster:  { name: 'Bunker Buster',  icon: '☢️', cooldown: 2.0,  speed: 13, fuse: 4.2, radius: 5.6, damage: 150, knock: 30, perLife: 1 },
};
/** Toolbar order (number keys 1..7). */
export const TOOLS = ['punch', 'grab', 'rocket', 'grenade', 'drill', 'mason', 'buster'];
export const SELF_DAMAGE_SCALE = 0.4;   // rocket jumping hurts, a little
export const BOT_DAMAGE_SCALE = 0.45;   // bots hit softer than you do
/** Mason shapes: a wall across the view, or a block. Sizes in metres (x across, y up, z along the view). */
export const MASON_SHAPES = [
    { name: 'Wall', size: [4, 3, 1] },
    { name: 'Block', size: [2, 2, 2] },
    { name: 'Floor', size: [3, 0.5, 3] },
];
```

### src/physics/box3d/B3.js
```
/**
 * box3d (Erin Catto, MIT; vendor/box3d, built by tools/box3d/build.sh from tools/box3d/bind.c) as a small JS object
 * model: B3World, B3Body, B3Collider. Single-threaded wasm; the SIMD build where wasm SIMD is supported, else the
 * scalar one (both give bit-identical results).
 *
 * Safety: bodies and colliders are int handles checked by the C side (slot + generation), so a call on a removed
 * object is a no-op instead of a crash, and nothing calls back into JS during a step or query.
 *
 * Reads: every body's pose lives in one shared Float32Array (the transform table, 8 floats per body slot: position,
 * quaternion, awake), refreshed after each step from box3d's move events and on every set; translation()/rotation()
 * read it without calling into wasm. Velocities, mass and centre of mass are fetched on demand.
 *
 * Collision groups are the 32-bit (memberships << 16) | filter words Physics.js uses: a pair collides when each one's
 * memberships meet the other's filter.
 */
import createBox3d from '../../../vendor/box3d/box3d.mjs';

const SLOT_MASK = 0x1ffff;
const XF = 8;
const HIT = 10;
export const BodyType = { Fixed: 0, Kinematic: 1, Dynamic: 2 };
const ZERO = new Float32Array(16);

// wasm SIMD feature test (a function returning a v128)
const SIMD_TEST = Uint8Array.of(0, 97, 115, 109, 1, 0, 0, 0, 1, 5, 1, 96, 0, 1, 123, 3, 2, 1, 0, 10, 10, 1, 8, 0, 65, 0, 253, 15, 253, 98, 11);
export const simdSupported = () => { try { return WebAssembly.validate(SIMD_TEST); } catch(e) { return false; } };

let modulePromise = null;
/** Load the wasm module once (`simd`: force a build, default by feature test). */
export function loadBox3d({ simd = simdSupported() } = {}) {
    return modulePromise ??= (async () => {
        const file = simd ? 'box3d-simd.wasm' : 'box3d-scalar.wasm';
        let wasmBinary;
        if(typeof process !== 'undefined' && process.versions?.node && typeof window === 'undefined') {
            const fs = await import('node:fs');
            wasmBinary = fs.readFileSync(new URL(`../../../vendor/box3d/${file}`, import.meta.url));
        } else {
            // bundled code runs from dist/: the wasm is served from vendor/ next to it
            const res = await fetch(new URL(`../vendor/box3d/${file}`, import.meta.url));
            if(!res.ok) throw new Error(`box3d: ${file} ${res.status}`);
            wasmBinary = new Uint8Array(await res.arrayBuffer());
        }
        const M = await createBox3d({ instantiateWasm: (imports, done) => { WebAssembly.instantiate(wasmBinary, imports).then(r => done(r.instance)); return {}; } });
        M.simd = simd;
        return M;
    })();
}

const groupsCat = (g) => (g >>> 16) >>> 0;
const groupsMask = (g) => (g & 0xffff) >>> 0;

export class B3Collider {
    constructor(world, handle, body, groups) {
        this.world = world; this.handle = handle; this.body = body; this.groups = groups;
        this.userData = null;
        this.enabled = true;
    }
    isValid() { return this.handle >= 0 && this.world.M._bw_shape_valid(this.handle) === 1; }
    isSensor() { return false; }
    setCollisionGroups(g) { this.groups = g >>> 0; this.world.M._bw_shape_set_filter(this.handle, groupsCat(g), groupsMask(g)); }
    collisionGroups() { return this.groups; }
    setEnabled(on) { this.enabled = !!on; this.world.M._bw_shape_enable(this.handle, on ? 1 : 0); }
    isEnabled() { return this.enabled; }
}

export class B3Body {
    constructor(world, handle, type) {
        this.world = world; this.handle = handle; this.type = type;
        this.colliders = [];
        this.row = (handle & SLOT_MASK) * XF;
        this.kt = null; this.kr = null; // pending kinematic target (applied at the next step)
        this.enabled = true;
    }
    get M() { return this.world.M; }
    isValid() { return this.M._bw_body_valid(this.handle) === 1; }
    // ---- pose (transform table)
    translation() { const x = this.world.xf(), r = this.row; return { x: x[r], y: x[r + 1], z: x[r + 2] }; }
    rotation() { const x = this.world.xf(), r = this.row; return { x: x[r + 3], y: x[r + 4], z: x[r + 5], w: x[r + 6] }; }
    setTranslation(t, wake = true) {
        const x = this.world.xf(), r = this.row;
        this.M._bw_body_set_transform(this.handle, t.x, t.y, t.z, x[r + 3], x[r + 4], x[r + 5], x[r + 6], wake ? 1 : 0);
    }
    setRotation(q, wake = true) {
        const x = this.world.xf(), r = this.row;
        this.M._bw_body_set_transform(this.handle, x[r], x[r + 1], x[r + 2], q.x, q.y, q.z, q.w, wake ? 1 : 0);
    }
    /** Kinematic: where the body should be after the next step (box3d moves it there by velocity). */
    setNextKinematicTranslation(t) { (this.kt ??= { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }); this.kt.x = t.x; this.kt.y = t.y; this.kt.z = t.z; this.world.kinematic.add(this); }
    setNextKinematicRotation(q) { (this.kr ??= { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, w: 1 }); this.kr.x = q.x; this.kr.y = q.y; this.kr.z = q.z; this.kr.w = q.w; this.world.kinematic.add(this); }
    // ---- velocity, mass (on demand)
    _get() { return this.M._bw_body_get(this.handle) ? this.world.out() : ZERO; }
    linvel() { const o = this._get(); return { x: o[0], y: o[1], z: o[2] }; }
    angvel() { const o = this._get(); return { x: o[3], y: o[4], z: o[5] }; }
    mass() { return this._get()[7]; }
    worldCom() { const o = this._get(); return { x: o[8], y: o[9], z: o[10] }; }
    setLinvel(v, wake = true) { this.M._bw_body_set_linvel(this.handle, v.x, v.y, v.z, wake ? 1 : 0); }
    setAngvel(v, wake = true) { this.M._bw_body_set_angvel(this.handle, v.x, v.y, v.z, wake ? 1 : 0); }
    applyImpulse(j, wake = true) { this.M._bw_body_impulse(this.handle, j.x, j.y, j.z, 0, 0, 0, 0, wake ? 1 : 0); }
    applyImpulseAtPoint(j, p, wake = true) { this.M._bw_body_impulse(this.handle, j.x, j.y, j.z, p.x, p.y, p.z, 1, wake ? 1 : 0); }
    applyTorqueImpulse(t, wake = true) { this.M._bw_body_angular_impulse(this.handle, t.x, t.y, t.z, wake ? 1 : 0); }
    /** Scale the mass from the colliders (and the inertia with it) to `m` kg. */
    setMass(m) { this.M._bw_body_set_mass(this.handle, m); }
    setLinearDamping(d) { this._lin = d; this.M._bw_body_set_damping(this.handle, d, this._ang ?? 0); }
    setAngularDamping(d) { this._ang = d; this.M._bw_body_set_damping(this.handle, this._lin ?? 0, d); }
    setBullet(on) { this.M._bw_body_set_bullet(this.handle, on ? 1 : 0); }
    // ---- state
    isDynamic() { return this.type === BodyType.Dynamic; }
    isKinematic() { return this.type === BodyType.Kinematic; }
    isFixed() { return this.type === BodyType.Fixed; }
    bodyType() { return this.type; }
    setBodyType(type, wake = true) {
        if(type === this.type) return;
        this.type = type;
        this.M._bw_body_set_type(this.handle, type);
        if(type !== BodyType.Kinematic) { this.kt = this.kr = null; this.world.kinematic.delete(this); }
        if(wake && type !== BodyType.Fixed) this.wakeUp();
    }
    isSleeping() { return this.world.xf()[this.row + 7] === 0; }
    wakeUp() { this.M._bw_body_set_awake(this.handle, 1); }
    sleep() { this.M._bw_body_set_awake(this.handle, 0); }
    setEnabled(on) { this.enabled = !!on; this.M._bw_body_enable(this.handle, on ? 1 : 0); }
    isEnabled() { return this.enabled; }
    numColliders() { return this.colliders.length; }
    collider(i) { return this.colliders[i]; }
}

/** One box3d world. */
export class B3World {
    constructor(M, { gravity = [0, -9.81, 0], hitThreshold = 1 } = {}) {
        this.M = M;
        M._bw_world_create(gravity[0], gravity[1], gravity[2], hitThreshold);
        this.xfPtr = M._bw_xf() >> 2;
        this.hitPtr = M._bw_hits() >> 2;
        this.outPtr = M._bw_out() >> 2;
        this.ignPtr = M._bw_ignore() >> 2;
        this.bodies = new Map();    // handle -> B3Body
        this.colliders = new Map(); // handle -> B3Collider
        this.kinematic = new Set(); // bodies with a pending kinematic target
        this.substeps = 4;
        this.scratch = { ptr: 0, bytes: 0 };
        this._xf = null;
    }
    /** The transform table (a view that follows memory growth). */
    xf() {
        const h = this.M.HEAPF32;
        if(!this._xf || this._xf.buffer !== h.buffer) this._xf = h.subarray(this.xfPtr, this.xfPtr + 32768 * XF);
        return this._xf;
    }
    out() { return this.M.HEAPF32.subarray(this.outPtr, this.outPtr + 64); }

    /** Fixed step. Pending kinematic targets are reached at the end of it (and the bodies stop there). Returns the hit
     *  events as { a, b (B3Collider), point [x,y,z], normal, speed (approach, m/s) }. */
    step(dt, hitsOut = null) {
        const M = this.M, kin = this.kinematic;
        if(kin.size) for(const b of kin) {
            const x = this.xf(), r = b.row, t = b.kt, q = b.kr;
            M._bw_body_set_target(b.handle, t ? t.x : x[r], t ? t.y : x[r + 1], t ? t.z : x[r + 2],
                q ? q.x : x[r + 3], q ? q.y : x[r + 4], q ? q.z : x[r + 5], q ? q.w : x[r + 6], dt);
        }
        const n = M._bw_step(dt, this.substeps);
        if(kin.size) {
