```
            for(const b of kin) { b.kt = b.kr = null; M._bw_body_set_linvel(b.handle, 0, 0, 0, 0); M._bw_body_set_angvel(b.handle, 0, 0, 0, 0); }
            kin.clear();
        }
        if(!hitsOut || !n) return n;
        const f = M.HEAPF32, i32 = M.HEAP32, p = this.hitPtr;
        for(let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
            const o = p + k * HIT;
            const a = this.colliders.get(i32[o]), b = this.colliders.get(i32[o + 1]);
            if(!a || !b) continue;
            hitsOut.push({ a, b, point: [f[o + 2], f[o + 3], f[o + 4]], normal: [f[o + 5], f[o + 6], f[o + 7]], speed: f[o + 8] });
        }
        return n;
    }
    awakeCount() { return this.M._bw_awake_count(); }

    createBody({ type = BodyType.Dynamic, position = [0, 0, 0], rotation = [0, 0, 0, 1], linvel = [0, 0, 0], angvel = [0, 0, 0], linearDamping = 0, angularDamping = 0, bullet = false, enabled = true, gravityScale = 1 } = {}) {
        const h = this.M._bw_body_create(type, position[0], position[1], position[2], rotation[0], rotation[1], rotation[2], rotation[3],
            linvel[0], linvel[1], linvel[2], angvel[0], angvel[1], angvel[2], linearDamping, angularDamping, bullet ? 1 : 0, enabled ? 1 : 0, gravityScale);
        if(h < 0) throw new Error('box3d: out of body slots');
        const b = new B3Body(this, h, type);
        b._lin = linearDamping; b._ang = angularDamping; b.enabled = enabled;
        this.bodies.set(h, b);
        return b;
    }
    removeBody(b) {
        if(!this.bodies.delete(b.handle)) return;
        for(const c of b.colliders) this.colliders.delete(c.handle);
        b.colliders.length = 0;
        this.kinematic.delete(b);
        this.M._bw_body_destroy(b.handle);
    }
    _add(body, h, groups) {
        if(h < 0) return null;
        const c = new B3Collider(this, h, body, groups >>> 0);
        body.colliders.push(c);
        this.colliders.set(h, c);
        return c;
    }
    /** Common collider options: { density, friction, restitution, groups, hitEvents, updateMass } */
    createBox(body, half, { center = [0, 0, 0], rotation = [0, 0, 0, 1], density = 1000, friction = 0.6, restitution = 0, groups = 0xffffffff, hitEvents = false, updateMass = true } = {}) {
        return this._add(body, this.M._bw_shape_box(body.handle, half[0], half[1], half[2], center[0], center[1], center[2], rotation[0], rotation[1], rotation[2], rotation[3],
            density, friction, restitution, groupsCat(groups), groupsMask(groups), hitEvents ? 1 : 0, updateMass ? 1 : 0), groups);
    }
    createSphere(body, radius, { center = [0, 0, 0], density = 1000, friction = 0.6, restitution = 0, groups = 0xffffffff, hitEvents = false, updateMass = true } = {}) {
        return this._add(body, this.M._bw_shape_sphere(body.handle, radius, center[0], center[1], center[2], density, friction, restitution,
            groupsCat(groups), groupsMask(groups), hitEvents ? 1 : 0, updateMass ? 1 : 0), groups);
    }
    /** Capsule along y (sphere centres at +-halfHeight). */
    createCapsule(body, halfHeight, radius, { density = 1000, friction = 0.6, restitution = 0, groups = 0xffffffff, hitEvents = false, updateMass = true } = {}) {
        return this._add(body, this.M._bw_shape_capsule(body.handle, halfHeight, radius, density, friction, restitution,
            groupsCat(groups), groupsMask(groups), hitEvents ? 1 : 0, updateMass ? 1 : 0), groups);
    }
    /** Convex hull of body-local points (xyz). Null if no hull could be made. */
    createHull(body, points, { density = 1000, friction = 0.6, restitution = 0, groups = 0xffffffff, hitEvents = false, updateMass = true } = {}) {
        const ptr = this._scratch(points.length * 4);
        this.M.HEAPF32.set(points, ptr >> 2);
        return this._add(body, this.M._bw_shape_hull(body.handle, ptr, points.length / 3, density, friction, restitution,
            groupsCat(groups), groupsMask(groups), hitEvents ? 1 : 0, updateMass ? 1 : 0), groups);
    }
    /** Static triangle mesh (world-space vertices, counter-clockwise outward triangles). */
    createMesh(body, vertices, indices, { friction = 0.9, groups = 0xffffffff } = {}) {
        const vb = vertices.length * 4, ptr = this._scratch(vb + indices.length * 4);
        const M = this.M;
        M.HEAPF32.set(vertices, ptr >> 2);
        M.HEAP32.set(indices, (ptr + vb) >> 2);
        return this._add(body, M._bw_shape_mesh(body.handle, ptr, vertices.length / 3, ptr + vb, indices.length / 3, friction, groupsCat(groups), groupsMask(groups)), groups);
    }
    removeCollider(c, updateMass = false) {
        if(!this.colliders.delete(c.handle)) return;
        const l = c.body.colliders, i = l.indexOf(c);
        if(i >= 0) l.splice(i, 1);
        this.M._bw_shape_destroy(c.handle, updateMass ? 1 : 0);
    }
    _scratch(bytes) {
        const s = this.scratch;
        if(s.bytes < bytes) {
            if(s.ptr) this.M._bw_free(s.ptr);
            s.bytes = Math.max(bytes, s.bytes * 2, 1 << 16);
            s.ptr = this.M._bw_malloc(s.bytes);
        }
        return s.ptr;
    }
    /** Bodies (handles) skipped by the next query / move. */
    setIgnore(bodies) {
        const i32 = this.M.HEAP32;
        let n = 0;
        if(bodies) for(const b of bodies) { if(n >= 32) break; i32[this.ignPtr + n++] = b.handle ?? b; }
        this.M._bw_ignore_count(n);
    }
    /** Closest hit: { collider, distance, point, normal } or null. `ignore`: bodies to skip. */
    castRay(origin, dir, maxDist, { groups = 0xffffffff, ignore = null } = {}) {
        this.setIgnore(ignore);
        const hit = this.M._bw_raycast(origin[0], origin[1], origin[2], dir[0], dir[1], dir[2], maxDist, groupsCat(groups), groupsMask(groups));
        this.M._bw_ignore_count(0);
        if(!hit) return null;
        const f = this.out(), h = this.M.HEAP32[this.outPtr + 1];
        return { collider: this.colliders.get(h) || null, distance: f[2], point: [f[3], f[4], f[5]], normal: [f[6], f[7], f[8]] };
    }
    /**
     * Character mover (tools/box3d/bind.c bw_move): moves a y-axis capsule centred at `c` by `t`, sliding along what it
     * touches (castOnly: stops at the first contact instead). Returns `out` = { x, y, z (the movement), grounded, floorY (normal.y of the ground) }.
     */
    move(c, halfHeight, radius, t, { walk = false, castOnly = false, floorCos = 0.5, snap = 0, pushMass = 0, dt = 0, groups = 0xffffffff, ignore = null } = {}, out = {}) {
        this.setIgnore(ignore);
        this.M._bw_move(c.x, c.y, c.z, halfHeight, radius, t.x, t.y, t.z, (walk ? 1 : 0) | (castOnly ? 2 : 0), floorCos, snap, pushMass, dt, groupsCat(groups), groupsMask(groups));
        this.M._bw_ignore_count(0);
        const f = this.out();
        out.x = f[0]; out.y = f[1]; out.z = f[2]; out.grounded = f[3] > 0; out.floorY = f[4];
        return out;
    }
    free() { this.M._bw_world_destroy(); }
}
```

### tools/bench/box3d/bind.c
```
// Minimal flat C binding over box3d for the voxel-wars evaluation (tools/bench/box3d/README.md).
// Handles are plain ints into a JS-side table; ids never cross as structs. Bodies are referenced by index.
#include "box3d/box3d.h"
#include "box3d/collision.h"
#ifdef BW_NATIVE
#define EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE
#else
#include <emscripten/emscripten.h>
#endif
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

#define MAX_BODIES 4096
static b3WorldId world;
static b3BodyId ground;
static b3BodyId bodies[MAX_BODIES];
static int bodyCount = 0;
#define MAX_MESHES 8192
static b3ShapeId meshShapes[MAX_MESHES];
static b3MeshData* meshData[MAX_MESHES];
static int meshCount = 0;
static int medianSplit = 0;

EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE void bw_create(float gx, float gy, float gz, int sleep) {
    b3WorldDef def = b3DefaultWorldDef();
    def.gravity = (b3Vec3){ gx, gy, gz };
    world = b3CreateWorld(&def);
    meshCount = 0;
    b3World_EnableSleeping(world, sleep != 0);
    b3BodyDef bd = b3DefaultBodyDef();
    ground = b3CreateBody(world, &bd);
    bodyCount = 0;
}

EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE void bw_destroy(void) { b3DestroyWorld(world); }

EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE void bw_mesh_median(int on) { medianSplit = on; }

// static triangle mesh (vertices xyz f32, indices i32); returns its slot (bw_remove_mesh)
EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE int bw_add_mesh(float* verts, int vcount, int* idx, int tcount) {
    b3MeshDef md = { 0 };
    md.vertices = (b3Vec3*)verts;
    md.vertexCount = vcount;
    md.indices = idx;
    md.triangleCount = tcount;
    md.identifyEdges = true;
    md.weldVertices = true;
    md.weldTolerance = 0.001f;
    md.useMedianSplit = medianSplit != 0;
    b3MeshData* mesh = b3CreateMesh(&md, NULL, 0);
    if(!mesh) return -1;
    b3ShapeDef sd = b3DefaultShapeDef();
    // b3CreateMeshShape does not copy: the mesh must outlive the shape (freed in bw_remove_mesh)
    if(meshCount >= MAX_MESHES) return -1;
    meshShapes[meshCount] = b3CreateMeshShape(ground, &sd, mesh, (b3Vec3){ 1, 1, 1 });
    meshData[meshCount] = mesh;
    return meshCount++;
}

EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE void bw_remove_mesh(int i) {
    if(!b3Shape_IsValid(meshShapes[i])) return;
    b3DestroyShape(meshShapes[i], false);
    b3DestroyMesh(meshData[i]);
    meshData[i] = NULL;
}

EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE int bw_add_body(float px, float py, float pz, float vx, float vy, float vz, float wx, float wy, float wz, int bullet) {
    if(bodyCount >= MAX_BODIES) return -1;
    b3BodyDef bd = b3DefaultBodyDef();
    bd.type = b3_dynamicBody;
    bd.position = (b3Pos){ px, py, pz };
    bd.linearVelocity = (b3Vec3){ vx, vy, vz };
    bd.angularVelocity = (b3Vec3){ wx, wy, wz };
    bd.isBullet = bullet != 0;
    bodies[bodyCount] = b3CreateBody(world, &bd);
    return bodyCount++;
}

// box collider (half extents, local centre) on body i
EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE void bw_add_box(int i, float hx, float hy, float hz, float cx, float cy, float cz, float density) {
    b3Transform t = { { cx, cy, cz }, b3Quat_identity };
    b3BoxHull box = b3MakeTransformedBoxHull(hx, hy, hz, t);
    b3ShapeDef sd = b3DefaultShapeDef();
    sd.density = density;
    sd.enableContactEvents = true;
    b3CreateHullShape(bodies[i], &sd, &box.base);
}

EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE void bw_step(float dt, int substeps) { b3World_Step(world, dt, substeps); }

// out: 7 floats per body (p xyz, q xyzw); returns awake count
EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE int bw_read(float* out) {
    int awake = 0;
    for(int i = 0; i < bodyCount; i++) {
        b3WorldTransform x = b3Body_GetTransform(bodies[i]);
        float* o = out + i * 7;
        o[0] = x.p.x; o[1] = x.p.y; o[2] = x.p.z;
        o[3] = x.q.v.x; o[4] = x.q.v.y; o[5] = x.q.v.z; o[6] = x.q.s;
        awake += b3Body_IsAwake(bodies[i]);
    }
    return awake;
}

EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE int bw_contact_begin_count(void) { return b3World_GetContactEvents(world).beginCount; }

EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE void* bw_malloc(int n) { return malloc(n); }
EMSCRIPTEN_KEEPALIVE void bw_free(void* p) { free(p); }
```

### tools/bench/box3d/native.c
```
// Native runner for the scene file written by run.mjs: same binding as the wasm build, prints step cost and a
// transform hash (FNV-1a over the raw float bits) so runs on different instruction paths can be compared.
//   native-avx2 scene.bin [steps]
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <time.h>

void bw_create(float gx, float gy, float gz, int sleep);
int bw_add_mesh(float* verts, int vcount, int* idx, int tcount);
int bw_add_body(float px, float py, float pz, float vx, float vy, float vz, float wx, float wy, float wz, int bullet);
void bw_add_box(int i, float hx, float hy, float hz, float cx, float cy, float cz, float density);
void bw_step(float dt, int substeps);
int bw_read(float* out);

static double now(void) { struct timespec t; clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &t); return t.tv_sec * 1e3 + t.tv_nsec / 1e6; }

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    FILE* fp = fopen(argv[1], "rb");
    fseek(fp, 0, SEEK_END); long n = ftell(fp) / 4; fseek(fp, 0, SEEK_SET);
    float* f = malloc(n * 4); fread(f, 4, n, fp); fclose(fp);
    int steps = argc > 2 ? atoi(argv[2]) : 240;
    int o = 0;
    bw_create(0, -30, 0, 1);
    int nm = (int)f[o++];
    for(int m = 0; m < nm; m++) {
        int nv = (int)f[o++], nt = (int)f[o++];
        float* v = f + o; o += nv * 3;
        int* idx = malloc(nt * 3 * sizeof(int));
        for(int i = 0; i < nt * 3; i++) idx[i] = (int)f[o + i];
        o += nt * 3;
        bw_add_mesh(v, nv, idx, nt);
    }
    int nb = (int)f[o++];
    for(int b = 0; b < nb; b++) {
        float* p = f + o; o += 9;
        int id = bw_add_body(p[0], p[1], p[2], p[3], p[4], p[5], p[6], p[7], p[8], 0);
        int nx = (int)f[o++];
        for(int k = 0; k < nx; k++, o += 7) bw_add_box(id, f[o], f[o + 1], f[o + 2], f[o + 3], f[o + 4], f[o + 5], f[o + 6]);
    }
    float* out = malloc(nb * 7 * 4);
    double worst = 0, t0 = now();
    for(int s = 0; s < steps; s++) { double a = now(); bw_step(1.0f / 60, 4); double d = now() - a; if(d > worst) worst = d; }
    double tt = now() - t0;
    int awake = bw_read(out);
    uint32_t h = 2166136261u; const uint8_t* bytes = (const uint8_t*)out;
    for(int i = 0; i < nb * 7 * 4; i++) { h ^= bytes[i]; h *= 16777619u; }
    float minY = 1e9f; for(int b = 0; b < nb; b++) if(out[b * 7 + 1] < minY) minY = out[b * 7 + 1];
    printf("native bodies %d: %d steps %.0f ms (avg %.2f, worst %.1f), awake %d, min y %.2f, hash %08x\n", nb, steps, tt, tt / steps, worst, awake, minY, h);
    return 0;
}
```
